    var html = '<div class="w116tb' + (st.showf ? ' showf' : '') + '">' +
      '<span class="w116seg">' + [['list', 'List'], ['board', 'Board'], ['cal', 'Calendar']].map(function (v) { return '<a data-act="view" data-v="' + v[0] + '" class="' + (st.view === v[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + v[1] + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      (st.view === 'list' ? '<select data-act="group" title="Group by" aria-label="Group by">' + groups.map(function (g) { return '<option value="' + g[0] + '"' + (st.group === g[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>By ' + g[1].toLowerCase() + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '') +
      '<span class="tog fbtn' + (st.showf ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="fbtn" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-expanded="' + (st.showf ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' + svg('filter') + 'Filters' + (nF ? '<b class="w287fn">' + nF + '</b>' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="flt">' +
      '<span class="tog' + (st.mine ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="mine">&#9679; Mine</span>' +
      '<select data-act="owner" title="Owner"><option value="">Anyone</option><option value="__none"' + (st.owner === '__none' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Unassigned</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '"' + (st.owner === m.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-act="status" title="Status"><option value="">Any status</option>' + STATUSES.map(function (b) { return '<option value="' + b + '"' + (st.status === b ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + b + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-act="project" title="Project"><option value="">Any project</option><option value="__none"' + (st.project === '__none' ? ' selected' : '') + '>No project</option>' + projs.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '"' + (String(st.project) === String(p.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(projLabel(p)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-act="due" title="Due"><option value="">Any due date</option>' + [['overdue', 'Overdue'], ['today', 'Due today'], ['week', 'Due this week'], ['none', 'No due date']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.due === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="tog' + (st.flag ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="flag" title="Needs an executive decision">&#9873; Exec</span>' +
      '<span class="tog' + (st.done ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="done">&#10003; Show done</span>' +
      '<select data-act="sort" title="Sort">' + [['manual', 'Manual order'], ['due', 'By due date'], ['updated', 'Recently updated'], ['title', 'A to Z']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.sort === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="tog" data-act="selall" title="Select every task shown">&#9745; Select all</span>' +
      '</span><span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-act="q" placeholder="Search tasks" aria-label="Search tasks" value="' + E(st.q) + '" />' +
      '</div>';
    if (nSel) {
      html += '<div class="w116bulk"><b>' + nSel + ' selected</b>' +
        '<select data-bulk="status"><option value="">Set status...</option>' + STATUSES.map(function (b) { return '<option value="' + b + '">' + b + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
        '<select data-bulk="owner"><option value="">Set owner...</option><option value="__none">Unassigned</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
        '<input type="date" data-bulk="due" title="Set due date" />' +
        '<select data-bulk="project"><option value="">Move to project...</option><option value="__none">No project</option>' + projs.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + E(projLabel(p)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
        '<button data-bulk="done">Mark done</button><button class="ghost" data-bulk="clear">Clear</button></div>';
    }
    return html;
  }
  /* ---------- wave287: the sidebar, the list's title and the active filters ---------- */
  function activeFilters() {
    var st = B.st, c = [];
    if (!st) return c;
    if (st.mine) c.push(['mine', 'Mine']);
    if (st.owner) c.push(['owner', st.owner === '__none' ? 'Unassigned' : 'Owner: ' + st.owner]);
    if (st.status) c.push(['status', 'Status: ' + st.status]);
    if (st.project) { var fp = st.project === '__none' ? null : projById(st.project); c.push(['project', st.project === '__none' ? 'No project' : 'Project: ' + (fp ? fp.name : st.project)]); }
    if (st.due) c.push(['due', { overdue: 'Overdue', today: 'Due today', week: 'Due this week', none: 'No due date' }[st.due] || st.due]);
    if (st.flag) c.push(['flag', 'Exec']);
    if (st.done && st.list !== 'completed') c.push(['done', 'Showing done']);
    return c;
  }
  function chipsHtml() {
    var c = activeFilters(); if (!c.length) return '';
    return '<div class="w287chips">' + c.map(function (x) { return '<button type="button" class="w287chip" data-act="clr" data-k="' + x[0] + '" title="Remove this filter">' + E(x[1]) + '<span aria-hidden="true">&#10005;</span></button>'; }).join('') +
      (c.length > 1 ? '<button type="button" class="w287clr" data-act="clr" data-k="*">Clear all</button>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function sideHtml(m) {
    var L = B.st.list || 'all';
    var tiles = SMART.map(function (x) {
      var n = m.smart[x.k] || 0;
      return '<button type="button" class="w287sl' + (L === x.k ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="list" data-l="' + x.k + '" style="--c:' + x.c + '" aria-pressed="' + (L === x.k ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' +
        '<span class="ic">' + svg(x.ic) + '</span><b class="n' + (n ? '' : ' z') + '">' + n + '</b><span class="nm">' + x.n + '</span></button>';
    }).join('');
    function prow(p, depth, other) {
      var k = Number(p.id), n = other ? (m.counts[k] || 0) : (m.pcount[k] || 0), away = !other && elsewhere(p);
      var nm = other ? p.name : p.name;
      return '<button type="button" class="w287pr' + ('p' + k === L ? ' on' : '') + (isGen(p) ? ' gen' : '') + (depth ? ' d' + Math.min(depth, 3) : '') + '"' +
        (away ? ' data-w116go="' + E(away) + '" title="' + E(p.name) + ' has its own page"' : ' data-act="list" data-l="p' + k + '" title="' + E(p.name) + '"') + ' data-pid="' + k + '" style="--c:' + pColor(p) + '" aria-pressed="' + ('p' + k === L ? 'true' : 'false') + '">' +
        '<i class="dot"></i><span class="nm">' + E(nm) + (other ? '<small>' + E((T.short && T.short[p.department]) || p.department) + '</small>' : '') + '</span>' +
        (away ? '<span class="n">' + svg('out') + '</span>' : '<span class="n' + (n ? '' : ' z') + '">' + n + '</span>') + '</button>';
    }
    var html = '<aside class="w287side" aria-label="Lists"><div class="w287smart">' + tiles + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w287pl"><div class="w287ph">Projects</div>' + m.ordered.map(function (o) { return prow(o.p, o.depth, false); }).join('');
    if (m.none || L === 'none') html += '<button type="button" class="w287pr' + (L === 'none' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-act="list" data-l="none" style="--c:#797773"><i class="dot"></i><span class="nm">No project</span><span class="n">' + m.none + '</span></button>';
    if (m.other.length) html += '<div class="w287ph q">' + (B.scope.board ? 'Other projects' : 'Other teams\u2019 projects') + '</div>' + m.other.map(function (p) { return prow(p, 0, true); }).join('');
    if (staffOk() && !B.scope.board && T.order.indexOf(B.scope.dept) >= 0) html += '<button type="button" class="w287newp" data-w116act="addproj" data-d="' + E(B.scope.dept) + '">' + svg('plus') + 'New project</button>';
    return html + '</div></aside>';
  }
  function headHtml(m, rows) {
    var L = B.st.list || 'all', name, c, sub = '', icon, open = rows.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }).length, n = L === 'completed' ? rows.length : open;
    if (isProjList(L)) {
      var p = L === 'none' ? null : projById(L.slice(1));
      name = p ? p.name : 'No project'; c = p ? pColor(p) : '#797773';
      icon = '<i class="w287hdot" style="background:' + c + '"></i>';
      var bits = [];
      if (p && p.parent_id) { var par = projById(p.parent_id); if (par) bits.push('In ' + E(par.name)); }
      if (p && isGen(p)) bits.push('The catch-all for ' + E(teamLabel(p.department)) + ' work that is not part of another project.');
      else if (p && p.department !== B.scope.dept) bits.push('A ' + E(teamLabel(p.department)) + ' project. Only ' + E(teamLabel(B.scope.dept)) + '\u2019s tasks show here.');
      if (p && Array.isArray(p.leads) && p.leads.length) bits.push('Led by ' + E(p.leads.join(', ').replace(/, ([^,]*)$/, ' and $1')));
      if (!p) bits.push('Tasks that are not filed in a project yet.');
      if (p) bits.push('<a data-w116go="#project/' + E(p.id) + '">Project page</a>');
      sub = bits.join(' \u00b7 ');
    } else {
      var sd = smartOf(L) || SMART[2]; name = sd.n; c = sd.c; sub = E(sd.sub);
      icon = '<span class="w287hic" style="background:' + c + '">' + svg(sd.ic) + '</span>';
    }
    return '<div class="w287lhead" style="--c:' + ink(c) + '"><div class="w287lrow"><h3' + (String(name).length > 44 ? ' class="long"' : '') + '>' + icon + '<span class="nm">' + E(name) + '<span class="w287lc" title="' + (L === 'completed' ? 'completed' : 'open') + '">' + n + '</span></span></h3>' +
      (L === 'completed' ? '' : '<button type="button" class="new w287new" data-act="new">' + svg('plus') + 'New task</button>') + '</div><p class="w287lsub">' + sub + '</p></div>';
  }
  function emptyHtml(L) {
    var sd = smartOf(L), filtered = activeFilters().length || B.st.q;
    var msg = filtered ? 'Nothing here with these filters.' : (sd ? sd.none : 'Nothing open in this project.');
    return '<div class="w287empty"><span class="ic">' + svg('check') + '</span><b>' + E(msg) + '</b>' + (filtered ? '<button type="button" class="w287clr" data-act="clr" data-k="*">Clear the filters</button>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function render() {
    if (!B.slot || !B.scope || !document.body.contains(B.slot)) return;
    var rows = scopeRows();
    var m = B.model, L = B.st.list || 'all';
    m.emptyList = !rows.length;
    var body;
    if (B.st.view === 'board') body = kanbanHtml(rows);
    else if (B.st.view === 'cal') body = calHtml(rows);
    else {
      var gs = groupsFor(rows); body = (rows.length ? '' : emptyHtml(L)) + '<div class="w287groups">' + gs.map(groupHtml).join('') + '</div>';
      /* a project with its own page (Grants, wave258) closes the All list with wave258's pointer, where its group used to be */
      if (L === 'all' && !B.scope.board) m.own.forEach(function (p) { var away = elsewhere(p); if (!away) return; var lab = away === '#grants' ? 'Grants' : p.name; body += '<div class="w258ptr" data-w258ptr="1"><span class="dot"></span><span>' + E(lab) + ' has its own page now: <a data-w116go="' + E(away) + '">open ' + E(lab) + '</a></span></div>'; });
    }
    var side = B.slot.querySelector('.w287side'), sx = side ? side.scrollLeft : 0, sy = side ? side.scrollTop : 0;
    var nSel = Object.keys(B.sel).length;
    B.slot.innerHTML = '<div class="w287 w287v-' + E(B.st.view) + '">' + sideHtml(m) + '<div class="w287main">' + headHtml(m, rows) +
      '<div class="w116b' + (nSel ? ' w287sel' : '') + '">' + toolbarHtml(rows) + chipsHtml() + body +
      '<p class="w116note">' + rows.length + ' shown \u00b7 ' + m.smart.all + ' open in total. Click a task for its details; drag a task to reorder it, onto another group, or onto a project on the left.</p></div></div></div>';
    side = B.slot.querySelector('.w287side');
    if (side) {
      side.scrollLeft = sx; side.scrollTop = sy;
      if (B.listMoved) { var on = side.querySelector('.on'); if (on && side.scrollWidth > side.clientWidth + 2) { var r0 = side.getBoundingClientRect(), r1 = on.getBoundingClientRect(); if (r1.left < r0.left || r1.right > r0.right) side.scrollLeft += (r1.left - r0.left) - 16; } }
    }
    B.listMoved = false;
    var q = B.slot.querySelector('input[data-act="q"]'); if (q && B.qFocus) { q.focus(); q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); B.qFocus = false; }
    if (B.qaOpen) { var box = B.slot.querySelector('.w116qa[data-qa="' + B.qaOpen + '"]'); if (box) openQa(box); else B.qaOpen = null; }
  }
  function openQa(box) {
    var g = box.closest('.w116g'); if (g) g.classList.remove('closed');
    var add = box.previousElementSibling; if (add && add.classList.contains('w287add')) add.style.display = 'none';
    box.style.display = ''; var inp = box.querySelector('input'); if (inp) inp.focus();
    B.qaOpen = box.getAttribute('data-qa');
  }
  function closeQa(box) {
    box.style.display = 'none'; var inp = box.querySelector('input'); if (inp) inp.value = '';
    var add = box.previousElementSibling; if (add && add.classList.contains('w287add')) add.style.display = '';
    B.qaOpen = null;
  }
  var qTimer = null;

  /* ---------- drawer ---------- */
  function drawerEls() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr'), bk = document.getElementById('w116drb');
    if (!dr) {
      bk = document.createElement('div'); bk.id = 'w116drb'; bk.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer); document.body.appendChild(bk);
      dr = document.createElement('aside'); dr.id = 'w116dr'; dr.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Task'); document.body.appendChild(dr);
      dr.addEventListener('click', drawerClick);
      dr.addEventListener('change', drawerChange);
      dr.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeDrawer(); });
    }
    return { dr: dr, bk: bk };
  }
  function taskById(id) { return (DB_TASKS || []).filter(function (t) { return String(t.id) === String(id); })[0] || null; }
  async function openDrawer(id) {
    var t = taskById(id); if (!t) return;
    var els = drawerEls(); B.drawer = t.id;
    var d = t.department; var ms = members(d); var as = assigneeIds(t);
    var projs = projects().filter(function (p) { return (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.department === d ? 0 : 1) - (b.department === d ? 0 : 1) || projLabel(a).localeCompare(projLabel(b)); });
    var b = bucket(t);
    var owners = ms.map(function (m) { return m.name; }); if (t.owner && owners.indexOf(t.owner) < 0) owners.unshift(t.owner);
    els.dr.innerHTML = '<div class="w116drh">' + stPill(t) + '<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">#' + E(t.id) + ' \u00b7 ' + E((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d) + '</span><button class="x" data-dact="close" title="Close (Esc)">&#10005;</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w116drc">' +
      '<textarea class="ttl" data-dfield="title" rows="1" style="overflow:hidden">' + E(t.title) + '</textarea>' +
      '<div class="w116f">' +
      '<label>Status</label><select data-dfield="status">' + STATUSES.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s + '"' + (b === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Owner</label><select data-dfield="owner"><option value="">Unassigned</option>' + owners.map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + E(n) + '"' + (t.owner === n ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(n) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '<option value="__custom">Type a name...</option></select>' +
      '<label>Assignees</label><div class="chips">' + as.map(function (i) { var m = memberById(i); return m ? '<span class="w116chip">' + av(m, 18) + E(m.name) + '<span class="rm" data-dact="rmas" data-id="' + i + '">&#10005;</span></span>' : ''; }).join('') +
      '<select data-dfield="addas" style="width:auto"><option value="">+ Add</option>' + ms.filter(function (m) { return as.indexOf(Number(m.id)) < 0; }).map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + m.id + '">' + E(m.name) + (m.department !== d ? ' (' + E(T.short[m.department] || m.department) + ')' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' +
      '<label>Due date</label><input type="date" data-dfield="due" value="' + E(t.due_date || '') + '" />' +
      /* wave287: the project is the task's one label (a task with none counts in its team's General); the Workstream field is gone */
      '<label>Project</label><select data-dfield="project"><option value="">No project</option>' + projs.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '"' + (effPid(t) === Number(p.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(projLabel(p)) + (p.department !== d ? ' (' + E(T.short[p.department] || p.department) + ')' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Team</label><select data-dfield="dept" title="Move this task to another team">' + T.order.map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (k === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[k]) || k) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Flags</label><div><label class="chk" style="text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-text)"><input type="checkbox" data-dfield="flag"' + (t.flag ? ' checked' : '') + ' /> Needs an executive decision</label>' +
      '<label class="chk" style="text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-text)"><input type="checkbox" data-dfield="conf"' + (t.confidential ? ' checked' : '') + ' /> Confidential (hidden from the board view)</label></div>' +
      (t.flag ? '<label>Why</label><input type="text" data-dfield="note" value="' + E(t.reason_note || '') + '" placeholder="One line for the executives" />' : '') +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116drs">Comments</div><div id="w116cm"><p class="w116note">Loading...</p></div>' +
      '<div class="w116cmtn"><textarea id="w116cmtx" placeholder="Add a comment..."></textarea><button data-dact="comment">Post</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w116drs">Activity</div><div id="w116acts"><p class="w116note">Loading...</p></div>' +
      '<p class="w116note">Added ' + E(t.opened_at ? String(t.opened_at).slice(0, 10) : 'earlier') + (t.updated_by ? ' \u00b7 last change by ' + E(window.waWho ? waWho(t.updated_by) : t.updated_by) : '') + (t.closed_at ? ' \u00b7 closed ' + E(String(t.closed_at).slice(0, 10)) : '') + '</p>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116drf"><button class="pri" data-dact="toggle">' + (t.done ? 'Reopen' : 'Mark done') + '</button><button data-dact="open">Department page</button><button class="warn" data-dact="del">Delete</button></div>';
    void els.dr.offsetWidth;   /* wave287: the first open slides in too (the closed position is laid out before the open class) */
    els.dr.classList.add('open'); els.bk.classList.add('open');
    var ta = els.dr.querySelector('textarea.ttl');
    if (ta) { var grow = function () { ta.style.height = 'auto'; ta.style.height = (ta.scrollHeight + 2) + 'px'; }; ta.addEventListener('input', grow); setTimeout(grow, 0); }
    loadThread(t);
  }
  async function loadThread(t) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('task_comments').select('*').eq('task_id', Number(t.id)).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(40);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      var el = document.getElementById('w116cm'); if (!el || B.drawer !== t.id) return;
      el.innerHTML = rows.length ? rows.map(function (c) {
        return '<div class="w116cmt"><div class="hd"><b>' + E(c.author || 'someone') + '</b><span>' + E(String(c.created_at || '').slice(0, 10)) + '</span>' + (c.source && c.source !== 'manual' ? '<span class="w116tag ws">' + E(c.source === 'transcript' ? 'meeting' : c.source) + '</span>' : '') + (c.source_ref && c.source !== 'manual' ? '<a data-w116go="#tx/' + E(c.source_ref) + '" style="color:' + GREEN + ';cursor:pointer;font-weight:600">' + E(c.source_label || 'open the meeting') + '</a>' : '') + '</div><div class="bd">' + (window.w138Mark ? window.w138Mark(E(c.body)) : E(c.body)) + '</div>' + (c.quote ? '<div class="qt">&quot;' + E(c.quote) + '&quot;</div>' : '') + '</div>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="w116note">No comments yet.</p>';
      window.__w116cm = window.__w116cm || {}; window.__w116cm[String(t.id)] = rows.length;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      var a = await db.from('change_log').select('field, old_value, new_value, changed_by, changed_at').eq('entity_id', String(t.id)).in('entity_type', ['dept_task', 'task']).order('changed_at', { ascending: false }).limit(20);
      var acts = (a && a.data) || [];
      var el2 = document.getElementById('w116acts'); if (!el2 || B.drawer !== t.id) return;
      el2.innerHTML = acts.length ? acts.map(function (c) { return '<div class="w116act"><b>' + E(c.field) + '</b>' + (c.old_value ? ' ' + E(String(c.old_value).slice(0, 60)) + ' \u2192' : '') + ' ' + E(String(c.new_value || '').slice(0, 80)) + ' <span>\u00b7 ' + E((window.waWho ? waWho(c.changed_by) : c.changed_by) || '') + ' \u00b7 ' + E(String(c.changed_at || '').slice(0, 10)) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="w116note">No recorded changes yet.</p>';
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function closeDrawer() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr'), bk = document.getElementById('w116drb');
    if (dr) dr.classList.remove('open'); if (bk) bk.classList.remove('open');
    B.drawer = null;
  }
  async function drawerClick(e) {
    var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-dact]'); if (!a) return;
    var t = taskById(B.drawer); if (!t) return;
    var act = a.getAttribute('data-dact');
    if (act === 'close') closeDrawer();
    if (act === 'toggle') { await setStatus(t, t.done ? 'Open' : 'Done'); openDrawer(t.id); }
    if (act === 'open') { closeDrawer(); location.hash = '#task/' + t.id; }
    if (act === 'del') delTask(t);
    if (act === 'rmas') { await setAssignees(t, assigneeIds(t).filter(function (i) { return i !== Number(a.getAttribute('data-id')); })); openDrawer(t.id); }
    if (act === 'comment') {
      var tx = document.getElementById('w116cmtx'); var txt = (tx && tx.value || '').trim(); if (!txt) { toast('Write the comment first'); return; }
      var r = await db.from('task_comments').insert({ task_id: Number(t.id), body: txt.slice(0, 800), author: who0(), source: 'manual' });
      if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not save the comment'); return; }
      tx.value = ''; toast('Comment added'); loadThread(t); log(t, 'comment', null, txt.slice(0, 120));
    }
  }
  async function drawerChange(e) {
    var f = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-dfield]'); if (!f) return;
    var t = taskById(B.drawer); if (!t) return;
    var k = f.getAttribute('data-dfield');
    if (k === 'title') { var v = f.value.trim(); if (v && v !== t.title) { var old = t.title; if (await patch(t, { title: v }, [['title', old, v]])) { toast('Title saved'); after(); } } }
    if (k === 'status') { await setStatus(t, f.value); openDrawer(t.id); }
    if (k === 'owner') { var nv = f.value; if (nv === '__custom') { nv = prompt('Owner name:', t.owner || ''); if (nv === null) { openDrawer(t.id); return; } } await setOwner(t, nv); openDrawer(t.id); }
    if (k === 'addas') { if (f.value) { await setAssignees(t, assigneeIds(t).concat([Number(f.value)])); openDrawer(t.id); } }
    if (k === 'due') await setDue(t, f.value || null);
    if (k === 'project') await setProject(t, f.value || null);
    if (k === 'dept') {
      var nd = f.value; if (nd && nd !== t.department) {
        var oldD = t.department; var keepWs = wsList(nd).indexOf(t.subsection || 'General') >= 0 ? (t.subsection || 'General') : 'General';
        var mv = { department: nd, subsection: keepWs === 'General' ? null : keepWs };
        /* wave287: a task in its old team's General goes to the new team's General (another team's General is never right) */
        var og = generalOf(oldD); if (og && effPid(t) === Number(og.id)) { var ng = generalOf(nd); mv.project_id = ng ? Number(ng.id) : null; }
        if (await patch(t, mv, [['department', oldD, nd]])) {
          toast('Moved to ' + ((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[nd]) || nd)); closeDrawer(); after();
          try { if (typeof renderSection === 'function') renderSection(nd); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
        }
      }
    }
    if (k === 'flag') { var on = f.checked; if (await patch(t, { flag: on }, [['flag', String(!on), String(on)]])) { toast(on ? 'Flagged for the executives' : 'Flag removed'); after(); openDrawer(t.id); } }
    if (k === 'conf') { var c = f.checked; if (await patch(t, { confidential: c }, [['confidential', String(!c), String(c)]])) { toast(c ? 'Marked confidential' : 'No longer confidential'); after(); } }
    if (k === 'note') { var n = f.value.trim(); if (await patch(t, { reason_note: n || null }, [['reason_note', t.reason_note, n]])) toast('Saved'); }
  }

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  function onClick(e) {
    if (!B.slot || !B.slot.contains(e.target)) return;
    var a = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
    var rowEl = e.target.closest('.w116r,.w116kd,.w116cp');
    var act = a ? a.getAttribute('data-act') : '';
    if (act === 'view') { B.st.view = a.getAttribute('data-v'); saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'list') { e.preventDefault(); setList(a.getAttribute('data-l')); return; }
    if (act === 'clr') { var ck = a.getAttribute('data-k'); if (ck === '*') { ['owner', 'status', 'project', 'due', 'q'].forEach(function (k) { B.st[k] = ''; }); B.st.mine = false; B.st.flag = false; B.st.done = false; } else if (['owner', 'status', 'project', 'due'].indexOf(ck) >= 0) B.st[ck] = ''; else B.st[ck] = false; saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'fbtn') { B.st.showf = !B.st.showf; saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'selall') { var shown = scopeRows(); var all = shown.length && shown.every(function (t) { return B.sel[t.id]; }); B.sel = {}; if (!all) shown.forEach(function (t) { B.sel[t.id] = 1; }); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'mine') { B.st.mine = !B.st.mine; saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'flag') { B.st.flag = !B.st.flag; saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'done') { B.st.done = !B.st.done; saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (act === 'fold') { var g = a.closest('.w116g'); var k = g.getAttribute('data-g'); B.st.closed = B.st.closed || {}; if (B.st.closed[k]) delete B.st.closed[k]; else B.st.closed[k] = 1; saveState(); g.classList.toggle('closed'); a.setAttribute('aria-expanded', g.classList.contains('closed') ? 'false' : 'true'); return; }
    if (act === 'qa') { e.stopPropagation(); var box = B.slot.querySelector('.w116qa[data-qa="' + a.getAttribute('data-g') + '"]'); if (box) openQa(box); return; }
    if (act === 'new') {
      /* the list's own place to add: No date when grouped by due window, else the first group */
      var tv = B.slot.querySelector('[data-w116tview].on'); if (tv) tv.click();   /* the Tree (wave116t) steps aside first */
      var first = B.slot.querySelector('.w116qa[data-qa="d5"]') || B.slot.querySelector('.w287groups .w116qa') || B.slot.querySelector('.w116qa');
      if (!first && B.st.view !== 'list') { B.st.view = 'list'; saveState(); render(); first = B.slot.querySelector('.w116qa[data-qa="d5"]') || B.slot.querySelector('.w116qa'); }
      if (first) { openQa(first); try { first.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', behavior: reduced() ? 'auto' : 'smooth' }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
      return;
    }
    if (act === 'cal') { var n = Number(a.getAttribute('data-n')); if (n === 0) B.calMonth = new Date(); else { var m = B.calMonth || new Date(); var mm = new Date(m.getFullYear() + '-' + String(m.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-01T12:00:00'); mm.setMonth(mm.getMonth() + n); B.calMonth = mm; } render(); return; }
    if (act === 'more') { B.st.view = 'list'; B.st.group = 'due'; saveState(); render(); return; }
    if (!rowEl) return;
    var tid = rowEl.getAttribute('data-tid'); var t = taskById(tid); if (!t) return;
    if (act === 'sel') { if (a.checked) B.sel[t.id] = 1; else delete B.sel[t.id]; render(); return; }
    if (act === 'toggle') { e.stopPropagation(); complete(t); return; }
    if (act === 'status') { e.stopPropagation(); inlineSelect(a, STATUSES.map(function (s) { return [s, s]; }), bucket(t), function (v) { setStatus(t, v); }); return; }
    if (act === 'owner') { e.stopPropagation(); var opts = [['', 'Unassigned']].concat(members(t.department).map(function (m) { return [m.name, m.name]; })); inlineSelect(a, opts, t.owner || '', function (v) { setOwner(t, v); }); return; }
    if (act === 'due') { e.stopPropagation(); inlineDate(a, t.due_date || '', function (v) { setDue(t, v || null); }); return; }
    openDrawer(t.id);
  }
  function inlineSelect(anchor, opts, cur, cb) {
    if (anchor.querySelector('select')) return;
    var sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.style.cssText = 'font-size:12px;padding:3px 6px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.3);max-width:180px;';
    sel.innerHTML = opts.map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + E(o[0]) + '"' + (o[0] === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(o[1]) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    anchor.innerHTML = ''; anchor.appendChild(sel); sel.focus();
    var done = false;
    sel.addEventListener('change', function () { done = true; cb(sel.value); });
    sel.addEventListener('blur', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!done) render(); }, 150); });
    sel.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); });
  }
  function inlineDate(anchor, cur, cb) {
    if (anchor.querySelector('input')) return;
    var inp = document.createElement('input'); inp.type = 'date'; inp.value = cur; inp.style.cssText = 'font-size:12px;padding:3px 6px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.3);';
    anchor.innerHTML = ''; anchor.appendChild(inp); inp.focus();
    var done = false;
    inp.addEventListener('change', function () { done = true; cb(inp.value); });
    inp.addEventListener('blur', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!done) render(); }, 150); });
    inp.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); });
  }
  function onChange(e) {
    if (!B.slot || !B.slot.contains(e.target)) return;
    var a = e.target.closest('[data-act]'); var bk = e.target.closest('[data-bulk]');
    if (a) {
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-act');
      if (['group', 'owner', 'status', 'project', 'due', 'sort'].indexOf(act) >= 0 && a.tagName === 'SELECT') { B.st[act] = a.value; saveState(); render(); }
      return;
    }
    if (bk) bulk(bk.getAttribute('data-bulk'), bk.value);
  }
  async function bulk(kind, val) {
    var ids = Object.keys(B.sel); if (!ids.length) return;
    if (kind === 'clear') { B.sel = {}; render(); return; }
    if (!val && kind !== 'done') return;
    var ts = ids.map(taskById).filter(Boolean);
    for (var i = 0; i < ts.length; i++) {
      var t = ts[i];
      if (kind === 'status') await patch(t, { status: val, done: val === 'Done', closed_at: val === 'Done' ? new Date().toISOString() : null }, [[val === 'Done' ? 'done' : 'status', bucket(t), val]]);
      if (kind === 'owner') await patch(t, { owner: val === '__none' ? '' : val }, [['owner', t.owner || 'unassigned', val === '__none' ? 'unassigned' : val]]);
      if (kind === 'due') await patch(t, { due_date: val }, [['due_date', t.due_date, val]]);
      if (kind === 'project') await patch(t, { project_id: val === '__none' ? null : Number(val) }, [['project', t.project_id ? projLabel(projById(t.project_id)) : 'none', val === '__none' ? 'none' : projLabel(projById(val))]]);
      if (kind === 'done') await patch(t, { status: 'Done', done: true, closed_at: new Date().toISOString() }, [['done', bucket(t), 'Done']]);
    }
    toast(ts.length + ' task' + (ts.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' updated');
    B.sel = {}; after();
  }
  function onKey(e) {
    if (!B.slot || !B.slot.contains(e.target)) return;
    var qa = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w116qa');
    if (qa && e.target.tagName === 'INPUT') {
      if (e.type !== 'keydown') return;
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.stopPropagation(); closeQa(qa); return; }
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        var gk = qa.getAttribute('data-qa'); var pre = {};
        var gs = groupsFor(scopeRows()); var g = gs.filter(function (x) { return x.key === gk; })[0];
        if (g) pre = g.pre; else if (gk.charAt(0) === 's') pre = { status: gk.slice(1) };
        pre = Object.assign({}, listPre(), pre);   /* wave287: the open list files it (its project, today for Today, the flag for Flagged) */
        var title = e.target.value; e.target.value = '';
        if (String(title || '').trim()) { B.qaOpen = gk; addTask(title, pre); }
      }
      return;
    }
    var tk = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-act="toggle"]');
    if (tk && e.type === 'keydown' && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); var rw = tk.closest('[data-tid]'); var tt = rw && taskById(rw.getAttribute('data-tid')); if (tt) complete(tt); return; }
    var kb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w287add,.w116gh,.fbtn');
    if (kb && e.type === 'keydown' && (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ')) { e.preventDefault(); kb.click(); return; }
    if (e.target.getAttribute && e.target.getAttribute('data-act') === 'q') {
      clearTimeout(qTimer);
      qTimer = setTimeout(function () { B.st.q = e.target.value; B.qFocus = true; render(); }, 220);
    }
  }
  /* drag and drop: rows and cards carry the task id; groups, columns and days are drop targets */
  function onDragStart(e) {
    var el = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w116r,.w116kd,.w116cp'); if (!el || !B.slot || !B.slot.contains(el)) return;
    B.drag = el.getAttribute('data-tid'); el.classList.add('dragging');
    try { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', B.drag); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function onDragEnd() { B.drag = null; if (B.slot) B.slot.querySelectorAll('.dragging,.drop,.over').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('dragging', 'drop', 'over'); }); }
  function onDragOver(e) {
    if (!B.drag || !B.slot || !B.slot.contains(e.target)) return;
    var tgt = e.target.closest('.w116g,.w116kc,.w116cd,.w287pr[data-act="list"][data-pid]'); if (!tgt) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    B.slot.querySelectorAll('.drop').forEach(function (x) { if (x !== tgt) x.classList.remove('drop'); });
    tgt.classList.add('drop');
    var row = e.target.closest('.w116r');
    B.slot.querySelectorAll('.w116r.over').forEach(function (x) { if (x !== row) x.classList.remove('over'); });
    if (row && row.getAttribute('data-tid') !== B.drag) row.classList.add('over');
  }
  async function onDrop(e) {
    if (!B.drag || !B.slot || !B.slot.contains(e.target)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var t = taskById(B.drag); var id = B.drag; onDragEnd();
    if (!t) return;
    var pr = e.target.closest('.w287pr[data-act="list"][data-pid]'); if (pr) { await setProject(t, Number(pr.getAttribute('data-pid'))); return; }   /* wave287: drop on a project in the sidebar */
    var col = e.target.closest('.w116kc'); if (col) { await setStatus(t, col.getAttribute('data-kst')); return; }
    var day = e.target.closest('.w116cd'); if (day) { await setDue(t, day.getAttribute('data-day')); return; }
    var g = e.target.closest('.w116g'); if (!g) return;
    var gk = g.getAttribute('data-g'); var gs = groupsFor(scopeRows()); var grp = gs.filter(function (x) { return x.key === gk; })[0]; if (!grp) return;
    var row = e.target.closest('.w116r'); var beforeId = row ? row.getAttribute('data-tid') : null;
    var same = grp.rows.some(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); });
    if (!same) {
      var p = grp.pre;
      if (B.st.group === 'project' || B.st.group === 'workstream') await setProject(t, p.project || null);
      else if (B.st.group === 'status') await setStatus(t, p.status);
      else if (B.st.group === 'assignee') await setOwner(t, p.owner || '');
      else if (B.st.group === 'due') { if (p.due) await setDue(t, p.due); else await setDue(t, null); }
      else if (B.st.group === 'board') await setWs(t, p.ws || 'General');
      grp = groupsFor(scopeRows()).filter(function (x) { return x.key === gk; })[0]; if (!grp) return;
    }
    if (String(beforeId) !== String(id)) await reorder(grp.rows, id, beforeId);
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116bWired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116bWired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', onClick);
    document.body.addEventListener('change', onChange);
    document.body.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    document.body.addEventListener('input', function (e) { if (e.target && e.target.getAttribute && e.target.getAttribute('data-act') === 'q') onKey(e); });
    document.body.addEventListener('dragstart', onDragStart);
    document.body.addEventListener('dragend', onDragEnd);
    document.body.addEventListener('dragover', onDragOver);
    document.body.addEventListener('drop', onDrop);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape' && B.drawer) closeDrawer(); });
    /* wave287: an empty quick add closes when you leave it */
    document.body.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) {
      var qa = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w116qa'); if (!qa || !B.slot || !B.slot.contains(qa)) return;
      setTimeout(function () { var inp = qa.querySelector('input'); if (document.body.contains(qa) && !qa.contains(document.activeElement) && inp && !inp.value.trim() && qa.style.display !== 'none') closeQa(qa); }, 150);
    });
  }

  async function loadCommentCounts() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('task_comments').select('task_id').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(800);
      var m = {}; ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (c) { m[String(c.task_id)] = (m[String(c.task_id)] || 0) + 1; });
      window.__w116cm = m;
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  window.w116Board = {
    render: function (slot, scope) {
      B.slot = slot; B.scope = scope; B.st = loadState(scope); B.sel = {}; B.qaOpen = null;
      if (B.preset) { Object.assign(B.st, B.preset); B.preset = null; B.listMoved = true; saveState(); }
      render();
      if (!window.__w116cm) loadCommentCounts().then(function () { if (B.slot === slot) render(); });
    },
    /* wave287: presets open a list (the Overview's tiles, Needs attention, the project cards); the older ones map onto lists */
    preset: function (q) {
      var clean = { view: 'list', due: '', status: '', project: '', flag: false, mine: false, done: false };
      var LISTS = { today: 'today', scheduled: 'scheduled', all: 'all', flagged: 'flagged', mine: 'mine', completed: 'completed', done: 'completed', projects: 'all', open: 'all' };
      q = String(q || '');
      if (LISTS[q]) B.preset = Object.assign(clean, { list: LISTS[q], group: 'project' });
      else if (q === 'overdue') B.preset = Object.assign(clean, { list: 'today', group: 'project', due: 'overdue' });
      else if (q === 'week') B.preset = Object.assign(clean, { list: 'scheduled', group: 'project', due: 'week' });
      else if (q.indexOf('project:') === 0) B.preset = Object.assign(clean, { list: 'p' + Number(q.slice(8)), group: 'due' });
      else B.preset = null;
    },
    set: function (patch) { if (!B.st || !patch) return; Object.assign(B.st, patch); saveState(); render(); },
    list: function (l) { if (B.st) setList(String(l)); },
    state: function () { return B.st ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(B.st)) : null; },
    model: function () { return B.scope ? model() : null; },
    complete: function (id) { return complete(taskById(id)); },
    refresh: render,
    closeDrawer: closeDrawer,
    open: openDrawer
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116c profiles: main responsibilities + job description (24/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I'd like for each person's profile to have a section with 3-5
   of their main responsibilities and link to their job description in
   the drive."
   - Every person page (#person/<id>) gains "Main responsibilities": up to
     five bullets and a link to the job description, both stored on
     team_members (responsibilities jsonb, jd_url, jd_name).
   - Admins, leaders and the person themselves can edit: a small editor
     with five lines, a job-description picker fed by the Drive mirror
     (wa_drive_docs, anything named like a job description), and "Pull
     from the job description", which lifts the responsibilities section
     out of the synced Google Doc text so nobody types them twice.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa116c) return; window.__wa116c = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa116ccss';
  css.textContent = '' +
    '.w116resp{margin:8px 0 12px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.04);border-radius:14px;padding:12px 16px;}' +
    '.w116resp h3.block{margin:0 0 6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;}' +
    '.w116resp h3.block a{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.4px;text-transform:none;font-weight:600;color:var(--wa-muted);cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w116resp h3.block a:hover{color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '.w116resp ol{margin:0;padding:0 0 0 20px;}' +
    '.w116resp li{font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.45;margin:0 0 4px;color:var(--wa-text);}' +
    '.w116resp .jd{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:8px 0 0;}' +
    '.w116resp .jd a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;text-decoration:none;}' +
    '.w116resp .jd a:hover{text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '.w116resp .empty{font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:0;}' +
    '.w116rl input,.w116rl select{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;padding:7px 9px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:9px;margin:0 0 6px;background:var(--wa-card);}' +
    '.w116rl label{display:block;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:10px 0 4px;}' +
    '.w116rl .row{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;}' +
    '.w116rl .row select{margin:0;}' +
    '.w116rl .pull{white-space:nowrap;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12px;font-weight:700;border:none;border-radius:9px;padding:8px 10px;cursor:pointer;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '.w116rl .note{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:4px 0 0;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || 'member'; } catch (e) { return 'member'; } }
  function canEdit(m) { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader' || (m && m.email && lower(m.email) === myEmail()); }
  function list(m) { var v = m && m.responsibilities; if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(function (x) { return String(x || '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 5); return []; }

  function sectionHtml(m) {
    var items = list(m);
    var edit = canEdit(m);
    return '<div class="w116resp" id="w116resp" data-mid="' + E(m.id) + '"><h3 class="block" style="color:' + GREEN + '">Main responsibilities' + (edit ? '<a data-w116r="edit">' + (items.length || m.jd_url ? 'Edit' : 'Add') + '</a>' : '') + '</h3>' +
      (items.length ? '<ol>' + items.map(function (x) { return '<li>' + E(x) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>' : '<p class="empty">No responsibilities listed yet.' + (edit ? ' Add three to five, or pull them from the job description.' : '') + '</p>') +
      '<p class="jd">' + (m.jd_url ? 'Job description: <a href="' + E(m.jd_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(m.jd_name || 'open in Drive') + '</a>' : 'No job description linked' + (edit ? ' yet' : '') + '.') + '</p></div>';
  }
  function inject(m) {
    var sec = document.getElementById('personpage'); if (!sec) return;
    var old = document.getElementById('w116resp'); if (old) old.remove();
    var stats = sec.querySelector('#w33stats');
    var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = sectionHtml(m);
    var el = box.firstChild;
    if (stats && stats.parentNode) stats.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', el);
    else { var card = sec.querySelector('.card'); if (card) card.appendChild(el); }
  }
  var CUR = null;
  if (typeof wa33Person === 'function') {
    var __wp116 = wa33Person;
    wa33Person = async function (mid) {
      var r = await __wp116.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var q = await db.from('team_members').select('id, name, email, role, department, responsibilities, jd_url, jd_name').eq('id', Number(mid)).maybeSingle();
        CUR = (q && q.data) || null;
        if (CUR) inject(CUR);
      } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return r;
    };
  }

  /* ---------- Drive job descriptions ---------- */
  var JDS = null;
  async function loadJds() {
    if (JDS) return JDS;
    try {
      var r = await db.from('wa_drive_docs').select('file_id, name, url, mime, modified_time').order('name', { ascending: true }).limit(2000);
      JDS = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (d) { var n = lower(d.name); return n.indexOf('job desc') >= 0 || /\bjd\b|jds\b/.test(n) || n.indexOf('role description') >= 0 || n.indexOf('roles & responsibilities') >= 0; });
    } catch (e) { JDS = []; }
    return JDS;
  }
  function extract(text) {
    var lines = String(text || '').split(/\r?\n/).map(function (l) { return l.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); });
    var start = -1;
    for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) { if (/responsibilit|duties|what you.ll do|what you will do|key tasks/i.test(lines[i]) && lines[i].length < 80) { start = i + 1; break; } }
    if (start < 0) { for (var i2 = 0; i2 < lines.length; i2++) { if (/role overview|about the role|the role/i.test(lines[i2]) && lines[i2].length < 80) { start = i2 + 1; break; } } }
    if (start < 0) start = 0;
    var out = [];
    for (var j = start; j < lines.length && out.length < 5; j++) {
      var l = lines[j];
      if (!l) continue;
      if (out.length && /^(requirements|qualifications|skills|time commitment|what we offer|benefits|reports to|about|how to apply|hours|compensation|experience)/i.test(l)) break;
      var b = l.replace(/^[-*\u2022\u25cf\u25e6\u2013\u2014\d.)\s]+/, '').trim();
      if (b.length < 12 || b.length > 180) continue;
      if (/^[A-Z][A-Za-z &]+:$/.test(b)) continue;
      if (/responsibilit|^key tasks|^duties/i.test(b) && b.split(' ').length <= 4) continue;
      if (b.split(' ').length <= 3 && !/[.!?]$/.test(b)) continue;
      out.push(b);
    }
    return out;
  }
  async function editor(m) {
    var items = list(m);
    var jds = await loadJds();
    var opts = '<option value="">No job description linked</option>' + jds.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + E(d.url) + '" data-fid="' + E(d.file_id) + '"' + (m.jd_url && m.jd_url === d.url ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(String(d.name).trim()) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    if (m.jd_url && !jds.some(function (d) { return d.url === m.jd_url; })) opts += '<option value="' + E(m.jd_url) + '" selected>' + E(m.jd_name || m.jd_url) + '</option>';
    var rows = ''; for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) rows += '<input type="text" class="w116ri" maxlength="180" placeholder="' + (i < 3 ? 'Responsibility ' + (i + 1) : 'Optional') + '" value="' + E(items[i] || '') + '" />';
    openModal('<div class="w116rl"><h3>' + E(m.name) + ': main responsibilities</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Three to five lines that say what this person owns. They show on the profile and help the assistant route work.</p>' +
      '<label>Responsibilities</label>' + rows +
      '<label>Job description (from the Drive)</label><div class="row"><select id="w116jd">' + opts + '</select><button class="pull" id="w116pull" type="button">Pull from the job description</button></div>' +
      '<input type="url" id="w116jdurl" placeholder="Or paste a Drive link" value="' + (m.jd_url && !jds.some(function (d) { return d.url === m.jd_url; }) ? E(m.jd_url) : '') + '" />' +
      '<p class="note">Documents appear here once they are shared with the hub\'s Drive account and named like a job description.</p>' +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116rc">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116rs">Save</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116rc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116pull').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var sel = document.getElementById('w116jd'); var o = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex]; var fid = o && o.getAttribute('data-fid');
      if (!fid) { toast('Pick a job description first'); return; }
      this.textContent = 'Reading...';
      try {
        var r = await db.from('wa_drive_docs').select('text_content').eq('file_id', fid).maybeSingle();
        var got = extract(r && r.data && r.data.text_content);
        if (!got.length) { toast('Could not find a responsibilities list in that document'); this.textContent = 'Pull from the job description'; return; }
        var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.w116ri');
        got.forEach(function (g, k) { if (inputs[k]) inputs[k].value = g; });
        toast(got.length + ' responsibilities pulled; edit them before saving');
      } catch (e) { toast('Could not read the document'); }
      this.textContent = 'Pull from the job description';
    });
    document.getElementById('w116rs').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var vals = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.w116ri')).map(function (i) { return i.value.trim(); }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 5);
      var sel = document.getElementById('w116jd'); var url = sel.value || (document.getElementById('w116jdurl').value || '').trim() || null;
      var name = null;
      if (sel.value) name = String(sel.options[sel.selectedIndex].textContent || '').trim(); else if (url) name = 'Job description';
      var patch = { responsibilities: vals, jd_url: url, jd_name: url ? name : null };
      var r = await db.from('team_members').update(patch).eq('id', m.id);
      if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not save'); return; }
      try { logChange('team_member', m.id, 'responsibilities', list(m).join(' | ') || 'none', vals.join(' | ') || 'none'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      Object.assign(m, patch);
      try { (DB_MEMBERS || []).forEach(function (x) { if (Number(x.id) === Number(m.id)) Object.assign(x, patch); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      closeModal(); inject(m); toast('Profile updated');
    });
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116cWired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116cWired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w116r]'); if (!a) return;
      var box = a.closest('#w116resp'); var mid = box && box.getAttribute('data-mid');
      var m = CUR && String(CUR.id) === String(mid) ? CUR : null;
      if (!m) return;
      if (!canEdit(m)) { toast('Only admins, leaders or the person can edit this'); return; }
      editor(m);
    });
  }
})();
