    '.w116pc .goal{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;min-width:0;}' +
    '.w116pc .goal span{white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;}' +
    '.w116pc .goal i{font-style:normal;font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.6px;text-transform:uppercase;background:rgba(220,165,39,.2);color:#7a5a0f;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 7px;flex:0 0 auto;}' +
    '.w116pc .pr{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}' +
    '.w116pc .pr .bar{flex:1 1 auto;height:6px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.10);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;}' +
    '.w116pc .pr .bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:' + GREEN + ';border-radius:3px;}' +
    '.w116pc .pr b{color:var(--wa-text);font-weight:700;}' +
    '.w116pc .subs{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;}' +
    '.w116pc .subs a{font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;color:' + GREEN + ';background:rgba(16,63,57,.08);border-radius:999px;padding:3px 9px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;}' +
    '.w116pc .subs a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.14);}' +
    '.w116pc .ft{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;}' +
    '.w116pc .ft a{color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:none;}' +
    '.w116pc .ft a:hover{text-decoration:underline;}' +
    '.w116pc .ft .sp{flex:1 1 auto;}' +
    '.w116pc select.inl{font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12px;padding:3px 6px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.3);max-width:100%;}' +
    /* list view */
    '.w116pl{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;}' +
    '.w116pl th{text-align:left;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);padding:6px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.10);}' +
    '.w116pl td{padding:8px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.06);vertical-align:middle;}' +
    '.w116pl tr:hover td{background:rgba(16,63,57,.04);}' +
    '.w116pl .nm{font-weight:700;color:var(--wa-text);cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w116pl .nm small{display:block;font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:var(--wa-muted);}' +
    '.w116pl .lead{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;cursor:pointer;}' +
    '.w116pl .lead.none{color:' + FLAG + ';font-weight:600;}' +
    '.w116pl .bar{width:90px;height:6px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.10);border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:6px;}' +
    '.w116pl .bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '.w116p .empty{font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:0;padding:6px 0 4px;}' +
    '.w116p .note{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:6px 0 0;}' +
    '.w116pm label{display:block;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:10px 0 4px;}' +
    '.w116pm input,.w116pm select,.w116pm textarea{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:13px;padding:7px 9px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:9px;background:var(--wa-card);}' +
    '.w116pm textarea{min-height:70px;}' +
    '@media (max-width:740px){.w116pc .who{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.w116p .tb input[type=search]{min-width:120px;flex:1 1 auto;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function who0() { try { return who(); } catch (e) { return 'staff'; } }
  function label(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || T.labels[d] || d || 'Other'; }
  function color(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function av(p, size) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size) : ''; }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; }); }
  function memberByName(n) { n = lower(n); return members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === n; })[0] || members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name).split(' ')[0] === n.split(' ')[0]; })[0] || null; }
  function leads(p) { var v = p && p.leads; if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.filter(Boolean).map(String); if (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim()) { try { var a = JSON.parse(v); return Array.isArray(a) ? a.filter(Boolean).map(String) : []; } catch (e) { return []; } } return []; }
  function status(p) { var s = lower(p.status || 'active'); if (s.indexOf('pause') >= 0 || s.indexOf('hold') >= 0) return 'paused'; if (s.indexOf('done') >= 0 || s.indexOf('complete') >= 0 || s.indexOf('closed') >= 0 || s.indexOf('archiv') >= 0) return 'done'; return 'active'; }
  function counts(p) {
    var ts = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id) === Number(p.id); });
    var done = ts.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length;
    return { total: ts.length, done: done, open: ts.length - done, pct: ts.length ? Math.round(done / ts.length * 100) : 0 };
  }
  /* wave205: a retired goal (the department tier, the earlier org goals) no longer counts: the card says Not linked yet */
  function goalOf(p) { return p.goal_id ? (P.goals.filter(function (g) { return Number(g.id) === Number(p.goal_id) && !g.retired_at; })[0] || null) : null; }
  function byId(id) { return P.rows.filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === Number(id); })[0] || null; }
  function key() { return 'wa116.projects'; }
  function loadState() {
    var def = { view: 'cards', team: '', status: 'active', lead: '', q: '', closed: {} };
    try { var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key()) || 'null'); if (s) Object.keys(def).forEach(function (k) { if (s[k] !== undefined) def[k] = s[k]; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    def.q = '';
    return def;
  }
  function saveState() { try { var s = Object.assign({}, P.st); s.q = ''; localStorage.setItem(key(), JSON.stringify(s)); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  async function load(force) {
    if (!force && P.loaded && Date.now() - P.loaded < 60000) return;
    try {
      /* the shared store (wave116i): the same rows the team pages, boards and goal pages read */
      if (window.w116LoadProjects) P.rows = (await window.w116LoadProjects(true)) || [];
      else { var r = await db.from('projects').select('*').order('sort', { ascending: true }); P.rows = (r && r.data) || []; }
      var g = await db.from('goals').select('id, title, department, quarter, status, number, retired_at').limit(500);
      P.goals = (g && g.data) || [];
      P.loaded = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function changed() { try { if (window.w116ProjectsChanged) window.w116ProjectsChanged(P.rows); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  /* wave205: a link made in the Strategy app's sheet (the goal and the milestone) repaints the cards */
  document.addEventListener('wa:projects', function () { try { var sec = document.getElementById('projects'); if (P.host && sec && sec.classList.contains('active') && !document.querySelector('#projects .w116pop')) render(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
  function wsOptions(dept, cur) {
    var bs = (T.boards && T.boards[dept]) || [];
    if (!bs.length) return '';
    return '<label>Board (sub-team)</label><select id="w116pws"><option value="">Not on a board</option>' + bs.map(function (b) { return '<option value="' + E(b.ws) + '"' + (lower(cur) === lower(b.ws) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(b.label) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><div class="hint" style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:3px 0 0">Which ' + E(label(dept)) + ' board lists this project and its tasks.</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- writes ---------- */
  async function patch(p, fields, field, oldV, newV) {
    var r = await db.from('projects').update(Object.assign({}, fields, { updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })).eq('id', p.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not save'); return false; }
    Object.assign(p, fields);
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'project', entity_id: String(p.id), field: field, old_value: oldV == null ? null : String(oldV), new_value: newV == null ? null : String(newV), changed_by: who0() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    try { (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).forEach(function (x) { if (Number(x.id) === Number(p.id)) Object.assign(x, fields); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    changed();
    return true;
  }
  async function setLead(p, name) {
    var cur = leads(p); var next = name ? [name] : [];
    if (cur.join('|') === next.join('|')) return;
    if (await patch(p, { leads: next }, 'leads', cur.join(', ') || null, next.join(', ') || null)) { toast(name ? 'Lead: ' + name : 'Lead cleared'); render(); }
  }
  async function setOwner(p, d) {
    if (!d || d === p.department) return;
    if (await patch(p, { department: d }, 'department', p.department, d)) { toast('Owner: ' + label(d)); render(); }
  }
  async function setStatus(p, s) {
    if (!s || s === (p.status || 'active')) return;
    if (await patch(p, { status: s }, 'status', p.status || 'active', s)) { toast('Status: ' + s); render(); }
  }
  function newProject(dept) {
    var ms = members();
    var parents = P.rows.filter(function (p) { return !p.parent_id && (!dept || p.department === dept); });
    openModal('<div class="w116pm"><h3>New project</h3><p class="m-sub">A named piece of work with a lead and an owning team. Tasks from meetings route into it automatically once it exists.</p>' +
      '<label>Name</label><input type="text" id="w116pn" placeholder="Community Kitchen Pilot" />' +
      '<label>What it is</label><textarea id="w116pd" placeholder="One or two lines: what the project delivers and when."></textarea>' +
      '<label>Owner team</label><select id="w116pt">' + T.order.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (d === dept ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Project lead</label><select id="w116pl"><option value="">Choose later</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '">' + E(m.name) + ' (' + E(T.short[m.department] || m.department || '') + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Part of (optional)</label><select id="w116pp"><option value="">Stands on its own</option>' + parents.map(function (p) { return '<option value="' + p.id + '">' + E(p.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div id="w116pwsbox">' + wsOptions(dept, '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116pc">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116ps">Create project</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116pc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116pt').addEventListener('change', function () { var box = document.getElementById('w116pwsbox'); if (box) box.innerHTML = wsOptions(this.value, ''); });
    document.getElementById('w116ps').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var name = document.getElementById('w116pn').value.trim(); if (!name) { toast('Give the project a name'); return; }
      var lead = document.getElementById('w116pl').value; var pid = document.getElementById('w116pp').value;
      var wsEl = document.getElementById('w116pws');
      var row = { name: name, description: document.getElementById('w116pd').value.trim() || null, department: document.getElementById('w116pt').value, status: 'active', leads: lead ? [lead] : [], parent_id: pid ? Number(pid) : null, workstream: wsEl && wsEl.value ? wsEl.value : null, sort: 999, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      var r = await db.from('projects').insert(row).select('*').single();
      if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not create the project'); return; }
      var saved = (r && r.data) || row; if (!saved.id) saved.id = -Date.now();
      P.rows.push(saved);
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'project', entity_id: String(saved.id), field: 'added', old_value: null, new_value: name, changed_by: who0() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      try { if (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined') WA27_PROJECTS.push(saved); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      changed();
      closeModal(); toast('Project created'); render();
    });
  }

  /* ---------- rendering ---------- */
  function visible() {
    var st = P.st, q = lower(st.q);
    return P.rows.filter(function (p) {
      if (st.status !== 'all' && status(p) !== st.status) return false;
      if (st.team && p.department !== st.team) return false;
      if (st.lead === '__none' && leads(p).length) return false;
      if (st.lead && st.lead !== '__none' && leads(p).map(lower).indexOf(lower(st.lead)) < 0) return false;
      if (q && lower(p.name).indexOf(q) < 0 && lower(p.description).indexOf(q) < 0 && leads(p).map(lower).join(' ').indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }
  function leadField(p) {
    var l = leads(p); var m = l.length ? memberByName(l[0]) : null;
    if (!l.length) return '<div class="f none" data-pact="lead" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" title="Set a project lead"><span class="nolead">+</span><div class="t"><small>Lead</small><span>No lead yet</span></div></div>';
    return '<div class="f" data-pact="lead" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" title="Change the lead">' + av(m || { name: l[0] }, 22) + '<div class="t"><small>Lead</small><span>' + E(l[0]) + (l.length > 1 ? ' +' + (l.length - 1) : '') + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function ownerField(p) {
    return '<div class="f" data-pact="owner" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" title="Change the owner team"><span class="tdot" style="background:' + color(p.department) + '"></span><div class="t"><small>Owner</small><span>' + E(T.short[p.department] || label(p.department)) + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function card(p, children) {
    var c = counts(p); var g = goalOf(p); var st = status(p); var par = p.parent_id ? byId(p.parent_id) : null;
    return '<div class="w116pc" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="top"><div class="nm" data-pact="open" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">' + (par ? '<small>' + E(par.name) + ' \u203a</small>' : '') + E(p.name) + '</div><span class="w116st2 ' + st + '" data-pact="status" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" title="Change status">' + st + '</span></div>' +
      (p.description ? '<div class="ds">' + E(p.description) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="who">' + leadField(p) + ownerField(p) + '</div>' +
      (g ? '<div class="goal"' + (staff() ? ' data-pact="goal" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" style="cursor:pointer" title="Change the goal or milestone it serves"' : '') + '><i>Goal' + (g.number ? ' ' + E(g.number) : '') + '</i><span title="' + E(g.title) + '">' + E(g.title) + '</span></div>' : '<div class="goal"' + (staff() ? ' data-pact="goal" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" style="cursor:pointer"' : '') + '><i style="background:rgba(121,119,115,.14);color:var(--wa-muted)">Goal</i><span>Not linked to a goal yet' + (staff() ? ': link it' : '') + '</span></div>') +
      '<div class="pr"><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + c.pct + '%"></i></div><span><b>' + c.open + '</b> open \u00b7 ' + c.done + ' done</span></div>' +
      (children && children.length ? '<div class="subs">' + children.map(function (ch) { var cc = counts(ch); return '<a data-pact="open" data-pid="' + E(ch.id) + '">\u203a ' + E(ch.name) + (cc.open ? ' \u00b7 ' + cc.open : '') + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="ft"><a data-pact="open" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">Open project \u2192</a><a data-pact="tasks" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">Tasks</a><span class="sp"></span>' + (!staff() ? '' : '<a data-pact="menu" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-weight:600">Edit</a>') + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
  }
  function groupHtml(d, rows, all) {
    var tops = rows.filter(function (p) { return !p.parent_id || !byId(p.parent_id) || byId(p.parent_id).department !== d; });
    var open = rows.reduce(function (n, p) { return n + counts(p).open; }, 0);
    var closed = P.st.closed && P.st.closed[d];
    var body;
    if (P.st.view === 'list') {
      body = '<table class="w116pl"><thead><tr><th>Project</th><th>Lead</th><th>Status</th><th>Progress</th><th>Goal</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>' + rows.map(function (p) {
        var c = counts(p); var g = goalOf(p); var l = leads(p); var m = l.length ? memberByName(l[0]) : null; var par = p.parent_id ? byId(p.parent_id) : null;
        return '<tr><td><span class="nm" data-pact="open" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">' + E(p.name) + (par ? '<small>' + E(par.name) + '</small>' : '') + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><span class="lead' + (l.length ? '' : ' none') + '" data-pact="lead" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">' + (l.length ? av(m || { name: l[0] }, 20) + E(l[0]) : '+ Set lead') + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><span class="w116st2 ' + status(p) + '" data-pact="status" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '">' + status(p) + '</span></td>' +
          '<td><span class="bar"><i style="width:' + c.pct + '%"></i></span>' + c.open + ' open \u00b7 ' + c.done + ' done</td>' +
          '<td style="max-width:220px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--wa-muted)">' + (g ? E(g.title) : '\u2014') + '</td>' +
          '<td><a data-pact="open" data-pid="' + E(p.id) + '" style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;cursor:pointer">Open</a></td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
    } else {
      body = '<div class="w116pgrid">' + tops.map(function (p) { return card(p, all.filter(function (ch) { return Number(ch.parent_id) === Number(p.id); })); }).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="w116pg' + (closed ? ' closed' : '') + '" data-pg="' + E(d) + '"><div class="gh" data-pact="fold" data-pg="' + E(d) + '"><span class="chev">&#9660;</span><span class="dot" style="background:' + color(d) + '"></span><b>' + E(label(d)) + '</b><span class="n">' + rows.length + '</span><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">' + open + ' open task' + (open === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (staff() ? '<span class="add" data-pact="new" data-pg="' + E(d) + '">+ Add project</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="gb">' + (rows.length ? body : '<p class="empty">No projects here' + (P.st.q || P.st.lead || P.st.status !== 'active' ? ' with these filters' : '') + '.</p>') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function render() {
    var host = P.host; if (!host || !document.body.contains(host)) return;
    var st = P.st; var rows = visible();
    var active = P.rows.filter(function (p) { return status(p) === 'active'; });
    var noLead = active.filter(function (p) { return !leads(p).length; });
    var openAll = active.reduce(function (n, p) { return n + counts(p).open; }, 0);
    var leadNames = []; P.rows.forEach(function (p) { leads(p).forEach(function (n) { if (leadNames.indexOf(n) < 0) leadNames.push(n); }); }); leadNames.sort();
    var teams = T.order.slice(); P.rows.forEach(function (p) { if (p.department && teams.indexOf(p.department) < 0) teams.push(p.department); });
    var html = '<div class="w116p">' +
      '<div class="sum">' +
      '<div class="k' + (st.status === 'active' && !st.lead && !st.team ? '' : '') + '" data-pact="sum" data-v="active"><b>' + active.length + '</b>active projects</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-pact="sum" data-v="open"><b>' + openAll + '</b>open tasks in projects</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (noLead.length ? ' warn' : '') + (st.lead === '__none' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-pact="sum" data-v="nolead"><b>' + noLead.length + '</b>without a lead</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-pact="sum" data-v="teams"><b>' + teams.filter(function (d) { return active.some(function (p) { return p.department === d; }); }).length + '</b>teams with projects</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="tb"><span class="w116seg"><a data-pact="view" data-v="cards" class="' + (st.view === 'cards' ? 'on' : '') + '">Cards</a><a data-pact="view" data-v="list" class="' + (st.view === 'list' ? 'on' : '') + '">List</a></span>' +
      '<select data-pact="team"><option value="">All teams</option>' + teams.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + E(d) + '"' + (st.team === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-pact="statusf">' + [['active', 'Active'], ['paused', 'Paused'], ['done', 'Done'], ['all', 'Everything']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.status === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-pact="leadf"><option value="">Any lead</option><option value="__none"' + (st.lead === '__none' ? ' selected' : '') + '>No lead yet</option>' + leadNames.map(function (n) { return '<option value="' + E(n) + '"' + (st.lead === n ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(n) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-pact="q" placeholder="Search projects" value="' + E(st.q) + '" />' +
      (staff() ? '<button class="new" data-pact="new" data-pg="' + E(st.team || '') + '">+ New project</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    var shownTeams = teams.filter(function (d) { return !st.team || st.team === d; });
    html += shownTeams.map(function (d) { return groupHtml(d, rows.filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; }), P.rows); }).join('');
    if (!P.rows.length) html += '<p class="empty">No projects yet.</p>';
    html += '<p class="note">' + rows.length + ' of ' + P.rows.length + ' projects shown. A project has one lead (the person accountable) and one owner team; tasks from meetings route into projects automatically.</p></div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
    var q = host.querySelector('input[data-pact="q"]'); if (q && P.qFocus) { q.focus(); q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); P.qFocus = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- inline pickers ---------- */
  function pickLead(el, p) {
    if (el.querySelector('select')) return;
    var ms = members().sort(function (a, b) { return (a.department === p.department ? 0 : 1) - (b.department === p.department ? 0 : 1) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var cur = leads(p)[0] || '';
    var sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.className = 'inl';
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">No lead</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '"' + (m.name === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + (m.department !== p.department ? ' (' + E(T.short[m.department] || m.department || '') + ')' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('');
    el.innerHTML = ''; el.appendChild(sel); sel.focus();
    var done = false;
    sel.addEventListener('change', function () { done = true; setLead(p, sel.value); });
    sel.addEventListener('blur', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!done) render(); }, 150); });
    sel.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); });
  }
  function pickOwner(el, p) {
    if (el.querySelector('select')) return;
    var sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.className = 'inl';
    sel.innerHTML = T.order.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + (d === p.department ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    el.innerHTML = ''; el.appendChild(sel); sel.focus();
    var done = false;
    sel.addEventListener('change', function () { done = true; setOwner(p, sel.value); });
    sel.addEventListener('blur', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!done) render(); }, 150); });
    sel.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); });
  }
  function pickStatus(el, p) {
    if (el.querySelector('select')) return;
    var sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.className = 'inl';
    sel.innerHTML = ['active', 'paused', 'done'].map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s + '"' + (status(p) === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('');
    el.innerHTML = ''; el.appendChild(sel); sel.focus();
    var done = false;
    sel.addEventListener('change', function () { done = true; setStatus(p, sel.value); });
    sel.addEventListener('blur', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!done) render(); }, 150); });
    sel.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); });
  }
  function editMenu(p) {
    openModal('<div class="w116pm"><h3>' + E(p.name) + '</h3>' +
      '<label>Name</label><input type="text" id="w116en" value="' + E(p.name) + '" />' +
      '<label>What it is</label><textarea id="w116ed">' + E(p.description || '') + '</textarea>' +
      wsOptions(p.department, p.workstream || '') +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116ec">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116es">Save</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116ec').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116es').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var name = document.getElementById('w116en').value.trim(); var desc = document.getElementById('w116ed').value.trim() || null;
      if (!name) { toast('The project needs a name'); return; }
      var ok = true;
      if (name !== p.name) ok = await patch(p, { name: name }, 'name', p.name, name);
      if (ok && desc !== (p.description || null)) ok = await patch(p, { description: desc }, 'description', (p.description || '').slice(0, 80), (desc || '').slice(0, 80));
      var wsEl = document.getElementById('w116pws');
      if (ok && wsEl && (wsEl.value || null) !== (p.workstream || null)) ok = await patch(p, { workstream: wsEl.value || null }, 'workstream', p.workstream || null, wsEl.value || null);
      if (ok) { closeModal(); toast('Project saved'); render(); }
    });
  }

  var qTimer = null;
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116eWired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116eWired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (!P.host || !P.host.contains(e.target)) return;
      var a = e.target.closest('[data-pact]'); if (!a) return;
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-pact'); var p = a.getAttribute('data-pid') ? byId(a.getAttribute('data-pid')) : null;
      if (act === 'view') { P.st.view = a.getAttribute('data-v'); saveState(); render(); return; }
      if (act === 'fold') { var k = a.getAttribute('data-pg'); P.st.closed = P.st.closed || {}; if (P.st.closed[k]) delete P.st.closed[k]; else P.st.closed[k] = 1; saveState(); a.closest('.w116pg').classList.toggle('closed'); return; }
      if (act === 'sum') { var v = a.getAttribute('data-v'); if (v === 'nolead') { P.st.lead = P.st.lead === '__none' ? '' : '__none'; P.st.status = 'active'; } else if (v === 'active') { P.st.status = 'active'; P.st.lead = ''; P.st.team = ''; } else if (v === 'open') { P.st.view = 'list'; } saveState(); render(); return; }
      if (act === 'new') { if (!staff()) return; e.stopPropagation(); newProject(a.getAttribute('data-pg') || P.st.team || T.order[0]); return; }
      if (!p) return;
      if (act === 'open') { location.hash = '#project/' + p.id; return; }
      /* wave205: the Strategy app's link sheet (the goal and the milestone) */
      if (act === 'goal') { if (p && window.w205 && window.w205.canComment()) { window.w205.load().then(function () { window.w205.open({ kind: 'link', goal: p.goal_id, project: p.id, goalPick: p.goal_id, msPick: p.milestone_id || null }); }); } return; }
      if (act === 'tasks') { try { if (window.w116Board) window.w116Board.preset('project:' + p.id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } location.hash = '#team/' + (T.order.indexOf(p.department) >= 0 ? p.department : T.order[0]) + '/tasks'; return; }
      if (!staff()) return;
      if (act === 'lead') { e.stopPropagation(); pickLead(a, p); return; }
      if (act === 'owner') { e.stopPropagation(); pickOwner(a, p); return; }
      if (act === 'status') { e.stopPropagation(); pickStatus(a, p); return; }
      if (act === 'menu') { editMenu(p); return; }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      if (!P.host || !P.host.contains(e.target)) return;
      var a = e.target.closest('[data-pact]'); if (!a || a.tagName !== 'SELECT') return;
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-pact');
      if (act === 'team') P.st.team = a.value; else if (act === 'statusf') P.st.status = a.value; else if (act === 'leadf') P.st.lead = a.value; else return;
      saveState(); render();
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      if (!P.host || !P.host.contains(e.target) || e.target.getAttribute('data-pact') !== 'q') return;
      clearTimeout(qTimer); var v = e.target.value;
      qTimer = setTimeout(function () { P.st.q = v; P.qFocus = true; render(); }, 220);
    });
  }

  window.w116ProjectsPage = {
    render: async function (host) {
      P.host = host; P.st = P.st || loadState();
      host.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Loading projects...</p>';
      await load(true);
      render();
    },
    refresh: function () { load(true).then(render); }
  };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116f Strategy pages (24/09/26): Organizational goals, Department
   goals, Goal alignment (the map)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "I'd like to do the same for Organizational goals, Department
   led goals, and the Map to have their own page under the Strategy
   section. Revamped with ClickUp robustness and Apple ease of use.
   Organizational goals should have a clear SMART goal layout,
   description, what achieved looks like, tasks involved, projects
   involved, departments involved."
   - #orggoals: one card per organizational goal with the SMART grid
     (Specific = description, Measurable = measure, Achievable =
     approach, Relevant = why it matters, Time-bound = horizon and due
     date), "What achieved looks like", progress, and the teams,
     projects, tasks and department goals that roll up into it.
   - #goals (Department goals): the wave30/68 page body is replaced by
     team groups of goal cards with quarter, team, status and search
     filters, inline status, expandable SMART detail, tasks with tick
     boxes, comments (the wave16 thread, synced to ClickUp when mapped)
     and the wave30 AI suggestions per team.
   - #goalmap (Goal alignment): a fresh three column map (projects ->
     department goals -> organizational goals) with hover and click
     focus, team filter, rollup counts and one-click linking for
     anything left unlinked.
   - One editor for every tier (w116GoalModal) writes goals.title,
     detail, measure, approach, why, success, owner, due_date, quarter,
     status, progress, parent_goal_id and projects.goal_id, with
     change_log rows.
   The wave16 comment handlers, wave30 suggestion handlers and the
   ClickUp status push are reused, not copied.
   ===================================================================== */
