  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function who0() { try { return who(); } catch (e) { return 'staff'; } }
  function label(d) { if (d === 'org') return 'Organization'; return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || T.labels[d] || d || 'Other'; }
  function shortLabel(d) { if (d === 'org') return 'Org'; return (T.short && T.short[d]) || label(d); }
  function color(d) { if (d === 'org') return GOLD; return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function av(p, size) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size) : ''; }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m.active !== false; }); }
  function memberByName(n) { n = lower(n); if (!n) return null; return members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === n; })[0] || members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name).split(' ')[0] === n.split(' ')[0]; })[0] || null; }
  function goals() { return (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) { return g && g.status !== 'archived'; }); }
  function goalById(id) { return (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined' ? GOALS_CACHE : []).filter(function (g) { return Number(g.id) === Number(id); })[0] || null; }
  function projects() { if (window.w116Projects) { var r = window.w116Projects(); if (r && r.length) return r; } return M.projects.length ? M.projects : (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []); }
  function projById(id) { return projects().filter(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === Number(id); })[0] || null; }
  function projName(p) { var par = p && p.parent_id ? projById(p.parent_id) : null; return (par ? par.name + ' \u203a ' : '') + (p ? p.name : ''); }
  function projActive(p) { var s = lower(p.status || 'active'); return !(s.indexOf('done') >= 0 || s.indexOf('complete') >= 0 || s.indexOf('closed') >= 0 || s.indexOf('archiv') >= 0); }
  function tasks() { return typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []; }
  function stKey(s) { s = lower(s || 'not started'); return s.replace(/[^a-z]/g, ''); }
  function stPill(g, act) { var s = lower(g.status || 'not started'); return '<span class="w116gs s-' + stKey(s) + '"' + (act ? ' data-gact="status" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '" title="Change status"' : '') + '>' + E(s) + '</span>'; }
  function isDone(g) { var s = lower(g.status); return s === 'done' || s === 'closed'; }
  function isLate(g) { if (!g.due_date || isDone(g)) return false; return String(g.due_date) < new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function fmtDate(d) { if (!d) return ''; try { var x = new Date(String(d).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); return x.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(d); } }
  function horizon(g) { return g.quarter || (g.department === 'org' ? 'Long term' : 'No quarter'); }
  function curQuarter() { try { return wa30Quarters()[0]; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function qKey(q) { try { return wa29QKey(q); } catch (e) { return 0; } }
  function taskById(id) { return tasks().filter(function (t) { return Number(t.id) === Number(id); })[0] || null; }

  /* the network around a goal: children, projects, tasks, teams */
  function inv(g) {
    var all = goals();
    var kids = g.department === 'org' ? all.filter(function (x) { return Number(x.parent_goal_id) === Number(g.id); }) : [];
    var gids = [Number(g.id)].concat(kids.map(function (k) { return Number(k.id); }));
    var ps = projects().filter(function (p) { return gids.indexOf(Number(p.goal_id)) >= 0; });
    var pids = ps.map(function (p) { return Number(p.id); });
    var tids = {};
    [g].concat(kids).forEach(function (x) { (x.task_ids || []).forEach(function (id) { tids[Number(id)] = 1; }); });
    var ts = tasks().filter(function (t) { return tids[Number(t.id)] || (t.project_id && pids.indexOf(Number(t.project_id)) >= 0); });
    var depts = [];
    kids.forEach(function (k) { if (k.department && k.department !== 'org' && depts.indexOf(k.department) < 0) depts.push(k.department); });
    ps.forEach(function (p) { if (p.department && depts.indexOf(p.department) < 0) depts.push(p.department); });
    ts.forEach(function (t) { if (t.department && depts.indexOf(t.department) < 0) depts.push(t.department); });
    if (g.department !== 'org' && depts.indexOf(g.department) < 0) depts.unshift(g.department);
    var order = T.order.slice(); depts.sort(function (a, b) { var ia = order.indexOf(a), ib = order.indexOf(b); return (ia < 0 ? 99 : ia) - (ib < 0 ? 99 : ib); });
    var open = ts.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }), done = ts.filter(function (t) { return t.done; });
    return { kids: kids, projects: ps, tasks: ts, open: open, done: done, depts: depts };
  }
  function progress(g, iv) {
    iv = iv || inv(g);
    if (g.progress != null && g.progress !== '' && !isNaN(Number(g.progress))) return { pct: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(Number(g.progress)))), basis: 'set by hand' };
    if (isDone(g)) return { pct: 100, basis: 'marked ' + lower(g.status) };
    if (iv.tasks.length) return { pct: Math.round(iv.done.length / iv.tasks.length * 100), basis: iv.done.length + ' of ' + iv.tasks.length + ' tasks done' };
    if (iv.kids.length) {
      var sum = 0, n = 0;
      iv.kids.forEach(function (k) { var p = progress(k); if (p.pct != null) { sum += p.pct; n++; } });
      if (n) return { pct: Math.round(sum / n), basis: 'average of ' + n + ' department goal' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') };
    }
    return { pct: null, basis: 'no tasks or projects linked yet' };
  }
  function progressHtml(g, iv, gold) {
    var p = progress(g, iv);
    if (p.pct == null) return '<div class="w116gp"><div class="bar' + (gold ? ' gold' : '') + '"><i style="width:0"></i></div><span>' + E(p.basis) + '</span></div>';
    return '<div class="w116gp"><div class="bar' + (gold ? ' gold' : '') + '"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></div><span><b>' + p.pct + '%</b> \u00b7 ' + E(p.basis) + '</span></div>';
  }

  async function load(force) {
    if (!force && M.loaded && Date.now() - M.loaded < 45000) return;
    try {
      if (typeof waLoadGoals === 'function') await waLoadGoals();
      if (window.w116LoadProjects) M.projects = (await window.w116LoadProjects(force)) || [];
      else { var r = await db.from('projects').select('*').order('sort', { ascending: true }); M.projects = (r && r.data) || []; }
      M.loaded = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }

  /* ---------- writes ---------- */
  async function logIt(g, field, oldV, newV) {
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'goal', entity_id: String(g.id), field: field, old_value: oldV == null ? null : String(oldV).slice(0, 200), new_value: newV == null ? null : String(newV).slice(0, 200), changed_by: who0() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
  }
  async function setStatus(g, s) {
    if (!s || s === g.status) return;
    var old = g.status;
    var r = await db.from('goals').update({ status: s, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who0() }).eq('id', g.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not update the status'); return; }
    g.status = s;
    await logIt(g, 'status', old, s);
    if (g.clickup_task_id) {
      try { db.functions.invoke('goal-sync', { body: { action: 'status', goal_id: g.id, status: s } }).then(function (x) { if (x && (x.error || !(x.data && x.data.ok))) toast('Saved here; the ClickUp copy did not update'); }); } catch (e) { /* best effort */ }
    }
    toast('Status: ' + s);
    refreshAll();
  }
  async function linkProject(pid, gid) {
    var p = projById(pid); if (!p) return;
    var r = await db.from('projects').update({ goal_id: gid || null, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', p.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not link the project'); return; }
    var old = p.goal_id; p.goal_id = gid || null;
    try { (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).forEach(function (x) { if (Number(x.id) === Number(p.id)) x.goal_id = p.goal_id; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'project', entity_id: String(p.id), field: 'goal', old_value: old == null ? null : String(old), new_value: gid == null ? null : String(gid), changed_by: who0() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    toast(gid ? 'Project linked' : 'Project unlinked');
    try { if (window.w116ProjectsChanged) window.w116ProjectsChanged(null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    refreshAll();
  }
  async function setParent(g, pid) {
    var old = g.parent_goal_id || null; pid = pid || null;
    if (String(old || '') === String(pid || '')) return;
    var r = await db.from('goals').update({ parent_goal_id: pid, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who0() }).eq('id', g.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not save the roll-up'); return; }
    g.parent_goal_id = pid;
    await logIt(g, 'rolls up to', old, pid);
    toast(pid ? 'Rolled up' : 'Roll-up cleared');
    refreshAll();
  }

  /* ---------- the editor (every tier) ---------- */
  function goalModal(g, presetDept, presetOrg) {
    var isOrg = g ? g.department === 'org' : presetDept === 'org';
    var depts = T.order.slice();
    var quarters = []; try { quarters = wa30Quarters(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    goals().forEach(function (x) { if (x.quarter && quarters.indexOf(x.quarter) < 0) quarters.push(x.quarter); });
    if (quarters.indexOf('FY2027') < 0) quarters.push('FY2027');
    if (quarters.indexOf('Long term') < 0) quarters.push('Long term');
    var orgGoals = goals().filter(function (x) { return x.department === 'org'; });
    var ms = members();
    var ownerNow = g ? (g.owner || '') : '';
    var ownerOpts = '<option value="">Not assigned</option>' + ms.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '"' + (m.name === ownerNow ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + ' (' + E(shortLabel(m.department)) + ')</option>'; }).join('') + (ownerNow && !memberByName(ownerNow) ? '<option value="' + E(ownerNow) + '" selected>' + E(ownerNow) + '</option>' : '');
    var linkedIds = g ? projects().filter(function (p) { return Number(p.goal_id) === Number(g.id); }).map(function (p) { return Number(p.id); }) : [];
    var pickable = projects().filter(function (p) { return projActive(p) || linkedIds.indexOf(Number(p.id)) >= 0; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { var da = a.department === (g ? g.department : presetDept) ? 0 : 1, dbb = b.department === (g ? g.department : presetDept) ? 0 : 1; return da - dbb || String(a.department).localeCompare(String(b.department)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var sHtml = function (letter, name, field, ph, val) {
      return '<label><i>' + letter + '</i>' + name + '</label><textarea data-gf="' + field + '" placeholder="' + E(ph) + '">' + E(val || '') + '</textarea>';
    };
    openModal('<div class="w116gm"><h3>' + (g ? 'Edit the goal' : (isOrg ? 'New organizational goal' : 'New department goal')) + '</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">' + (isOrg ? 'The direction of Welfare Avenue for a year or more. Department goals and projects roll up into it.' : 'A quarterly goal for one team. Link it to an organizational goal so the map shows how it contributes.') + ' Write it SMART: each field below is one letter.</p>' +
      '<label>Goal (one line)</label><input type="text" data-gf="title" value="' + E(g ? g.title : '') + '" placeholder="' + (isOrg ? 'Reach 10,000 members across our online community' : 'Recruit 50 volunteers per Rally Paper team by Sept 15') + '" />' +
      '<div class="row3">' +
      '<div><label>Team</label><select data-gf="department"' + (g ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (isOrg ? '<option value="org" selected>Organization (overarching)</option>' : depts.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + ((g ? g.department : presetDept) === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '<option value="org"' + (g && g.department === 'org' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Organization (overarching)</option>') + '</select></div>' +
      '<div><label>' + (isOrg ? 'Horizon' : 'Quarter') + '</label><input type="text" data-gf="quarter" list="w116gql" value="' + E(g ? (g.quarter || '') : (isOrg ? 'FY2027' : (curQuarter() || 'Q1 FY2027'))) + '" placeholder="' + (isOrg ? 'FY2027 or Long term' : 'Q1 FY2027') + '" /><datalist id="w116gql">' + quarters.map(function (q) { return '<option value="' + E(q) + '"></option>'; }).join('') + '</datalist></div>' +
      '<div><label>Status</label><select data-gf="status">' + STATUSES.concat(g && g.status && STATUSES.indexOf(g.status) < 0 ? [g.status] : []).map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + E(s) + '"' + ((g ? g.status : 'not started') === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(s) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="row3">' +
      '<div><label>Owner</label><select data-gf="owner">' + ownerOpts + '</select></div>' +
      '<div><label><i>T</i>Due date</label><input type="date" data-gf="due_date" value="' + E(g ? (g.due_date || '') : '') + '" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Progress (%)</label><input type="number" min="0" max="100" data-gf="progress" value="' + E(g && g.progress != null ? g.progress : '') + '" placeholder="From tasks" /><div class="hint">Leave blank and it follows the linked tasks.</div></div>' +
      '</div>' +
      sHtml('S', 'Specific: what exactly', 'detail', 'What we set out to do, in plain words.', g ? g.detail : '') +
      sHtml('M', 'Measurable: how we know', 'measure', 'The number or outcome that counts as done.', g ? g.measure : '') +
      sHtml('A', 'Achievable: how we get there', 'approach', 'The approach, the resources and who carries it.', g ? g.approach : '') +
      sHtml('R', 'Relevant: why it matters', 'why', 'Why this matters to Welfare Avenue right now.', g ? g.why : '') +
      '<label>What achieved looks like</label><textarea data-gf="success" placeholder="Describe the day this goal is met: what is different for the people we serve and for the team.">' + E(g ? (g.success || '') : '') + '</textarea>' +
      (!isOrg ? '<label>Rolls up to</label><select data-gf="parent_goal_id"><option value="">No organizational goal yet</option>' + orgGoals.map(function (x) { var sel = g ? Number(g.parent_goal_id) === Number(x.id) : Number(presetOrg) === Number(x.id); return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(String(x.title).slice(0, 80)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '') +
      '<label>Projects that serve this goal</label><div class="pk">' + (pickable.length ? pickable.map(function (p) { return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-gpk="' + p.id + '"' + (linkedIds.indexOf(Number(p.id)) >= 0 ? ' checked' : '') + ' />' + E(projName(p)) + ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11px">' + E(shortLabel(p.department)) + '</span></label>'; }).join('') : '<div class="hint">No projects yet.</div>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="m-foot">' + (g && staff() ? '<span class="danger" id="w116gar">Archive this goal</span>' : '') + '<span style="flex:1"></span><button class="btn ghost" id="w116gcx">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116gsv">' + (g ? 'Save the goal' : 'Add the goal') + '</button></div></div>');
    var box = document.querySelector('.w116gm'); if (!box) return;
    var V = function (f) { var el = box.querySelector('[data-gf="' + f + '"]'); return el ? el.value : ''; };
    document.getElementById('w116gcx').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    var ar = document.getElementById('w116gar');
    if (ar) ar.addEventListener('click', async function () {
      if (!confirm('Archive this goal? It leaves every page and the map; projects stay where they are.')) return;
      var r = await db.from('goals').update({ status: 'archived', updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who0() }).eq('id', g.id);
      if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not archive'); return; }
      g.status = 'archived'; await logIt(g, 'archived', g.title, null);
      closeModal(); toast('Goal archived'); refreshAll();
    });
    document.getElementById('w116gsv').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var title = V('title').trim(); if (!title) { toast('The goal needs a title'); return; }
      var btn = document.getElementById('w116gsv'); btn.disabled = true;
      var prog = V('progress').trim();
      var patch = {
        title: title, detail: V('detail').trim() || null, measure: V('measure').trim() || null,
        approach: V('approach').trim() || null, why: V('why').trim() || null, success: V('success').trim() || null,
        owner: V('owner').trim() || null, due_date: V('due_date') || null,
        quarter: V('quarter').trim() || (isOrg ? 'FY2027' : (curQuarter() || 'Q1 FY2027')),
        status: V('status') || 'not started',
        progress: prog === '' ? null : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(prog))),
        updated_by: who0(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      var pgEl = box.querySelector('[data-gf="parent_goal_id"]');
      if (pgEl) patch.parent_goal_id = Number(pgEl.value) || null;
      var r, row = g;
      if (g) {
        r = await db.from('goals').update(patch).eq('id', g.id);
        if (r && r.error) { btn.disabled = false; toast('Could not save the goal'); return; }
        var changed = [];
        Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { if (k !== 'updated_by' && k !== 'updated_at' && String(g[k] == null ? '' : g[k]) !== String(patch[k] == null ? '' : patch[k])) changed.push(k); });
        Object.assign(g, patch);
        if (changed.length) await logIt(g, 'edited', null, changed.join(', '));
      } else {
        patch.department = V('department') || (isOrg ? 'org' : T.order[0]);
        patch.source = 'dashboard'; patch.sort = 999; patch.task_ids = [];
        r = await db.from('goals').insert(patch).select('*').single();
        if (r && r.error) { btn.disabled = false; toast('Could not add the goal'); return; }
        row = (r && r.data) || patch; if (!row.id) row.id = -Date.now();
        try { if (typeof GOALS_CACHE !== 'undefined') GOALS_CACHE.push(row); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        await logIt(row, 'added', null, title);
      }
      /* projects: apply the tick boxes that changed */
      var boxes = box.querySelectorAll('[data-gpk]');
      for (var i = 0; i < boxes.length; i++) {
        var pid = Number(boxes[i].getAttribute('data-gpk')); var on = boxes[i].checked; var p = projById(pid); if (!p) continue;
        var was = Number(p.goal_id) === Number(row.id);
        if (on && !was) { await db.from('projects').update({ goal_id: row.id }).eq('id', pid); p.goal_id = row.id; }
        else if (!on && was) { await db.from('projects').update({ goal_id: null }).eq('id', pid); p.goal_id = null; }
        else continue;
        try { (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []).forEach(function (x) { if (Number(x.id) === pid) x.goal_id = p.goal_id; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'project', entity_id: String(pid), field: 'goal', old_value: was ? String(row.id) : null, new_value: on ? String(row.id) : null, changed_by: who0() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      }
      closeModal(); toast(g ? 'Goal saved' : 'Goal added');
      try { if (typeof renderDeptGoalsCards === 'function') renderDeptGoalsCards(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      refreshAll();
    });
  }
  window.w116GoalModal = goalModal;

  /* quick pickers used by the map and the involvement lists */
  function pickGoalFor(p) {
    /* wave205: the Strategy app's link sheet picks the goal and the milestone */
    try { if (window.w205 && window.w205.open && window.w205.canComment()) { window.w205.load().then(function () { window.w205.open({ kind: 'link', goal: p.goal_id, project: p.id, goalPick: p.goal_id, msPick: p.milestone_id || null }); }); return; } } catch (e) { /* the older dialog */ }
    var opts = goals().filter(function (x) { return x.department !== 'org' && !isDone(x); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.department === p.department ? 0 : 1) - (b.department === p.department ? 0 : 1); });
    var orgs = goals().filter(function (x) { return x.department === 'org'; });
    openModal('<div class="w116gm"><h3>Link ' + E(p.name) + '</h3><p class="m-sub">Pick the goal this project serves. Department goals roll up into organizational goals on the map.</p>' +
      '<label>Goal</label><select id="w116lg"><option value="">No goal</option>' +
      (opts.length ? '<optgroup label="Department goals">' + opts.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (Number(p.goal_id) === Number(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(shortLabel(x.department)) + ': ' + E(String(x.title).slice(0, 70)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>' : '') +
      (orgs.length ? '<optgroup label="Organizational goals">' + orgs.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (Number(p.goal_id) === Number(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(String(x.title).slice(0, 70)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>' : '') +
      '</select><div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116lc">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116ls">Save</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116lc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116ls').addEventListener('click', async function () { var v = Number(document.getElementById('w116lg').value) || null; closeModal(); await linkProject(p.id, v); });
  }
  function pickParentFor(g) {
    var orgs = goals().filter(function (x) { return x.department === 'org'; });
    openModal('<div class="w116gm"><h3>Roll up ' + E(String(g.title).slice(0, 60)) + '</h3><p class="m-sub">Which organizational goal does this department goal contribute to?</p>' +
      '<label>Organizational goal</label><select id="w116rg"><option value="">None</option>' + orgs.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (Number(g.parent_goal_id) === Number(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(String(x.title).slice(0, 80)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116rc">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116rs">Save</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116rc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116rs').addEventListener('click', async function () { var v = Number(document.getElementById('w116rg').value) || null; closeModal(); await setParent(g, v); });
  }
  function pickStatus(el, g) {
    if (el.querySelector('select')) return;
    var sel = document.createElement('select'); sel.className = 'inl';
    var opts = STATUSES.slice(); if (g.status && opts.indexOf(g.status) < 0) opts.unshift(g.status);
    sel.innerHTML = opts.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + E(s) + '"' + (s === (g.status || 'not started') ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(s) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    el.innerHTML = ''; el.appendChild(sel); sel.focus();
    var done = false;
    sel.addEventListener('change', function () { done = true; setStatus(g, sel.value); });
    sel.addEventListener('blur', function () { setTimeout(function () { if (!done) refreshAll(); }, 150); });
    sel.addEventListener('click', function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); });
  }

  /* ---------- shared blocks ---------- */
  function smartHtml(g, compact) {
    return '<div class="w116smart">' + SMART.map(function (s) {
      var val;
      if (s[2] === 'time') {
        var bits = [];
        if (g.quarter) bits.push(g.quarter);
        if (g.due_date) bits.push('due ' + fmtDate(g.due_date) + (isLate(g) ? ' (past due)' : ''));
        val = bits.join(' \u00b7 ');
      } else val = g[s[2]];
      var miss = !val;
      return '<div class="s"><div class="l"><i>' + s[0] + '</i><b>' + s[1] + '</b></div><div class="t' + (miss ? ' miss' : '') + '"' + (miss && staff() ? ' data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '" title="Write it"' : '') + '>' + (miss ? (staff() ? 'Not written yet. Click to add it.' : 'Not written yet.') : E(val)) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function winHtml(g) {
    var miss = !g.success;
    return '<div class="w116win"><b>What achieved looks like</b><div class="t' + (miss ? ' miss' : '') + '"' + (miss && staff() ? ' data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '"' : '') + '>' + (miss ? (staff() ? 'Describe the day this goal is met. Click to add it.' : 'Not described yet.') : E(g.success)) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function taskRow(t) {
    return '<div class="row' + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" data-gltgl="' + E(t.id) + '" data-gldept="' + E(t.department || '') + '"' + (t.done ? ' checked' : '') + (staff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' /><a class="t" href="#task/' + E(t.id) + '">' + E(t.title) + '</a><span class="m">' + E(t.owner || '') + (t.due_date ? (t.owner ? ' \u00b7 ' : '') + E(t.due_date) : '') + '</span></div>';
  }
  function invHtml(g, iv, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var isOrg = g.department === 'org';
    var html = '<div class="w116inv">';
    if (isOrg) {
      html += '<div class="c"><h4>Teams involved <span class="n">' + iv.depts.length + '</span></h4>' +
        (iv.depts.length ? iv.depts.map(function (d) {
          var kg = iv.kids.filter(function (k) { return k.department === d; }).length, kp = iv.projects.filter(function (p) { return p.department === d; }).length, kt = iv.open.filter(function (t) { return t.department === d; }).length;
          return '<div class="row"><span class="d" style="background:' + color(d) + '"></span><a class="t" data-gact="team" data-team="' + E(d) + '">' + E(label(d)) + '</a><span class="m">' + [kg ? kg + ' goal' + (kg === 1 ? '' : 's') : '', kp ? kp + ' project' + (kp === 1 ? '' : 's') : '', kt ? kt + ' open' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') + '</span></div>';
        }).join('') : '<div class="none">No team is linked yet.' + (staff() ? ' <a data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">Link a project</a> or roll a department goal up here.' : '') + '</div>') + '</div>';
      html += '<div class="c"><h4>Department goals rolling up <span class="n">' + iv.kids.length + '</span></h4>' +
        (iv.kids.length ? iv.kids.map(function (k) { return '<div class="row"><span class="d" style="background:' + color(k.department) + '"></span><a class="t" data-gact="opengoal" data-gid="' + E(k.id) + '" title="' + E(k.title) + '">' + E(k.title) + '</a>' + stPill(k, false) + '</div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="none">None yet. Department goals pick this goal in their "Rolls up to" field.</div>') + '</div>';
    }
    html += '<div class="c"><h4>Projects <span class="n">' + iv.projects.length + '</span>' + (staff() ? '<a data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">Link</a>' : '') + '</h4>' +
      (iv.projects.length ? iv.projects.map(function (p) { var op = iv.open.filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id) === Number(p.id); }).length; return '<div class="row"><span class="d" style="background:' + color(p.department) + '"></span><a class="t" href="#project/' + E(p.id) + '">' + E(projName(p)) + '</a><span class="m">' + (projActive(p) ? op + ' open' : E(p.status)) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="none">No project linked yet.</div>') + '</div>';
    var shown = iv.open.slice(0, opts.taskLimit || 6);
    html += '<div class="c"><h4>Tasks <span class="n">' + iv.open.length + ' open</span>' + (iv.done.length ? '<span class="m" style="font-weight:600;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none">' + iv.done.length + ' done</span>' : '') + '</h4>' +
      (shown.length ? shown.map(taskRow).join('') + (iv.open.length > shown.length ? '<div class="more" data-gact="alltasks" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">Show all ' + iv.open.length + ' open tasks</div>' : '') : '<div class="none">Nothing open under this goal' + (iv.done.length ? '; ' + iv.done.length + ' done' : '') + '.</div>') + '</div>';
    html += '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  function commentsHtml(g) {
    var cmts = (typeof GL_COMMENTS !== 'undefined' ? GL_COMMENTS : []).filter(function (c) { return Number(c.goal_id) === Number(g.id); });
    var open = (typeof WA_GL_OPEN !== 'undefined' && WA_GL_OPEN[g.id] && WA_GL_OPEN[g.id].c);
    return { n: cmts.length, html: '<div class="w116cm"><div class="gl-sub gl-cmts' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '" data-glcbox="' + E(g.id) + '">' +
      (cmts.length ? cmts.map(function (c) {
        var mine = staff() && typeof waFirstTok === 'function' && waFirstTok(c.author || '') === waFirstTok(who0() || '');
        return '<div class="gl-cmt" data-glc="' + E(c.id) + '">' + (mine ? '<span class="ops"><button data-glce="' + E(c.id) + '">edit</button><button data-glcx="' + E(c.id) + '">delete</button></span>' : '') +
          '<span class="who">' + E(c.author || 'someone') + '</span><span class="when">' + (typeof fmtDay === 'function' ? fmtDay(c.created_at) : E(String(c.created_at || '').slice(0, 10))) + (c.updated_at ? ' (edited)' : '') + '</span><div data-glcb="' + E(c.id) + '">' + (window.w138Mark ? window.w138Mark(E(c.body)) : E(c.body)) + '</div></div>';
      }).join('') : '<div class="mp-empty">No comments yet.</div>') +
      (staff() ? '<div class="gl-addc"><input data-glci="' + E(g.id) + '" placeholder="Add a comment" /><button data-glca="' + E(g.id) + '">Post</button></div>' : '') + '</div></div>' };
  }

  /* ---------- Organizational goals page ---------- */
  function orgState() { if (!M.org.st) M.org.st = { horizon: '', status: 'active', q: '' }; return M.org.st; }
  function orgCard(g) {
    var iv = inv(g); var cm = commentsHtml(g);
    var ownerM = g.owner ? memberByName(g.owner) : null;
    return '<article class="w116og" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="hd"><div class="ttl"><small>Organizational goal \u00b7 ' + E(horizon(g)) + '</small><h3>' + E(g.title) + '</h3></div>' +
      '<div class="meta">' + stPill(g, staff()) +
      '<span class="tag' + (g.owner ? '' : ' warn') + '"' + (staff() ? ' data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '" style="cursor:pointer"' : '') + '>' + (g.owner ? av(ownerM || { name: g.owner }, 18) : '') + '<small>Owner</small>' + (g.owner ? E(g.owner) : 'Not assigned') + '</span>' +
      (g.due_date ? '<span class="tag' + (isLate(g) ? ' warn' : '') + '"><small>Due</small>' + E(fmtDate(g.due_date)) + '</span>' : '') +
      '</div></div>' +
      progressHtml(g, iv, true) +
      smartHtml(g) + winHtml(g) + invHtml(g, iv, { taskLimit: 6 }) +
      '<div class="ft">' + (staff() ? '<a data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">Edit</a><a data-gact="newdept" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">+ Department goal under this</a>' : '') +
      '<a data-glcmt="' + E(g.id) + '">Comments (' + cm.n + ')</a><a data-gact="map" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">See it on the map</a><span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<span class="mut">' + (g.updated_at ? 'Updated ' + E(fmtDate(g.updated_at)) + (g.updated_by ? ' by ' + E(window.waWho ? waWho(g.updated_by) : g.updated_by) : '') : '') + '</span></div>' + cm.html +
      '</article>';
  }
  function renderOrg() {
    var host = M.org.host; if (!host || !document.body.contains(host)) return;
    var st = orgState(); var all = goals().filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org'; });
    var horizons = []; all.forEach(function (g) { var h = horizon(g); if (horizons.indexOf(h) < 0) horizons.push(h); });
    var rows = all.filter(function (g) {
      if (st.status === 'active' && isDone(g)) return false;
      if (st.status === 'done' && !isDone(g)) return false;
      if (st.horizon && horizon(g) !== st.horizon) return false;
      var q = lower(st.q); if (q && lower(g.title + ' ' + (g.detail || '') + ' ' + (g.measure || '') + ' ' + (g.owner || '')).indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
    var live = all.filter(function (g) { return !isDone(g); });
    var onTrack = live.filter(function (g) { var s = lower(g.status); return s === 'on track' || s === 'in progress'; }).length;
    var risk = live.filter(function (g) { var s = lower(g.status); return s === 'at risk' || s === 'delayed' || s === 'incomplete' || isLate(g); }).length;
    var deptSet = {}, projN = 0, openN = 0;
    live.forEach(function (g) { var iv = inv(g); iv.depts.forEach(function (d) { deptSet[d] = 1; }); projN += iv.projects.length; openN += iv.open.length; });
    var html = '<div class="w116g">' +
      '<div class="sum">' +
      '<div class="k' + (st.status === 'active' && !st.horizon ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gact="sum" data-v="active"><b>' + live.length + '</b>organizational goal' + (live.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' in play</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="sum" data-v="ontrack"><b>' + onTrack + '</b>on track or in progress</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (risk ? ' warn' : '') + '" data-gact="sum" data-v="risk"><b>' + risk + '</b>at risk, delayed or past due</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="sum" data-v="teams"><b>' + Object.keys(deptSet).length + '</b>teams aligned</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="sum" data-v="projects"><b>' + projN + '</b>projects serving them</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="sum" data-v="open"><b>' + openN + '</b>open tasks underneath</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="tb">' +
      '<select data-gact="horizon"><option value="">All horizons</option>' + horizons.map(function (h) { return '<option value="' + E(h) + '"' + (st.horizon === h ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(h) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<select data-gact="statusf">' + [['active', 'In play'], ['done', 'Achieved or closed'], ['all', 'Everything']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.status === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<span class="sp"></span><input type="search" data-gact="q" placeholder="Search goals" value="' + E(st.q) + '" />' +
      (staff() ? '<button class="new" data-gact="neworg">+ New organizational goal</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    html += rows.length ? rows.map(orgCard).join('') : '<p class="empty">' + (all.length ? 'No organizational goal matches these filters.' : 'No organizational goals yet.' + (staff() ? ' Add the first one: it is the direction every team goal rolls up into.' : '')) + '</p>';
    html += '<p class="note">S, M, A, R and T are the five parts of a SMART goal. Department goals roll up here through their "Rolls up to" field; projects link through "Projects that serve this goal"; tasks follow their projects. <a data-gact="gomap">Open the map</a> to see the whole picture.</p></div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
    var q = host.querySelector('input[data-gact="q"]'); if (q && M.org.qFocus) { q.focus(); q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); M.org.qFocus = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- Department goals page ---------- */
  function deptState() {
    if (!M.dept.st) {
      var def = { team: '', quarter: '__cur', status: 'active', q: '', closed: {}, open: {} };
      try { var s = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('wa116.deptgoals') || 'null'); if (s) ['team', 'quarter', 'status', 'closed'].forEach(function (k) { if (s[k] !== undefined) def[k] = s[k]; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      M.dept.st = def;
    }
    return M.dept.st;
  }
  function saveDeptState() { try { var st = deptState(); localStorage.setItem('wa116.deptgoals', JSON.stringify({ team: st.team, quarter: st.quarter, status: st.status, closed: st.closed })); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function deptCard(g) {
    var iv = inv(g); var open = deptState().open[g.id]; var par = g.parent_goal_id ? goalById(g.parent_goal_id) : null; var cm = commentsHtml(g);
    var ownerM = g.owner ? memberByName(g.owner) : null;
    var html = '<div class="w116gc' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">' +
      '<div class="top"><div class="nm" data-gact="toggle" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">' + E(g.title) + '</div>' + stPill(g, staff()) + '</div>' +
      (g.measure ? '<div class="ms"><b>Measure:</b> ' + E(g.measure) + '</div>' : (g.detail && !open ? '<div class="ms">' + E(String(g.detail).slice(0, 140)) + (g.detail.length > 140 ? '\u2026' : '') + '</div>' : '')) +
      '<div class="tags"><span>' + E(horizon(g)) + '</span>' + (g.due_date ? '<span class="' + (isLate(g) ? 'late' : '') + '">' + (isLate(g) ? 'Past due ' : 'Due ') + E(fmtDate(g.due_date)) + '</span>' : '') +
      (par ? '<span class="up" title="' + E(par.title) + '">\u2191 ' + E(String(par.title).slice(0, 34)) + (par.title.length > 34 ? '\u2026' : '') + '</span>' : (staff() ? '<span class="up" style="background:rgba(121,119,115,.14);color:var(--wa-muted);cursor:pointer" data-gact="rollup" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">\u2191 Roll up to an org goal</span>' : '')) +
      (iv.projects.length ? '<span>' + iv.projects.length + ' project' + (iv.projects.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>' : '') + '<span>' + iv.open.length + ' open task' + (iv.open.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>' +
      (g.owner ? '<span class="own">' + av(ownerM || { name: g.owner }, 16) + E(g.owner) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (progress(g, iv).pct != null || open ? progressHtml(g, iv, false) : '');
    if (open) {
      html += '<div class="det">' + smartHtml(g, true) + winHtml(g) + invHtml(g, iv, { taskLimit: 8 }) + '</div>';
    }
    html += '<div class="ft"><a data-gact="toggle" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">' + (open ? 'Collapse' : 'Open') + '</a>' + (staff() ? '<a data-gact="edit" data-gid="' + E(g.id) + '">Edit</a>' : '') + '<a data-glcmt="' + E(g.id) + '">Comments (' + cm.n + ')</a><span class="sp"></span>' + (g.clickup_task_id ? '<span class="mut" title="Status changes sync to the ClickUp task">ClickUp linked</span>' : '') + '</div>' + cm.html + '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  function suggestionsHtml(d, q) {
    var ideas = (typeof WA30_IDEAS !== 'undefined' && WA30_IDEAS[d] && WA30_IDEAS[d].suggestions) || [];
    if (!staff()) return '';
    if (!ideas.length) return '<div class="w116sug"><div class="gen">The Hub can draft goal ideas for ' + E(label(d)) + ' from recent transcripts and the board: <a data-w30r="' + E(d) + '">generate suggestions</a>. Nothing is added until you review it.</div></div>';
    return '<details class="w116sug"><summary>' + ideas.length + ' suggested goal' + (ideas.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' (from the Hub, drawn from transcripts and the board; nothing is added until you say so)</summary>' +
      ideas.map(function (s, i) { return '<div class="w30sug"><b>' + E(s.title || '') + '</b><div class="m">' + E(s.detail || '') + (s.measure ? ' Measure: ' + E(s.measure) : '') + '</div><div class="why">' + E(s.why || '') + '</div><div style="margin-top:6px"><button class="btn mini" data-w30a="' + E(d) + '|' + i + '">Review and add</button></div></div>'; }).join('') +
      '<div class="gen" style="margin-top:8px">Generated ' + E(String((WA30_IDEAS[d] && WA30_IDEAS[d].generated) || '').slice(0, 10)) + ' \u00b7 <a data-w30r="' + E(d) + '">refresh suggestions</a></div></details>';
  }
  function renderDept() {
    var host = M.dept.host; if (!host || !document.body.contains(host)) return;
    var st = deptState(); var all = goals().filter(function (g) { return g.department !== 'org'; });
    var cur = curQuarter(); var qs = []; try { qs = wa30Quarters().slice(0, 2); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    all.forEach(function (g) { var q = g.quarter || 'No quarter'; if (qs.indexOf(q) < 0) qs.push(q); });
    qs.sort(function (a, b) { return qKey(a) - qKey(b); });
    var quarter = st.quarter === '__cur' ? cur : st.quarter;
    var teams = T.order.filter(function (d) { return d !== 'board' || all.some(function (g) { return g.department === 'board'; }); });
    all.forEach(function (g) { if (g.department && teams.indexOf(g.department) < 0) teams.push(g.department); });
    var rows = all.filter(function (g) {
      if (quarter && quarter !== '__all' && (g.quarter || 'No quarter') !== quarter) return false;
      if (st.status === 'active' && isDone(g)) return false;
      if (st.status === 'done' && !isDone(g)) return false;
      if (st.status === 'risk' && !(['at risk', 'delayed', 'incomplete'].indexOf(lower(g.status)) >= 0 || isLate(g))) return false;
      var q = lower(st.q); if (q && lower(g.title + ' ' + (g.detail || '') + ' ' + (g.measure || '') + ' ' + (g.owner || '')).indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
    var inQ = all.filter(function (g) { return !quarter || quarter === '__all' || (g.quarter || 'No quarter') === quarter; });
    var live = inQ.filter(function (g) { return !isDone(g); });
    var done = inQ.length - live.length;
    var risk = live.filter(function (g) { return ['at risk', 'delayed', 'incomplete'].indexOf(lower(g.status)) >= 0 || isLate(g); }).length;
    var rolled = live.filter(function (g) { return g.parent_goal_id && goalById(g.parent_goal_id); }).length;
    var html = '<div class="w116g">' +
      '<div class="sum">' +
      '<div class="k' + (st.status === 'active' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gact="sum" data-v="active"><b>' + live.length + '</b>goal' + (live.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' in play' + (quarter && quarter !== '__all' ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(quarter) : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (risk ? ' warn' : '') + (st.status === 'risk' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gact="sum" data-v="risk"><b>' + risk + '</b>at risk, delayed or past due</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (st.status === 'done' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gact="sum" data-v="done"><b>' + done + '</b>achieved or closed</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="sum" data-v="rolled"><b>' + (live.length ? Math.round(rolled / live.length * 100) : 0) + '%</b>roll up to an organizational goal</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="chips">' + [['', 'All teams', null]].concat(teams.map(function (d) { return [d, label(d), color(d)]; })).map(function (c) { var n = c[0] ? inQ.filter(function (g) { return g.department === c[0]; }).length : inQ.length; return '<span class="chip' + (st.team === c[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gact="team" data-team="' + E(c[0]) + '">' + (c[2] ? '<span class="d" style="background:' + c[2] + '"></span>' : '') + E(c[1]) + '<span class="n">' + n + '</span></span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="tb"><span class="w116seg">' + qs.map(function (q) { var v = q === cur ? '__cur' : q; return '<a data-gact="quarter" data-v="' + E(v) + '" class="' + (st.quarter === v ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(q === cur ? 'This quarter' : q) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '<a data-gact="quarter" data-v="__all" class="' + (st.quarter === '__all' ? 'on' : '') + '">All quarters</a></span>' +
      (quarter && quarter !== '__all' ? '<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">' + E(quarter) + (function () { try { var r = wa30QRange(quarter); return r ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(r) : ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } })() + '</span>' : '') +
      '<span class="sp"></span><select data-gact="statusf">' + [['active', 'In play'], ['risk', 'Needs attention'], ['done', 'Achieved or closed'], ['all', 'Everything']].map(function (o) { return '<option value="' + o[0] + '"' + (st.status === o[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<input type="search" data-gact="q" placeholder="Search goals" value="' + E(st.q) + '" />' +
      (staff() ? '<button class="new" data-gact="newdept" data-team="' + E(st.team || '') + '">+ New goal</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    var shown = teams.filter(function (d) { return !st.team || st.team === d; });
    shown.forEach(function (d) {
      var gs = rows.filter(function (g) { return g.department === d; });
      var openN = gs.reduce(function (n, g) { return n + inv(g).open.length; }, 0);
      var closed = st.closed && st.closed[d];
      html += '<div class="w116gg' + (closed ? ' closed' : '') + '" data-gg="' + E(d) + '"><div class="gh" data-gact="fold" data-team="' + E(d) + '"><span class="chev">&#9660;</span><span class="dot" style="background:' + color(d) + '"></span><b>' + E(label(d)) + '</b><span class="n">' + gs.length + '</span><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">' + openN + ' open task' + (openN === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (staff() ? '<span class="add" data-gact="newdept" data-team="' + E(d) + '">+ Add goal</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="gb">' + (gs.length ? '<div class="w116ggrid">' + gs.map(deptCard).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="empty">No goals here' + (st.q || st.status !== 'active' || (quarter && quarter !== '__all') ? ' with these filters' : '') + '.' + (staff() ? ' <a data-gact="newdept" data-team="' + E(d) + '" style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-weight:700;cursor:pointer">Add one</a>.' : '') + '</p>') + suggestionsHtml(d, quarter) + '</div></div>';
    });
    html += '<p class="note">' + rows.length + ' of ' + all.length + ' department goals shown. Open a goal for its SMART detail, tasks and comments; status changes sync to ClickUp where a goal is still mapped there. <a id="w34how" style="cursor:pointer">How this fits together</a> \u00b7 <a data-gact="gomap">Open the map</a></p></div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
    var q = host.querySelector('input[data-gact="q"]'); if (q && M.dept.qFocus) { q.focus(); q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); M.dept.qFocus = false; }
  }

  /* ---------- Goal alignment: the map ---------- */
  function mapState() { if (!M.map.st) M.map.st = { team: '', q: '' }; return M.map.st; }
  function node(kind, id, dept, title, meta, orphan, linkLabel) {
    return '<div class="w116mn' + (orphan ? ' orph' : '') + '" data-mn="' + kind + id + '" style="border-left-color:' + color(dept) + '">' + E(title) + (meta ? '<div class="m">' + meta + '</div>' : '') + (orphan && staff() && linkLabel ? '<span class="lk" data-gact="' + (kind === 'P' ? 'linkproj' : 'rollup') + '" data-gid="' + E(id) + '">' + linkLabel + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function renderMap() {
    var host = M.map.host; if (!host || !document.body.contains(host)) return;
    /* wave205 (Steel, 04/10/26): "organizational goals, and departments own projects": department goals are retired, so the map
       is projects to the organizational goals (and the milestone each project serves) */
    var st = mapState(); var all = goals();
    var org = all.filter(function (g) { return g.department === 'org' && !isDone(g); }).sort(function (a, b) { return (a.number || 99) - (b.number || 99) || a.id - b.id; });
    var ps = projects().filter(function (p) { return projActive(p) && (!st.team || p.department === st.team); });
    var q = lower(st.q);
    if (q) {
      var match = function (s) { return lower(s).indexOf(q) >= 0; };
      ps = ps.filter(function (p) { return match(projName(p)); });
    }
    var orgIds = {}; org.forEach(function (g) { orgIds[String(g.id)] = 1; });
    var MS = []; try { MS = (window.w205 && window.w205.state().milestones) || []; } catch (e) { MS = []; }
    function msName(id) { for (var i = 0; i < MS.length; i++) { if (String(MS[i].id) === String(id)) return MS[i].fy + ': ' + MS[i].body; } return ''; }
    M.map.edges = [];
    ps.forEach(function (p) { if (p.goal_id && orgIds[String(p.goal_id)]) M.map.edges.push(['P' + p.id, 'O' + p.goal_id]); });
    var teams = T.order.slice();
    var linkedP = ps.filter(function (p) { return p.goal_id && orgIds[String(p.goal_id)]; }).length;
    var bare = org.filter(function (g) { return !ps.some(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(g.id); }); }).length;
    var html = '<div class="w116g">' +
      '<div class="sum">' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="mapsum" data-v="p"><b>' + ps.length + '</b>active projects</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (ps.length - linkedP ? ' warn' : '') + '" data-gact="mapsum" data-v="po"><b>' + (ps.length - linkedP) + '</b>projects without a goal</div>' +
      '<div class="k" data-gact="mapsum" data-v="o"><b>' + org.length + '</b>organizational goals</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (bare ? ' warn' : '') + '" data-gact="mapsum" data-v="o"><b>' + bare + '</b>goals with no project yet</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="chips">' + [['', 'All teams', null]].concat(teams.map(function (d) { return [d, label(d), color(d)]; })).map(function (c) { return '<span class="chip' + (st.team === c[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-gact="mapteam" data-team="' + E(c[0]) + '">' + (c[2] ? '<span class="d" style="background:' + c[2] + '"></span>' : '') + E(c[1]) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '<span class="sp" style="flex:1"></span><input type="search" data-gact="mapq" placeholder="Find a project" value="' + E(st.q) + '" style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12.5px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.14);border-radius:10px;padding:6px 10px;background:var(--wa-card)" /></div>' +
      '<div class="w116mleg"><span><span class="sw"></span>serves</span><span><span class="sw hl"></span>the chain you hover or click</span><span><span class="sw orph"></span>not linked yet' + (staff() ? ' (click the red link to fix it)' : '') + '</span>' + (M.map.focus ? '<span class="clr" data-gact="mapclear">Clear focus</span>' : '<span style="margin-left:auto">Click a node to pin its chain</span>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116map"><svg></svg><div class="w116mcols" style="grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr">' +
      '<div class="w116mcol"><h4>Projects</h4><div class="hint">each department’s work underway</div>' + (ps.length ? ps.map(function (p) { var op = tasks().filter(function (t) { return !t.done && Number(t.project_id) === Number(p.id); }).length; var linked = p.goal_id && orgIds[String(p.goal_id)]; var ms = linked && p.milestone_id ? msName(p.milestone_id) : ''; return node('P', p.id, p.department, projName(p), '<span>' + E(shortLabel(p.department)) + '</span><span>' + op + ' open</span>' + (ms ? '<span title="' + E(ms) + '">' + E(String(ms).slice(0, 34)) + (String(ms).length > 34 ? '…' : '') + '</span>' : ''), !linked, 'Link to a goal'); }).join('') : '<p class="empty">No projects match.</p>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116mcol"><h4>Organizational goals</h4><div class="hint">FY27 to FY29, gated to FY31</div>' + (org.length ? org.map(function (g) { var n = ps.filter(function (p) { return String(p.goal_id) === String(g.id); }).length; return node('O', g.id, 'org', (g.number ? g.number + '. ' : '') + g.title, stPill(g, false) + '<span>' + n + ' project' + (n === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>', false); }).join('') : '<p class="empty">No organizational goals yet.</p>') + '</div>' +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<p class="note">Hover anything to light up its chain; click to pin it. Link a project from the red link here, from Projects, or from its goal on Goals (to a milestone too). <a data-gact="goorg">Goals</a></p></div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
    var qi = host.querySelector('input[data-gact="mapq"]'); if (qi && M.map.qFocus) { qi.focus(); qi.setSelectionRange(qi.value.length, qi.value.length); M.map.qFocus = false; }
    drawMap();
    if (M.map.focus) mapHighlight(M.map.focus);
    setTimeout(drawMap, 80); setTimeout(drawMap, 400);
  }
  function drawMap() {
    var host = M.map.host; if (!host) return;
    var map = host.querySelector('.w116map'); if (!map || map.offsetParent === null) return;
    var svg = map.querySelector('svg'); if (!svg) return;
    var box = map.getBoundingClientRect();
    var h = map.scrollHeight;
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 ' + Math.max(1, box.width) + ' ' + Math.max(1, h));
    svg.style.height = h + 'px';
    var pos = {};
    map.querySelectorAll('.w116mn').forEach(function (n) { var r = n.getBoundingClientRect(); pos[n.getAttribute('data-mn')] = { l: r.left - box.left, r: r.right - box.left, y: r.top - box.top + r.height / 2 }; });
    var parts = [];
    M.map.edges.forEach(function (e) {
      var a = pos[e[0]], b = pos[e[1]]; if (!a || !b) return;
      var x1 = a.r, y1 = a.y, x2 = b.l, y2 = b.y, mx = (x1 + x2) / 2;
      parts.push('<path class="w116me" data-me="' + e[0] + '|' + e[1] + '" d="M' + x1.toFixed(1) + ' ' + y1.toFixed(1) + ' C' + mx.toFixed(1) + ' ' + y1.toFixed(1) + ', ' + mx.toFixed(1) + ' ' + y2.toFixed(1) + ', ' + x2.toFixed(1) + ' ' + y2.toFixed(1) + '" />');
    });
    svg.innerHTML = parts.join('');
    if (M.map.focus) mapHighlight(M.map.focus);
  }
  function chain(id) {
    var set = {}; set[id] = 1; var grew = true;
    while (grew) { grew = false; M.map.edges.forEach(function (e) { if (set[e[0]] && !set[e[1]]) { set[e[1]] = 1; grew = true; } if (set[e[1]] && !set[e[0]]) { set[e[0]] = 1; grew = true; } }); }
    return set;
  }
  function mapHighlight(id) {
    var host = M.map.host; if (!host) return; var map = host.querySelector('.w116map'); if (!map) return;
    if (!id) { map.querySelectorAll('.w116mn, path.w116me').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('hl'); x.classList.remove('dim'); }); return; }
    var set = chain(id);
    map.querySelectorAll('.w116mn').forEach(function (n) { var k = n.getAttribute('data-mn'); n.classList.toggle('hl', !!set[k]); n.classList.toggle('dim', !set[k]); });
    map.querySelectorAll('path.w116me').forEach(function (p) { var e = (p.getAttribute('data-me') || '').split('|'); var on = set[e[0]] && set[e[1]]; p.classList.toggle('hl', !!on); p.classList.toggle('dim', !on); });
  }

  /* ---------- panels, routing, refresh ---------- */
  function panel(id, crumb, title, intro, hostId) {
    var main = document.querySelector('main'); if (!main) return null;
    var sec = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!sec) {
      sec = document.createElement('section'); sec.id = id; sec.className = 'panel';
      sec.innerHTML = '<div class="w116ptop"><div class="crumb">' + crumb + '</div><h2>' + title + '</h2><p>' + intro + '</p></div><div class="card" id="' + hostId + '"><p class="read">Loading...</p></div>';
      var after = document.getElementById('projects') || document.getElementById('goals');
      if (after && after.parentNode === main) after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', sec); else main.appendChild(sec);
    }
    return sec;
  }
  function ensurePanels() {
    panel('orggoals', 'Strategy', 'Organizational goals', 'The direction of Welfare Avenue for the year and beyond, written SMART, with the teams, projects and tasks that carry each one.', 'w116orghost');
    panel('goalmap', 'Strategy', 'Goal alignment', 'How it all connects: every department\u2019s projects and the organizational goal each one serves.', 'w116maphost');
  }
  function visiblePanel(id) { var s = document.getElementById(id); return !!(s && s.classList.contains('active')); }
  async function showOrg() { ensurePanels(); if (!visiblePanel('orggoals')) return; M.org.host = document.getElementById('w116orghost'); if (!M.org.host) return; if (!M.loaded) M.org.host.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Loading goals...</p>'; await load(); try { if (typeof wa30LoadIdeas === 'function') await wa30LoadIdeas(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } renderOrg(); }
  async function showMap() { ensurePanels(); if (!visiblePanel('goalmap')) return; M.map.host = document.getElementById('w116maphost'); if (!M.map.host) return; if (!M.loaded) M.map.host.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Loading the map...</p>'; await load(); renderMap(); }
  async function refreshAll() {
    try { if (M.org.host && visiblePanel('orggoals')) renderOrg(); } catch (e) { console.warn('org goals', e); }
    try { if (M.dept.host && visiblePanel('goals')) renderDept(); } catch (e) { console.warn('dept goals', e); }
    try { if (M.map.host && visiblePanel('goalmap')) renderMap(); } catch (e) { console.warn('goal map', e); }
  }
  window.w116Goals = { refresh: function () { M.loaded = 0; return load(true).then(refreshAll); }, refreshLive: function () { try { refreshAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, org: renderOrg, dept: renderDept, map: renderMap, modal: goalModal };

  /* the Department goals page takes over the goals page body after the older waves have run */
  if (typeof renderGoalsPage === 'function') {
    var __rg116f = renderGoalsPage;
    renderGoalsPage = async function () {
      var r = await __rg116f.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var body = document.getElementById('glBody');
        if (body && staff()) {
          M.dept.host = body;
          if (!M.projects.length) await load();
          renderDept();
        } else if (body && !staff()) {
          body.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Department goals are available to staff accounts.</p>';
        }
        var head = document.querySelector('#goals .card h2');
        if (head) { var help = head.querySelector('button, .w43help'); head.textContent = 'Department goals'; if (help) head.appendChild(help); }
        var intro = document.querySelector('#goals .card p.read');
        if (intro) intro.textContent = 'Every team\'s quarterly goals in one place: filter by team and quarter, change a status inline, open a goal for its SMART detail, tasks and comments. Each goal rolls up to an organizational goal.';
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave116f dept goals', e); }
      return r;
    };
  }
  /* the wave16 comment and task handlers repaint through waGlRerender */
  if (typeof waGlRerender === 'function') {
    var __glr116f = waGlRerender;
    waGlRerender = function () { var r = __glr116f.apply(this, arguments); try { refreshAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return r; };
  }

  /* ---------- interactions ---------- */
  var qTimer = null;
  function inHosts(t) { return [M.org.host, M.dept.host, M.map.host].some(function (h) { return h && h.contains(t); }); }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116fWired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116fWired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      if (!inHosts(e.target)) return;
      var a = e.target.closest('[data-gact]');
      if (!a) {
        var n = e.target.closest('.w116mn');
        if (n && M.map.host && M.map.host.contains(n)) { var id = n.getAttribute('data-mn'); M.map.focus = M.map.focus === id ? null : id; mapHighlight(M.map.focus); var leg = M.map.host.querySelector('.w116mleg'); if (leg) renderMap(); }
        return;
      }
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-gact'); var gid = a.getAttribute('data-gid'); var g = gid ? goalById(gid) : null;
      var isOrgHost = M.org.host && M.org.host.contains(a), isDeptHost = M.dept.host && M.dept.host.contains(a);
      if (act === 'sum') {
        var v = a.getAttribute('data-v');
        if (isOrgHost) { var so = orgState(); if (v === 'active') { so.status = 'active'; so.horizon = ''; } else if (v === 'risk') { so.status = 'active'; so.q = ''; } renderOrg(); }
        else { var sd = deptState(); if (v === 'active') sd.status = 'active'; else if (v === 'risk') sd.status = sd.status === 'risk' ? 'active' : 'risk'; else if (v === 'done') sd.status = sd.status === 'done' ? 'active' : 'done'; else if (v === 'rolled') { location.hash = '#goalmap'; return; } saveDeptState(); renderDept(); }
        return;
      }
      if (act === 'mapsum') { var mv = a.getAttribute('data-v'); if (mv === 'o') { location.hash = '#orggoals'; } else if (mv === 'g' || mv === 'go') { location.hash = '#goals'; } else { location.hash = '#projects'; } return; }
      if (act === 'team') { var d = a.getAttribute('data-team') || ''; if (isOrgHost) { deptState().team = d; saveDeptState(); location.hash = '#goals'; return; } var sdt = deptState(); sdt.team = sdt.team === d ? '' : d; if (!d) sdt.team = ''; saveDeptState(); renderDept(); return; }
      if (act === 'mapteam') { mapState().team = a.getAttribute('data-team') || ''; M.map.focus = null; renderMap(); return; }
      if (act === 'mapclear') { M.map.focus = null; renderMap(); return; }
      if (act === 'quarter') { deptState().quarter = a.getAttribute('data-v'); saveDeptState(); renderDept(); return; }
      if (act === 'fold') { var k = a.getAttribute('data-team'); var sf = deptState(); sf.closed = sf.closed || {}; if (sf.closed[k]) delete sf.closed[k]; else sf.closed[k] = 1; saveDeptState(); a.closest('.w116gg').classList.toggle('closed'); return; }
      if (act === 'toggle') { var so2 = deptState(); if (so2.open[gid]) delete so2.open[gid]; else so2.open[gid] = 1; renderDept(); return; }
      if (act === 'opengoal') { if (!g) return; var sd2 = deptState(); sd2.team = g.department; sd2.quarter = '__all'; sd2.status = 'all'; sd2.open[g.id] = 1; saveDeptState(); location.hash = '#goals'; return; }
      if (act === 'alltasks') { if (!g) return; try { var first = inv(g).projects[0]; if (window.w116Board && first) window.w116Board.preset('project:' + first.id); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } var dept = g.department === 'org' ? (inv(g).depts[0] || T.order[0]) : g.department; location.hash = '#team/' + (T.order.indexOf(dept) >= 0 ? dept : T.order[0]) + '/tasks'; return; }
      if (act === 'gomap') { location.hash = '#goalmap'; return; }
      if (act === 'goorg') { location.hash = '#orggoals'; return; }
      if (act === 'godept') { location.hash = '#goals'; return; }
      if (act === 'map') { if (g) M.map.focus = (g.department === 'org' ? 'O' : 'G') + g.id; location.hash = '#goalmap'; return; }
      if (!staff()) return;
      if (act === 'status') { if (g) { e.stopPropagation(); pickStatus(a, g); } return; }
      if (act === 'edit') { if (g) goalModal(g); return; }
      if (act === 'neworg') { goalModal(null, 'org'); return; }
      if (act === 'newdept') { goalModal(null, a.getAttribute('data-team') || (g ? (inv(g).depts[0] || T.order[0]) : (deptState().team || T.order[0])), g && g.department === 'org' ? g.id : null); return; }
      if (act === 'rollup') { if (g) pickParentFor(g); return; }
      if (act === 'linkproj') { var p = projById(gid); if (p) pickGoalFor(p); return; }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      if (!inHosts(e.target)) return;
      var a = e.target.closest('[data-gact]'); if (!a || a.tagName !== 'SELECT') return;
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-gact');
      if (act === 'horizon') { orgState().horizon = a.value; renderOrg(); }
      else if (act === 'statusf') { if (M.org.host && M.org.host.contains(a)) { orgState().status = a.value; renderOrg(); } else { deptState().status = a.value; saveDeptState(); renderDept(); } }
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
      if (!inHosts(e.target)) return;
      var act = e.target.getAttribute('data-gact'); if (act !== 'q' && act !== 'mapq') return;
      clearTimeout(qTimer); var v = e.target.value; var t = e.target;
      qTimer = setTimeout(function () {
        if (act === 'mapq') { mapState().q = v; M.map.qFocus = true; renderMap(); return; }
        if (M.org.host && M.org.host.contains(t)) { orgState().q = v; M.org.qFocus = true; renderOrg(); } else { deptState().q = v; M.dept.qFocus = true; renderDept(); }
      }, 220);
    });
    document.body.addEventListener('mouseover', function (e) { var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w116mn'); if (n && !M.map.focus && M.map.host && M.map.host.contains(n)) mapHighlight(n.getAttribute('data-mn')); });
    document.body.addEventListener('mouseout', function (e) { var n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w116mn'); if (n && !M.map.focus && M.map.host && M.map.host.contains(n)) mapHighlight(null); });
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () { clearTimeout(window.__w116mrz); window.__w116mrz = setTimeout(drawMap, 200); });
  }

  /* ---------- menu and permissions ---------- */
  function enrol() {
    try {
      if (typeof WA40_TABS === 'undefined' || typeof WA40_DEFAULTS === 'undefined') return;
      ['orggoals', 'goalmap'].forEach(function (id) {

      });
    } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
  }
  enrol();

  }

      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave116f boot', e); }
      return r;
    };
  }

     executive who decides it (owner column; legacy Both = Steel and
     Suhaib), the area sets the default (Marketing, Events, Fundraising
     -> Ian; Ops MTL, Program Development -> Shima; Grants, Ops TO, HR,

     folder shows the same names.

  /* ---------- 3. announcements under the KPI strip ---------- */
  var N = { items: [], manual: [], loaded: 0, all: false, tries: 0 };
  async function loadNews() {
    var since = new Date(Date.now() - DAYS * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    var items = [];
    try {
      var a = await db.from('hub_announcements').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(60);
      N.manual = ((a && a.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return !x.expires_at || new Date(x.expires_at) > new Date(); });
      N.manual.forEach(function (x) { items.push({ id: 'a' + x.id, kind: KINDS[x.kind] ? x.kind : 'general', title: x.title, body: x.body || '', when: x.happened_at || x.created_at, dept: x.department, link: x.link || (x.ask ? '#announce/' + x.id : null), pinned: !!x.pinned, manual: x, by: x.created_name || x.created_by }); });   /* wave180: a poll or a form opens where it is answered */
    } catch (e) { /* none */ }
    try {
      var ch = (typeof DB_CHANGES !== 'undefined' ? DB_CHANGES : []).filter(function (c) { return c.changed_at && c.changed_at.slice(0, 10) >= since; });
      var left = ch.filter(function (c) { return c.entity_type === 'team_member' && c.field === 'active' && /false/.test(String(c.new_value || '')); });
      var names = {};
      if (left.length) {
        var ids = left.map(function (c) { return Number(c.entity_id); }).filter(Boolean);
        try { var tm = await db.from('team_members').select('id, name, department').in('id', ids); ((tm && tm.data) || []).forEach(function (m) { names[String(m.id)] = m; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      }
      left.forEach(function (c) { var m = names[String(c.entity_id)]; items.push({ id: 'l' + c.id, kind: 'departure', title: (m ? m.name : 'A team member') + ' has left', body: m && m.department ? label(m.department) : '', when: c.changed_at, dept: m ? m.department : null, link: m ? '#person/' + m.id : '#orgchart' }); });
      ch.filter(function (c) { return c.entity_type === 'member' && c.field === 'invited' && /sent|admin|leader|member/.test(String(c.new_value || '')); }).forEach(function (c) {
        var em = String(c.entity_id || ''); var nm = em.split('@')[0].split('.').map(function (s) { return s ? s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1) : s; }).join(' ');
        items.push({ id: 'i' + c.id, kind: 'arrival', title: nm + ' joins the hub', body: 'Invited as ' + String(c.new_value || '').split(' ')[0] + ' by ' + String(c.changed_by || '').split('@')[0].replace('.', ' '), when: c.changed_at, link: '#orgchart' });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (canSee('finances')) {
      try {
        var f = await db.from('finance_transactions').select('id, txn_date, description, amount, department, category, duplicate_of').gte('txn_date', since).order('txn_date', { ascending: false }).limit(400);
        ((f && f.data) || []).filter(function (t) { return !t.duplicate_of && String(t.txn_date || '') >= since && Math.abs(Number(t.amount || 0)) >= BIG; }).forEach(function (t) {
          var isDon = /donation/i.test(String(t.category || '')) && Number(t.amount) > 0;
          items.push({ id: 'f' + t.id, kind: isDon ? 'donation' : 'spend', title: (isDon ? 'Donation of ' : (Number(t.amount) < 0 ? 'Spent ' : 'Received ')) + money(t.amount), body: [t.description, t.category, t.department ? label(t.department) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 '), when: t.txn_date, dept: t.department, link: '#finances' });
        });
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    try {
      var v = await db.from('volunteer_events').select('id, title, code, event_date, status, location, notes').eq('status', 'cancelled').gte('event_date', since).order('event_date', { ascending: false }).limit(40);
      var reasons = {};
      (typeof DB_CHANGES !== 'undefined' ? DB_CHANGES : []).filter(function (c) { return c.entity_type === 'volunteer_event' && c.field === 'status' && /^cancelled/.test(String(c.new_value || '')); }).forEach(function (c) { reasons[String(c.entity_id)] = { why: String(c.new_value).replace(/^cancelled:?\s*/, ''), when: c.changed_at }; });
      ((v && v.data) || []).filter(function (ev) { return ev.status === 'cancelled' && String(ev.event_date || '') >= since; }).forEach(function (ev) {
        var r = reasons[String(ev.id)] || {};
        items.push({ id: 'v' + ev.id, kind: 'event', title: (ev.title || (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(String(ev.event_date || '')) && new Date(String(ev.event_date).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').getDay() === 0 ? 'Sunday serving' : 'Serving event')) + ' cancelled', body: [ev.event_date ? 'Was planned for ' + fmtDate(ev.event_date) : '', ev.location, r.why].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 '), when: r.when || ev.event_date, link: '#volunteering' });
      });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    items.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.pinned ? 1 : 0) - (a.pinned ? 1 : 0) || new Date(b.when || 0) - new Date(a.when || 0); });
    N.items = items; N.loaded = Date.now();
  }
  function itemHtml(it) {
    var k = KINDS[it.kind] || KINDS.general;
    var mine = it.manual && (role() === 'admin' || String(it.manual.created_by || '').toLowerCase() === String((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || '').toLowerCase());
    return '<div class="w116an' + (it.pinned ? ' pin' : '') + (it.link ? ' go' : '') + '" data-anlink="' + E(it.link || '') + '">' +
      '<span class="k" style="background:' + k[1] + '">' + E(k[0].charAt(0)) + '</span>' +
      '<div class="b"><small>' + E(k[0]) + (it.pinned ? ' \u00b7 pinned' : '') + '</small><div class="t">' + E(it.title) + '</div>' + (it.body ? '<div class="d">' + E(it.body) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w">' + (isNew(it.when) ? '<span class="new" title="New since you last looked"></span>' : '') + '<span>' + E(fmtDate(it.when)) + '</span>' + (it.by ? '<span>' + E(String(it.by).split('@')[0].replace('.', ' ')) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
      (mine && canPost() ? '<span class="x" data-anx="' + E(it.manual.id) + '" title="Take this down">&times;</span>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function renderNews() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w116news'); if (!box) return;
    var items = N.items; var newN = items.filter(function (i) { return isNew(i.when); }).length;
    var shown = N.all ? items : items.slice(0, 6);
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><h3>What\'s new</h3>' + (newN ? '<span class="badge">' + newN + ' new since you last looked</span>' : '<span class="sub">nothing new since you last looked</span>') + '<span class="sub">major announcements only: departures, big spending, large donations, cancelled events</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (canPost() ? '<a data-anpost="1">+ Post an announcement</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (shown.length ? '<div class="grid">' + shown.map(itemHtml).join('') + '</div>' + (items.length > shown.length ? '<span class="more" data-anmore="1">Show all ' + items.length + '</span>' : (N.all && items.length > 6 ? '<span class="more" data-anmore="1">Show fewer</span>' : '')) : '<p class="empty">Nothing major in the last ' + DAYS + ' days.' + (canPost() ? ' Post one when something big happens: a departure, a large gift, a cancelled event.' : '') + '</p>');
  }
  function mount() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('overview'); if (!panel) return false;
    if (document.getElementById('w116news')) return true;
    var strip = document.getElementById('w35stats');
    if (!strip && N.tries < 40) return false;
    var wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.id = 'w116kpi';
    var news = document.createElement('div'); news.id = 'w116news';
    news.innerHTML = '<div class="hd"><h3>What\'s new</h3><span class="sub">looking for major announcements...</span></div>';
    if (strip && strip.parentElement === panel) { panel.insertBefore(wrap, strip); wrap.appendChild(strip); wrap.appendChild(news); }
    else if (strip && strip.parentElement) { strip.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', news); wrap = null; }
    else { panel.insertBefore(wrap, panel.firstChild); wrap.appendChild(news); }
    return true;
  }
  async function newsBoot() {
    N.tries++;
    if (!mount()) { if (N.tries < 40) setTimeout(newsBoot, 300); return; }
    try { await loadNews(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    renderNews();
  }
  async function refreshNews() { try { await loadNews(); renderNews(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  window.w116News = { refresh: refreshNews };

  function postModal() {
    var depts = (typeof DEPT_ORDER !== 'undefined' ? DEPT_ORDER : []).slice();
    openModal('<div class="w116am"><h3>Post an announcement</h3><p class="m-sub">For the whole hub, on the Executive dashboard. Keep it to what everyone should know: a departure, a large gift, a big spend, a cancelled event, a change of plan.</p>' +
      '<label>Kind</label><select id="w116ak">' + Object.keys(KINDS).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '"' + (k === 'general' ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(KINDS[k][0]) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Headline</label><input type="text" id="w116at" placeholder="Rally Paper moves to October 18" />' +
      '<label>Detail (optional)</label><textarea id="w116ab" placeholder="One or two lines of context."></textarea>' +
      '<div class="row"><div><label>Team (optional)</label><select id="w116ad"><option value="">Whole organization</option>' + depts.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '">' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div>' +
      '<div><label>Link (optional)</label><input type="text" id="w116al" placeholder="#team/fundraising or https://..." /></div></div>' +
      '<div class="row"><div><label>Happened on</label><input type="date" id="w116ah" value="' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) + '" /></div>' +
      '<div><label>Comes down on</label><input type="date" id="w116ae" /></div></div>' +
      '<label class="chk" style="margin-top:12px"><input type="checkbox" id="w116ap" /> Pin it to the top</label>' +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116ac">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116as">Post</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116ac').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116as').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var title = document.getElementById('w116at').value.trim(); if (!title) { toast('Give it a headline'); return; }
      var row = { kind: document.getElementById('w116ak').value, title: title, body: document.getElementById('w116ab').value.trim() || null, department: document.getElementById('w116ad').value || null, link: document.getElementById('w116al').value.trim() || null, pinned: document.getElementById('w116ap').checked, happened_at: document.getElementById('w116ah').value || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), expires_at: document.getElementById('w116ae').value ? document.getElementById('w116ae').value + 'T23:59:59' : null, created_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.email) || who0() };
      var r = await db.from('hub_announcements').insert(row);
      if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not post'); return; }
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'announcement', entity_id: row.kind, field: 'posted', old_value: null, new_value: title, changed_by: who0() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      closeModal(); toast('Posted'); refreshNews();
    });
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116gWired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116gWired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
      var box = document.getElementById('w116news'); if (!box || !box.contains(e.target)) return;
      if (e.target.closest('[data-anpost]')) { if (canPost()) postModal(); return; }
      if (e.target.closest('[data-anmore]')) { N.all = !N.all; renderNews(); return; }
      var x = e.target.closest('[data-anx]');
      if (x) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (!confirm('Take this announcement down?')) return;
        var r = await db.from('hub_announcements').update({ expires_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', Number(x.getAttribute('data-anx')));
        if (r && r.error) { toast('Could not take it down'); return; }
        toast('Taken down'); refreshNews(); return;
      }
      var card = e.target.closest('.w116an.go');
      if (card) { var l = card.getAttribute('data-anlink'); if (!l) return; if (/^https?:/i.test(l)) window.open(l, '_blank', 'noopener'); else location.hash = l; }
    });
    var t116g = null;
    try {
      new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(t116g); t116g = setTimeout(function () { decisionsCard(); fixAging(); }, 150); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }

  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa116g = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa116g.apply(this, arguments);
      try { decisionsCard(); fixAging(); setTimeout(newsBoot, 250); } catch (e) { console.warn('wave116g boot', e); }
      return r;
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116h the Hub inbox (24/09/26): hub@welfareavenue.com, filed automatically
   =====================================================================
   Steel: members cc the hub on emails so it can pull updates on
   initiatives and tasks: invoices, Loom meeting summaries, anything
   worth keeping. His call on the flow: fully automatic, with an
   activity trail and an undo.
   - mail-pull v2 (edge function, cron every 30 minutes) reads the
     inbox through domain-wide delegation, classifies each email
     (invoice, meeting summary, update, noise) with the model (keyword
     rules as fallback) and files it: invoices become Finance tasks with
     the attachments stored; meeting summaries go through the transcript
     pipeline; other threads land as an update on the team's page; noise
     is ignored. hub_mail keeps the trail; change_log gets a row; Slack
     gets one line per filing.
   - wave164 (29/09/26): every money email (invoice, bill, receipt,
     payment notice, reimbursement) is Finance's, not Fundraising's: a
     task on the Finance app's board, and #accounting-finance hears
     about each one. Filing to a team offers Finance too.
   - This page (#hubmail, Meetings menu, leaders and admins) shows what
     came in and what happened to it, with Undo, "File to a team" for
     emails the model could not place, "File anyway" for ignored mail,
     attachment links and a Sync now button.
   ===================================================================== */
