  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || (staff() ? 'member' : 'viewer'); } catch (e) { return 'viewer'; } }
  function canAct() { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader'; }
  function label(d) { return (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || (T.labels && T.labels[d]) || d || ''; }
  function color(d) { return (typeof DEPT_COLOR !== 'undefined' && DEPT_COLOR[d]) || '#797773'; }
  function fmt(d) { if (!d) return ''; try { return new Date(d).toLocaleString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit', timeZone: 'America/Toronto' }); } catch (e) { return String(d); } }
  function ago(d) { if (!d) return 'never'; var m = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(d).getTime()) / 60000); if (m < 1) return 'just now'; if (m < 60) return m + ' min ago'; var h = Math.round(m / 60); if (h < 36) return h + ' h ago'; return Math.round(h / 24) + ' days ago'; }
  function money(n) { return '$' + Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('en-CA', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }); }

  async function load() {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('hub_mail').select('id, internal_date, from_email, from_name, subject, title, summary, kind, department, amount, due_date, status, filed, attachments, links, error, reason, undone_at, undone_by, mailbox, ask, assignee, forwarded_by').order('internal_date', { ascending: false }).limit(300);
      H.rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      var s = await db.from('hub_mail_state').select('*').eq('id', 1).maybeSingle();
      H.state = (s && s.data) || null;
      H.loaded = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { /* offline */ }
  }
  function filedText(x) {
    var f = x.filed || {};
    if (x.status === 'undone') return { st: 'undone', cls: 'mut', text: 'Undone ' + (x.undone_by ? 'by ' + E(String(x.undone_by).split('@')[0]) : '') };
    if (x.status === 'failed') return { st: 'failed', cls: 'warn', text: E(x.error || 'Filing failed') };
    if (x.status === 'ignored') return { st: 'ignored', cls: 'mut', text: E(x.reason || 'noise') };
    if (f.type === 'task') return { st: 'Finance task', cls: '', link: '#task/' + f.id, text: 'Task on the Finance board' };
    if (f.type === 'update') return { st: 'Team update', cls: '', link: '#team/' + f.department, text: 'Update on the ' + E(label(f.department)) + ' page' };
    if (f.type === 'transcript') return { st: 'Transcript pipeline', cls: '', link: '#transcripts', text: 'In the Transcript Archive; tasks, updates and decisions follow' };
    if (f.type === 'inbox') return { st: 'needs a team', cls: 'warn', text: 'The model could not tell which team this belongs to' };
    /* wave170: info@ passed on (or filed quietly when someone on the team is already on the email), and emails sent on from an inbox */
    if (f.type === 'info') return f.on_thread ? { st: 'info@, filed quietly', cls: '', link: '#requests/m/' + x.id, text: E(f.on_thread) + ' is already on the email' } : { st: 'info@, passed on', cls: '', link: '#requests/m/' + x.id, text: (x.assignee ? 'To ' + E(x.assignee) : 'To the ' + E(label(f.department || x.department)) + ' inbox') + ', with what to do' };
    if (f.type === 'forward') return { st: 'Sent on from an inbox', cls: '', link: '#requests/m/' + x.id, text: 'By ' + E(f.by || x.forwarded_by || 'a colleague') + ', to the ' + E(label(x.department)) + ' inbox' };
    if (f.type === 'candidate') return { st: 'Applicant', cls: '', link: '#hrcentre/recruitment', text: 'In Recruitment, in the HR centre' };
    return { st: x.status || 'new', cls: 'mut', text: '' };
  }
  function attHtml(x) {
    var atts = Array.isArray(x.attachments) ? x.attachments : [];
    if (!atts.length) return '';
    return atts.map(function (a) { return a.path ? '<a class="att" data-hact="att" data-path="' + E(a.path) + '" title="' + E(a.filename) + '">&#128206; ' + E(String(a.filename).slice(0, 34)) + '</a>' : '<span class="att" title="Still in the hub inbox">&#128206; ' + E(String(a.filename).slice(0, 34)) + '</span>'; }).join('');
  }
  function row(x) {
    var k = KIND[x.kind] || KIND.update; var ft = filedText(x); var f = x.filed || {};
    var dept = x.department || f.department;
    return '<div class="w116mr" data-mid="' + E(x.id) + '"><span class="k" style="background:' + k[1] + '">' + k[2] + '</span>' +
      '<div class="b"><div class="m1"><span class="kd" style="color:' + k[1] + '">' + E(k[0]) + '</span><span>' + E(x.from_name || x.from_email || '') + (x.from_name ? ' &lt;' + E(x.from_email) + '&gt;' : '') + '</span><span>' + E(fmt(x.internal_date)) + '</span>' + (x.amount != null ? '<span><b>' + E(money(x.amount)) + '</b>' + (x.due_date ? ' due ' + E(x.due_date) : '') + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="t">' + E(x.title || x.subject || '(no subject)') + '</div>' + (x.ask ? '<div class="s"><b>To do:</b> ' + E(x.ask) + '</div>' : x.summary ? '<div class="s">' + E(x.summary) + '</div>' : (x.subject && x.title !== x.subject ? '<div class="s">' + E(x.subject) + '</div>' : '')) +   /* wave170: the ask first */
      '<div class="f">' + (dept ? '<span class="d" style="background:' + color(dept) + '"></span><span>' + E(label(dept)) + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="st ' + ft.cls + '">' + E(ft.st) + '</span>' + (ft.link ? '<a data-hact="go" data-hash="' + E(ft.link) + '">' + ft.text + ' &rarr;</a>' : (ft.text ? '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">' + ft.text + '</span>' : '')) + attHtml(x) + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="a">' + (!canAct() ? '' :
        (x.status === 'filed' && f.type !== 'inbox' ? '<a class="warn" data-hact="undo">Undo</a><a data-hact="refile">Move to another team</a>' : '') +
        (x.status === 'filed' && f.type === 'inbox' ? '<a data-hact="refile">File to a team</a>' : '') +
        (x.status === 'ignored' || x.status === 'undone' || x.status === 'failed' ? '<a data-hact="refile">File anyway</a>' : '')) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function render() {
    var host = H.host; if (!host || !document.body.contains(host)) return;
    var rows = H.rows; var st = H.state || {};
    var month = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86400000).toISOString();
    var recent = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.internal_date && x.internal_date > month; });
    var n = function (fn) { return recent.filter(fn).length; };
    var needs = rows.filter(function (x) { return x.status === 'filed' && (x.filed || {}).type === 'inbox'; }).length;
    var shown = rows.filter(function (x) {
      if (H.filter === 'all') return x.status !== 'ignored';
      if (H.filter === 'needs') return x.status === 'filed' && (x.filed || {}).type === 'inbox';
      if (H.filter === 'ignored') return x.status === 'ignored';
      if (H.filter === 'other') return x.status === 'undone' || x.status === 'failed';
      return x.kind === H.filter && x.status !== 'ignored';
    });
    var html = '<div class="w116mb">' +
      '<div class="top"><span>Last check ' + E(ago(st.last_sync_at)) + (st.last_result ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(st.last_result) : '') + (st.last_error ? ' \u00b7 <span style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(st.last_error) + '</span>' : '') + ' \u00b7 the hub checks every 30 minutes</span><span class="sp"></span>' + (canAct() ? '<button data-hact="sync"' + (H.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (H.busy ? 'Checking...' : 'Check the inbox now') + '</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sum">' +
      '<div class="k' + (H.filter === 'all' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="all"><b>' + n(function (x) { return x.status === 'filed'; }) + '</b>filed in 30 days</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (H.filter === 'invoice' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="invoice"><b>' + n(function (x) { return x.kind === 'invoice' && x.status === 'filed'; }) + '</b>money emails to Finance</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (H.filter === 'meeting_summary' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="meeting_summary"><b>' + n(function (x) { return x.kind === 'meeting_summary' && x.status === 'filed'; }) + '</b>meeting summaries</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (H.filter === 'update' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="update"><b>' + n(function (x) { return x.kind === 'update' && x.status === 'filed'; }) + '</b>team updates</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (needs ? ' warn' : '') + (H.filter === 'needs' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="needs"><b>' + needs + '</b>waiting for a team</div>' +
      '<div class="k' + (H.filter === 'ignored' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="ignored"><b>' + n(function (x) { return x.status === 'ignored'; }) + '</b>ignored as noise</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="how"><b>How it works.</b> Cc <code>hub@welfareavenue.com</code> on anything worth keeping. Invoices, receipts, payment notices and reimbursements go to Finance (never to Fundraising): a task on the Finance board with the attachment saved, and a post in #accounting-finance; Loom, Otter and other meeting summaries go through the transcript pipeline (tasks, updates and decisions); anything else lands as an update on the team\'s page. Every filing is logged in Activity and posted to Slack; Undo takes it back.</div>' +
      '<div class="chips">' + [['all', 'Everything filed'], ['invoice', 'Finance'], ['meeting_summary', 'Meeting summaries'], ['update', 'Updates'], ['needs', 'Waiting for a team'], ['other', 'Undone or failed'], ['ignored', 'Ignored']].map(function (c) { return '<span class="chip' + (H.filter === c[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-hact="filter" data-f="' + c[0] + '">' + c[1] + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    html += shown.length ? shown.map(row).join('') : '<p class="empty">' + (rows.length ? 'Nothing here with this filter.' : 'Nothing filed yet. The first email cc\'d to hub@welfareavenue.com shows up here within half an hour.') + '</p>';
    html += '</div>';
    host.innerHTML = html;
  }
  async function show() {
    var sec = panel(); if (!sec) return;
    H.host = document.getElementById('w116mailhost'); if (!H.host) return;
    if (!H.loaded) H.host.innerHTML = '<p class="read">Loading the inbox...</p>';
    await load(); render();
  }
  async function call(body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke('mail-pull', { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
    if (r && r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return (r && r.data) || {};
  }
  function refileModal(x) {
    var depts = (T.inbox && T.inbox.length ? T.inbox : T.order).slice();   /* wave164: Finance too */
    var kinds = [['update', 'Team update'], ['invoice', 'Money email (a Finance task)'], ['meeting_summary', 'Meeting summary (transcript pipeline)']];
    openModal('<div class="w116mm"><h3>File this email</h3><p class="m-sub">' + E(x.title || x.subject || '') + '</p>' +
      '<label>As</label><select id="w116mk">' + kinds.map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k[0] + '"' + ((x.kind === 'noise' ? 'update' : x.kind) === k[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + k[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<label>Team</label><select id="w116md">' + depts.map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '"' + ((x.department || '') === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' +
      '<div class="m-foot"><button class="btn ghost" id="w116mc">Cancel</button><button class="btn" id="w116ms">File it</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116mc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116ms').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var b = document.getElementById('w116ms'); b.disabled = true;
      try { await call({ action: 'refile', mail_id: x.id, kind: document.getElementById('w116mk').value, department: document.getElementById('w116md').value }); closeModal(); toast('Filed'); await load(); render(); }
      catch (e) { b.disabled = false; toast('Could not file it: ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 80)); }
    });
  }
  if (!document.body.dataset.wa116hWired) {
    document.body.dataset.wa116hWired = '1';
    document.body.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
      if (!H.host || !H.host.contains(e.target)) return;
      var a = e.target.closest('[data-hact]'); if (!a) return;
      var act = a.getAttribute('data-hact');
      var rowEl = a.closest('.w116mr'); var x = rowEl ? H.rows.filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === rowEl.getAttribute('data-mid'); })[0] : null;
      if (act === 'filter') { H.filter = a.getAttribute('data-f'); render(); return; }
      if (act === 'go') { location.hash = a.getAttribute('data-hash'); return; }
      if (act === 'att') {
        try { var s = await db.storage.from('hub-mail').createSignedUrl(a.getAttribute('data-path'), 600); if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) window.open(s.data.signedUrl, '_blank', 'noopener'); else toast('Could not open the attachment'); } catch (err) { toast('Could not open the attachment'); }
        return;
      }
      if (!canAct()) return;
      if (act === 'sync') { if (H.busy) return; H.busy = true; render(); try { var r = await call({ action: 'sync', wait: true, max: 40 }); toast((r.fresh || 0) + ' new, ' + (r.filed || 0) + ' filed, ' + (r.ignored || 0) + ' ignored'); } catch (err) { toast('Check failed: ' + String(err.message || err).slice(0, 80)); } H.busy = false; await load(); render(); return; }
      if (!x) return;
      if (act === 'undo') { if (!confirm('Undo this filing? What it created is removed; the email stays here so it can be filed again.')) return; try { var u = await call({ action: 'undo', mail_id: x.id }); toast('Undone: ' + ((u.removed || []).join(', ') || 'nothing to remove')); await load(); render(); } catch (err) { toast('Could not undo: ' + String(err.message || err).slice(0, 80)); } return; }
      if (act === 'refile') { refileModal(x); return; }
    });
  }

  /* ---------- panel, menu, permissions ---------- */
  function panel() {
    var main = document.querySelector('main'); if (!main) return null;
    var sec = document.getElementById('hubmail');
    if (!sec) {
      sec = document.createElement('section'); sec.id = 'hubmail'; sec.className = 'panel';
      sec.innerHTML = '<div class="w116ptop"><div class="crumb">Organization</div><h2>Hub inbox</h2><p>What the team cc\'s to hub@welfareavenue.com, filed automatically: invoices, receipts and reimbursements to Finance, meeting summaries through the transcript pipeline, everything else as team updates. The trail lives here, with Undo.</p></div><div class="card" id="w116mailhost"><p class="read">Loading...</p></div>';
      main.appendChild(sec);
    }
    return sec;
  }
  panel();
  if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.register) {
    WA_NAV.register({ id: 'hubmail', label: 'Hub inbox', group: 'meetings', dot: '#2A8C7A', gate: 'leader', panel: false, onShow: function () { show(); } });
  }
  /* the tab belongs in the Meetings group after every rebuild of the bar */
  function place() {
    try {
      if (window.WA_NAV && WA_NAV.layout) return; /* wave134: the menu layout places Hub inbox */
      var bar = document.getElementById('tabBar'); if (!bar) return;
      var b = bar.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="hubmail"]'); if (!b) return;
      var host = bar.querySelector('.tab-group[data-w42g="meetings"] .tab-group-p'); if (!host) return;
      if (b.parentNode !== host || b !== host.lastElementChild) host.appendChild(b);
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }
  try { var __t116h; new MutationObserver(function () { clearTimeout(__t116h); __t116h = setTimeout(place, 120); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa116h = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa116h.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        panel(); place(); [400, 1200, 3000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(place, ms); });
        /* people with a per-person tab list: leaders and admins get the page too */
        if (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && Array.isArray(WA40_MINE.tabs) && WA40_MINE.tabs.length && canAct() && WA40_MINE.tabs.indexOf('hubmail') < 0) WA40_MINE.tabs.push('hubmail');

      return r;
    };
  }
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116i the classic department page retires (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "We don't need this page anymore for all departments because
   of the reorganising we did... as long as we aren't losing
   functionality." Everything the classic page did now lives on the team
   pages (wave116 Overview: progress this period, high priority, team
   pulse, the team's executive decisions with AI recommendations, add an
   update or a task, all updates, invite a team member, add a project,
   tools; wave116b Tasks: list, board, calendar, workstreams, move a task
   to another team). This segment closes the old doors:
   - #dept/<team> and #<team> land on the team Overview.
   - #task/<id> opens the team's task board with that task's drawer.
   - showPanel(<team>) from older code routes to the team page too.
   - renderSection(<team>) (older write paths: add task, add update,
     transcripts) repaints the Overview when it is the page on screen.
   The classic panels stay in the DOM, hidden, so nothing older breaks.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa116i) return; window.__wa116i = true;
  var T = window.WA_TEAMS; if (!T) return;
  function isDept(id) { return T.order.indexOf(String(id || '')) >= 0; }
  function taskDept(id) { try { var t = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; return t ? t.department : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa116icss';
  css.textContent = T.order.map(function (d) { return '#' + d + '.panel'; }).join(',') + '{display:none !important;}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  if (typeof applyHash === 'function') {
    var __ah116i = applyHash;
    applyHash = function () {
      try {
        var h = decodeURIComponent(location.hash || '').replace(/^#/, '');
        var parts = h.split('/'); var a = parts[0], b = parts[1];
        if (a === 'dept' && isDept(b)) { location.hash = '#team/' + b; return; }
        if (isDept(a) && !b) { location.hash = '#team/' + a; return; }
        if (a === 'task' && b) {
          var d = taskDept(b);
          if (d && isDept(d)) {
            window.__w116openTask = String(b);
            var target = '#team/' + d + '/tasks';
            if (location.hash === target) { try { __ah116i.apply(this, arguments); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } else location.hash = target;
            return;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fall through to the normal router */ }
      return __ah116i.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp116i = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) {
      if (isDept(id)) {
        var target = '#team/' + id;
        if (location.hash !== target) { location.hash = target; }
        else { try { applyHash(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
        return;
      }
      return __sp116i.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }
  /* older write paths repaint the classic panel; mirror that on the Overview when it is on screen */
  if (typeof renderSection === 'function') {
    var __rs116i = renderSection, t116i = null;
    renderSection = function (id) {
      var r = __rs116i.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        var team = document.getElementById('team');
        if (team && team.classList.contains('active') && window.w116Route) {
          var cur = String((location.hash || '')).replace(/^#/, '').split('/');
          if (cur[0] === 'team' && cur[1] === id && (!cur[2] || cur[2] === 'overview')) { clearTimeout(t116i); t116i = setTimeout(function () { try { window.w116Route(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 150); }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      return r;
    };
  }
})();

    '.w116js .pick .list{max-height:220px;overflow:auto;margin:6px 0 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.08);}' +
    '.w116js .pick .list a{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:baseline;padding:7px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.06);cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-text);}' +
    '.w116js .pick .list a:hover{background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);}' +
    '.w116js .pick .list a span{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--wa-muted);}' +
    /* HR centre hours block */
    '#w116jhours{margin:16px 0 0;}' +
    '#w116jhours .g{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(140px,1.6fr) minmax(110px,1.2fr) repeat(4,minmax(70px,.8fr));font-size:13px;}' +
    '#w116jhours .g > span{padding:7px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.06);}' +
    '#w116jhours .g > span.h{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.8px;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wa-muted);padding:6px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.10);}' +
    '#w116jhours .g > span.n{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;}' +
    '@media (max-width:740px){#w116jhours .g{grid-template-columns:minmax(120px,1.6fr) repeat(3,minmax(60px,.8fr));}#w116jhours .g > span.t,#w116jhours .g > span.s5{display:none;}}' +
    '@media (max-width:740px){#w116jck .t{display:none;}#w116jpres .n{display:none;}.wa-toolbar .pill.w115pill #whoName{display:none;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []); }
  function me() {
    var em = myEmail(); var all = members();
    var hit = all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null;
    try { if (!hit && typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.id) hit = all.filter(function (m) { return String(m.id) === String(WA_ME.id); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (!hit && typeof WA_ME !== 'undefined' && WA_ME && WA_ME.name) hit = all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === lower(WA_ME.name); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { if (!hit && typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) hit = all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === lower(USER.name); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return hit;
  }
  function myName() { var m = me(); return (m && m.name) || (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || myEmail().split('@')[0]; }
  function personByEmail(em) { em = lower(em); return members().filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === em; })[0] || null; }
  function firstName(n) { return String(n || '').trim().split(/\s+/)[0] || ''; }
  function av(p, size, cls) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size, cls) : ''; }
  function fmtMins(m) { m = Math.max(0, Math.round(m)); var h = Math.floor(m / 60), r = m % 60; return h ? h + 'h ' + (r < 10 ? '0' : '') + r + 'm' : r + 'm'; }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) throw new Error(String(r.error.message || r.error));
    if (r && r.data && r.data.error && !('ok' in r.data)) throw new Error(String(r.data.error));
    return (r && r.data) || {};
  }

  /* ---------- 1. the identity bubble ---------- */
  function bubble() {
    try {
      var who = document.getElementById('whoName'); if (!who) return;
      var pill = who.closest('.pill'); if (!pill) return;
      pill.classList.add('w115pill');
      Array.prototype.slice.call(pill.childNodes).forEach(function (n) { if (n.nodeType === 3 && /signed in/i.test(n.textContent)) n.textContent = ''; });
      var nm = myName();
      if (who.textContent !== firstName(nm)) who.textContent = firstName(nm);
      if (!pill.querySelector('.w116jcaret')) { var c = document.createElement('span'); c.className = 'w116jcaret'; c.innerHTML = '&#9660;'; pill.appendChild(c); }
      if (!pill.dataset.w116j) {
        pill.dataset.w116j = '1';
        pill.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
          if (e.target.closest('#w115btn') || e.target.closest('#whoName')) return; /* wave115 handles these */
          e.stopPropagation(); var b = document.getElementById('w115btn'); if (b) b.click();
        });
      }
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }

  /* ---------- 2. the menu: relabel and extend wave115's items ---------- */
  function menu() {
    var m = document.getElementById('w115menu'); if (!m || m.dataset.w116j === m.innerHTML.length + '') return;
    var items = m.querySelectorAll('.w115item'); if (!items.length) return;
    var mp = m.querySelector('[data-w115act="mypage"]'); if (mp) { mp.textContent = 'My profile'; mp.setAttribute('data-w116j', 'profile'); }
    var nt = m.querySelector('[data-w115act="notify"]'); if (nt) { nt.textContent = 'Notifications'; nt.setAttribute('data-w116j', 'notifications'); }
    var ed = m.querySelector('[data-w115act="edit"]'); if (ed) ed.textContent = 'Edit profile and photo';
    if (!m.querySelector('[data-w116j="settings"]') && staff()) {
      var after = nt || ed || items[items.length - 1];
      var s = document.createElement('button'); s.className = 'w115item'; s.type = 'button'; s.setAttribute('data-w116j', 'settings'); s.textContent = 'Settings: Slack, Google Calendar, Drive';
      var p = document.createElement('button'); p.className = 'w115item'; p.type = 'button'; p.setAttribute('data-w116j', 'password'); p.textContent = 'Change password';
      after.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', s); s.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', p);
    }
    if (mp && mp.parentNode === m && m.firstElementChild && m.firstElementChild.nextElementSibling !== mp) { m.firstElementChild.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', mp); }
    m.dataset.w116j = m.innerHTML.length + '';
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w115menu [data-w116j]'); if (!b) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    var what = b.getAttribute('data-w116j');
    var menuEl = document.getElementById('w115menu'); if (menuEl) menuEl.style.display = 'none';
    if (what === 'profile') { var m = me(); if (m && m.id) location.hash = '#person/' + m.id; else location.hash = '#mypage'; return; }
    if (what === 'notifications') { openSettings('notifications'); return; }
    if (what === 'settings') { openSettings('connections'); return; }
    if (what === 'password') { openSettings('account'); return; }
  }, true);
  try { new MutationObserver(function () { menu(); }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- 3. who is active ---------- */
  function presenceMount() {
    var st = document.getElementById('cloudStatus'); if (!st) return null;
    var pill = st.closest('.pill'); if (!pill) return null;
    pill.classList.add('w116jpill');
    st.style.display = 'none'; var dot = document.getElementById('cloudDot'); if (dot) dot.style.display = 'none';
    var box = document.getElementById('w116jpres');
    if (!box) { box = document.createElement('span'); box.id = 'w116jpres'; box.title = 'Who is on the hub right now'; pill.appendChild(box); box.addEventListener('click', function () { location.hash = '#mypage'; }); }
    return box;
  }
  function renderPresence() {
    var box = presenceMount(); if (!box) return;
    var online = []; try { online = typeof waOnlineEmails === 'function' ? waOnlineEmails() : []; } catch (e) { online = []; }
    var meEm = myEmail();
    var people = online.map(function (p) { var who = (lower(p.email) === meEm) ? (me() || { name: myName(), email: p.email }) : (personByEmail(p.email) || { name: (typeof waNameForEmail === 'function' ? waNameForEmail(p.email) : p.email), email: p.email }); return { name: who.name, avatar_url: who.avatar_url, email: p.email }; });
    people.sort(function (a, b) { return (lower(a.email) === meEm ? -1 : 0) - (lower(b.email) === meEm ? -1 : 0); });
    var inSet = J.checked || {};
    var shown = people.slice(0, 7);
    box.innerHTML = '<span class="st">' + shown.map(function (p) { var ci = !!inSet[lower(p.email)]; return '<span title="' + E(p.name) + (ci ? ' (checked in)' : '') + '" style="display:inline-flex">' + av(p, 26, ci ? 'in' : '') + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="n"><b>' + people.length + '</b> active' + (people.length > shown.length ? ' (+' + (people.length - shown.length) + ')' : '') + '</span>';
    box.title = people.length ? 'On the hub now: ' + people.map(function (p) { return p.name; }).join(', ') : 'Nobody else is on the hub right now';
  }
  async function refreshPresence() {
    try { if (typeof waLoadPresence === 'function') await waLoadPresence(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try {
      /* wave192: who is in comes from loop_in_now() (emails only, no times); session rows are the person's own and HR's */
      var ems = null;
      try { var q = await db.rpc('loop_in_now'); if (q && !q.error && Array.isArray(q.data)) ems = q.data.map(function (x) { return typeof x === 'string' ? x : (x && (x.email || x.loop_in_now)) || ''; }); } catch (e2) { ems = null; }
      if (!ems) { var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').select('email').is('ended_at', null); ems = ((r && r.data) || []).map(function (x) { return x.email; }); }
      J.checked = {}; ems.forEach(function (em) { if (em) J.checked[lower(em)] = 1; });
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    renderPresence();
  }

  /* ---------- 4. check in / check out ---------- */
  function ckButton() {
    var pill = document.querySelector('.wa-toolbar .pill.w115pill'); if (!pill || !staff()) return null;
    var b = document.getElementById('w116jck');
    if (!b) {
      b = document.createElement('button'); b.id = 'w116jck'; b.type = 'button';
      pill.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', b);
      b.addEventListener('click', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); toggleCheck(); });
    }
    return b;
  }
  function renderCk() {
    var b = ckButton(); if (!b) return;
    if (J.open) {
      var mins = (Date.now() - new Date(J.open.started_at).getTime()) / 60000;
      b.className = 'in'; b.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span>Checked in <span class="t">' + E(fmtMins(mins)) + ' &middot; check out</span>'; b.title = 'Checked in since ' + new Date(J.open.started_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }) + '. Click to check out.';
    } else { b.className = ''; b.innerHTML = '<span class="dot"></span>Check in'; b.title = 'Start counting your hours on the hub'; }
  }
  async function loadOpen() {
    try {
      var em = myEmail(); if (!em) return;
      var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').select('*').eq('email', em).is('ended_at', null).order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(3);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      /* a session left open closes itself at eight hours */
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
        var age = (Date.now() - new Date(rows[i].started_at).getTime()) / 3600000;
        if (age > 14 || i > 0) { var end = new Date(new Date(rows[i].started_at).getTime() + Math.min(age, 8) * 3600000).toISOString(); await db.from('hub_checkins').update({ ended_at: end, minutes: Math.round(Math.min(age, 8) * 60), auto_closed: true }).eq('id', rows[i].id); rows[i] = null; }
      }
      J.open = rows.filter(Boolean)[0] || null;
    } catch (e) { J.open = null; }
    renderCk();
  }
  /* wave116q takes over the button (Slack post, spoken summary) through this handle */
  window.w116jCk = { open: function () { return J.open; }, busy: function () { return J.busy; }, toggle: function () { return toggleCheck(); }, reload: function () { return loadOpen(); }, render: renderCk, presence: function () { return refreshPresence(); }, fmtMins: fmtMins, clear: function () { J.open = null; } };
  async function toggleCheck() {
    if (J.busy) return; J.busy = true;
    try {
      var em = myEmail(); var m = me();
      if (J.open) {
        var mins = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(J.open.started_at).getTime()) / 60000);
        var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').update({ ended_at: new Date().toISOString(), minutes: mins }).eq('id', J.open.id);
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        toastS('Checked out: ' + fmtMins(mins) + ' on the hub'); J.open = null;
      } else {
        var ins = await db.from('hub_checkins').insert({ email: em, name: (m && m.name) || (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || em, department: m ? m.department : null, started_at: new Date().toISOString(), source: 'hub' }).select('*').single();
        if (ins && ins.error) throw ins.error;
        J.open = ins.data; toastS('Checked in. Your hours count from now.');
      }
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'checkin', entity_id: em, field: J.open ? 'in' : 'out', old_value: null, new_value: J.open ? 'checked in' : 'checked out', changed_by: (m && m.name) || em }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save the check-in'); }
    J.busy = false; renderCk(); refreshPresence();
  }

  /* ---------- 5. HR centre: hours checked in on the hub ---------- */
  async function hoursBlock() {
    try {
      var host = document.getElementById('w52hours'); if (!host || document.getElementById('w116jhours')) return;
      var since = new Date(Date.now() - 35 * 86400000).toISOString();
      var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').select('email, name, department, started_at, ended_at, minutes').gte('started_at', since).order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(2000);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      var now = new Date(); var day = now.getDay(); var monday = new Date(now); monday.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); monday.setDate(now.getDate() - ((day + 6) % 7));
      var lastMon = new Date(monday.getTime() - 7 * 86400000);
      var by = {};
      rows.forEach(function (x) {
        var k = lower(x.email); by[k] = by[k] || { name: x.name || k, department: x.department, week: 0, last: 0, total: 0, sessions: 0, open: false };
        var mins = x.minutes != null ? Number(x.minutes) : (x.ended_at ? (new Date(x.ended_at) - new Date(x.started_at)) / 60000 : (Date.now() - new Date(x.started_at).getTime()) / 60000);
        var st = new Date(x.started_at);
        by[k].total += mins; by[k].sessions++;
        if (st >= monday) by[k].week += mins; else if (st >= lastMon) by[k].last += mins;
        if (!x.ended_at) by[k].open = true;
      });
      var list = Object.keys(by).map(function (k) { return by[k]; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.week - a.week || b.total - a.total; });
      var div = document.createElement('div'); div.id = 'w116jhours'; div.className = 'card';
      div.innerHTML = '<h2 style="color:' + GREEN + '">Hours checked in on the hub</h2><p class="read">People check in and out from the button at the top of the hub. Sessions left open close themselves at eight hours. Slack check-ins stay in the table above.</p>' +
        (list.length ? '<div class="g"><span class="h">Person</span><span class="h t">Team</span><span class="h n">This week</span><span class="h n">Last week</span><span class="h n s5">Last 5 weeks</span><span class="h n">Sessions</span>' + list.map(function (p) { return '<span>' + E(p.name) + (p.open ? ' <span style="color:#2A8C7A;font-weight:700;font-size:11px">checked in now</span>' : '') + '</span><span class="t">' + E((typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[p.department]) || p.department || '') + '</span><span class="n">' + E(fmtMins(p.week)) + '</span><span class="n">' + E(fmtMins(p.last)) + '</span><span class="n s5">' + E(fmtMins(p.total)) + '</span><span class="n">' + p.sessions + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--wa-muted)">Nobody has checked in on the hub yet.</p>');
      host.appendChild(div);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp116j = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { var r = __sp116j.apply(this, arguments); if (id === 'hrcentre') { [600, 1500, 3000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(hoursBlock, ms); }); } return r; };
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var tb = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w47tab'); if (tb && tb.getAttribute('data-w47v') === 'hours') { [400, 1200].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(hoursBlock, ms); }); } });

  /* ---------- 6. Settings ---------- */
  var EVENTS = [['request_filed', 'A request is filed for my team'], ['task_reassigned', 'A task is handed to me'], ['suggestion_accepted', 'A meeting suggestion becomes a request for my team'], ['mentioned', 'A meeting mentions me'], ['invited', 'Someone I invited joins the Hub'], ['request_update', 'A request I asked for is done, or someone adds a note to it'], ['comment_mention', 'Someone @mentions me in the Hub (a Slack message with what they wrote)']];
  var PR = { disabled: [], loaded: false };
  async function loadPrefs() { try { var r = await db.from('wa_notify_prefs').select('disabled').eq('email', myEmail()).maybeSingle(); PR.disabled = (r && r.data && Array.isArray(r.data.disabled)) ? r.data.disabled : []; PR.loaded = true; } catch (e) { PR.disabled = []; } }
  async function savePrefs() { try { await db.from('wa_notify_prefs').upsert({ email: myEmail(), disabled: PR.disabled, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }); toastS('Notification settings saved'); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save settings'); } }
  async function loadConn() {
    try { var r = await fn('hub-connect', { action: 'status' }); J.conn = r.connections || {}; }
    catch (e) { J.conn = J.conn || {}; J.connErr = String(e.message || e); }
    window.w116Conn = { calendar: !!(J.conn.google_calendar && J.conn.google_calendar.enabled), drive: !!(J.conn.google_drive && J.conn.google_drive.enabled), slack: !!(J.conn.slack && J.conn.slack.enabled) };
  }
  function sw(on, key) { return '<span class="sw' + (on ? ' on' : '') + '" data-jsw="' + key + '" role="switch" aria-checked="' + (on ? 'true' : 'false') + '"><i></i></span>'; }
  function connRow(icon, color, title, sub, right) { return '<div class="row"><span class="ic" style="background:' + color + '">' + icon + '</span><div><b>' + title + '</b><div class="s' + (sub && sub.cls ? ' ' + sub.cls : '') + '">' + (sub && sub.text != null ? sub.text : sub || '') + '</div></div>' + (right || '') + '</div>'; }
  function connStatus(c, onText) {
    if (!c) return { text: 'Not connected yet.' };
    if (c.enabled === false) return c.last_error ? { text: E(c.last_error), cls: 'err' } : { text: 'Off.' };
    return { text: onText + (c.last_ok_at ? ' Last checked ' + E(new Date(c.last_ok_at).toLocaleString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })) + '.' : ''), cls: 'ok' };
  }
  function settingsHtml() {
    var m = me() || {}; var c = J.conn || {};
    var tabs = '<div class="tabs">' + [['connections', 'Connections'], ['notifications', 'Notifications'], ['account', 'Account']].map(function (t) { return '<a data-jtab="' + t[0] + '" class="' + (J.tab === t[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + t[1] + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    var body = '';
    if (J.tab === 'connections') {
      var slack = c.slack, cal = c.google_calendar, drv = c.google_drive;
      var slackOn = !!(slack && slack.enabled !== false && slack.external_id);
      body += connRow('S', '#4A154B', 'Slack', slackOn ? { text: 'Connected as ' + E(slack.external_name || '') + (slack.meta && slack.meta.handle ? ' (@' + E(slack.meta.handle) + ')' : '') + '. Hub updates, mentions and requests reach your DMs. <span class="lnk" data-jslackpick="1">Not you? Choose another account</span>', cls: 'ok' } : (slack && slack.enabled === false ? { text: 'Off. Hub messages skip your DMs.' } : { text: 'Not connected yet. Switch it on and the hub looks for your account by email and name, or <span class="lnk" data-jslackpick="1">choose your Slack account</span> yourself.' }), sw(slackOn, 'slack'));
      if (J.slackPick) {
        var q = lower(J.slackQ || ''); var list = (J.slackUsers || []).filter(function (u) { return !q || lower(u.name + ' ' + u.handle + ' ' + (u.title || '')).indexOf(q) >= 0; }).slice(0, 40);
        body += '<div class="pick" id="w116jspick"><div class="note" style="margin:0 0 6px">Pick the Slack account that is you. Hub notifications will go to its DMs.</div><input type="text" id="w116jsq" placeholder="Search by name" value="' + E(J.slackQ || '') + '" autocomplete="off" />' +
          (J.slackUsers === null ? '<div class="note">Loading the workspace...</div>' : (list.length ? '<div class="list">' + list.map(function (u) { return '<a data-jslackid="' + E(u.id) + '"><b>' + E(u.name) + '</b><span>@' + E(u.handle) + (u.title ? ' &middot; ' + E(u.title) : '') + '</span></a>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="note">Nobody matches that.</div>')) +
          '<div class="note"><span class="lnk" data-jslackpick="0">Close</span></div></div>';
      }
      body += (window.w138ConnRow ? window.w138ConnRow() : ''); /* wave138: Send @mentions as you */
      body += connRow('C', '#1a73e8', 'Google Calendar', connStatus(cal, 'On. Accept or decline hub meetings from Home; replies land on your calendar' + (cal && cal.external_name ? ' (' + E(cal.external_name) + ')' : '') + '.'), sw(!!(cal && cal.enabled), 'google_calendar'));
      body += connRow('D', '#188038', 'Google Drive', connStatus(drv, 'On. Your recent files show on your profile and here.'), sw(!!(drv && drv.enabled), 'google_drive'));
      body += connRow('@', GOLD, 'Email (hub@welfareavenue.com)', 'Cc the hub on anything worth keeping: invoices go to Finance, Loom or Otter summaries through the transcript pipeline, other threads to your team page. No setup needed.', '<span class="lnk" data-jgo="#hubmail">Hub inbox</span>');
      body += connRow('L', PURPLE, 'Loom, Otter, Plaud', 'Meetings recorded on the Hub\u2019s Loom account (hub@welfareavenue.com) come in by themselves on Monday, Wednesday and Friday mornings. Otter and Plaud reach the hub through the Transcript Archive upload or by cc\'ing hub@ on the summary email.', '<span class="lnk" data-jgo="#transcripts">Transcripts</span>');
      if (drv && drv.enabled) body += '<div class="files" id="w116jfiles"><div class="note">Loading your recent Drive files...</div></div>';
      body += '<div class="note">Google connections use the hub\'s Workspace delegation: the hub acts for you only after you switch a service on here, and only with that service. Switching it off stops it immediately.' + (J.connErr ? ' <span style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(J.connErr) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    } else if (J.tab === 'notifications') {
      body += '<div class="note" style="margin:0 0 6px">Delivered to your Slack DMs' + (c.slack && c.slack.external_id ? ' (' + E(c.slack.external_name || '') + ')' : ' once Slack is connected') + '.</div>';

    } else {
      body += '<div class="acct">' + av(m.name ? m : { name: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || myEmail() }, 52) + '<div><b>' + E(m.name || (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || '') + '</b><div class="s">' + E([m.role, (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[m.department]) || m.department].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div><div class="s">' + E(myEmail()) + '</div></div></div>' +
        '<div class="note"><span class="lnk" data-jact="edit">Edit profile and photo</span> &middot; <span class="lnk" data-jact="profile">Open my profile page</span></div>' +

  }
  async function openSettings(tab) {
    J.tab = tab || 'connections';
    openModal('<div class="w116js"><h3>Settings</h3><p class="m-sub">Loading...</p></div>');

  }
  function paintSettings() {

    box.innerHTML = settingsHtml();
    try { if (window.w138AfterPaint) window.w138AfterPaint(J.tab); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    if (J.tab === 'connections' && J.conn && J.conn.google_drive && J.conn.google_drive.enabled) loadFiles();
  }
  async function loadFiles() {

      var el = document.getElementById('w116jfiles'); if (!el) return;
      var files = r.files || [];

'<div class="note" style="margin:0 0 4px">Your recent Drive files</div>' + files.map(function (f) { return '<a href="' + E(f.webViewLink || '#') + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + (f.iconLink ?     } catch (e) { var el2 = document.getElementById('w116jfiles'); if (el2) el2.innerHTML = '<div class="note" style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(String(e.message || e)) + '</div>'; }
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var box = document.getElementById('modalBox'); if (!box || !box.querySelector('.w116js')) return;
    var t = e.target;
    var tab = t.closest('[data-jtab]') || t.closest('[data-jtab2]');
    if (tab) { J.tab = tab.getAttribute('data-jtab') || tab.getAttribute('data-jtab2'); paintSettings(); return; }
    var go = t.closest('[data-jgo]'); if (go) { closeModal(); location.hash = go.getAttribute('data-jgo'); return; }
    var sp = t.closest('[data-jslackpick]');
    if (sp) {
      J.slackPick = sp.getAttribute('data-jslackpick') === '1';
      if (J.slackPick && J.slackUsers === undefined) { J.slackUsers = null; paintSettings(); try { var su = await fn('hub-connect', { action: 'slack_users' }); J.slackUsers = su.users || []; } catch (err) { J.slackUsers = []; toastS('Could not read the Slack workspace'); } }

    }
    var sid = t.closest('[data-jslackid]');
    if (sid) {
      try { var pr = await fn('hub-connect', { action: 'slack_pick', slack_id: sid.getAttribute('data-jslackid') }); if (pr.ok === true) { toastS('Slack connected as ' + (pr.slack && pr.slack.name ? pr.slack.name : 'you')); J.slackPick = false; } else toastS(String(pr.error || 'Could not connect Slack').slice(0, 160)); }
      catch (err) { toastS('Could not connect Slack'); }
      await loadConn(); paintSettings(); return;
    }
    var s = t.closest('[data-jsw]');
    if (s) {
      var prov = s.getAttribute('data-jsw'); var on = !s.classList.contains('on');
      s.classList.add('busy');
      try {
        var r = await fn('hub-connect', { action: 'toggle', provider: prov, enabled: on });
        if (r.ok === true) toastS(on ? 'Connected' : 'Switched off');
        else if (r.pick) { J.slackPick = true; if (J.slackUsers === undefined) { J.slackUsers = null; try { var su2 = await fn('hub-connect', { action: 'slack_users' }); J.slackUsers = su2.users || []; } catch (err2) { J.slackUsers = []; } } toastS('Pick your Slack account from the list'); }
        else toastS(String(r.error || 'The connection service did not answer').slice(0, 160));
      } catch (err) { toastS('Could not reach the connection service'); }
      await loadConn(); paintSettings();
      try { if (window.w116Refresh && document.getElementById('mypage') && document.getElementById('mypage').classList.contains('active') && typeof renderMyPage === 'function') renderMyPage(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      return;
    }

    var act = t.closest('[data-jact]');
    if (act) {
      var a = act.getAttribute('data-jact');
      if (a === 'signout') { closeModal(); var so = document.getElementById('signOutBtn'); if (so) so.click(); return; }
      if (a === 'profile') { closeModal(); var m = me(); location.hash = m && m.id ? '#person/' + m.id : '#mypage'; return; }
      if (a === 'edit') { closeModal(); var b = document.getElementById('w115btn'); if (b) { b.click(); setTimeout(function () { var ed = document.querySelector('#w115menu [data-w115act="edit"]'); if (ed) ed.click(); }, 60); } return; }
      if (a === 'setpw') {
        var p1 = (document.getElementById('w116jp1') || {}).value || '', p2 = (document.getElementById('w116jp2') || {}).value || '';
        if (p1.length < 8) { toastS('Use at least 8 characters'); return; }
        if (p1 !== p2) { toastS('The two passwords differ'); return; }
        act.disabled = true;
        try { var u = await db.auth.updateUser({ password: p1 }); if (u && u.error) throw u.error; toastS('Password saved'); document.getElementById('w116jp1').value = ''; document.getElementById('w116jp2').value = ''; }
        catch (err) { toastS('Could not save the password: ' + String(err.message || err).slice(0, 80)); }
        act.disabled = false; return;
      }
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    if (!e.target || e.target.id !== 'w116jsq') return;
    J.slackQ = e.target.value; var list = document.querySelector('#w116jspick .list'); var q = lower(J.slackQ || '');

  });

    var k = c.getAttribute('data-jev'); PR.disabled = PR.disabled.filter(function (x) { return x !== k; }); if (!c.checked) PR.disabled.push(k); savePrefs();
  });
  window.w116Settings = openSettings;

  /* ---------- 7. meeting replies from Home ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var a = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.w116rsvpb a[data-r]'); if (!a) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    var wrap = a.closest('[data-w116rsvp]'); var id = wrap && wrap.getAttribute('data-w116rsvp'); var resp = a.getAttribute('data-r');
    if (!id) return;
    if (!(window.w116Conn && window.w116Conn.calendar)) { openSettings('connections'); toastS('Turn on Google Calendar to reply from the hub'); return; }
    a.textContent = '...';
    try {
      var r = await fn('hub-connect', { action: 'rsvp', event_id: id, response: resp });
      if (r.ok === false) throw new Error(r.error || 'failed');
      toastS(resp === 'accepted' ? 'Accepted' : 'Declined');
      try { ((window.W116 && window.W116.events) || []) /* audit 26/09/26: the events cache is W116.events */.forEach(function (ev) { if (String(ev.id) === String(id)) (ev.attendees || []).forEach(function (at) { if (lower(at.email) === myEmail()) at.status = resp; }); }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      try { if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') renderMyPage(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (err) { toastS('Could not send the reply: ' + String(err.message || err).slice(0, 100)); a.textContent = resp === 'accepted' ? 'Accept' : 'Decline'; }
  }, true);

  /* ---------- boot ---------- */
  if (typeof startApp === 'function') {
    var __sa116j = startApp;
    startApp = async function () {
      var r = await __sa116j.apply(this, arguments);
      try {
        bubble(); [500, 1500, 4000].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(bubble, ms); });
        if (staff()) { loadOpen(); loadConn().catch(function () { /* fine */ }); }
        refreshPresence();
        setInterval(refreshPresence, 60000);
        setInterval(function () { if (J.open) renderCk(); }, 60000);
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave116j boot', e); }
      return r;
    };
  }
})();

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function role() { try { return (typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role) || 'member'; } catch (e) { return 'member'; } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function label(d) { return (T && T.label && T.label[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; }
  function shortLabel(d) { return (T && T.short && T.short[d]) || label(d); }
  function color(d) { try { if (T && T.color && T.color[d]) return T.color[d]; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } try { var tab = document.querySelector('.tab[data-tab="' + d + '"] .tabdot'); if (tab) return tab.style.background || GREEN; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return GREEN; }
  function av(p, size, cls) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size, cls) : '<span class="w116av" style="width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px">' + E(String((p && p.name) || '?').slice(0, 1)) + '</span>'; }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []); }
  function firstName(n) { return lower(String(n || '').split(/\s+/)[0]); }
  function fmtMins(m) { m = Math.max(0, Math.round(m)); var h = Math.floor(m / 60), r = m % 60; return h ? h + 'h ' + (r < 10 ? '0' : '') + r + 'm' : r + 'm'; }
  function fyStart() { var now = new Date(); var y = now.getMonth() + 1 >= 10 ? now.getFullYear() : now.getFullYear() - 1; return y + '-10-01'; }
  function fyLabel() { return 'FY' + (Number(fyStart().slice(0, 4)) + 1); }
  function dateShort(s) { try { return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(s || '').slice(0, 10); } }
  function isSelf(m) { return !!(m && m.email && lower(m.email) === myEmail()); }
  function canEdit(m) { var r = role(); return r === 'admin' || r === 'leader' || isSelf(m); }
  /* wave192 (The Loop): hours are the person's own, and HR's: their own page, leaders, admins and the HR profile (the database
     says the same: hub_checkins rows are readable by the person, admins and wa169_can_assign()) */
  function seesHours(m) { if (isSelf(m)) return true; var r = role(); if (r === 'admin' || r === 'leader') return true; try { return !!(typeof WA40_MINE !== 'undefined' && WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.profile === 'hr' && r !== 'viewer' && r !== 'blocked'); } catch (e) { return false; } }

  /* ---------- who owns what ---------- */
  function personOwns(t, m) {
    try {
      var ids = (typeof DB_ASSIGN !== 'undefined' ? DB_ASSIGN : []).filter(function (a) { return Number(a.task_id) === Number(t.id); }).map(function (a) { return Number(a.member_id); });
      if (ids.length) { if (ids.indexOf(Number(m.id)) >= 0) return true; }
      var own = lower(t.owner); if (!own) return false;
      var full = lower(m.name); var fn = firstName(m.name);
      if (own.indexOf(full) >= 0) return true;
      var parts = own.split(/[,&\/+]| and /).map(function (x) { return lower(x); });
      return parts.some(function (p) { return p === fn || p.split(/\s+/)[0] === fn; });
    } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function tasksOf(m) { return (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return personOwns(t, m); }); }
  function leadsOf(p) { var v = p && p.leads; if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.filter(Boolean).map(String); if (typeof v === 'string' && v.trim()) { try { var a = JSON.parse(v); return Array.isArray(a) ? a.filter(Boolean).map(String) : []; } catch (e) { return v.split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean); } } return []; }
  function projectsLed(m) {
    var all = (window.w116Projects ? window.w116Projects() : (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : [])) || [];
    var full = lower(m.name), fn = firstName(m.name);
    return all.filter(function (p) { return leadsOf(p).some(function (l) { var ll = lower(l); return ll === full || ll === fn || ll.split(/\s+/)[0] === fn; }); });
  }
  function pstatus(p) { var s = lower(p.status || 'active'); if (s.indexOf('pause') >= 0 || s.indexOf('hold') >= 0) return 'paused'; if (s.indexOf('done') >= 0 || s.indexOf('complete') >= 0 || s.indexOf('closed') >= 0 || s.indexOf('archiv') >= 0) return 'done'; return 'active'; }
  function pcounts(p) { var ts = (typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' ? DB_TASKS : []).filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id) === Number(p.id); }); var done = ts.filter(function (t) { return t.done; }).length; return { total: ts.length, done: done, open: ts.length - done, pct: ts.length ? Math.round(done / ts.length * 100) : 0 }; }

  /* ---------- pieces ---------- */
  function taskRow(t, done) {
    var over = !done && t.due_date && new Date(t.due_date) < new Date(new Date().toDateString());
    return '<div class="t' + (done ? ' done' : '') + (over ? ' over' : '') + '" data-w116kt="' + E(t.id) + '"><span>' + E(t.title) + '</span><span class="r">' +
      (t.department ? '<span class="pl" style="background:' + color(t.department) + '">' + E(shortLabel(t.department)) + '</span>' : '') +
      (done ? (t.closed_at ? '<span class="when">' + E(dateShort(t.closed_at)) + '</span>' : '') : (t.due_date ? '<span class="due">' + (over ? 'overdue ' : 'due ') + E(dateShort(t.due_date)) + '</span>' : '')) +
      '</span></div>';
  }
  function taskList(list, done, id, limit) {
    if (!list.length) return '<p class="empty">' + (done ? 'Nothing finished yet this fiscal year.' : 'Nothing assigned right now.') + '</p>';
    var shown = list.slice(0, limit);
    return '<div class="w116ktasks" id="' + id + '">' + shown.map(function (t) { return taskRow(t, done); }).join('') + '</div>' +
      (list.length > limit ? '<a class="more" data-w116kmore="' + id + '">Show all ' + list.length + '</a>' : '');
  }
  function projectsHtml(list) {
    if (!list.length) return '<p class="empty">Not leading a project right now. Leads are set on the Projects page.</p>';
    return '<div class="w116kpro">' + list.map(function (p) {
      var c = pcounts(p), st = pstatus(p);
      return '<div class="p ' + st + '"><div class="n" data-w116kp="' + E(p.id) + '"><span>' + E(p.name) + '</span>' + (p.department ? '<span class="pl" style="background:' + color(p.department) + '">' + E(shortLabel(p.department)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="s"><span>' + (st === 'active' ? 'Active' : st === 'paused' ? 'Paused' : 'Done') + '</span><span>' + c.done + ' of ' + c.total + ' tasks done</span>' + (p.due_date || p.target_date ? '<span>Target ' + E(dateShort(p.due_date || p.target_date)) + '</span>' : '') + (leadsOf(p).length > 1 ? '<span>With ' + E(leadsOf(p).join(', ')) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + c.pct + '%"></i></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  function hoursHtml(h, self) {
    if (!h) return '<p class="empty">' + (self ? 'No sessions yet. Press I\u2019m in at the top of the Hub when you start. Your hours are for you and HR, never the post.' : 'No sessions yet.') + '</p>';
    return '<div class="w116khours"><div' + (h.open ? ' class="now"' : '') + '><b>' + E(fmtMins(h.month)) + '</b>this month' + (h.open ? ' (checked in now)' : '') + '</div><div><b>' + E(fmtMins(h.last)) + '</b>last month</div><div><b>' + h.sessions + '</b>sessions in 60 days</div></div>' +
      (h.recent.length ? '<div class="w116ksess">' + h.recent.map(function (s) { return '<span>' + E(dateShort(s.started_at)) + ' &middot; ' + E(fmtMins(s.mins)) + (s.open ? ' (open)' : '') + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
  }
  function teamHtml(m) {
    var mates = members().filter(function (x) { return x.department === m.department && Number(x.id) !== Number(m.id); }).slice(0, 14);
    if (!mates.length) return '<p class="empty">Nobody else on this team yet.</p>';
    return '<div class="w116kteam">' + mates.map(function (x) { return '<a href="#person/' + E(x.id) + '">' + av(x, 24) + E(x.name) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function loadHours(m) {
    try {
      if (!m.email) return null;
      var since = new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 86400000).toISOString();
      var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').select('started_at, ended_at, minutes').eq('email', lower(m.email)).gte('started_at', since).order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(400);
      var rows = (r && r.data) || [];
      if (!rows.length) return null;
      var now = new Date(); var m0 = new Date(now.getTime()); m0.setDate(1); m0.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); var l0 = new Date(m0.getTime()); l0.setMonth(l0.getMonth() - 1);
      var h = { month: 0, last: 0, sessions: rows.length, open: false, recent: [] };
      rows.forEach(function (x) {
        var mins = x.minutes != null ? Number(x.minutes) : (x.ended_at ? (new Date(x.ended_at) - new Date(x.started_at)) / 60000 : (Date.now() - new Date(x.started_at).getTime()) / 60000);
        var st = new Date(x.started_at);
        if (st >= m0) h.month += mins; else if (st >= l0) h.last += mins;
        if (!x.ended_at) h.open = true;
        if (h.recent.length < 6) h.recent.push({ started_at: x.started_at, mins: mins, open: !x.ended_at });
      });
      return h;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  async function loadMeetings(m) {
    try {
      if (!window.w116LoadEvents || !m.email) return [];
      var ev = await window.w116LoadEvents(false) || [];
      var em = lower(m.email); var now = Date.now();
      return ev.filter(function (e) { return Date.parse(e.end_at || e.start_at) >= now && ((e.attendees || []).some(function (a) { return lower(a.email) === em; }) || lower(e.organizer) === em); }).sort(function (a, b) { return Date.parse(a.start_at) - Date.parse(b.start_at); }).slice(0, 5);
    } catch (e) { return []; }
  }
  async function loadMember(mid) {
    var m = members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(mid); })[0] || null;
    try {
      var q = await db.from('team_members').select('*').eq('id', Number(mid)).maybeSingle();
      if (q && q.data) m = Object.assign({}, m || {}, q.data);
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return m;
  }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  var CUR = { id: null, building: null, open: [], done: [] };
  function meaningfulBio(bio, m) {
    bio = String(bio || '').trim();
    if (!bio || /^Writing the job description/i.test(bio) || /^Opening the profile/i.test(bio)) return '';
    var fallback = ((m.role || 'Team member') + ', ' + label(m.department) + '.').trim();
    return bio === fallback ? '' : bio;
  }
  async function build(sec, mid, lifted) {
    var m = await loadMember(mid);
    if (String(CUR.building) !== String(mid)) return;
    if (!m) { sec.innerHTML = '<div class="w116k"><div class="w116card"><p class="empty">Person not found.</p></div></div>'; return; }
    var mine = tasksOf(m);
    var fy = fyStart();
    var today = new Date(new Date().toDateString());
    var open = mine.filter(function (t) { return !t.done; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (b.flag ? 1 : 0) - (a.flag ? 1 : 0) || (b.priority ? 1 : 0) - (a.priority ? 1 : 0) || String(a.due_date || '9').localeCompare(String(b.due_date || '9')); });
    var overdue = open.filter(function (t) { return t.due_date && new Date(t.due_date) < today; }).length;
    var doneAll = mine.filter(function (t) { return t.done; });
    var doneFy = doneAll.filter(function (t) { return (t.closed_at || t.updated_at || '') >= fy; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.closed_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.closed_at || '')); });
    CUR.open = open; CUR.done = doneFy;
    var led = projectsLed(m);
    var seeHours = staff() && seesHours(m);
    var hours = seeHours ? await loadHours(m) : null;
    var meetings = await loadMeetings(m);
    if (String(CUR.building) !== String(mid)) return;
    var self = isSelf(m);
    var bio = meaningfulBio(lifted && lifted.bio, m);
    var checked = !!(hours && hours.open);
    var teamPage = !!(m.department && T && T.order && T.order.indexOf(m.department) >= 0);
    var hasResp = !!(m.jd_url || (Array.isArray(m.responsibilities) && m.responsibilities.length));

    var html = '<div class="w116k">' +
      '<div class="crumbs"><a href="#orgchart">&larr; Org chart</a>' + (teamPage ? '<a href="#team/' + E(m.department) + '">' + E(label(m.department)) + ' team page</a>' : '') + (self ? '<a href="#mypage">Home</a>' : '') + '<span style="flex:1"></span><button class="w43help" data-w43help="personpage" style="float:none">&#63; How this page works</button></div>' +
      '<div class="w116card"><div class="w116khead">' + av(m, 72) +
        '<div><h2>' + E(m.name) + '</h2><div class="role">' + E(m.role || 'Team member') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="meta">' + (m.department ? '<span class="pill" style="color:' + CREAM + ';background:' + color(m.department) + '">' + E(label(m.department)) + '</span>' : '') + (m.email ? '<a href="mailto:' + E(m.email) + '">' + E(m.email) + '</a>' : '') + (checked ? '<span class="pill"><i></i>On the hub now</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
          '<div id="w116konb"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="acts">' + (self ? '<a data-w116ka="edit">Edit profile and photo</a><a data-w116ka="settings">Settings</a>' : '') + (canEdit(m) ? '<a data-w116ka="resp">' + (hasResp ? 'Edit responsibilities' : 'Add responsibilities') + '</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="w116kpis" style="margin-top:16px">' +
        '<div class="w116kpi go" data-w116kgo="open"><b>' + open.length + '</b>open now' + (overdue ? '<small>' + overdue + ' overdue</small>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="w116kpi go" data-w116kgo="done"><b>' + doneFy.length + '</b>finished in ' + E(fyLabel()) + '<small>' + doneAll.length + ' all time</small></div>' +
        (seeHours ? '<div class="w116kpi"><b>' + E(hours ? fmtMins(hours.month).replace(/ 00m$/, '') : '0m') + '</b>on the Hub this month' + (hours ? '<small>' + E(fmtMins(hours.last)) + ' last month</small>' : '<small>no sessions yet</small>') + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div class="w116kpi go" data-w116kgo="projects"><b>' + led.length + '</b>' + (led.length === 1 ? 'project' : 'projects') + ' leading</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="w116grid">' +
        '<div class="w116col">' +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116krespcard"><h3>Main responsibilities' + (m.jd_url ? '<a class="lnk" href="' + E(m.jd_url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open the job description</a>' : '') + '</h3><div id="w33stats"></div><div id="w116krespslot"><p class="empty">Loading...</p></div></div>' +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116kprojects"><h3>Projects they lead<a class="lnk" href="#projects">All projects</a></h3>' + projectsHtml(led) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116kopen"><h3>On their plate (' + open.length + ')' + (teamPage ? '<a class="lnk" href="#team/' + E(m.department) + '/tasks">Team board</a>' : '') + '</h3>' + taskList(open, false, 'w116kopenlist', 10) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116kdone"><h3>Finished this fiscal year (' + doneFy.length + ')</h3>' + taskList(doneFy, true, 'w116kdonelist', 8) + '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="w116col" id="w116kright">' +
          (bio ? aboutCard(m, bio) : '') +
          (seeHours ? '<div class="w116card" id="w116khours"><h3>' + (self ? 'Your hours' : 'Hours on the Hub') + (self ? '<span class="lnk" data-w116ka="ck">' + (checked ? 'Close the loop' : 'I\u2019m in') + '</span>' : '') + '</h3>' + hoursHtml(hours, self) + (self ? '' : '<p class="empty" style="margin-top:8px">You see this as HR, a leader or an admin. Teammates see what moved, never the hours.</p>') + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116kmeet"><h3>Their meetings' + (self ? '<a class="lnk" href="#mypage">Reply from Home</a>' : '') + '</h3>' + (meetings.length && window.w116EventRow ? meetings.map(function (e) { return window.w116EventRow(e, { mine: false }); }).join('') : '<p class="empty">No upcoming meetings on the hub calendar.</p>') + '</div>' +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116ktrain"><h3><span id="w116ktrainh">Training</span></h3><div id="w116ktrainslot"><p class="empty">No training assigned yet.</p></div></div>' +
          '<div class="w116card" id="w116kteam"><h3>' + E(label(m.department) || 'Team') + (teamPage ? '<a class="lnk" href="#team/' + E(m.department) + '">Team page</a>' : '') + '</h3>' + teamHtml(m) + '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
      '</div></div>';
    sec.innerHTML = html;
    place(lifted, m);
    /* audit 26/09/26: the 'Check your profile' step is ticked by wave112 when you open your own page */
  }
  function aboutCard(m, bio) { return '<div class="w116card" id="w116kabout"><h3>About<a class="lnk" data-w33bref="' + E(m.id) + '">Rewrite from the latest work</a></h3><div class="w116kabout" id="w33bio"><p>' + E(bio) + '</p></div></div>'; }
  /* blocks the older chain rendered: lift them into the new layout */
  function place(lifted, m) {
    if (!lifted) return;
    try {
      var slot = document.getElementById('w116krespslot');
      if (slot && lifted.resp) { slot.innerHTML = ''; slot.appendChild(lifted.resp); }
      else if (slot && lifted.final && !slot.querySelector('#w116resp')) slot.innerHTML = '<p class="empty">No responsibilities listed yet.</p>';
      var ts = document.getElementById('w116ktrainslot');
      if (ts && lifted.train) { ts.innerHTML = ''; ts.appendChild(lifted.train); var th = lifted.train.querySelector('h3'); var hh = document.getElementById('w116ktrainh'); if (th && hh) hh.textContent = th.textContent; }
      var ob = document.getElementById('w116konb'); if (ob && lifted.onb) { ob.innerHTML = ''; ob.appendChild(lifted.onb); }
      if (m && lifted.bio && !document.getElementById('w116kabout')) {
        var bio = meaningfulBio(lifted.bio, m); var col = document.getElementById('w116kright');
        if (bio && col) { var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = aboutCard(m, bio); col.insertBefore(box.firstChild, col.firstChild); }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }
  function lift(decoy, final) {
    var out = { resp: null, train: null, onb: null, bio: '', final: !!final };
    try {
      var r = decoy.querySelector('#w116resp'); if (r) { r.parentNode.removeChild(r); out.resp = r; }
      var t = decoy.querySelector('.w36prof'); if (t) { t.parentNode.removeChild(t); out.train = t; }
      var o = decoy.querySelector('.w37profbar'); if (o) { o.parentNode.removeChild(o); out.onb = o; }
      var b = decoy.querySelector('#w33bio'); if (b) out.bio = b.textContent || '';
    } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    return out;
  }
  function waitFor(test, ms) { return new Promise(function (res) { var t0 = Date.now(); (function tick() { var ok = false; try { ok = !!test(); } catch (e) { ok = false; } if (ok || Date.now() - t0 > ms) res(ok); else setTimeout(tick, 80); })(); }); }

  if (typeof wa33Person === 'function') {
    var __pp116k = wa33Person;
    wa33Person = async function (mid) {
      CUR.id = String(mid); CUR.building = String(mid);
      await Promise.resolve(); /* let wave33's showPanel('personpage') act on the real section first */
      var real = document.querySelector('section#personpage:not([data-w116kdecoy])'); if (!real) return __pp116k.apply(this, arguments);
      real.innerHTML = '<div class="w116k"><div class="w116card"><p class="empty">Opening the profile...</p></div></div>';
      /* the older chain renders into a decoy that sits ahead of the real section in the DOM */
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-w116kdecoy]').forEach(function (d) { try { d.parentNode.removeChild(d); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
      var decoy = document.createElement('section'); decoy.id = 'personpage'; decoy.className = 'panel'; decoy.style.display = 'none'; decoy.setAttribute('data-w116kdecoy', '1');
      var main = real.parentNode || document.querySelector('main') || document.body; main.insertBefore(decoy, main.firstChild);
      var lifted = null, done = false;
      var chain = (async function () { try { await __pp116k.apply(null, [mid]); } catch (e) { /* older layout failed: fine */ } lifted = lift(decoy, true); done = true; try { decoy.parentNode.removeChild(decoy); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } })();
      try {
        /* the base wave33 paint is quick; the AI summary and the training block can take longer, so paint once the base is in */
        await waitFor(function () { return done || decoy.querySelector('#w33stats'); }, 4000);
        if (String(CUR.building) !== String(mid)) return;
        if (done) { await build(real, mid, lifted); }
        else {
          await build(real, mid, lift(decoy, false));
          chain.then(function () { try { if (String(CUR.building) === String(mid)) { var m = members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(mid); })[0] || { id: mid, role: '', department: '' }; place(lifted, m); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } });
        }
      } catch (e) { console.warn('wave116k profile', e); try { if (decoy.parentNode) decoy.parentNode.removeChild(decoy); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } }
    };
  }
  window.w116Person = function (mid) { try { wa33Person(mid || CUR.id); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } };
  /* keep the job-description link in the card header in step with the wave116c block after an edit */
  try {
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      var hit = false;
      muts.forEach(function (mu) { Array.prototype.forEach.call(mu.addedNodes || [], function (n) { if (n.nodeType === 1 && (n.id === 'w116resp' || (n.querySelector && n.querySelector('#w116resp')))) hit = true; }); });
      if (!hit) return;
      var card = document.getElementById('w116krespcard'); var blk = document.getElementById('w116resp'); if (!card || !blk) return;
      var slot = document.getElementById('w116krespslot'); if (slot && blk.parentNode !== slot) { slot.innerHTML = ''; slot.appendChild(blk); }
      var jd = blk.querySelector('.jd a'); var h3 = card.querySelector('h3'); if (!h3) return;
      var lnk = h3.querySelector('a.lnk');
      if (jd) { if (!lnk) { lnk = document.createElement('a'); lnk.className = 'lnk'; lnk.target = '_blank'; lnk.rel = 'noopener'; lnk.textContent = 'Open the job description'; h3.appendChild(lnk); } lnk.href = jd.getAttribute('href'); }
      else if (lnk) lnk.remove();
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  /* ---------- clicks ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t.closest) return;
    var sec = t.closest('#personpage'); if (!sec) return;
    var tr = t.closest('[data-w116kt]'); if (tr) { location.hash = '#task/' + tr.getAttribute('data-w116kt'); return; }
    var pr = t.closest('[data-w116kp]'); if (pr) { location.hash = '#project/' + pr.getAttribute('data-w116kp'); return; }
    var more = t.closest('[data-w116kmore]');
    if (more) {
      var id = more.getAttribute('data-w116kmore'); var box = document.getElementById(id); if (!box) return;
      var list = id === 'w116kopenlist' ? CUR.open : CUR.done; var done = id === 'w116kdonelist';
      box.innerHTML = list.map(function (x) { return taskRow(x, done); }).join(''); more.remove(); return;
    }
    var go = t.closest('[data-w116kgo]');
    if (go) { var target = { open: 'w116kopen', done: 'w116kdone', projects: 'w116kprojects' }[go.getAttribute('data-w116kgo')]; var el = target && document.getElementById(target); if (el) { try { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (x) { el.scrollIntoView(); } } return; }
    var a = t.closest('[data-w116ka]');
    if (a) {
      var what = a.getAttribute('data-w116ka');
      if (what === 'edit') { var b = document.getElementById('w115btn'); if (b) { b.click(); setTimeout(function () { var ed = document.querySelector('#w115menu [data-w115act="edit"]'); if (ed) ed.click(); }, 60); } return; }
      if (what === 'settings') { if (window.w116Settings) window.w116Settings('connections'); return; }
      if (what === 'resp') { var link = document.querySelector('#personpage #w116resp [data-w116r="edit"]'); if (link) link.click(); else { try { toast('Responsibilities are edited from the block below'); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } return; }
      if (what === 'ck') { var ck = document.getElementById('w116jck'); if (ck) { ck.click(); setTimeout(function () { window.w116Person(CUR.id); }, 1500); } return; }
    }
  });
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116l the roster follows Google Workspace (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "Go for all of them": new @welfareavenue.com accounts land in a
   "Place on the roster" queue in People and access; an account suspended
   or deleted in Workspace marks the profile inactive the same day, with
   a change_log line; Google profile photos fill in for anyone without a
   hub photo. The edge function directory-sync (daily cron
   wa-directory-sync, 06:20 Montreal) does the reading and the marking;
   this segment is the admin card:
   - "Workspace accounts" sits above People and access (admins): last
     check, what the last pass did, the queue with "Add to the roster"
     (a modal: team, role title from the Google title, hub access, the
     invitation email, or "this is an existing profile without an email"
     which links the account to that profile) and "Not a person" (shared
     mailboxes, test accounts), the accounts marked inactive from
     Workspace this month, and "Check Workspace now".
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa116l) return; window.__wa116l = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', CREAM = '#FFFAF2', GOLD = '#DCA527', FLAG = '#B3402F', TEAL = '#2A8C7A';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var HEAD = "'Barlow Condensed','Arial Narrow','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var FOUNDERS = ['steel.macdonald@welfareavenue.com', 'steelmacdonald@gmail.com', 'suhaib.abdillahi@welfareavenue.com'];
  var L = { rows: [], state: null, loaded: false, loading: null, busy: false, showDismissed: false };

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa116lcss';
  css.textContent = '' +
    '#w116lcard{margin-bottom:16px;font-family:' + BODY + ';}' +

    '#w116lcard .sum span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.06);border-radius:999px;padding:5px 12px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-text);}' +
