    '.w116lb .ck input{width:16px;height:16px;accent-color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '.w116lb .w113acts .w113ok{font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted);align-self:center;}' +
    '@media (max-width:700px){#w116lcard .q .r{grid-template-columns:36px minmax(0,1fr);}#w116lcard .q .r .acts{grid-column:1 / -1;}.w116lb .brow{grid-template-columns:28px minmax(0,1fr);}.w116lb .brow .arrow{display:none;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function isAdmin() { if (FOUNDERS.indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0) return true; try { return !!(WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function label(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.label && WA_TEAMS.label[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function depts() { var o = (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.order) ? WA_TEAMS.order.slice() : ['opsmtl', 'progdev', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr']; if (o.indexOf('board') < 0) o.push('board'); return o; }
  function av(p, size) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size) : ''; }
  function when(s) { if (!s) return 'never'; try { var d = new Date(s); var diff = (Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 60000; if (diff < 2) return 'just now'; if (diff < 60) return Math.round(diff) + ' min ago'; if (diff < 36 * 60) return Math.round(diff / 60) + ' h ago'; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(s).slice(0, 10); } }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []); }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var msg = ''; try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed'); }
    if (!r || !r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error && r.data.ok !== true) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function load(force) {
    if (L.loading) return L.loading;
    if (L.loaded && !force) return Promise.resolve();
    L.loading = (async function () {
      try { var r = await fn('directory-sync', { action: 'status' }); L.rows = r.rows || []; L.state = r.state || null; L.loaded = true; L.err = null; }
      catch (e) { L.err = String(e.message || e); L.loaded = true; }
      L.loading = null;
    })();
    return L.loading;
  }
  function queue() { return L.rows.filter(function (r) { return !r.member_id && !r.dismissed && !r.skipped && !r.suspended && !r.archived && !r.deleted_at; }); }
  function dismissed() { return L.rows.filter(function (r) { return !r.member_id && (r.dismissed || r.skipped) && !r.deleted_at; }); }
  function leftRecently() { var cut = Date.now() - 31 * 86400000; return L.rows.filter(function (r) { return r.deactivated_at && new Date(r.deactivated_at).getTime() > cut; }); }
  function memberName(id) { var m = members().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; return m ? m.name : null; }

  /* ---------- the card ---------- */
  function ensureCard() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('adminpanel'); if (!panel || !isAdmin()) return null;
    var card = document.getElementById('w116lcard'); if (card) return card;
    var people = document.getElementById('w113card');
    card = document.createElement('div'); card.className = 'card'; card.id = 'w116lcard';
    card.innerHTML = '<h2>Workspace accounts</h2><p class="read">The roster follows Google Workspace: new welfareavenue.com accounts wait here to be placed on a team, accounts suspended or deleted in Workspace go inactive on the hub the same day, and Google profile photos fill in for anyone without a hub photo. Checked once a day.</p><div id="w116lbody"><p class="empty">Loading...</p></div>';
    if (people && people.parentNode === panel) panel.insertBefore(card, people); else panel.insertBefore(card, panel.firstChild);
    return card;
  }
  function paint() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w116lbody'); if (!body) return;
    if (!L.loaded) { body.innerHTML = '<p class="empty">Loading...</p>'; return; }
    var st = L.state || {}; var res = st.last_result || null; var q = queue(); var dis = dismissed(); var left = leftRecently();
    var h2 = document.querySelector('#w116lcard h2'); if (h2) { var old = h2.querySelector('.n'); if (old) old.remove(); if (q.length) { var n = document.createElement('span'); n.className = 'n'; n.textContent = q.length + ' to place'; h2.appendChild(n); } }
    var html = '<div class="bar"><span>Last checked: <b>' + E(when(st.last_sync_at)) + '</b></span>' +
      (res ? '<span>' + E(res.users || 0) + ' accounts in Workspace, ' + E(res.matched || 0) + ' on the roster' + (res.photos ? ', ' + res.photos + ' photo' + (res.photos === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' filled in' : '') + (res.skipped ? ', ' + res.skipped + ' shared mailbox' + (res.skipped === 1 ? '' : 'es') + ' skipped' : '') + '</span>' : '') +
      '<span class="sp"></span>' + (matchable(q).length >= 2 ? '<button class="w113btn" data-w116l="bulk">Link ' + matchable(q).length + ' to existing profiles</button>' : '') + '<button class="w113btn ghost" data-w116l="sync"' + (L.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (L.busy ? 'Checking...' : 'Check Workspace now') + '</button></div>';
    if (L.err) html += '<div class="err">Could not reach the roster sync: ' + E(L.err) + '</div>';
    if (st.last_error) html += '<div class="err">The last check failed: ' + E(st.last_error).replace(/(https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)/g, '<a href="$1" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>') + '</div>';
    html += '<h4>Place on the roster (' + q.length + ')</h4>';
    if (!q.length) html += '<p class="empty">' + (st.last_sync_at ? 'Every Workspace account is on the roster.' : 'Nothing yet: the first check has not run.') + '</p>';
    else html += '<div class="q">' + q.map(function (r) {
      return '<div class="r" data-w116lrow="' + E(r.email) + '">' + av({ name: r.full_name || r.email }, 36) + '<div class="who"><b>' + E(r.full_name || r.email) + '</b><span>' + E(r.email) + (r.title ? ' &middot; ' + E(r.title) : '') + (r.account_created_at ? ' &middot; account created ' + E(when(r.account_created_at)) : '') + (r.last_login_at ? ' &middot; last sign-in ' + E(when(r.last_login_at)) : ' &middot; never signed in') + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="acts"><button class="w113btn" data-w116l="place" data-email="' + E(r.email) + '">Add to the roster</button><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w116l="dismiss" data-email="' + E(r.email) + '">Not a person</button></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (left.length) html += '<h4>Marked inactive from Workspace this month</h4><p class="left">' + left.map(function (r) { return E(memberName(r.deactivated_member_id) || r.full_name || r.email) + ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:12px">(' + E(r.deleted_at ? 'deleted' : 'suspended') + ', ' + E(when(r.deactivated_at)) + ')</span>'; }).join(', ') + '.</p>';
    if (dis.length) html += '<p class="empty" style="margin-top:12px"><span class="lnk" data-w116l="toggledis">' + (L.showDismissed ? 'Hide' : 'Show') + ' the ' + dis.length + ' skipped account' + (dis.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' (shared mailboxes, not people)</span></p>' +
      (L.showDismissed ? '<div class="q" style="margin-top:8px">' + dis.map(function (r) { return '<div class="r off">' + av({ name: r.full_name || r.email }, 36) + '<div class="who"><b>' + E(r.full_name || r.email) + '</b><span>' + E(r.email) + (r.dismissed ? ' &middot; marked not a person' : ' &middot; skipped by rule') + '</span></div><div class="acts"><button class="w113btn ghost" data-w116l="undismiss" data-email="' + E(r.email) + '">It is a person</button></div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
    body.innerHTML = html;
  }

  /* ---------- placing ---------- */
  function candidates(r) {
    var fn0 = lower((r.full_name || '').split(/\s+/)[0]); var last = lower((r.full_name || '').split(/\s+/).slice(-1)[0]);
    var pool = members().filter(function (m) { return !m.email && m.active !== false; });
    pool.sort(function (a, b) { return score(b) - score(a) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    function score(m) { var n = lower(m.name); var s = 0; if (fn0 && n.split(/\s+/)[0] === fn0) s += 2; if (last && n.indexOf(last) >= 0 && last !== fn0) s += 1; return s; }
    return pool.map(function (m) { return { id: m.id, name: m.name, dept: m.department, role: m.role, score: score(m) }; });
  }
  function placeModal(r) {
    var cands = candidates(r); var best = cands[0] && cands[0].score > 0 ? cands[0] : null;
    var opts = '<option value="">No, create a new profile</option>' + cands.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + E(c.id) + '"' + (best && c.id === best.id ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c.name) + (c.role ? ' (' + E(c.role) + ')' : '') + (c.dept ? ', ' + E(label(c.dept)) : '') + '</option>'; }).join('');
    openModal('<div class="w113modal w116lm"><h3>Add ' + E(r.full_name || r.email) + ' to the roster</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">' + E(r.email) + (r.title ? ' &middot; Google title: ' + E(r.title) : '') + '</p>' +
      '<label>Is this an existing profile that has no email yet?</label><select id="w116lex">' + opts + '</select>' +
      '<div class="hint">' + (best ? 'Looks like <b>' + E(best.name) + '</b>. Linking keeps their tasks, projects and history and adds the email.' : 'Nobody on the roster without an email looks like this account.') + '</div>' +
      '<div id="w116lnew">' +
      '<div class="w113two"><div><label>Full name</label><input type="text" id="w116ln" value="' + E(r.full_name || '') + '" /></div><div><label>Team</label><select id="w116ld">' + depts().map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + d + '">' + E(label(d)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></div></div>' +
      '<div class="w113two"><div><label>Role title</label><input type="text" id="w116lr" value="' + E(r.title || '') + '" placeholder="e.g. Content developer" /></div><div><label>Hub access</label><select id="w116lh"><option value="member">Member</option><option value="leader">Leader</option><option value="viewer">Viewer (read only)</option><option value="admin">Admin</option></select></div></div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:12px"><input type="checkbox" id="w116lse" checked /> Email them a sign-in invitation now (they can also sign in with Google straight away)</label>' +
      '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn" id="w116lgo">Add to the roster</button><button class="w113btn ghost" id="w116lc">Cancel</button></div><div id="w116lout"></div></div>');
    var ex = document.getElementById('w116lex');
    function syncForm() { var linking = !!ex.value; document.getElementById('w116lnew').style.display = linking ? 'none' : ''; }
    ex.addEventListener('change', syncForm); syncForm();
    document.getElementById('w116lc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116lgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var btn = this; btn.disabled = true; var out = document.getElementById('w116lout');
      var linking = ex.value ? Number(ex.value) : null; var sendEmail = document.getElementById('w116lse').checked;
      var name = linking ? (cands.filter(function (c) { return c.id === linking; })[0] || {}).name : document.getElementById('w116ln').value.trim();
      var dept = linking ? null : document.getElementById('w116ld').value; var role = linking ? null : document.getElementById('w116lr').value.trim(); var hub = linking ? 'member' : document.getElementById('w116lh').value;
      if (!name) { toastS('A name is needed'); btn.disabled = false; return; }
      out.innerHTML = '<div class="res">Working...</div>';
      try {
        if (linking) { await fn('hub-invite', { action: 'set_email', member_id: linking, email: r.email }); }
        var inv = await fn('hub-invite', { action: 'invite', name: name, email: r.email, department: dept || undefined, role_title: role || undefined, hub_role: hub, send_email: sendEmail });
        var pl = await fn('directory-sync', { action: 'place', email: r.email, member_id: linking || inv.member_id || undefined });
        out.innerHTML = '<div class="res"><b style="color:' + GREEN + '">' + (linking ? 'Linked to ' + E(name) : 'Profile created for ' + E(name)) + '</b> with ' + E(inv.hub_role || hub) + ' access. Sign-in email: ' + (inv.email_sent ? 'sent' : 'not sent' + (inv.note && inv.note !== 'not requested' ? ' (' + E(String(inv.note).slice(0, 100)) + ')' : '')) + '. Slack welcome: ' + E(inv.slack || 'skipped') + '. Photo: ' + E(pl.photo || 'none') + '.</div><div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn" id="w116ldone">Done</button></div>';
        document.getElementById('w116ldone').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
        try { if (typeof loadAll === 'function') await loadAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
        await load(true); paint();
        try { if (window.w113Debug && typeof wa40Render === 'function') wa40Render(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      } catch (e) { out.innerHTML = '<div class="res" style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(String(e.message || e)) + '</div>'; btn.disabled = false; }
    });
  }

  function proposal(r) { var c = candidates(r); var best = c[0] && c[0].score > 0 ? c[0] : null; var unique = !!(best && !(c[1] && c[1].score === best.score)); return { r: r, cands: c, best: best, sure: !!(best && best.score >= 2 && unique) }; }
  function matchable(rows) { return rows.map(proposal).filter(function (p) { return p.best; }); }
  function bulkModal() {
    var items = queue().map(proposal);
    var rows = items.map(function (it, i) {
      var opts = '<option value="">Leave in the queue</option>' + it.cands.slice(0, 12).map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + E(c.id) + '"' + (it.best && c.id === it.best.id ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c.name) + (c.role ? ' (' + E(c.role) + ')' : '') + (c.dept ? ', ' + E(label(c.dept)) : '') + '</option>'; }).join('');
      return '<div class="brow' + (it.best ? '' : ' none') + '"><label class="ck"><input type="checkbox" data-bi="' + i + '"' + (it.sure ? ' checked' : '') + (it.best ? '' : ' disabled') + ' /></label><div class="who"><b>' + E(it.r.full_name || it.r.email) + '</b><span>' + E(it.r.email) + '</span></div><div class="arrow">&rarr;</div><select data-bs="' + i + '"' + (it.best ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + opts + '</select>' + (it.best && !it.sure ? '<span class="warn">check</span>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    openModal('<div class="w113modal w116lm w116lb"><h3>Link Workspace accounts to existing profiles</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub">Each account on the left gets the email of the profile on the right, keeping that person\'s tasks, projects and history; the Google photo is copied. Ticked rows are first-name matches with one candidate; rows marked <b>check</b> have a first-name match but another profile could fit too. No invitation emails are sent here.</p>' +
      '<div class="w116lbl">' + (rows || '<p class="empty">Nothing in the queue.</p>') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="w113acts"><button class="w113btn" id="w116lbgo">Link the ticked rows</button><button class="w113btn ghost" id="w116lbc">Cancel</button><span class="w113ok" id="w116lbout"></span></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w116lbc').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('w116lbgo').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var btn = this; btn.disabled = true; var out = document.getElementById('w116lbout');
      var picks = [];
      items.forEach(function (it, i) { var cb = document.querySelector('[data-bi="' + i + '"]'); var sel = document.querySelector('[data-bs="' + i + '"]'); if (cb && cb.checked && sel && sel.value) picks.push({ email: it.r.email, member_id: Number(sel.value), name: (it.cands.filter(function (c) { return c.id === Number(sel.value); })[0] || {}).name }); });
      if (!picks.length) { out.textContent = 'Nothing ticked.'; btn.disabled = false; return; }
      var done = 0, failed = [];
      for (var k = 0; k < picks.length; k++) {
        var pk = picks[k]; out.textContent = 'Linking ' + (k + 1) + ' of ' + picks.length + ': ' + pk.name + '...';
        try { await fn('hub-invite', { action: 'set_email', member_id: pk.member_id, email: pk.email }); await fn('directory-sync', { action: 'place', email: pk.email, member_id: pk.member_id }); done++; }
        catch (err) { failed.push(pk.name + ' (' + String(err.message || err).slice(0, 80) + ')'); }
      }
      out.textContent = done + ' linked' + (failed.length ? '; could not link ' + failed.join(', ') : '') + '.';
      try { if (typeof loadAll === 'function') await loadAll(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      await load(true); paint();
      try { if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') wa40Render(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      btn.textContent = 'Done'; btn.disabled = false; btn.onclick = closeModal;
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w116lcard [data-w116l]'); if (!b) return;
    var what = b.getAttribute('data-w116l'); var email = b.getAttribute('data-email');
    if (what === 'sync') {
      if (L.busy) return; L.busy = true; paint();
      try { var r = await fn('directory-sync', { action: 'sync' }); if (r.ok === false) toastS(String(r.error || 'The check failed').slice(0, 160)); else { var res = r.result || {}; toastS('Checked: ' + (res.users || 0) + ' accounts, ' + (res.queued || 0) + ' to place' + (res.deactivated && res.deactivated.length ? ', ' + res.deactivated.length + ' marked inactive' : '') + (res.photos ? ', ' + res.photos + ' photos' : '')); try { if (typeof loadAll === 'function') await loadAll(); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } } }
      catch (err) { toastS('Could not run the check: ' + String(err.message || err).slice(0, 120)); }
      L.busy = false; await load(true); paint(); return;
    }
    if (what === 'place') { var row = L.rows.filter(function (x) { return x.email === email; })[0]; if (row) placeModal(row); return; }
    if (what === 'bulk') { bulkModal(); return; }
    if (what === 'dismiss' || what === 'undismiss') {
      try { await fn('directory-sync', { action: 'dismiss', email: email, undo: what === 'undismiss' }); toastS(what === 'dismiss' ? 'Skipped: not a person' : 'Back in the queue'); } catch (err) { toastS('Could not save that'); }
      await load(true); paint(); return;
    }
    if (what === 'toggledis') { L.showDismissed = !L.showDismissed; paint(); return; }
  });

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __w40r116l = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      var out = await __w40r116l.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (ensureCard()) { paint(); await load(false); paint(); } } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };

  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false; }); }
  function resolve(name) {
    var n = lower(name).replace(/\s*\(.*$/, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' '); if (!n) return null;
    var all = members();
    var hit = all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name) === n; })[0];
    if (hit) return hit.id;
    var fn = n.split(' ')[0];
    var firsts = all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name).split(' ')[0] === fn; });
    if (firsts.length === 1) return firsts[0].id;
    /* "Steel M" or "S. MacDonald" style: last token match, unique */
    var last = n.split(' ').slice(-1)[0];
    if (last && last.length > 2) { var lasts = all.filter(function (m) { return lower(m.name).split(' ').slice(-1)[0] === last; }); if (lasts.length === 1 && firsts.length === 0) return lasts[0].id; }
    return null;
  }
  function open(id) { if (!id) return false; try { var oc = document.getElementById('ocModal'); if (oc) oc.style.display = 'none'; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } location.hash = '#person/' + id; return true; }

  }, true);

  /* 3. wrap plain-text names where the click has nothing to hold on to */
  function wrapNames(el, names) {
    try {
      if (!el || el.dataset.w116m) return;
      var html = el.innerHTML;
      names.forEach(function (n) { if (!n) return; var re = new RegExp('(^|[\\s,:>])(' + n.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') + ')(?=$|[\\s,.<])'); html = html.replace(re, '$1<span class="w116pname" data-name="' + n.replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '">$2</span>'); });
      el.innerHTML = html; el.dataset.w116m = '1';
    } catch (e) { /* cosmetic */ }
  }
  function decorateHome() {
    var line = document.querySelector('#mypage .mp-online'); if (!line || line.dataset.w116m) return;
    var text = line.textContent || ''; var m = text.match(/right now:\s*(.+)$/); if (!m) return;
    wrapNames(line, m[1].split(',').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).filter(Boolean));
  }
  function decorateExec() {
    /* "decides: <b>Name</b>" on the Executive dashboard, the Decisions waiting folder and the team pages */
    document.querySelectorAll('#overview b, #w111body b, #w116host .w116dec b').forEach(function (bEl) {
      if (bEl.dataset.w116m) return;
      var prev = bEl.previousSibling; var pt = prev && prev.nodeType === 3 ? prev.textContent : '';
      if (!/decides:\s*$/i.test(pt)) return;
      var n = bEl.textContent.trim();
      if (/&|\band\b/i.test(n)) {
        /* "Steel & Suhaib": each name opens its own person */
        bEl.innerHTML = n.split(/\s*(?:&|\band\b)\s*/i).map(function (part, i) { var ok = resolve(part); return (i ? ' &amp; ' : '') + (ok ?       } else if (resolve(n)) { bEl.classList.add('w116pname'); bEl.setAttribute('data-name', n); }
      bEl.dataset.w116m = '1';
    });
  }
  function decorateDrawer() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr'); if (!dr) return;
    var sel = dr.querySelector('select[data-dfield="owner"]'); if (!sel) return;
    var owner = sel.value; var id = (!owner || owner === '__custom') ? null : resolve(owner);

  }

  function when(s) { if (!s) return 'never'; try { var d = new Date(s); var diff = (Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 60000; if (diff < 2) return 'just now'; if (diff < 60) return Math.round(diff) + ' min ago'; if (diff < 36 * 60) return Math.round(diff / 60) + ' h ago'; return Math.round(diff / 1440) + ' d ago'; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function short(id) { var s = String(id || ''); s = s.replace(/^~/, ''); var v = s.split('/')[0], m = s.split('/').slice(1).join('/'); var names = { anthropic: 'Claude', openai: 'OpenAI', moonshotai: 'Moonshot', qwen: 'Qwen', google: 'Google', 'z-ai': 'Z.ai', deepseek: 'DeepSeek', 'x-ai': 'xAI', typesafe: 'Jev', mistralai: 'Mistral' }; m = m.replace(/^claude-/, '').replace(/^gemini-/, 'Gemini ').replace(/^kimi-/, 'Kimi ').replace(/^qwen/, 'Qwen ').replace(/^gpt-/, 'GPT-').replace(/-/g, ' '); if (v === 'typesafe') return 'Jev'; return (names[v] || v) + ' ' + m.replace(/\b\w/g, function (c) { return c.toUpperCase(); }).replace(/Gpt/g, 'GPT'); }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var msg = ''; try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed'); }
    if (!r || !r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error && r.data.ok !== true) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }

  function paint() {

      '<div class="tile"><b>' + calls7 + '</b><span>model calls this week</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile"><b>' + money(cost7) + '</b><span>spent this week</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile"><b>' + money(cost30) + '</b><span>last 30 days</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile"><b>' + (avgMs ? (avgMs >= 1000 ? (avgMs / 1000).toFixed(1) + 's' : avgMs + 'ms') : '-') + '</b><span>typical answer time</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile' + (fails ? ' warn' : '') + '"><b>' + fails + '</b><span>failed calls this week (fallbacks took over)</span></div>' +
      '</div>';
    html += '<div class="bar"><span>Credit and keys live on OpenRouter; the hub keeps one key in the vault.</span><span class="sp"></span><a class="lnk" href="https://openrouter.ai/settings/credits" target="_blank" rel="noopener">OpenRouter credit</a><button class="btn" data-w116n="reload">Refresh</button><button class="btn primary" data-w116n="new">Add a job</button></div>';
    html += '<div class="rt">' + sortedRoutes().map(function (r) {
      var u7 = usage(r.task, 7);
      var chips = (r.chain || []).map(function (m, i) { var jv = /^~?typesafe/.test(m); return '<span class="chip' + (jv ? ' jv' : (i === 0 ? ' p' : '')) + '" title="' + E(m) + '">' + E(short(m)) + '</span>'; }).join('');
      if (r.mode === 'jev' && (r.chain || []).length > 1) chips = '<span class="chip j" title="Jev reads each request and picks the cheapest model in this list that will do it well">Jev picks</span>' + chips;
      var use = u7 ? '<b>' + Number(u7.calls) + '</b> call' + (Number(u7.calls) === 1 ? '' : 's') + ', ' + money(u7.cost) + (u7.last_model ? '<br>last: ' + E(short(u7.last_model)) + ' ' + when(u7.last_at) : '') : 'no calls this week';
      var t = N.tests[r.task];
      var tres = t ? '<div class="tres' + (t.ok ? '' : ' bad') + '">' + E(t.text) + '</div>' : '';
      return '<div class="r" data-task="' + E(r.task) + '"><div class="job"><b>' + E(r.label || r.task) + '</b><span>' + (r.notes ? E(r.notes) + ' ' : '') + '<code>' + E(r.task) + '</code></span></div>' +
        '<div class="chips">' + chips + '</div><div class="use">' + use + '</div>' +
        '<div class="acts"><button class="btn" data-w116n="test" data-task="' + E(r.task) + '"' + (t && t.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (t && t.busy ? 'Testing...' : 'Test') + '</button><button class="btn" data-w116n="edit" data-task="' + E(r.task) + '">Edit</button></div>' + tres + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    html += '<h4><span class="lnk" data-w116n="recent">' + (N.showRecent ? 'Hide the last calls' : 'Show the last ' + N.recent.length + ' calls') + '</span></h4>';
    if (N.showRecent) {
      html += '<table class="rc"><thead><tr><th>When</th><th>Job</th><th>Model</th><th>Result</th><th>Time</th><th>Cost</th></tr></thead><tbody>' + N.recent.map(function (c) {
        var res = c.ok ? (c.via === 'jev' && /jev/.test(String(c.requested_model || '')) ? 'Jev ' + E((c.error || '').replace(/^picked /, 'picked ')) : 'ok' + (c.fallback_used ? ' (fallback)' : '')) : E(String(c.error || 'failed').slice(0, 90));
        return '<tr><td>' + E(when(c.created_at)) + '</td><td>' + E(c.task) + (c.caller ? '<br><span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">' + E(c.caller) + '</span>' : '') + '</td><td title="' + E(c.model || c.requested_model) + '">' + E(short(c.model || c.requested_model)) + '</td><td class="' + (c.ok ? '' : 'bad') + '">' + res + '</td><td>' + (c.ms ? (c.ms >= 1000 ? (c.ms / 1000).toFixed(1) + 's' : c.ms + 'ms') : '') + '</td><td>' + (c.cost != null ? (Number(c.cost) < 0.001 ? '<$0.001' : '$' + Number(c.cost).toFixed(3)) : '') + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
    }
    body.innerHTML = html;
  }

  /* ---------- test ---------- */
  async function test(task) {
    N.tests[task] = { busy: true, ok: true, text: '' }; paint();
    try {
      var r = await fn('llm', { action: 'test', task: task });
      if (r.ok) N.tests[task] = { ok: true, text: (task === 'decide' ? 'Jev answered' : 'Answered by ' + short(r.model)) + ' in ' + (r.ms >= 1000 ? (r.ms / 1000).toFixed(1) + 's' : r.ms + 'ms') + (r.cost != null ? ' for ' + (Number(r.cost) < 0.001 ? 'under a tenth of a cent' : '$' + Number(r.cost).toFixed(3)) : '') + '.' };
      else N.tests[task] = { ok: false, text: 'No answer: ' + (r.error || 'unknown') };
    } catch (e) { N.tests[task] = { ok: false, text: 'Test failed: ' + (e.message || e) }; }
    paint();
  }

  /* ---------- edit ---------- */
  var M = { task: null, chain: [], mode: 'chain' };
  async function ensureModels() {
    if (N.models) return N.models;
    try { var r = await fn('llm', { action: 'models' }); N.models = r.models || []; } catch (e) { N.models = []; }
    return N.models;
  }
  function priceOf(id) { var m = (N.models || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (!m || m.in_per_m == null) return ''; return '$' + m.in_per_m + ' in / $' + m.out_per_m + ' out per M'; }
  function paintChain() {
    var box = document.getElementById('w116nchain'); if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = M.chain.map(function (m, i) {
      return '<div class="c"><span class="n">' + (i + 1) + '</span><div class="m"><b>' + E(short(m)) + '</b><span>' + E(m) + (priceOf(m) ? ' &middot; ' + E(priceOf(m)) : '') + '</span></div><div class="b">' +
        (i > 0 ? '<button class="btn" data-w116nc="up" data-i="' + i + '">&#8593;</button>' : '') + (i < M.chain.length - 1 ? '<button class="btn" data-w116nc="down" data-i="' + i + '">&#8595;</button>' : '') + '<button class="btn" data-w116nc="rm" data-i="' + i + '">Remove</button></div></div>';
    }).join('') || '<div class="hint">No models yet: pick one below.</div>';
    var modes = document.getElementById('w116nmodes'); if (modes) modes.style.display = M.chain.length > 1 ? '' : 'none';
  }
  async function editModal(task) {
    var r = task ? N.routes.filter(function (x) { return x.task === task; })[0] : null;
    M.task = r ? r.task : null; M.chain = r ? (r.chain || []).slice() : ['anthropic/claude-haiku-4.5', 'openai/gpt-5-mini']; M.mode = r ? (r.mode || 'chain') : 'chain';
    openModal('<div class="w113modal w116nm"><h3>' + (r ? 'Models for ' + E(r.label || r.task) : 'A new AI job') + '</h3>' +
      (r ? '<p class="m-sub"><code>' + E(r.task) + '</code></p>' : '<label>Job key</label><input type="text" id="w116nkey" placeholder="lower case, e.g. grant_writer"><div class="hint">The key a function sends as its task. Jobs the hub does not know use the <code>default</code> list.</div>') +
      '<label>Name</label><input type="text" id="w116nlabel" value="' + E(r ? r.label : '') + '">' +
      '<label>Models, in order</label><div class="hint">The first one answers; the rest take over if it fails. With "Jev picks", Jev reads each request and chooses the cheapest one in the list that will do it well.</div>' +
      '<div class="chain" id="w116nchain"></div>' +
      '<div class="pick"><select id="w116nadd"><option value="">Loading the model list...</option></select><button class="btn" id="w116naddb">Add</button></div>' +
      '<div id="w116nmodes"><label>Who picks</label><div class="modes"><label><input type="radio" name="w116nmode" value="jev"' + (M.mode === 'jev' ? ' checked' : '') + '> Jev picks per request</label><label><input type="radio" name="w116nmode" value="chain"' + (M.mode !== 'jev' ? ' checked' : '') + '> Always the first one</label></div></div>' +
      '<label>Notes</label><input type="text" id="w116nnotes" value="' + E(r ? (r.notes || '') : '') + '" placeholder="Why these models">' +
      '<div class="w113acts"><span class="w113ok" id="w116nsaved"></span><span style="flex:1"></span>' + (r && r.task !== 'default' && r.task !== 'decide' ? '<button class="btn" id="w116ndel">Remove this job</button>' : '') + '<button class="btn" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button><button class="btn primary" id="w116nsave">Save</button></div></div>');
    paintChain();
    var models = await ensureModels();
    var sel = document.getElementById('w116nadd'); if (!sel) return;
    var groups = {};
    models.forEach(function (m) { var v = String(m.id).split('/')[0]; (groups[v] = groups[v] || []).push(m); });
    var order = ['anthropic', 'openai', 'moonshotai', 'qwen', 'google', 'z-ai', 'deepseek', 'x-ai', 'mistralai', 'meta-llama'];
    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Pick a model to add</option>' + order.filter(function (v) { return groups[v]; }).map(function (v) {
      return '<optgroup label="' + E(short(v + '/x').replace(/ X$/, '')) + '">' + groups[v].map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.id) + '">' + E(m.name || m.id) + (m.in_per_m != null ? ' ($' + m.in_per_m + ' / $' + m.out_per_m + ')' : '') + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</optgroup>';
    }).join('') + '<optgroup label="Jev"><option value="~typesafe/jev-latest">Jev (decisions only)</option></optgroup>';
    paintChain();
  }
  async function save() {
    var task = M.task || lower((document.getElementById('w116nkey') || {}).value || '').replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, '_');
    var label = (document.getElementById('w116nlabel') || {}).value || '';
    var notes = (document.getElementById('w116nnotes') || {}).value || '';
    var modeEl = document.querySelector('input[name="w116nmode"]:checked'); var mode = modeEl ? modeEl.value : 'chain';
    var ok = document.getElementById('w116nsaved');
    if (!task || task.length < 2) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Give the job a key.'; return; }
    if (!M.chain.length) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Add at least one model.'; return; }
    if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Saving...';
    try {
      await fn('llm', { action: 'set_route', task: task, chain: M.chain, label: label, notes: notes, mode: mode });
      toastS('Saved: ' + (label || task)); closeModal(); await load(true); paint();
    } catch (e) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Could not save: ' + (e.message || e); }
  }
  async function remove() {
    if (!M.task) return;
    if (!confirm('Remove the ' + M.task + ' job? Functions that send it will use the default list.')) return;
    try { await fn('llm', { action: 'delete_route', task: M.task }); toastS('Removed'); closeModal(); await load(true); paint(); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not remove: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('[data-w116n]');
    if (b) {
      var a = b.getAttribute('data-w116n');
      if (a === 'reload') { load(true).then(paint); return; }
      if (a === 'recent') { N.showRecent = !N.showRecent; paint(); return; }
      if (a === 'test') { test(b.getAttribute('data-task')); return; }
      if (a === 'edit') { editModal(b.getAttribute('data-task')); return; }
      if (a === 'new') { editModal(null); return; }
    }
    var c = t.closest('[data-w116nc]');
    if (c) {
      var i = Number(c.getAttribute('data-i')), act = c.getAttribute('data-w116nc');
      if (act === 'rm') M.chain.splice(i, 1);
      if (act === 'up' && i > 0) { var x = M.chain[i]; M.chain[i] = M.chain[i - 1]; M.chain[i - 1] = x; }
      if (act === 'down' && i < M.chain.length - 1) { var y = M.chain[i]; M.chain[i] = M.chain[i + 1]; M.chain[i + 1] = y; }
      paintChain(); return;
    }
    if (t.id === 'w116naddb') { var sel = document.getElementById('w116nadd'); var v = sel ? sel.value : ''; if (v && M.chain.indexOf(v) < 0) { M.chain.push(v); paintChain(); } if (sel) sel.value = ''; return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116nsave') { save(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116ndel') { remove(); return; }
  });

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __w40r116n = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () {
      var out = await __w40r116n.apply(this, arguments);
      try { if (ensureCard()) { paint(); await load(false); paint(); } } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ }
      return out;
    };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp116n = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { var out = __sp116n.apply(this, arguments); try { if (id === 'adminpanel' && isAdmin()) setTimeout(function () { if (ensureCard()) { paint(); load(true).then(paint); } }, 900); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  window.w116nDebug = function () { return { routes: N.routes.length, usage7: N.usage7.length, recent: N.recent.length, err: N.err }; };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116o Ask the Hub: proposals you can edit, with suggestions (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "Any requests that I file through the Ask the Hub should allow
   me to make some edits and also give me suggestions."
   The confirmation card (wave38) becomes a small form: for a task the
   title, team, owner, due date, project, priority and details; for a
   request the title, team, type, who it is for and the details; a note
   for closing a task; the status for a request. Under the fields, chips
   the hub works out on the spot (people on that team whose role or
   responsibilities match the words, projects that match, quick dates)
   and, a moment later, the model's suggestions (router task
   brain_suggest, the cheap tier). A chip fills the field; Confirm sends
   what is on the form, not what the Brain first drafted.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa116o) return; window.__wa116o = true;
  var GREEN = '#103F39', GOLD = '#DCA527';
  var BODY = "'Space Grotesk','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
  var FOUNDERS = ['Steel MacDonald', 'Suhaib Abdillahi'];
  var STOP = ['the', 'and', 'for', 'with', 'from', 'that', 'this', 'about', 'into', 'over', 'under', 'task', 'request', 'please', 'need', 'needs', 'make', 'have', 'will', 'team', 'new', 'add', 'get', 'set', 'our', 'their', 'them', 'they', 'week', 'next', 'some', 'more'];
  var O = { forms: {}, models: {} };

  var css = document.createElement('style');
  css.id = 'wa116ocss';
  css.textContent = '' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px 8px;margin-top:8px;font-family:' + BODY + ';}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed label{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.6px;text-transform:uppercase;color:#797773;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed label.wide{grid-column:1 / -1;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed label.chk{flex-direction:row;align-items:center;gap:6px;text-transform:none;font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:0;color:#2b3b37;padding-top:14px;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed input[type=text],.wac-card .w116oed input[type=date],.wac-card .w116oed select,.wac-card .w116oed textarea{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:8px;font-size:12.5px;font-family:' + BODY + ';background:#FFFAF2;color:#1f2d29;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed textarea{min-height:52px;resize:vertical;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed input:focus,.wac-card .w116oed select:focus,.wac-card .w116oed textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:' + GREEN + ';box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(16,63,57,.12);}' +
    '.wac-card .w116oed input[type=checkbox]{width:15px;height:15px;accent-color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug{margin-top:8px;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .h{font-size:10.5px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.6px;text-transform:uppercase;color:#797773;margin:0 0 4px;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .chips{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;font-size:11.5px;border-radius:999px;padding:4px 10px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.07);color:#1f2d29;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid transparent;font-family:' + BODY + ';font-weight:600;max-width:100%;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .chip:hover{border-color:' + GREEN + ';}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .chip.ai{background:rgba(220,165,39,.18);}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .chip.on{background:' + GREEN + ';color:#FFFAF2;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .chip span{opacity:.75;font-weight:500;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .tip{font-size:11.5px;color:#797773;margin:4px 0 0;line-height:1.4;}' +
    '.wac-card .w116osug .wait{font-size:11px;color:#797773;font-style:italic;}' +
    '@media (max-width:420px){.wac-card .w116oed{grid-template-columns:1fr;}.wac-card .w116oed label.chk{padding-top:0;}}';
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function label(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.label && WA_TEAMS.label[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function depts() { var o = (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.order) ? WA_TEAMS.order.slice() : ['opsmtl', 'progdev', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr']; if (o.indexOf('board') < 0) o.push('board'); return o; }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && m.name; }); }
  function people(dept) { var all = members(); var inTeam = all.filter(function (m) { return m.department === dept; }); var founders = all.filter(function (m) { return FOUNDERS.indexOf(m.name) >= 0 && inTeam.indexOf(m) < 0; }); return inTeam.concat(founders); }
  function projects(dept) { var rows = (typeof WA27_PROJECTS !== 'undefined' ? WA27_PROJECTS : []) || []; return rows.filter(function (p) { return p && (!dept || p.department === dept) && p.status !== 'done' && p.status !== 'archived'; }); }
  function words(s) { return lower(s).replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(function (w) { return w.length > 3 && STOP.indexOf(w) < 0; }); }
  function iso(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + ('0' + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' + d.getDate()).slice(-2); }
  function quickDates() {
    var now = new Date(); now.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
    var fri = new Date(now.getTime()); var dow = fri.getDay(); var add = (5 - dow + 7) % 7 || 7; fri.setDate(fri.getDate() + add);
    var week = new Date(now.getTime()); week.setDate(week.getDate() + 7);
    var eom = new Date(now.getTime()); eom.setMonth(eom.getMonth() + 1); eom.setDate(0);
    var two = new Date(now.getTime()); two.setDate(two.getDate() + 14);
    return [{ t: 'This Friday', v: iso(fri) }, { t: 'In a week', v: iso(week) }, { t: 'Two weeks', v: iso(two) }, { t: 'End of month', v: iso(eom) }];
  }
  /* wave132: when a banked task comes back */
  function bankDates() {
    var now = new Date(); now.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
    var m3 = new Date(now.getTime()); m3.setMonth(m3.getMonth() + 3);
    var m6 = new Date(now.getTime()); m6.setMonth(m6.getMonth() + 6);
    return [{ t: 'In 3 months', v: iso(m3) }, { t: 'In 6 months', v: iso(m6) }];
  }
  function score(text, keys) { var t = lower(text); return keys.reduce(function (a, k) { return a + (t.indexOf(k) >= 0 ? 1 : 0); }, 0); }
  function peopleFor(title, dept) {
    var keys = words(title); if (!keys.length) return [];
    return people(dept).map(function (m) {
      var resp = Array.isArray(m.responsibilities) ? m.responsibilities.join(' ') : String(m.responsibilities || '');
      var s = score(resp, keys) * 2 + score(m.role || '', keys);
      var why = keys.filter(function (k) { return lower(resp + ' ' + (m.role || '')).indexOf(k) >= 0; }).slice(0, 2).join(', ');
      return { m: m, s: s, why: why };
    }).filter(function (x) { return x.s > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.s - a.s; }).slice(0, 3);
  }
  function projectsFor(title, dept) {
    var keys = words(title); if (!keys.length) return [];
    return projects(dept).map(function (p) { return { p: p, s: score((p.name || '') + ' ' + (p.description || ''), keys) }; }).filter(function (x) { return x.s > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.s - a.s; }).slice(0, 2);
  }

  /* ---------- the form ---------- */
  function opt(v, t, sel) { return '<option value="' + E(v) + '"' + (sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(t) + '</option>'; }
  function ownerOptions(dept, cur) {
    var ps = people(dept); var names = ps.map(function (m) { return m.name; });
    var html = opt('', 'Nobody yet', !cur);
    ps.forEach(function (m) { html += opt(m.name, m.name + (m.role ? ' (' + m.role + ')' : ''), cur === m.name); });
    if (cur && names.indexOf(cur) < 0) html += opt(cur, cur + ' (as written)', true);
    return html;
  }
  function projectOptions(dept, cur) { var html = opt('', 'General list', !cur); projects(dept).forEach(function (p) { html += opt(p.id, p.name, String(cur) === String(p.id)); }); return html; }
  function typeOptions(cur) { var types = (window.WA46 && WA46.types) || {}; var keys = Object.keys(types); var html = ''; keys.forEach(function (k) { html += opt(k, types[k].label || k, cur === k); }); html += opt('other', 'Other', cur === 'other' || (cur && keys.indexOf(cur) < 0)); return html; }
  function formHtml(p) {
    var i = p.input || {}; var d = i.department || depts()[0];
    var dsel = depts().map(function (k) { return opt(k, label(k), d === k); }).join('');
    if (p.name === 'create_task') {
      return '<div class="w116oed">' +
        '<label class="wide">Task<input type="text" data-f="title" value="' + E(i.title || '') + '"></label>' +
        '<label>Team<select data-f="department">' + dsel + '</select></label>' +
        '<label>Owner<select data-f="owner">' + ownerOptions(d, i.owner || '') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label>Due<input type="date" data-f="due_date" value="' + E(i.due_date || '') + '"></label>' +
        '<label>Project<select data-f="project_id">' + projectOptions(d, i.project_id || '') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label class="wide">Details<textarea data-f="detail">' + E(i.detail || '') + '</textarea></label>' +
        '<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" data-f="priority"' + (i.priority ? ' checked' : '') + '> Priority</label>' +
        '</div>';
    }
    if (p.name === 'create_request') {
      return '<div class="w116oed">' +
        '<label class="wide">Request<input type="text" data-f="title" value="' + E(i.title || '') + '"></label>' +
        '<label>To<select data-f="department">' + dsel + '</select></label>' +
        '<label>Type<select data-f="type_key">' + typeOptions(i.type_key || 'other') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label class="wide">For<select data-f="assignee">' + ownerOptions(d, i.assignee || '') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label class="wide">Details<textarea data-f="details">' + E(i.details || '') + '</textarea></label>' +
        '</div>';
    }
    if (p.name === 'bank_task') return '<div class="w116oed"><label>Comes back<input type="date" data-f="bank_until" value="' + E(i.bank_until || '') + '"></label><label class="wide">Why it waits<input type="text" data-f="reason" value="' + E(i.reason || '') + '" placeholder="One line, for when it comes back"></label></div>';
    if (p.name === 'close_task') return '<div class="w116oed"><label class="wide">Closing note<input type="text" data-f="note" value="' + E(i.note || '') + '" placeholder="Optional: what settled it"></label></div>';
    if (p.name === 'set_request_status') return '<div class="w116oed"><label class="wide">New status<input type="text" data-f="status" value="' + E(i.status || '') + '" list="w116ost"></label><datalist id="w116ost"><option value="new"><option value="in progress"><option value="waiting"><option value="interviewing"><option value="offer accepted"><option value="done"></datalist></div>';
    return '';
  }
  function readForm(card, p) {
    var out = {}; var i = p.input || {};
    Object.keys(i).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = i[k]; });
    card.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (el) {
      var k = el.getAttribute('data-f');
      if (el.type === 'checkbox') out[k] = !!el.checked;
      else if (k === 'project_id') out[k] = el.value ? Number(el.value) : null;
      else out[k] = el.value.trim();
    });
    if (out.due_date === '') out.due_date = null;
    if (out.owner === '') out.owner = null;
    if (out.assignee === '') out.assignee = null;
    return out;
  }
  function localChips(p, card) {
    var i = readForm(card, p); var out = [];
    var title = i.title || '';
    if (p.name === 'bank_task') bankDates().forEach(function (q) { out.push({ f: 'bank_until', v: q.v, t: q.t, why: q.v }); });
    if (p.name === 'create_task' || p.name === 'create_request') {
      var fld = p.name === 'create_task' ? 'owner' : 'assignee';
      peopleFor(title, i.department).forEach(function (x) { out.push({ f: fld, v: x.m.name, t: x.m.name.split(' ')[0], why: x.why || x.m.role || '' }); });
      if (p.name === 'create_task') {
        projectsFor(title, i.department).forEach(function (x) { out.push({ f: 'project_id', v: String(x.p.id), t: x.p.name, why: 'project' }); });
        if (!i.due_date) quickDates().forEach(function (q) { out.push({ f: 'due_date', v: q.v, t: q.t, why: q.v.slice(5) }); });
      }
    }
    return out;
  }
  function chipHtml(c, ai) { return '<span class="chip' + (ai ? ' ai' : '') + '" data-w116oc="' + E(c.f) + '" data-v="' + E(c.v) + '" title="' + E(c.f === 'title' ? c.v : '') + '">' + E(c.t) + (c.why ? ' <span>' + E(c.why) + '</span>' : '') + '</span>'; }
  function paintSug(card, key) {
    var box = card.querySelector('.w116osug'); if (!box) return;
    var p = WA38_PENDING[key]; if (!p) return;
    var loc = localChips(p, card); var ai = O.models[key];
    var html = '';
    var chips = loc.map(function (c) { return chipHtml(c, false); });
    if (ai && ai.chips) ai.chips.forEach(function (c) { chips.push(chipHtml(c, true)); });
    if (chips.length) html += '<div class="h">Suggestions</div><div class="chips">' + chips.join('') + '</div>';
    if (ai && ai.tips && ai.tips.length) html += ai.tips.map(function (t) { return '<div class="tip">' + E(t) + '</div>'; }).join('');
    if (!ai && (p.name === 'create_task' || p.name === 'create_request')) html += '<div class="wait">Asking the hub for more...</div>';
    box.innerHTML = html;
    box.style.display = html ? '' : 'none';
  }
  async function modelSuggest(key, card) {
    var p = WA38_PENDING[key]; if (!p || (p.name !== 'create_task' && p.name !== 'create_request')) return;
    var i = readForm(card, p);
    var roster = people(i.department).map(function (m) { return { name: m.name, role: m.role || '', responsibilities: (Array.isArray(m.responsibilities) ? m.responsibilities : []).slice(0, 5) }; });
    var projs = p.name === 'create_task' ? projects(i.department).map(function (pr) { return { id: pr.id, name: pr.name }; }) : [];
    var tool = { name: 'suggest_edits', description: 'Suggestions for the proposed action.', input_schema: { type: 'object', properties: {
      owner: { type: ['string', 'null'], description: 'The best person on the roster for it, only if clearly better than the current one; exact roster name.' },
      why_owner: { type: ['string', 'null'], description: 'Under 8 words.' },
      due_date: { type: ['string', 'null'], description: 'YYYY-MM-DD only if the details imply a date.' },
      project_id: { type: ['integer', 'null'], description: 'A project from the list this belongs to, or null.' },
      title: { type: ['string', 'null'], description: 'A sharper title if the current one is vague, else null.' },
      tips: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' }, description: 'Up to 2 short, useful notes (a missing detail, a duplicate risk, a better split). No em dashes.' }
    }, required: ['owner', 'why_owner', 'due_date', 'project_id', 'title', 'tips'] } };
    try {
      var r = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { body: { task: 'brain_suggest', max_tokens: 400, temperature: 0.2,
        system: 'You help Welfare Avenue staff file tasks and requests well. Suggest only what helps; null is fine. Never invent people or projects outside the lists. Today is ' + iso(new Date()) + '.',
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'PROPOSED ' + (p.name === 'create_task' ? 'TASK' : 'REQUEST') + ': ' + JSON.stringify({ title: i.title, team: label(i.department), owner: i.owner || i.assignee || null, due_date: i.due_date || null, details: i.detail || i.details || '' }) + '\nROSTER: ' + JSON.stringify(roster) + (projs.length ? '\nPROJECTS: ' + JSON.stringify(projs) : '') }],
        tools: [tool], tool_choice: { type: 'tool', name: 'suggest_edits' } } });
      var data = r && r.data; if (!data || r.error) throw (r && r.error) || new Error('no reply');
      var tu = (data.content || []).filter(function (c) { return c.type === 'tool_use'; })[0]; var s = (tu && tu.input) || {};
      var chips = []; var names = roster.map(function (x) { return x.name; });
      var fld = p.name === 'create_task' ? 'owner' : 'assignee';
      if (s.owner && names.indexOf(s.owner) >= 0 && s.owner !== (i.owner || i.assignee)) chips.push({ f: fld, v: s.owner, t: s.owner.split(' ')[0], why: s.why_owner || 'suggested' });
      if (s.due_date && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s.due_date) && s.due_date !== i.due_date) chips.push({ f: 'due_date', v: s.due_date, t: 'Due ' + s.due_date.slice(5), why: 'from the details' });
      if (s.project_id && projs.some(function (x) { return x.id === s.project_id; })) chips.push({ f: 'project_id', v: String(s.project_id), t: projs.filter(function (x) { return x.id === s.project_id; })[0].name, why: 'project' });
      if (s.title && s.title !== i.title) chips.push({ f: 'title', v: s.title, t: 'Sharper title', why: s.title.length > 46 ? s.title.slice(0, 44) + '\u2026' : s.title });
      O.models[key] = { chips: chips, tips: (s.tips || []).slice(0, 2).map(function (t) { return String(t).replace(/\u2014/g, ','); }) };
    } catch (e) { O.models[key] = { chips: [], tips: [] }; }
    var c2 = document.getElementById('wac-' + key); if (c2 && WA38_PENDING[key]) paintSug(c2, key);
  }

  /* ---------- take over the card ---------- */
  if (typeof wa38Card === 'function') {
    var __card = wa38Card;
    wa38Card = function (p) {
      __card(p);
      var key = 'p' + WA38_N; var card = document.getElementById('wac-' + key); if (!card || !WA38_PENDING[key]) return;
      var form = formHtml(p); if (!form) return;
      var f = card.querySelector('.f'); if (f) f.remove();
      var act = card.querySelector('.act');
      var wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.innerHTML = form + '<div class="w116osug" style="display:none"></div>';
      while (wrap.firstChild) card.insertBefore(wrap.firstChild, act);
      var go = card.querySelector('.go'); if (go) go.textContent = 'Confirm';
      try { if (typeof wa27Load === 'function' && !WA27_LOADED) wa27Load(false).then(function () { var d = card.querySelector('[data-f="department"]'); var ps = card.querySelector('[data-f="project_id"]'); if (ps && d) ps.innerHTML = projectOptions(d.value, ps.value); paintSug(card, key); }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      paintSug(card, key);
      modelSuggest(key, card);
      var m = document.getElementById('wacMsgs'); if (m) m.scrollTop = m.scrollHeight;
    };
  }
  if (typeof wa38Execute === 'function') {
    var __exec = wa38Execute;
    wa38Execute = function (key) {
      var p = WA38_PENDING[key]; var card = document.getElementById('wac-' + key);
      if (p && card && card.querySelector('[data-f]')) {
        var edited = readForm(card, p);
        if ((p.name === 'create_task' || p.name === 'create_request') && !edited.title) { var res = card.querySelector('.w116osug'); if (res) { res.style.display = ''; res.innerHTML = '<div class="tip" style="color:#b3402f">Give it a title first.</div>'; } return; }
        p.input = edited;
        /* keep a plain summary of what was confirmed */
        var b = card.querySelector('b'); if (b) b.textContent = p.name === 'bank_task' ? 'Move to the bank: ' + (edited.title || ('task #' + edited.task_id)) + (edited.bank_until ? ' (back ' + edited.bank_until + ')' : '') : (p.name === 'create_task' ? 'Add a task to ' + label(edited.department) + ': ' : p.name === 'create_request' ? 'File a request to ' + label(edited.department) + ': ' : '') + (edited.title || b.textContent);
        card.querySelectorAll('[data-f]').forEach(function (el) { el.disabled = true; });
        var sug = card.querySelector('.w116osug'); if (sug) sug.remove();
      }
      return __exec(key);
    };
  }
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
    var el = e.target; if (!el || !el.matches || !el.matches('.wac-card [data-f]')) return;
    var card = el.closest('.wac-card'); var key = card && card.id.replace('wac-', ''); if (!key || !WA38_PENDING[key]) return;
    if (el.getAttribute('data-f') === 'department') {
      var p = WA38_PENDING[key]; var o = card.querySelector('[data-f="owner"], [data-f="assignee"]'); if (o) o.innerHTML = ownerOptions(el.value, o.value);
      var ps = card.querySelector('[data-f="project_id"]'); if (ps) ps.innerHTML = projectOptions(el.value, '');
      O.models[key] = null; paintSug(card, key); modelSuggest(key, card);
    } else if (el.getAttribute('data-f') === 'title') { O.models[key] = null; paintSug(card, key); modelSuggest(key, card); }
    else paintSug(card, key);
  });
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var chip = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.wac-card .w116osug [data-w116oc]'); if (!chip) return;
    var card = chip.closest('.wac-card'); var f = chip.getAttribute('data-w116oc'), v = chip.getAttribute('data-v');
    var el = card.querySelector('[data-f="' + f + '"]'); if (!el) return;
    if (el.tagName === 'SELECT' && !Array.prototype.some.call(el.options, function (o) { return o.value === v; })) el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<option value="' + E(v) + '">' + E(v) + '</option>');
    el.value = v;
    chip.parentNode.querySelectorAll('[data-w116oc="' + f + '"]').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.remove('on'); }); chip.classList.add('on');
  });
  window.w116oDebug = function () { return { models: O.models }; };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116q check in and out post to Slack, with a spoken summary (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "Check in should send a message in Slack channel #check-in-out-wa
   and checking out should do the same with bullet points about what one
   has done. Allow them to use voice to text to summarise what they've
   done; the AI makes it into bullet point form and even suggests closing
   tasks."
   - Check in: same button as before (wave116j); the hub posts "checked
     in \u00b7 9:02 AM" to #check-in-out-wa through the edge function
     checkin-post.
   - Check out: a small sheet asks what you got done. Talk (the browser's
     speech recognition, Chrome and Edge and Safari) or type; "Make it
     bullets" sends your words to the model (router task 'checkin'),
     which returns 2 to 6 bullets and the open tasks your words show are
     finished. Edit the bullets, tick the tasks, "Check out and post":
     the tasks close (with a change_log line), the note is saved on the
     check-in row and posted to Slack with your hours.
   - "Check out without a note" still posts the check-out line.
   ===================================================================== */

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function ck() { return window.w116jCk || null; }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function myName() { try { var em = myEmail(); var m = (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (x) { return lower(x.email) === em; })[0]; return (m && m.name) || (USER && USER.name) || em; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var msg = ''; try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed'); }
    if (!r || !r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error && r.data.ok !== true) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }
  function SR() { return window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition || null; }

  /* ---------- check in ---------- */
  async function checkIn() {
    var c = ck(); if (!c) return;
    await c.toggle();
    var row = c.open(); if (!row || !row.id) return;
    try { var r = await fn('checkin-post', { action: 'in', checkin_id: row.id }); toastS(r.posted ? 'Checked in and posted to #check-in-out-wa' : 'Checked in (Slack post failed: ' + (r.error || 'unknown') + ')'); }
    catch (e) { toastS('Checked in (could not post to Slack: ' + (e.message || e) + ')'); }
  }

  /* ---------- check out ---------- */
  function mins(row) { return Math.max(1, Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(row.started_at).getTime()) / 60000)); }
  function stopRec() { try { if (Q.rec) Q.rec.stop(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } Q.listening = false; var b = document.getElementById('w116qmic'); if (b) { b.classList.remove('on'); b.innerHTML = '<span class="ic"></span>Talk instead'; } }
  function startRec() {
    var R = SR(); if (!R) return;
    try {
      var rec = new R(); rec.lang = (navigator.language && /^fr/i.test(navigator.language)) ? 'fr-CA' : 'en-CA'; rec.continuous = true; rec.interimResults = true;
      var ta = document.getElementById('w116qtext'); Q.finalText = ta ? ta.value : '';
      rec.onresult = function (ev) {
        var interim = '';
        for (var i = ev.resultIndex; i < ev.results.length; i++) { var t = ev.results[i][0].transcript; if (ev.results[i].isFinal) Q.finalText = (Q.finalText + ' ' + t).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); else interim += t; }
        var box = document.getElementById('w116qtext'); if (box) { box.value = (Q.finalText + (interim ? ' ' + interim : '')).trim(); box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight; }
      };
      rec.onerror = function (ev) { stopRec(); var h = document.getElementById('w116qhint'); if (h) h.textContent = ev.error === 'not-allowed' ? 'The browser blocked the microphone. Allow it in the address bar, or type instead.' : 'Listening stopped (' + (ev.error || 'error') + '). Type or try again.'; };
      rec.onend = function () { if (Q.listening) { try { rec.start(); } catch (e) { stopRec(); } } };
      rec.start(); Q.rec = rec; Q.listening = true;
      var b = document.getElementById('w116qmic'); if (b) { b.classList.add('on'); b.innerHTML = '<span class="ic"></span>Listening&hellip; tap to stop'; }
    } catch (e) { stopRec(); }
  }
  function openCheckout(row) {

      '<div class="w113acts"><span class="w113ok" id="w116qok"></span><span style="flex:1"></span><button class="btn" id="w116qskip">Check out without a note</button><button class="btn primary" id="w116qsum">Make it bullets</button></div></div>');
    setTimeout(function () { var t = document.getElementById('w116qtext'); if (t) t.focus(); }, 50);

  async function summarise() {
    var ta = document.getElementById('w116qtext'); var text = ta ? ta.value.trim() : '';
    var ok = document.getElementById('w116qok');
    if (text.length < 3) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Say or type a line first.'; return; }
    stopRec();
    if (Q.busy) return; Q.busy = true; if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Turning that into bullets...';
    var btn = document.getElementById('w116qsum'); if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await fn('checkin-post', { action: 'summarise', text: text });
      Q.bullets = (r.bullets || []).slice(); Q.suggest = r.suggest || []; Q.others = (r.open_tasks || []).filter(function (t) { return !Q.suggest.some(function (s) { return s.id === t.id; }); });
      Q.suggest.forEach(function (s) { Q.picked[s.id] = true; });
      Q.text = text; paintReview();
    } catch (e) { if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Could not summarise: ' + (e.message || e) + '. You can still check out with your words as they are.'; Q.bullets = [text]; Q.suggest = []; Q.others = []; if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Use my words as they are'; btn.setAttribute('data-raw', '1'); } }
    Q.busy = false;
  }
  function paintReview() {
    var c = ck(); var hrs = c ? c.fmtMins(mins(Q.row)) : '';
    var bul = Q.bullets.map(function (b, i) { return '<div class="b"><span class="dot"></span><input type="text" data-w116qb="' + i + '" value="' + E(b) + '"><button type="button" class="rm" data-w116qrm="' + i + '" title="Remove">&#10005;</button></div>'; }).join('');
    var sug = Q.suggest.map(function (s) { return '<label class="sug"><input type="checkbox" data-w116qt="' + s.id + '"' + (Q.picked[s.id] ? ' checked' : '') + '><div><b>' + E(s.title) + '</b>' + (s.why ? '<span>' + E(s.why) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></label>'; }).join('');
    var oth = Q.others.map(function (t) { return '<label><input type="checkbox" data-w116qt="' + t.id + '"' + (Q.picked[t.id] ? ' checked' : '') + '><div><b>' + E(t.title) + '</b>' + (t.owner ? '<span>' + E(t.owner) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></label>'; }).join('');
    openModal('<div class="w113modal w116qm"><h3>Your check-out note</h3>' +
      '<p class="m-sub"><span class="hrs">' + E(hrs) + '</span> on the hub. Edit anything, tick what is finished, then post.</p>' +
      '<div class="bul" id="w116qbul">' + bul + '</div><span class="addl" id="w116qadd">+ add a line</span>' +
      (Q.suggest.length ? '<h4>Finished these? Tick to close them</h4><div class="tk">' + sug + '</div>' : '') +
      (Q.others.length ? '<span class="more" id="w116qmore">' + (Q.suggest.length ? 'Close other tasks too' : 'Close a task you finished') + '</span><div class="tk" id="w116qoth" style="display:none;margin-top:8px">' + oth + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="w113acts"><span class="w113ok" id="w116qok"></span><span style="flex:1"></span><button class="btn" id="w116qback">Back</button><button class="btn primary" id="w116qgo">Check out and post</button></div></div>');
  }
  function readBullets() { var out = []; document.querySelectorAll('[data-w116qb]').forEach(function (i) { var v = i.value.trim(); if (v) out.push(v.slice(0, 220)); }); return out; }
  function readPicked() { var out = []; document.querySelectorAll('[data-w116qt]').forEach(function (i) { if (i.checked) out.push(Number(i.getAttribute('data-w116qt'))); }); return out; }
  async function finish(bullets, closeIds, skipped) {
    var c = ck(); if (!c || !Q.row || Q.busy) return; Q.busy = true;
    var ok = document.getElementById('w116qok'); if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Posting...';
    var go = document.getElementById('w116qgo') || document.getElementById('w116qskip'); if (go) go.disabled = true;
    try {
      var r = await fn('checkin-post', { action: 'out', checkin_id: Q.row.id, bullets: bullets, close_task_ids: closeIds, skipped: !!skipped });
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'checkin', entity_id: myEmail(), field: 'out', old_value: null, new_value: 'checked out' + (bullets.length ? ' with a note' : ''), changed_by: myName() }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      c.clear(); closeModal();
      toastS('Checked out: ' + c.fmtMins(r.minutes || mins(Q.row)) + (r.posted ? ', posted to #check-in-out-wa' : ' (Slack post failed: ' + (r.error || 'unknown') + ')') + (r.closed && r.closed.length ? '. Closed ' + r.closed.length + (r.closed.length === 1 ? ' task' : ' tasks') : ''));
      try { var shut = (r.closed || []).map(String); if (shut.length && typeof DB_TASKS !== 'undefined' && DB_TASKS) { var nowIso = new Date().toISOString(); DB_TASKS.forEach(function (t) { if (shut.indexOf(String(t.id)) >= 0) { t.done = true; t.closed_at = t.closed_at || nowIso; } }); if (typeof renderMyPage === 'function') renderMyPage(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } /* audit 26/09/26: there was no loadTasks; the closed tasks are marked here */
      c.render(); c.presence();
    } catch (e) {
      if (ok) ok.textContent = 'Could not check out: ' + (e.message || e);
      if (go) go.disabled = false;
    }
    Q.busy = false;
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('#w116jck');
    if (b) {
      var c = ck(); if (!c) return; /* wave116j not booted yet: its own handler runs */
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      if (c.busy() || Q.busy) return;
      if (c.open()) openCheckout(c.open()); else checkIn();
      return;
    }
    if (t.id === 'w116qmic' || t.closest('#w116qmic')) { if (Q.listening) stopRec(); else startRec(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116qsum') { if (t.getAttribute('data-raw')) { Q.bullets = [(document.getElementById('w116qtext') || {}).value || '']; paintReview(); } else summarise(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116qskip') { stopRec(); finish([], [], true); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116qback') { Q.bullets = readBullets(); openCheckout(Q.row); var ta = document.getElementById('w116qtext'); if (ta) ta.value = Q.text || ''; return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116qadd') { Q.bullets = readBullets(); Q.bullets.push(''); Q.picked = {}; readPicked().forEach(function (id) { Q.picked[id] = true; }); paintReview(); var last = document.querySelectorAll('[data-w116qb]'); if (last.length) last[last.length - 1].focus(); return; }
    var rm = t.closest('[data-w116qrm]'); if (rm) { Q.bullets = readBullets(); Q.bullets.splice(Number(rm.getAttribute('data-w116qrm')), 1); Q.picked = {}; readPicked().forEach(function (id) { Q.picked[id] = true; }); paintReview(); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116qmore') { var o = document.getElementById('w116qoth'); if (o) { o.style.display = o.style.display === 'none' ? '' : 'none'; } return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116qgo') { finish(readBullets(), readPicked(), false); return; }
  }, true);
  window.w116qDebug = function () { return { row: Q.row, bullets: Q.bullets, suggest: Q.suggest, listening: Q.listening }; };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116p the HR centre (wa116x 25/09/26: idempotent hero and tab counts; the rewrite loop flickered the page), revamped (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "the HR centre really needs a revamp. Apple kind of branding:
   easy to use, big, and the robustness of ClickUp. Use photos from the
   database and make it look nice in terms of the brand." And: "make the
   did-you-knows give shout-outs to individuals for completing projects
   or doing something amazing."
   What changes (everything older keeps working underneath):
   - A hero at the top: big title, the numbers that matter (people, on
     the hub this week, new applicants, open positions, mid-onboarding,
     outstanding trainings), and the four dashboards become big pills.
   - People: a new first tab. Everyone with their photo, role, team,
     when they started (from Workspace, editable by HR), hours on the
     hub this month, open tasks, and a button to their profile. Filter
     by team, search by name.
   - Shout-outs: at the top of People. Lines the hub writes every
     morning (projects finished, task closers, big weeks, anniversaries,
     new starters, hires) and ones staff post. The same lines rotate on
     the loading screen between the fun facts (wave117) once the hub has
     loaded them once.
   - Recruitment: "Applications" from the hub@ inbox (wave mail-pull v7):
     the applicant, role, contact, CV, stage, who is handling it, notes,
     and the position it belongs to.
   - The old Onboarding, Training, Recruitment and Commitment dashboards
     are restyled (bigger numbers, more air), not rewritten.
   ===================================================================== */
