  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function isAdmin() { if (FOUNDERS.indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0) return true; try { return !!(WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function canHr() { try { if (typeof wa46IsHr === 'function') return wa46IsHr(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return isAdmin(); }
  function label(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.label && WA_TEAMS.label[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function depts() { var o = (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.order) ? WA_TEAMS.order.slice() : ['opsmtl', 'progdev', 'opsto', 'marketing', 'fundraising', 'hr']; if (o.indexOf('board') < 0) o.push('board'); return o; }
  function members() { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && m.name && !/\btest\b/i.test(m.name); }); }
  function av(p, size) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size) : ''; }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function tasksOf(m) { try { var f = lower(m.name).split(' ')[0]; return (DB_TASKS || []).filter(function (t) { return !t.done && lower(t.owner).indexOf(f) >= 0; }).length; } catch (e) { return 0; } }
  function tenure(sd) { if (!sd) return ''; var d = new Date(sd + 'T12:00:00'); var ms = Date.now() - d.getTime(); if (ms < 0) return 'starts soon'; var months = Math.floor(ms / (30.44 * 86400000)); if (months < 1) return 'this month'; if (months < 12) return months + (months === 1 ? ' month' : ' months'); var y = Math.floor(months / 12), r = months % 12; return y + (y === 1 ? ' year' : ' years') + (r ? ' ' + r + ' mo' : ''); }
  function nice(sd) { try { return new Date(sd + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return sd; } }
  function when(s) { if (!s) return ''; try { var d = new Date(s); var diff = (Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 86400000; if (diff < 1) return 'today'; if (diff < 2) return 'yesterday'; if (diff < 14) return Math.round(diff) + ' days ago'; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  async function fn(name, body) {
    var r = await db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body });
    if (r && r.error) { var msg = ''; try { if (r.error.context && r.error.context.json) msg = (await r.error.context.json()).error; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } throw new Error(msg || r.error.message || 'failed'); }
    if (!r || !r.data) throw new Error('no reply from the Hub');
    if (r.data.error && r.data.ok !== true) throw new Error(r.data.error);
    return r.data;
  }

  /* ---------- the loading screen: shout-outs between the fun facts ----------
     wave149: the first-paint script in 000_head_base now runs the whole rotation (milestones, shout-outs, lessons,
     learnings and fun facts) and reads this cache itself when the newer one is missing, so this no longer runs. */
  (function veil() {
    return;
    try {
      var raw = localStorage.getItem('wa116s'); if (!raw) return;
      var cached = JSON.parse(raw); var shouts = (cached && cached.lines) || []; if (!shouts.length) return;
      if (cached.at && Date.now() - cached.at > 14 * 86400000) return;
      var el = document.getElementById('w117fact'); if (!el || document.documentElement.classList.contains('wa-ready')) return;
      var ov = el.previousElementSibling && el.previousElementSibling.classList.contains('w117ov') ? el.previousElementSibling : null;
      var facts = [];
      try { var sc = el.closest('#wa110veil') && el.closest('#wa110veil').nextElementSibling; var txt = sc && sc.tagName === 'SCRIPT' ? sc.textContent : ''; var m = txt.match(/var F=(\[[\s\S]*?\]);var i=/); if (m) facts = JSON.parse(m[1]); } catch (e) { facts = []; }
      /* the wave117 rotation stops when it no longer finds the element by id */
      el.id = 'w117fact_x';
      var mix = []; var si = 0, fi = 0;
      shouts = shouts.slice().sort(function () { return Math.random() - 0.5; });
      for (var k = 0; k < 12; k++) { if (k % 3 === 2 && facts.length) mix.push({ t: facts[fi++ % facts.length], s: false }); else if (shouts.length) mix.push({ t: shouts[si++ % shouts.length], s: true }); }
      if (!mix.length) return;
      var i = 0;
      function show() { var x = mix[i % mix.length]; el.textContent = x.t; if (ov) ov.textContent = x.s ? 'Shout-out' : 'Did you know'; }
      show();
      var t = setInterval(function () {
        if (document.documentElement.classList.contains('wa-ready') || !document.body.contains(el)) { clearInterval(t); return; }
        el.classList.add('out'); setTimeout(function () { i++; show(); el.classList.remove('out'); }, 360);
      }, 3400);
    } catch (e) { /* the veil is decoration */ }
  })();

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function loadShouts(force) {
    if (!force && P.shoutsAt && Date.now() - P.shoutsAt < 120000) return;
    if (P.loading.shouts) return P.loading.shouts;
    P.loading.shouts = (async function () {
      try {
        var r = await db.from('hub_shoutouts').select('*').eq('active', true).gt('expires_at', new Date().toISOString()).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(40);
        P.shouts = (r && r.data) || []; P.shoutsAt = Date.now();
        /* wave149: the loading screen's cache is wave149's (Hub shout-outs only, filtered); this one is no longer written */
      } catch (e) { P.shouts = P.shouts || []; }
      P.loading.shouts = null;
    })();
    return P.loading.shouts;
  }
  async function loadCands(force) {
    if (!force && P.candsAt && Date.now() - P.candsAt < 60000) return;
    if (P.loading.cands) return P.loading.cands;
    P.loading.cands = (async function () {
      try { var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(200); P.cands = (r && r.data) || []; P.candsAt = Date.now(); } catch (e) { P.cands = P.cands || []; }
      P.loading.cands = null;
    })();
    return P.loading.cands;
  }
  async function loadHours(force) {
    if (!force && P.hoursAt && Date.now() - P.hoursAt < 120000) return;
    if (P.loading.hours) return P.loading.hours;
    P.loading.hours = (async function () {
      try {
        var start = new Date(); start.setDate(1); start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
        var week = new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString();
        var r = await db.from('hub_checkins').select('email, minutes, started_at').gte('started_at', start.toISOString()).limit(3000);
        var h = {}, w = {};
        ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (c) { var em = lower(c.email); h[em] = (h[em] || 0) + Number(c.minutes || 0); if (c.started_at >= week) w[em] = (w[em] || 0) + 1; });
        P.hours = h; P.week = w; P.hoursAt = Date.now();
      } catch (e) { P.hours = P.hours || {}; P.week = P.week || {}; }
      P.loading.hours = null;
    })();
    return P.loading.hours;
  }

  /* ---------- hero ---------- */
  function num(sel, re) { try { var b = document.querySelector(sel); if (!b) return null; var m = b.textContent.match(re); return m ? Number(m[1]) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function hero() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!panel || !body) return;
    var h = document.getElementById('w116phero');
    if (!h) { h = document.createElement('div'); h.id = 'w116phero'; var card = body.closest('.card') || body; panel.insertBefore(h, card); }
    var ppl = members(); var onHub = 0; var week = P.week || {}; ppl.forEach(function (m) { if (week[lower(m.email)]) onHub++; });
    var open = 0; try { open = (WA46.reqs || []).filter(function (r) { return r.type_key === 'hiring-request' && !r.done; }).length; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var newApps = P.cands.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'new'; }).length;
    var mid = num('#w46body .w46sec .w44tile', /(\d+)\s*mid-onboarding/) ; if (mid === null) { try { var t = Array.prototype.filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w46body .w44tile'), function (x) { return /mid-onboarding/.test(x.textContent); })[0]; mid = t ? Number((t.querySelector('b') || {}).textContent || 0) : 0; } catch (e) { mid = 0; } }
    var outst = 0; try { var t2 = Array.prototype.filter.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w46body .w44tile'), function (x) { return /outstanding/.test(x.textContent); })[0]; outst = t2 ? Number((t2.querySelector('b') || {}).textContent || 0) : 0; } catch (e) { outst = 0; }
    var html = '<p>People, hours, hiring, onboarding and training for the whole organisation, in one place. Photos and start dates come from Google Workspace; hours from check-ins on the hub.</p>' +
      '<div class="tiles">' +
      '<div class="tile" data-w116pv="people"><b>' + ppl.length + '</b><span>people on the roster</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile" data-w116pv="hours"><b>' + onHub + '</b><span>checked in this week</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile" data-w116pv="recruitment"><b>' + newApps + '</b><span>new applicant' + (newApps === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile" data-w116pv="recruitment"><b>' + open + '</b><span>open position' + (open === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile" data-w116pv="onboarding"><b>' + (mid || 0) + '</b><span>mid-onboarding</span></div>' +
      '<div class="tile" data-w116pv="training"><b>' + (outst || 0) + '</b><span>trainings outstanding</span></div>' +
      '</div>';
    /* only touch the DOM when the numbers changed: a rewrite every pass woke the observers below (wa116x) */
    if (h.getAttribute('data-html') !== html) { h.innerHTML = html; h.setAttribute('data-html', html); }
  }

  /* ---------- People ---------- */
  function ensurePeopleTab() {
    var tabs = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tabs'); if (!tabs) return false;
    if (!tabs.querySelector('[data-w47v="people"]')) tabs.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<button class="w47tab" data-w47v="people">People</button>');
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body');
    if (!document.getElementById('w116ppeople')) { var d = document.createElement('div'); d.id = 'w116ppeople'; d.style.display = 'none'; tabs.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d); }
    return true;
  }
  function currentView() { var on = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tab.on'); return on ? on.getAttribute('data-w47v') : ''; }
  function paintPeople() {
    var host = document.getElementById('w116ppeople'); if (!host) return;
    var q = lower(P.q); var team = P.team;
    var rows = members().filter(function (m) { return (!team || m.department === team) && (!q || lower(m.name).indexOf(q) >= 0 || lower(m.role).indexOf(q) >= 0); });
    rows.sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var chips = '<button class="tf' + (!team ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w116pt="">Everyone <span class="n"></span></button>' + depts().map(function (d) { var n = members().filter(function (m) { return m.department === d; }).length; return n ? '<button class="tf' + (team === d ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w116pt="' + E(d) + '">' + E(label(d)) + ' ' + n + '</button>' : ''; }).join('');
    var hr = canHr();
    var cards = rows.map(function (m) {
      var em = lower(m.email); var mins = P.hours[em] || 0; var open = tasksOf(m);
      var started = m.start_date ? nice(m.start_date) + ' <small style="display:inline;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;color:var(--wa-muted);font-weight:500">(' + tenure(m.start_date) + ')</small>' : '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">unknown</span>';
      var startCell = hr ? '<div><small>Started</small>' + (P.editStart === m.id ? '<input type="date" data-w116psd="' + m.id + '" value="' + E(m.start_date || '') + '">' : '<span data-w116pes="' + m.id + '" style="cursor:pointer" title="Click to set">' + started + '</span>') + '</div>' : '<div><small>Started</small>' + started + '</div>';
      return '<div class="pp" data-mid="' + m.id + '"><div class="top" data-w116pgo="' + m.id + '">' + av(m, 64) + '<div class="who"><b>' + E(m.name) + '</b><span>' + E(m.role || '') + '</span><span class="chip">' + E(label(m.department)) + '</span></div></div>' +
        '<div class="facts">' + startCell + '<div><small>On the hub this month</small><b>' + (mins ? (Math.round(mins / 6) / 10) + 'h' : '0h') + '</b></div><div><small>Open tasks</small><b>' + open + '</b></div><div><small>Email</small>' + (m.email ? '<a href="mailto:' + E(m.email) + '" style="color:' + GREEN + ';font-size:12px;word-break:break-all">' + E(m.email) + '</a>' : '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">none</span>') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="acts"><button class="btn" data-w116pgo="' + m.id + '">Open profile</button></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    host.innerHTML = shoutsHtml() + '<div class="bar"><input type="search" id="w116pq" placeholder="Search people by name or role" value="' + E(P.q) + '">' + chips + '</div>' +
      (cards ? '<div class="grid">' + cards + '</div>' : '<p class="empty">Nobody matches.</p>');
  }
  function shoutsHtml() {
    var me = canHr() || true; var opts = members().map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '">' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    var lines = P.shouts.length ? P.shouts.slice(0, 12).map(function (s) {
      var m = s.member_id ? members().filter(function (x) { return x.id === s.member_id; })[0] : null;
      return '<div class="l">' + (m ? av(m, 34) : '<span class="w116av" style="width:34px;height:34px;background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + GREEN + ';font-size:13px">&#9733;</span>') + '<div>' + E(s.text) + '</div><div><span class="by">' + (s.kind === 'manual' ? 'from ' + E(s.by || 'a colleague') : 'the hub') + ' &middot; ' + E(when(s.created_at)) + '</span>' + (isAdmin() || (s.kind === 'manual' && lower(s.by) === lower((typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || '')) ? ' <button class="rm" data-w116psr="' + s.id + '" title="Take down">&#10005;</button>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }).join('') : '<div class="l"><span class="w116av" style="width:34px;height:34px;background:' + GOLD + ';color:' + GREEN + ';font-size:13px">&#9733;</span><div>Nothing yet this week. The hub writes shout-outs every morning from finished projects, closed tasks, big weeks, anniversaries and new starters; you can post one below.</div><div></div></div>';
    return '<div id="w116pshouts"><h3><span class="st"></span>Shout-outs</h3><p class="sub">Good work, noticed. These also rotate on the loading screen between the fun facts.</p><div class="ls">' + lines + '</div>' +
      (me ? '<form id="w116psf"><select id="w116psp"><option value="">Who (optional)</option>' + opts + '</select><input type="text" id="w116pst" maxlength="240" placeholder="What did they do? One line."><button class="btn primary" type="submit">Post</button></form>' : '') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- Applications (Recruitment) ---------- */
  function ensureCandsBlock() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body) return false;
    if (document.getElementById('w116pcands')) return true;
    var rec = Array.prototype.filter.call(body.querySelectorAll('.w46sec'), function (s) { var h = s.querySelector('h3'); return h && /^Recruitment/.test(h.textContent); })[0];
    var d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'w46sec'; d.id = 'w116pcands'; d.innerHTML = '<h3>Recruitment: applications</h3><div id="w116pcbody"></div>';
    if (rec) rec.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', d); else body.appendChild(d);
    return true;
  }
  function stageName(s) { var x = STAGES.filter(function (p) { return p[0] === s; })[0]; return x ? x[1] : s; }
  function paintCands() {
    var host = document.getElementById('w116pcbody'); if (!host) return;
    var openStages = ['new', 'reviewing', 'interview', 'offer'];
    var rows = P.cands.filter(function (c) { return P.stage === 'all' ? true : P.stage === 'open' ? openStages.indexOf(c.status) >= 0 : c.status === P.stage; });
    var counts = {}; P.cands.forEach(function (c) { counts[c.status] = (counts[c.status] || 0) + 1; });
    var openN = P.cands.filter(function (c) { return openStages.indexOf(c.status) >= 0; }).length;
    var bar = '<button class="sf' + (P.stage === 'open' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w116pst="open">In play ' + openN + '</button>' + STAGES.map(function (s) { return counts[s[0]] ? '<button class="sf' + (P.stage === s[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w116pst="' + s[0] + '">' + s[1] + ' ' + counts[s[0]] + '</button>' : ''; }).join('') + '<button class="sf' + (P.stage === 'all' ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w116pst="all">All ' + P.cands.length + '</button><span style="flex:1"></span><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">Applications emailed to hub@ land here (label Hub/Recruitment).</span>';
    var positions = []; try { positions = (WA46.reqs || []).filter(function (r) { return r.type_key === 'hiring-request' && !r.done; }); } catch (e) { positions = []; }
    var people = members();
    var cards = rows.map(function (c) {
      var pos = positions.map(function (r) { return '<option value="' + r.id + '"' + (String(c.request_id) === String(r.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(r.title || ('Request #' + r.id)) + '</option>'; }).join('');
      if (c.request_id && !positions.some(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(c.request_id); })) pos += '<option value="' + E(c.request_id) + '" selected>Position #' + E(c.request_id) + '</option>';
      var links = (c.links || []).map(function (l) { return '<a href="' + E(l) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + E(l.replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?/, '').slice(0, 40)) + '</a>'; }).join(' &middot; ');
      return '<div class="c" data-cid="' + c.id + '"><div class="who"><b>' + E(c.name) + '</b><span><span class="st ' + E(c.status) + '">' + E(stageName(c.status)) + '</span>' + (c.role ? E(c.role) : 'role not stated') + ' &middot; applied ' + E(when(c.created_at)) + (c.source === 'email' ? ' by email' : '') + '</span>' +
        '<span>' + (c.email ? '<a href="mailto:' + E(c.email) + '">' + E(c.email) + '</a>' : '') + (c.phone ? ' &middot; ' + E(c.phone) : '') + (links ? ' &middot; ' + links : '') + '</span>' +
        (c.summary ? '<span>' + E(c.summary) + '</span>' : '') +
        '<div class="acts">' + (c.cv_path ? '<button class="btn" data-w116pcv="' + E(c.cv_path) + '">Open CV' + (c.cv_name ? ' (' + E(c.cv_name.slice(0, 30)) + ')' : '') + '</button>' : '<span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">No CV attached</span>') + (c.mail_id ? '<a class="btn" href="#hubmail">Original email</a>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="ctl"><label>Stage<select data-w116pcs="' + c.id + '">' + STAGES.map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + s[0] + '"' + (c.status === s[0] ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s[1] + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label>Handled by<select data-w116pco="' + c.id + '"><option value="">Nobody yet</option>' + people.map(function (m) { return '<option value="' + E(m.name) + '"' + (c.owner === m.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label>' +
        '<label style="grid-column:1 / -1">Position<select data-w116pcp="' + c.id + '"><option value="">Not linked to a position</option>' + pos + '</select></label>' +
        '<textarea data-w116pcn="' + c.id + '" placeholder="Notes: interview impressions, next step">' + E(c.notes || '') + '</textarea></div></div>';
    }).join('');
    host.innerHTML = '<div class="bar">' + bar + '</div>' + (cards || '<p class="empty">' + (P.cands.length ? 'Nothing in this stage.' : 'No applications yet. When someone emails hub@ with a CV or a cover note, they appear here with the Hub/Recruitment label in Gmail.') + '</p>');
  }
  async function updateCand(id, patch, msg) {
    try {
      patch.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); patch.updated_by = (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || myEmail();
      if (patch.status) patch.stage_at = patch.updated_at;
      var r = await db.from('hub_candidates').update(patch).eq('id', id);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      var c = P.cands.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (c) Object.keys(patch).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = patch[k]; });
      try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'candidate', entity_id: String(id), field: Object.keys(patch).filter(function (k) { return k !== 'updated_at' && k !== 'updated_by' && k !== 'stage_at'; })[0] || 'update', old_value: null, new_value: String(patch.status || patch.owner || patch.request_id || (patch.notes ? 'notes' : '')), changed_by: patch.updated_by }); } catch (e) { /* audit */ }
      if (msg) toastS(msg);
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not save: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- tab counts and view ---------- */
  function decorateTabs() {
    var tabs = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tabs'); if (!tabs) return;
    var t = tabs.querySelector('[data-w47v="people"]'); var th = 'People<span class="n">' + members().length + '</span>'; if (t && t.innerHTML !== th) t.innerHTML = th;
    var r = tabs.querySelector('[data-w47v="recruitment"]'); var newApps = P.cands.filter(function (c) { return c.status === 'new'; }).length;
    var rh = 'Recruitment' + (newApps ? '<span class="n">' + newApps + '</span>' : ''); if (r && r.innerHTML !== rh) r.innerHTML = rh;
    var h = tabs.querySelector('[data-w47v="hours"]'); if (h && h.textContent === 'Commitment') { /* wave55 name kept */ }
  }
  function applyView() {
    var v = currentView(); var people = document.getElementById('w116ppeople');
    if (people) people.style.display = v === 'people' ? '' : 'none';
    if (v === 'people' && people && !people.dataset.painted) { people.dataset.painted = '1'; paintPeople(); }
  }
  function decorate() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w46body'); if (!body || !body.querySelector('.w46sec')) return;
    if (!ensurePeopleTab()) return;
    ensureCandsBlock(); decorateTabs(); hero(); applyView();
    if (!document.getElementById('w116pcbody').children.length) paintCands();
    var sub = String(location.hash || '').split('/')[1] || '';
    /* wave153: land on People only while the HR centre is the page on screen (a render finishing after you left was rewriting the address) */
    var onScreen = (function () { var pn = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); return !!pn && pn.classList.contains('active') && String(location.hash || '').indexOf('#hrcentre') === 0; })();
    if (onScreen && ((!currentView() && sub === 'people') || (!sub && !P.landed))) { P.landed = true; var t = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tab[data-w47v="people"]'); if (t) { t.click(); setTimeout(applyView, 30); } }
  }
  function schedule() { clearTimeout(P.pend); P.pend = setTimeout(function () { try { decorate(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 150); }
  function watch() {
    var panel = document.getElementById('hrcentre'); if (!panel || panel.dataset.w116pmo) return; panel.dataset.w116pmo = '1';
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) { for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) { var t = muts[i].target; if (t && t.closest && (t.closest('#w116ppeople') || t.closest('#w116pcands') || t.closest('#w116phero') || (muts[i].type === 'childList' && t.closest('.w47tab')))) continue; schedule(); return; } }).observe(panel, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class', 'style'] });
    schedule();
  }
  async function boot() {
    watch();
    await Promise.all([loadShouts(false), loadCands(false), loadHours(false)]);
    var people = document.getElementById('w116ppeople'); if (people) people.dataset.painted = '';
    decorate(); paintCands(); if (currentView() === 'people') paintPeople();
  }
  if (typeof wa46Render === 'function') {
    var __r46p = wa46Render;
    wa46Render = async function () { var out = await __r46p.apply(this, arguments); try { boot(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __spp = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { var out = __spp.apply(this, arguments); try { if (id === 'hrcentre') setTimeout(boot, 700); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  /* wave149: the loading screen no longer depends on this early load (wave149 caches the shout-outs itself) */

  /* ---------- events ---------- */
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var v = t.closest('#w116phero [data-w116pv]'); if (v) { var tab = document.querySelector('#w46body .w47tab[data-w47v="' + v.getAttribute('data-w116pv') + '"]'); if (tab) { tab.click(); try { tab.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } } return; }
    var tf = t.closest('#w116ppeople [data-w116pt]'); if (tf) { P.team = tf.getAttribute('data-w116pt'); paintPeople(); return; }
    var go = t.closest('#w116ppeople [data-w116pgo]'); if (go) { location.hash = '#person/' + go.getAttribute('data-w116pgo'); return; }
    var es = t.closest('#w116ppeople [data-w116pes]'); if (es && canHr()) { P.editStart = Number(es.getAttribute('data-w116pes')); paintPeople(); var inp = document.querySelector('[data-w116psd="' + P.editStart + '"]'); if (inp) inp.focus(); return; }
    var sr = t.closest('#w116pshouts [data-w116psr]'); if (sr) { var id = Number(sr.getAttribute('data-w116psr')); try { await fn('shoutouts', { action: 'remove', id: id }); P.shouts = P.shouts.filter(function (s) { return s.id !== id; }); paintPeople(); } catch (e3) { toastS('Could not take it down: ' + (e3.message || e3)); } return; }
    var sf = t.closest('#w116pcands [data-w116pst]'); if (sf) { P.stage = sf.getAttribute('data-w116pst'); paintCands(); return; }
    var cv = t.closest('#w116pcands [data-w116pcv]'); if (cv) { try { var s = await db.storage.from('hub-mail').createSignedUrl(cv.getAttribute('data-w116pcv'), 600); if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) window.open(s.data.signedUrl, '_blank', 'noopener'); else toastS('Could not open the CV'); } catch (e4) { toastS('Could not open the CV'); } return; }
    if (t.closest('#w46body .w47tab')) setTimeout(applyView, 30);
  });
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w116pq') { P.q = t.value; var grid = document.querySelector('#w116ppeople .grid, #w116ppeople .empty'); var cur = document.activeElement; paintPeople(); var q = document.getElementById('w116pq'); if (q) { q.focus(); try { q.setSelectionRange(q.value.length, q.value.length); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } } } });
  document.addEventListener('change', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute) return;
    var sd = t.getAttribute('data-w116psd');
    if (sd) {
      var id = Number(sd); var val = t.value || null; P.editStart = null;

'manual' : null }).eq('id', id); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; var m = members().filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (m) { m.start_date = val; m.start_source = val ? 'manual' : null; } toastS(val ? 'Start date saved' : 'Start date cleared'); try { await db.from('change_log').insert({ entity_type: 'team_member', entity_id: String(id), field: 'start_date', old_value: null, new_value: val, changed_by: (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || myEmail() }); } catch (e2) { /* audit */ } }
      catch (e3) { toastS('Could not save the start date: ' + (e3.message || e3)); }
      paintPeople(); return;
    }
    var cs = t.getAttribute('data-w116pcs'); if (cs) { await updateCand(Number(cs), { status: t.value }, 'Stage: ' + stageName(t.value)); paintCands(); decorateTabs(); hero(); return; }
    var co = t.getAttribute('data-w116pco'); if (co) { await updateCand(Number(co), { owner: t.value || null }, t.value ? 'Handled by ' + t.value : 'Nobody handling it yet'); return; }
    var cp = t.getAttribute('data-w116pcp'); if (cp) { await updateCand(Number(cp), { request_id: t.value ? Number(t.value) : null }, t.value ? 'Linked to the position' : 'Unlinked'); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w116pcn')) { var id = Number(t.getAttribute('data-w116pcn')); var c = P.cands.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; if (c && (c.notes || '') !== t.value) updateCand(id, { notes: t.value.slice(0, 2000) }, 'Notes saved'); } });
  document.addEventListener('submit', async function (e) {
    if (!e.target || e.target.id !== 'w116psf') return; e.preventDefault();
    var person = (document.getElementById('w116psp') || {}).value || ''; var text = ((document.getElementById('w116pst') || {}).value || '').trim();
    if (text.length < 4) { toastS('Write the shout-out first'); return; }
    try { await fn('shoutouts', { action: 'add', person: person, text: text }); toastS('Posted'); await loadShouts(true); paintPeople(); } catch (e2) { toastS('Could not post: ' + (e2.message || e2)); }
  });
  window.w116pDebug = function () { return { cands: P.cands.length, shouts: P.shouts.length, hours: Object.keys(P.hours).length, view: currentView() }; };
})();

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function myEmail() { try { return lower(USER && USER.email); } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function isAdmin() { if (FOUNDERS.indexOf(myEmail()) >= 0) return true; try { return !!(WA40_MINE && WA40_MINE.role === 'admin'); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function label(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.label && WA_TEAMS.label[d]) || (typeof DEPT_LABEL !== 'undefined' && DEPT_LABEL[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  function av(p, size) { return window.w116Avatar ? window.w116Avatar(p, size) : ''; }
  function member(email) { try { return (DB_MEMBERS || []).filter(function (m) { return lower(m.email) === lower(email); })[0] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function money(v) { v = Number(v || 0); if (!v) return '$0'; if (v < 0.01) return '<$0.01'; return '$' + (v < 10 ? v.toFixed(2) : Math.round(v)); }
  function ago(s) { if (!s) return 'never'; var d = new Date(s); var diff = (Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 60000; if (diff < 2) return 'just now'; if (diff < 60) return Math.round(diff) + ' min ago'; if (diff < 36 * 60) return Math.round(diff / 60) + ' h ago'; if (diff < 30 * 1440) return Math.round(diff / 1440) + ' d ago'; return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); }
  function stamp(s) { var d = new Date(s); return d.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }); }

  function load(force) {

  function ensureCard() {

  function pcol(title, p) {
    if (!p) return '<div class="pc"><h4>' + title + '</h4><p class="empty">No data</p></div>';
    return '<div class="pc"><h4>' + title + '</h4>' +
      '<div class="big"><div><b>' + Number(p.visitors) + '</b><span>people came</span></div><div><b>' + Number(p.hours) + 'h</b><span>checked in</span></div><div><b>' + Number(p.tasks_closed) + '</b><span>tasks closed</span></div><div><b>' + money(p.ai_cost) + '</b><span>AI spend</span></div></div>' +
      '<table>' +
      '<tr><td>Visits</td><td>' + Number(p.visits) + '</td></tr>' +
      '<tr><td>People who checked in</td><td>' + Number(p.people_checked_in) + ' (' + Number(p.checkins) + ' check-ins)</td></tr>' +
      '<tr><td>Tasks opened</td><td>' + Number(p.tasks_opened) + '</td></tr>' +
      '<tr><td>AI calls (models)</td><td>' + Number(p.ai_calls) + '</td></tr>' +
      '<tr><td>Jev checks and picks</td><td>' + Number(p.jev_calls) + '</td></tr>' +
      '<tr><td>Questions to the Brain</td><td>' + Number(p.questions) + '</td></tr>' +

      '<tr><td>Emails filed</td><td>' + Number(p.emails_filed) + '</td></tr>' +
      '<tr><td>Shout-outs</td><td>' + Number(p.shoutouts) + '</td></tr>' +
      '</table></div>';
  }
  var SERIES = [['visitors', 'People per day'], ['hours', 'Hours checked in'], ['tasks_closed', 'Tasks closed'], ['ai_calls', 'AI calls'], ['ai_cost', 'AI spend ($)']];
  function bars(days) {
    var k = R.series; var vals = days.map(function (d) { return Number(d[k] || 0); }); var max = Math.max.apply(null, vals.concat([1]));
    return '<div class="bars">' + days.map(function (d, i) { var v = vals[i]; var h = Math.max(2, Math.round(v / max * 64)); return '<i style="height:' + h + 'px" title="' + E(d.day) + ': ' + (k === 'ai_cost' ? money(v) : v + (k === 'hours' ? 'h' : '')) + '"></i>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="axis"><span>' + E(days[0] ? days[0].day : '') + '</span><span>' + E(days[days.length - 1] ? days[days.length - 1].day : '') + '</span></div>';
  }
  function people(rows) {
    var team = R.team; var list = rows.filter(function (p) { return !team || p.department === team; });
    var k = R.sort;
    list.sort(function (a, b) { if (k === 'name') return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); var av2 = a[k] == null ? -1 : (typeof a[k] === 'string' ? new Date(a[k]).getTime() : Number(a[k])); var bv = b[k] == null ? -1 : (typeof b[k] === 'string' ? new Date(b[k]).getTime() : Number(b[k])); return bv - av2; });
    var teams = {}; rows.forEach(function (p) { teams[p.department] = (teams[p.department] || 0) + 1; });
    var th = function (key, t) { return '<th data-w116rs="' + key + '" class="' + (k === key ? 'on' : '') + '">' + t + (k === key ? ' &#9662;' : '') + '</th>'; };
    return '<div class="tb"><b style="font-size:13px">People</b><select id="w116rteam"><option value="">All teams</option>' + Object.keys(teams).map(function (d) { return '<option value="' + E(d) + '"' + (team === d ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(label(d)) + ' (' + teams[d] + ')</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">Sign-ins: hub visits since today, plus the sessions Supabase remembers. Hours, tasks and questions: last 30 days.</span></div>' +
      '<table class="pp"><thead><tr>' + th('name', 'Person') + th('last_active', 'Last active') + '<th>Last 3 sign-ins</th>' + th('visits_30d', 'Visits') + th('hours_30d', 'Hours') + th('tasks_closed_30d', 'Tasks closed') + th('questions_30d', 'Questions') + '</tr></thead><tbody>' +
      list.map(function (p) {
        var m = member(p.email) || { name: p.name };
        var quiet = !p.last_active || (Date.now() - new Date(p.last_active).getTime()) > 14 * 86400000;
        var sis = (p.sign_ins || []).map(function (s, i) { return '<span class="si' + (i ? ' old' : '') + '">' + E(stamp(s)) + '</span>'; }).join('') || '<span class="si old">' + (p.has_account ? 'no sign-in on record' : 'no hub account yet') + '</span>';
        var dot = !p.last_active ? '#cccccc' : quiet ? FLAG : (Date.now() - new Date(p.last_active).getTime()) < 2 * 86400000 ? TEAL : GOLD;
        return '<tr class="' + (quiet ? 'quiet' : '') + '"><td class="who">' + av(m, 30) + '<div><b>' + E(p.name) + '</b><span>' + E(label(p.department)) + (p.role ? ' &middot; ' + E(p.role) : '') + '</span></div></td>' +
          '<td><span class="dot" style="background:' + dot + '"></span>' + E(ago(p.last_active)) + '</td><td>' + sis + '</td><td class="num">' + Number(p.visits_30d || 0) + '</td><td class="num">' + Number(p.hours_30d || 0) + 'h</td><td class="num">' + Number(p.tasks_closed_30d || 0) + '</td><td class="num">' + Number(p.questions_30d || 0) + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</tbody></table>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var body = document.getElementById('w116rbody'); if (!body) return;
    if (R.err && !R.data) { body.innerHTML = '<div class="err">' + E(R.err) + '</div><div class="bar"><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn" data-w116r="reload">Try again</button></div>'; return; }
    if (!R.data) { body.innerHTML = 'Loading...'; return; }
    var d = R.data;
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="per">' + pcol('Today', d.today) + pcol('Last 7 days', d.week) + pcol('Last 30 days', d.month) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="strip">' + SERIES.map(function (s) { return '<button class="sf' + (R.series === s[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w116rser="' + s[0] + '">' + s[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '<span style="flex:1"></span><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">Last 30 days, one bar a day</span></div>' + bars(d.days || []) +
      people(d.people || []) +
      '<div class="bar" style="margin-top:10px"><span>Visits count once per person per six hours per browser. AI numbers come from the router log (Admin &gt; AI models has the detail per job).</span><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn" data-w116r="reload">Refresh</button></div>';
  }
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var b = t.closest('#w116rcard [data-w116r]'); if (b) { load(true).then(paint); return; }
    var s = t.closest('#w116rcard [data-w116rser]'); if (s) { R.series = s.getAttribute('data-w116rser'); paint(); return; }
    var h = t.closest('#w116rcard th[data-w116rs]'); if (h) { R.sort = h.getAttribute('data-w116rs'); paint(); return; }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target && e.target.id === 'w116rteam') { R.team = e.target.value; paint(); } });

  if (typeof wa40Render === 'function') {
    var __w40r116r = wa40Render;
    wa40Render = async function () { var out = await __w40r116r.apply(this, arguments); try { if (ensureCard()) { paint(); await load(false); paint(); } } catch (e) { /* non-fatal */ } return out; };
  }
  if (typeof showPanel === 'function') {
    var __sp116r = showPanel;
    showPanel = function (id) { var out = __sp116r.apply(this, arguments); try { if (id === 'adminpanel' && isAdmin()) setTimeout(function () { if (ensureCard()) { paint(); load(false).then(paint); } }, 950); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return out; };
  }
  window.w116rDebug = function () { return { loaded: !!R.data, err: R.err, people: R.data ? (R.data.people || []).length : 0 }; };

/* =====================================================================
   WA v116t tasks: subtasks, descriptions, attachments, the tree (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Ahmed, via Steel: "Tasks hierarchy like in ClickUp: SOP & Requests ->
   Marketing -> Posts Requests -> tasks. Formatting task descriptions
   (bold, bullet points) and attaching files and pictures."
   The hierarchy was already Team > Project > Sub-project > Task, with
   workstreams inside a team. This wave adds the two missing levels and
   the way to see them:
   - Subtasks: any task can hold subtasks (dept_tasks.parent_task_id).
     The task drawer (wave116b) shows them with a tick, an "Add a
     subtask" line, and the parent's breadcrumb (Team > Project >
     Parent task) at the top. Subtask rows on the boards are indented
     with the parent's name; a parent row shows "2/5 subtasks".
   - Description: a proper description on every task (dept_tasks.
     description). Edit in place, formatting shows as you type (bold,
     italic, bullets, numbered lists, links; "- " and "1. " start a
     list; Ctrl+B/I/K), saved on its own a second after you stop and on
     blur. Stored as light Markdown so Slack, prompts and the classic
     page read it.
   - Attachments: files and pictures on a task (task_files + the
     private task-files bucket): drop them on the drawer, pick them, or
     paste a screenshot into the description. Pictures show as
     thumbnails; everything opens through a signed link. Google Drive
     links attach too (Steel: "attachments should also be attached via
     Google Drive link"): paste the link, the hub looks up its name and
     kind (hub-connect drive_meta) and shows it as a Docs, Sheets,
     Slides, folder or Drive chip that opens in Drive.
   - Tree: a "Tree" view on every team's task page (next to List, Board,
     Calendar): Team > Projects (with sub-projects) > tasks > subtasks,
     collapsible, with counts, each line opening its task or project.
   ===================================================================== */

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }

  /* ---------- markdown, small and safe ---------- */
  function inline(s) {
    s = E(s);
    s = s.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
    s = s.replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
    s = s.replace(/(^|[^*])\*([^*\n]+)\*/g, '$1<em>$2</em>');
    s = s.replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\((https?:\/\/[^\s)]+)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>');
    s = s.replace(/(^|\s)(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g, function (m, a, u) { return a + '<a href="' + u + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + u.replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?/, '').slice(0, 50) + '</a>'; });
    return s;
  }
  function md(text) {
    var lines = String(text || '').replace(/\r/g, '').split('\n'); var out = ''; var list = null; var para = [];
    function flushP() { if (para.length) { out += '<p>' + para.map(inline).join('<br>') + '</p>'; para = []; } }
    function flushL() { if (list) { out += '</' + list + '>'; list = null; } }
    lines.forEach(function (l) {
      var mb = l.match(/^\s*[-*\u2022]\s+(.*)$/), mo = l.match(/^\s*\d+[.)]\s+(.*)$/);
      if (mb || mo) { flushP(); var kind = mb ? 'ul' : 'ol'; if (list !== kind) { flushL(); out += '<' + kind + '>'; list = kind; } out += '<li>' + inline((mb || mo)[1]) + '</li>'; return; }
      flushL();
      if (!l.trim()) { flushP(); return; }
      para.push(l);
    });
    flushL(); flushP();
    return out;
  }
  window.w116tMarkdown = md;

  /* ---------- the drawer ---------- */
  function drawerTaskId() { var hs = document.querySelectorAll('#w116dr .w116drh span'); for (var i = 0; i < hs.length; i++) { var m = hs[i].textContent.match(/#(\d+)/); if (m) return Number(m[1]); } return null; }
  function crumbHtml(t) {
    var bits = ['<a data-w116tgo="#team/' + E(t.department) + '">' + E(label(t.department)) + '</a>'];
    var p = t.project_id ? projById(t.project_id) : null;
    if (p && p.parent_id) { var pp = projById(p.parent_id); if (pp) bits.push('<a data-w116tgo="#project/' + pp.id + '">' + E(pp.name) + '</a>'); }
    if (p) bits.push('<a data-w116tgo="#project/' + p.id + '">' + E(p.name) + '</a>');
    if (t.subsection && t.subsection !== 'General') bits.push('<span>' + E(t.subsection) + '</span>');
    var par = t.parent_task_id ? taskById(t.parent_task_id) : null;
    if (par) bits.push('<a data-w116topen="' + par.id + '" title="Parent task">' + E(par.title.length > 40 ? par.title.slice(0, 38) + '\u2026' : par.title) + '</a>');
    return '<div class="w116tcrumb">' + bits.join('<span class="sep">&rsaquo;</span>') + '<span class="sep">&rsaquo;</span><span>this task</span></div>';
  }
  function descHtml(t) {
    var text = t.description || '';
    return '<div class="w116tdw"><div class="w116drs">Description</div><div class="w116tdesc" data-tid="' + t.id + '">' +
      '<div class="w116ttb"><button data-w116tf="bold" title="Bold (Ctrl+B)"><b>B</b></button><button data-w116tf="italic" title="Italic (Ctrl+I)"><i>I</i></button><button data-w116tf="ul" title="Bullet list (type - and a space)">&bull; List</button><button data-w116tf="ol" title="Numbered list (type 1. and a space)">1. List</button><button data-w116tf="link" title="Link (Ctrl+K)">Link</button><span class="hint">Ctrl+click opens a link</span><span class="sp"></span><span class="st" id="w116tdst"></span></div>' +
      '<div class="w116ted w116md" id="w116ted" contenteditable="true" spellcheck="true" data-ph="What this is, what done looks like, links. Bold, bullets and numbered lists work as you type; paste a screenshot to attach it.">' + (text.trim() ? md(text) : '') + '</div></div></div>';
  }
  /* the editor holds HTML; the task keeps light Markdown (same as before, so Slack, prompts and the classic page read it) */
  function htmlToMd(root) {
    function walk(node, ctx) {
      var out = '';
      node.childNodes.forEach(function (n) {
        if (n.nodeType === 3) { out += n.nodeValue.replace(/\u00a0/g, ' '); return; }
        if (n.nodeType !== 1) return;
        var tag = n.tagName.toLowerCase();
        if (tag === 'br') { out += '\n'; return; }
        if (tag === 'b' || tag === 'strong') { var b = walk(n, ctx).trim(); out += b ? '**' + b + '**' : ''; return; }
        if (tag === 'i' || tag === 'em') { var i = walk(n, ctx).trim(); out += i ? '*' + i + '*' : ''; return; }
        if (tag === 'code') { out += '`' + walk(n, ctx) + '`'; return; }
        if (tag === 'a') { var href = n.getAttribute('href') || ''; var tx = walk(n, ctx).trim() || href; out += /^https?:/.test(href) ? '[' + tx + '](' + href + ')' : tx; return; }
        if (tag === 'ul' || tag === 'ol') {
          var k = 0; out += (out && !/\n$/.test(out) ? '\n' : '');
          n.childNodes.forEach(function (li) { if (li.nodeType !== 1 || li.tagName.toLowerCase() !== 'li') return; k++; var inner = walk(li, ctx).replace(/\n+$/, '').replace(/\n/g, ' '); out += (tag === 'ul' ? '- ' : k + '. ') + inner.trim() + '\n'; });
          return;
        }
        if (tag === 'li') { out += walk(n, ctx) + '\n'; return; }
        if (tag === 'p' || tag === 'div' || tag === 'h1' || tag === 'h2' || tag === 'h3' || tag === 'blockquote') { var inner2 = walk(n, ctx); out += (out && !/\n$/.test(out) ? '\n' : '') + inner2 + '\n'; return; }
        out += walk(n, ctx);
      });
      return out;
    }
    return walk(root, {}).replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, '\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
  }
  function edText(el) { return htmlToMd(el); }
  function cleanPaste(html) {
    var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = html;
    box.querySelectorAll('script,style,img,svg,iframe,object').forEach(function (x) { x.remove(); });
    /* keep only what the description knows: bold, italic, lists, links, code, line breaks */
    var mdText = htmlToMd(box);
    return md(mdText);
  }
  function subsHtml(t) {
    var kids = children(t.id); var done = kids.filter(function (k) { return k.done; }).length;
    return '<div class="w116tsw" data-tid="' + t.id + '"><div class="w116drs">Subtasks' + (kids.length ? ' <span style="font-weight:500;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none">' + done + '/' + kids.length + '</span>' : '') + '</div><div class="w116tsub" data-tid="' + t.id + '">' +
      (kids.length ? '<div class="bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(done / kids.length * 100) + '%"></i></div>' : '') +
      kids.map(function (k) { return '<div class="s' + (k.done ? ' done' : '') + '" data-sid="' + k.id + '"><span class="dn" data-w116tsd="' + k.id + '">' + (k.done ? '&#10003;' : '') + '</span><span class="t" data-w116topen="' + k.id + '">' + E(k.title) + '</span><span class="m">' + E(k.owner ? k.owner.split(' ')[0] : '') + (k.due_date ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(String(k.due_date).slice(5)) : '') + '</span></div>'; }).join('') +
      '<div class="add"><input type="text" id="w116tsa" placeholder="Add a subtask and press Enter"><button data-w116tsadd="' + t.id + '">Add</button></div></div></div>';
  }
  function filesHtml(t) {
    var list = K.files[t.id] || null;
    var grid = list === null ? '<p class="up">Loading...</p>' : (list.length ? '<div class="grid">' + list.map(function (f) {
      if (f.kind === 'link') { var k = linkKind(f); return '<div class="f lnk" data-w116tfo="' + f.id + '" title="' + E(f.name) + ' \u00b7 opens in Google Drive' + (f.by ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(f.by) : '') + '"><div class="ic"><span class="g ' + k.cls + '">' + k.glyph + '</span></div><div class="n">' + E(f.name) + '</div><span class="x" data-w116tfx="' + f.id + '" title="Remove">&#10005;</span></div>'; }
      var img = /^image\//.test(f.mime || ''); var u = K.urls[f.path];
      return '<div class="f" data-w116tfo="' + f.id + '" title="' + E(f.name) + (f.by ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(f.by) : '') + '">' + (img && u ? '<img src="' + E(u) + '" alt="">' : '<div class="ic">' + E((f.name.split('.').pop() || 'file').toUpperCase().slice(0, 5)) + '</div>') + '<div class="n">' + E(f.name) + '</div><span class="x" data-w116tfx="' + f.id + '" title="Remove">&#10005;</span></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
    return '<div class="w116tfw" data-tid="' + t.id + '"><div class="w116drs">Attachments' + (list && list.length ? ' <span style="font-weight:500;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none">' + list.length + '</span>' : '') + '</div><div class="w116tfiles" data-tid="' + t.id + '">' + grid +
      '<div class="drop" data-w116tpick="' + t.id + '"><b>Add files or pictures</b> \u00b7 drop them here, click to pick, or paste a screenshot into the description<input type="file" multiple style="display:none" data-w116tfile="' + t.id + '"></div>' +
      '<div class="lnkrow"><input type="url" id="w116tlink" placeholder="Or paste a Google Drive link (Doc, Sheet, Slides, folder, file)"><button data-w116tlinkadd="' + t.id + '">Attach link</button></div><p class="up" id="w116tup"></p></div></div>';
  }
  function inject() {
    var dr = document.getElementById('w116dr'); if (!dr || !dr.classList.contains('open')) return;
    var id = drawerTaskId(); if (!id) return; var t = taskById(id); if (!t) return;
    if (dr.querySelector('.w116tdesc') && K.task === id) return;
    K.task = id;
    var body = dr.querySelector('.w116drc'); var fields = dr.querySelector('.w116f'); if (!body || !fields) return;
    var old = dr.querySelectorAll('.w116tcrumb, .w116tdesc, .w116tsub, .w116tfiles, .w116tsec'); old.forEach(function (x) { x.remove(); });
    var ttl = dr.querySelector('textarea.ttl'); if (ttl) ttl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', crumbHtml(t));
    var wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'w116tsec'; wrap.innerHTML = descHtml(t) + subsHtml(t) + filesHtml(t);
    fields.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', wrap);
    loadFiles(t);
  }
  async function loadFiles(t) {
    try {
      var r = await db.from('task_files').select('*').eq('task_id', Number(t.id)).order('created_at');
      var rows = (r && r.data) || []; K.files[t.id] = rows;
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) { var f = rows[i]; if (/^image\//.test(f.mime || '') && !K.urls[f.path]) { try { var s = await db.storage.from('task-files').createSignedUrl(f.path, 3600); if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) K.urls[f.path] = s.data.signedUrl; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
    } catch (e) { K.files[t.id] = K.files[t.id] || []; }
    var box = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116tfw[data-tid="' + t.id + '"]'); if (box && K.task === t.id) box.outerHTML = filesHtml(t);
  }
  async function saveDesc(t, text) {
    text = String(text || '').slice(0, 20000);
    if ((t.description || '') === text) return;
    var st = document.getElementById('w116tdst'); if (st) st.textContent = 'Saving...';
    try {
      var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ description: text, updated_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_by: who0() }).eq('id', t.id);
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      t.description = text; logT(t, 'description', null, text.slice(0, 120));
      if (st) st.textContent = 'Saved';
      setTimeout(function () { var s2 = document.getElementById('w116tdst'); if (s2 && s2.textContent === 'Saved') s2.textContent = ''; }, 2000);
    } catch (e) { if (st) st.textContent = 'Not saved'; toastS('Could not save the description'); }
  }
  async function addSub(t, title) {
    title = String(title || '').trim(); if (!title) return;
    var row = { department: t.department, subsection: t.subsection || null, title: title, owner: t.owner || myName() || '', status: 'Open', flag: false, priority: false, done: false, sort: 999, due_date: null, project_id: t.project_id || null, parent_task_id: t.id, updated_by: who0(), opened_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').insert(row).select('*').single();
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not add the subtask'); return; }
    var saved = (r && r.data) || row; if (!saved.id) saved.id = -Date.now();
    tasks().push(saved); logT(saved, 'added', null, title + ' (subtask of #' + t.id + ')');
    var box = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116tsw[data-tid="' + t.id + '"]'); if (box) box.outerHTML = subsHtml(t);
    var inp = document.getElementById('w116tsa'); if (inp) inp.focus();
    repaint();
  }
  async function toggleSub(id) {
    var k = taskById(id); if (!k) return; var nowDone = !k.done; var now = new Date().toISOString();
    var r = await db.from('dept_tasks').update({ done: nowDone, closed_at: nowDone ? now : null, status: nowDone ? 'Done' : 'Open', updated_at: now, updated_by: who0() }).eq('id', k.id);
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not update'); return; }
    k.done = nowDone; k.closed_at = nowDone ? now : null; k.status = nowDone ? 'Done' : 'Open'; logT(k, 'done', String(!nowDone), String(nowDone));
    var par = taskById(k.parent_task_id); if (par) { var box = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116tsw[data-tid="' + par.id + '"]'); if (box) box.outerHTML = subsHtml(par); }
    repaint();
  }
  async function upload(t, files) {
    var up = document.getElementById('w116tup'); var done = 0, list = Array.prototype.slice.call(files || []);
    if (!list.length) return;
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      var f = list[i];
      if (f.size > 25 * 1024 * 1024) { toastS(f.name + ' is over 25 MB'); continue; }
      if (up) up.textContent = 'Uploading ' + f.name + ' (' + (i + 1) + '/' + list.length + ')...';
      var safe = String(f.name || 'file').replace(/[^\w.\-]+/g, '_').slice(0, 100);
      var path = t.id + '/' + Date.now().toString(36) + '_' + safe;
      try {
        var r = await db.storage.from('task-files').upload(path, f, { contentType: f.type || 'application/octet-stream', upsert: false });
        if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
        var ins = await db.from('task_files').insert({ task_id: t.id, name: f.name || safe, path: path, mime: f.type || null, size: f.size || null, by: who0() }).select('*').single();
        if (ins && ins.error) throw ins.error;
        (K.files[t.id] = K.files[t.id] || []).push(ins.data);
        if (/^image\//.test(f.type || '')) { try { var s = await db.storage.from('task-files').createSignedUrl(path, 3600); if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) K.urls[path] = s.data.signedUrl; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
        logT(t, 'attachment', null, f.name); done++;
      } catch (e) { toastS('Could not upload ' + f.name + ': ' + (e.message || e)); }
    }
    var box = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116tfw[data-tid="' + t.id + '"]'); if (box && K.task === t.id) box.outerHTML = filesHtml(t);
    if (done) toastS(done + (done === 1 ? ' file attached' : ' files attached'));
  }
  var DRIVE_RE = /https?:\/\/(?:docs|drive|forms)\.google\.com\/[^\s<>"']+/i;
  function linkKind(f) {
    var m = String(f.mime || ''), u = String(f.url || '');
    if (/vnd\.google-apps\.document/.test(m) || /docs\.google\.com\/document/.test(u)) return { cls: 'doc', glyph: 'D', label: 'Google Doc' };
    if (/vnd\.google-apps\.spreadsheet/.test(m) || /spreadsheets/.test(u)) return { cls: 'sheet', glyph: 'S', label: 'Google Sheet' };
    if (/vnd\.google-apps\.presentation/.test(m) || /presentation/.test(u)) return { cls: 'slide', glyph: 'P', label: 'Google Slides' };
    if (/vnd\.google-apps\.form/.test(m) || /forms\.google/.test(u)) return { cls: 'form', glyph: 'F', label: 'Google Form' };
    if (/vnd\.google-apps\.folder/.test(m) || /\/folders\//.test(u)) return { cls: 'folder', glyph: '\u25a3', label: 'Drive folder' };
    if (/pdf/.test(m)) return { cls: 'pdf', glyph: 'PDF', label: 'PDF in Drive' };
    return { cls: 'drive', glyph: '\u25b3', label: 'Drive file' };
  }
  async function addLink(t, url) {
    url = String(url || '').trim(); var up = document.getElementById('w116tup');
    if (!DRIVE_RE.test(url)) { toastS('Paste a Google Drive, Docs, Sheets, Slides or Forms link'); return; }
    if ((K.files[t.id] || []).some(function (f) { return f.kind === 'link' && f.url === url; })) { toastS('That link is already attached'); return; }
    if (up) up.textContent = 'Looking up the link...';
    var meta = { name: null, mime: null, icon: null, url: url };
    try { var r = await db.functions.invoke('hub-connect', { body: { action: 'drive_meta', url: url } }); if (r && r.data && r.data.ok) meta = r.data; } catch (e) { /* the link still attaches, with a generic name */ }
    var kind = linkKind(meta);
    var name = meta.name || prompt('Name for this link (the hub could not read the file\u2019s title):', kind.label) || kind.label;
    try {
      var ins = await db.from('task_files').insert({ task_id: t.id, name: String(name).slice(0, 200), path: null, url: meta.url || url, mime: meta.mime || null, icon: meta.icon || null, size: meta.size || null, kind: 'link', by: who0() }).select('*').single();
      if (ins && ins.error) throw ins.error;
      (K.files[t.id] = K.files[t.id] || []).push(ins.data);
      logT(t, 'attachment', null, name + ' (Drive link)');
      toastS('Link attached');
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not attach the link: ' + (e.message || e)); }
    var box = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116tfw[data-tid="' + t.id + '"]'); if (box && K.task === t.id) box.outerHTML = filesHtml(t);
  }
  async function openFile(t, fid) {
    var f = (K.files[t.id] || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(fid); })[0]; if (!f) return;
    if (f.kind === 'link') { if (f.url) window.open(f.url, '_blank', 'noopener'); return; }
    try { var s = await db.storage.from('task-files').createSignedUrl(f.path, 600); if (s && s.data && s.data.signedUrl) window.open(s.data.signedUrl, '_blank', 'noopener'); else toastS('Could not open the file'); } catch (e) { toastS('Could not open the file'); }
  }
  async function removeFile(t, fid) {
    var f = (K.files[t.id] || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(fid); })[0]; if (!f) return;
    if (!confirm('Remove ' + f.name + ' from this task?')) return;
    try { var r = await db.from('task_files').delete().eq('id', f.id); if (r && r.error) throw r.error; if (f.path) { try { await db.storage.from('task-files').remove([f.path]); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } K.files[t.id] = (K.files[t.id] || []).filter(function (x) { return x.id !== f.id; }); logT(t, 'attachment removed', f.name, null); var box = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116tfw[data-tid="' + t.id + '"]'); if (box) box.outerHTML = filesHtml(t); }
    catch (e) { toastS('Could not remove it: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }

  /* ---------- the boards: subtask rows ---------- */
  function decorateRows() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.w116r[data-tid]').forEach(function (r) {
      var t = taskById(r.getAttribute('data-tid')); if (!t) return;
      var kids = children(t.id); var ttl = r.querySelector('.w116t');
      if (t.parent_task_id && !r.classList.contains('w116tchild')) { var par = taskById(t.parent_task_id); r.classList.add('w116tchild'); if (ttl && par && !ttl.querySelector('.w116tag.sub')) ttl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="w116tag sub" title="Subtask of ' + E(par.title) + '">&#8627; ' + E(par.title.length > 28 ? par.title.slice(0, 26) + '\u2026' : par.title) + '</span>'); }
      if (kids.length && ttl && !ttl.querySelector('.w116tag.subs')) ttl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<span class="w116tag subs" title="Subtasks">' + kids.filter(function (k) { return k.done; }).length + '/' + kids.length + ' subtasks</span>');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- the tree view ---------- */
  function ensureTreeButton() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.w116tb').forEach(function (tb) {
      if (tb.querySelector('[data-w116tview]')) return;
      var views = tb.querySelectorAll('.w116seg a[data-act="view"]'); var last = views[views.length - 1]; if (!last) return;
      last.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', '<a data-w116tview="tree" class="' + (K.tree.on ? 'on' : '') + '" title="Team, projects, tasks and subtasks as a tree">Tree</a>');
    });
  }
  function scopeDept() { var m = String(location.hash || '').match(/^#team\/([a-z0-9_-]+)/); if (m) return m[1]; try { var g = document.querySelector('.w116r[data-tid]'); var t = g && taskById(g.getAttribute('data-tid')); return t ? t.department : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function treeHtml(d) {
    var all = tasks().filter(function (t) { return t.department === d; });
    var showDone = !!K.tree.showDone;
    var visible = all.filter(function (t) { return showDone || !t.done; });
    var byId = {}; visible.forEach(function (t) { byId[t.id] = t; });
    var projs = projects().filter(function (p) { return p.department === d && (p.status || 'active') === 'active'; });
    var closed = K.tree.closed || {};
    function taskNode(t, depth) {
      var kids = visible.filter(function (k) { return String(k.parent_task_id) === String(t.id); });
      var key = 't' + t.id; var isClosed = !!closed[key];
      var st = window.w116Board ? '' : ''; var stp = '<span class="w116st" style="font-size:10.5px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(16,63,57,.08)">' + E(t.status || (t.done ? 'Done' : 'Open')) + '</span>';
      return '<div class="n task' + (t.done ? ' done' : '') + '" data-w116tn="' + key + '"><span class="tg' + (kids.length ? '' : ' leaf') + '" data-w116ttg="' + key + '">' + (isClosed ? '+' : '\u2212') + '</span><span class="lb" data-w116topen="' + t.id + '">' + E(t.title) + '</span>' + (kids.length ? '<span class="ct"><b>' + kids.filter(function (k) { return k.done; }).length + '</b>/' + kids.length + '</span>' : '') + (t.owner ? '<span class="ct">' + E(t.owner.split(' ')[0]) + '</span>' : '') + (t.due_date ? '<span class="ct">' + E(String(t.due_date).slice(5)) + '</span>' : '') + stp + '<span class="add" data-w116tsq="' + t.id + '" title="Add a subtask">+ subtask</span></div>' +
        (kids.length ? '<div class="ch' + (isClosed ? ' closed' : '') + '">' + kids.map(function (k) { return taskNode(k, depth + 1); }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
    }
    function taskList(list) {
      /* top-level tasks in this bucket: no parent, or a parent outside the bucket */
      var ids = {}; list.forEach(function (t) { ids[t.id] = 1; });
      var roots = list.filter(function (t) { return !t.parent_task_id || !ids[t.parent_task_id]; });
      /* wave287: projects replace workstreams, so the tree no longer sorts a project's tasks into workstreams */
      return roots.map(function (t) { return taskNode(t, 0); }).join('');
    }
    function projNode(p) {
      var subs = projs.filter(function (x) { return Number(x.parent_id) === Number(p.id); });
      var mine = visible.filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id || gmap[t.id]) === Number(p.id); });
      var total = mine.length + subs.reduce(function (a, s) { return a + visible.filter(function (t) { return Number(t.project_id || gmap[t.id]) === Number(s.id); }).length; }, 0);
      var key = 'p' + p.id; var isClosed = !!closed[key];
      return '<div class="n project" data-w116tn="' + key + '"><span class="tg" data-w116ttg="' + key + '">' + (isClosed ? '+' : '\u2212') + '</span><span class="lb" data-w116tgo="#project/' + p.id + '">' + E(p.name) + '</span><span class="ct"><b>' + total + '</b> task' + (total === 1 ? '' : 's') + (subs.length ? ' \u00b7 ' + subs.length + ' sub-project' + (subs.length === 1 ? '' : 's') : '') + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="ch' + (isClosed ? ' closed' : '') + '">' + subs.map(projNode).join('') + taskList(mine) + '</div>';
    }
    var tops = projs.filter(function (p) { return !p.parent_id || !projs.some(function (x) { return Number(x.id) === Number(p.parent_id); }); });
    /* wave287: a task with no project sits in its team's General project (window.w116GeneralOf) */
    var gen0 = window.w116GeneralOf ? window.w116GeneralOf(d) : null, gmap = {};
    if (gen0 && projs.some(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === Number(gen0.id); })) {
      visible.forEach(function (t) { if (!t.project_id) gmap[t.id] = gen0.id; });
      projs.sort(function (a, b) { return (Number(b.id) === Number(gen0.id) ? 1 : 0) - (Number(a.id) === Number(gen0.id) ? 1 : 0); });
    }
    var general = visible.filter(function (t) { var pid = t.project_id || gmap[t.id]; return !pid || !projs.some(function (p) { return Number(p.id) === Number(pid); }); });
    return '<div class="w116tree"><div class="n team"><span class="tg leaf"></span><span class="lb" data-w116tgo="#team/' + E(d) + '">' + E(label(d)) + '</span><span class="ct"><b>' + visible.length + '</b> open task' + (visible.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' \u00b7 ' + tops.length + ' project' + (tops.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span><span class="add" data-w116tdone="1">' + (showDone ? 'hide done' : 'show done') + '</span></div><div class="ch">' +
      tops.map(projNode).join('') +
      (general.length ? '<div class="n project" data-w116tn="pg"><span class="tg" data-w116ttg="pg">' + (closed.pg ? '+' : '\u2212') + '</span><span class="lb">No project</span><span class="ct"><b>' + general.length + '</b></span></div><div class="ch' + (closed.pg ? ' closed' : '') + '">' + taskList(general) + '</div>' : '') +
      '</div></div>';
  }
  function showTree() {
    var host = document.querySelector('.w116b'); if (!host) return;
    var d = scopeDept(); if (!d) return;
    var slot = host.querySelector('.w116tree'); if (slot) slot.remove();
    var content = host.querySelector('.w116groups, .w116kan, .w116cal, .w116list');
    var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = treeHtml(d);
    var tb = host.querySelector('.w116tb');
    if (tb) tb.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box.firstChild); else host.appendChild(box.firstChild);
    host.querySelectorAll(':scope > *:not(.w116tb):not(.w116tree)').forEach(function (el) { el.setAttribute('data-w116thid', '1'); el.style.display = 'none'; });
    host.querySelectorAll('.w116tb .w116seg a[data-act="view"]').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.remove('on'); });
    var tv = host.querySelector('[data-w116tview]'); if (tv) tv.classList.add('on');
    K.tree.on = d;
  }
  function hideTree() {
    var host = document.querySelector('.w116b'); if (!host) return;
    var slot = host.querySelector('.w116tree'); if (slot) slot.remove();
    host.querySelectorAll('[data-w116thid]').forEach(function (el) { el.style.display = ''; el.removeAttribute('data-w116thid'); });
    var tv = host.querySelector('[data-w116tview]'); if (tv) tv.classList.remove('on');
    K.tree.on = null;
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  var mo = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
    var dr = false, board = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) { var t = muts[i].target; if (!t || !t.closest) continue; if (t.id === 'w116dr' || t.closest('#w116dr')) { if (!t.closest('.w116tsec') && !t.closest('.w116tcrumb')) dr = true; } else if (t.closest('.w116b') || (t.querySelector && t.querySelector('.w116b'))) board = true; }
    if (dr) { clearTimeout(K.pend); K.pend = setTimeout(inject, 60); }
    if (board) { clearTimeout(K.pend2); K.pend2 = setTimeout(function () { ensureTreeButton(); decorateRows(); if (K.tree.on && !document.querySelector('.w116tree')) showTree(); }, 80); }
  });
  mo.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });

  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var go = t.closest('[data-w116tgo]'); if (go) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); try { if (window.w116Board) window.w116Board.closeDrawer(); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } location.hash = go.getAttribute('data-w116tgo'); return; }
    var op = t.closest('[data-w116topen]'); if (op) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); openTask(op.getAttribute('data-w116topen')); return; }
    var task = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null;
    var f = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tf]');
    if (f && task) {
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      var ed = document.getElementById('w116ted'); if (!ed) return;
      ed.focus();
      var act = f.getAttribute('data-w116tf');
      if (act === 'bold') document.execCommand('bold');
      if (act === 'italic') document.execCommand('italic');
      if (act === 'ul') document.execCommand('insertUnorderedList');
      if (act === 'ol') document.execCommand('insertOrderedList');
      if (act === 'link') { var sel = String(window.getSelection() || ''); var url = prompt('Link address (https://...)', /^https?:/.test(sel) ? sel : 'https://'); if (url && /^https?:\/\//.test(url.trim())) { if (!sel) document.execCommand('insertText', false, url.trim()); document.execCommand('createLink', false, url.trim()); } }
      syncTb(); queueSave(task);
      return;
    }
    var lnk = t.closest('#w116ted a'); if (lnk && (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); window.open(lnk.getAttribute('href'), '_blank', 'noopener'); return; }
    var sd = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tsd]'); if (sd) { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSub(sd.getAttribute('data-w116tsd')); return; }
    var sa = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tsadd]'); if (sa && task) { e.stopPropagation(); var inp = document.getElementById('w116tsa'); if (inp) { await addSub(task, inp.value); } return; }
    var pick = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tpick]'); if (pick && task) { e.stopPropagation(); var fi = pick.querySelector('input[type=file]'); if (fi) fi.click(); return; }
    var la = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tlinkadd]'); if (la && task) { e.stopPropagation(); var li = document.getElementById('w116tlink'); if (li) { await addLink(task, li.value); } return; }
    var fx = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tfx]'); if (fx && task) { e.stopPropagation(); removeFile(task, fx.getAttribute('data-w116tfx')); return; }
    var fo = t.closest('#w116dr [data-w116tfo]'); if (fo && task) { e.stopPropagation(); openFile(task, fo.getAttribute('data-w116tfo')); return; }
    /* tree */
    var tv = t.closest('[data-w116tview]'); if (tv) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (K.tree.on) hideTree(); else showTree(); return; }
    var otherView = t.closest('.w116tb a[data-act="view"]'); if (otherView && K.tree.on) { hideTree(); return; }
    var tg = t.closest('[data-w116ttg]'); if (tg) { e.stopPropagation(); var key = tg.getAttribute('data-w116ttg'); K.tree.closed = K.tree.closed || {}; K.tree.closed[key] = !K.tree.closed[key]; var ch = tg.parentNode.nextElementSibling; if (ch && ch.classList.contains('ch')) ch.classList.toggle('closed', !!K.tree.closed[key]); tg.textContent = K.tree.closed[key] ? '+' : '\u2212'; return; }
    var dn = t.closest('[data-w116tdone]'); if (dn) { e.stopPropagation(); K.tree.showDone = !K.tree.showDone; showTree(); return; }
    var sq = t.closest('[data-w116tsq]'); if (sq) { e.stopPropagation(); var par = taskById(sq.getAttribute('data-w116tsq')); if (!par) return; var title = prompt('Subtask of "' + par.title.slice(0, 60) + '":'); if (title) { await addSub(par, title); showTree(); } return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t) return;
    if (t.id === 'w116tsa' && e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); var task = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (task) addSub(task, t.value); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116tlink' && e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); var task3 = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (task3) addLink(task3, t.value); return; }
    if (t.id === 'w116ted') {
      var task2 = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (!task2) return;
      var mod = e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey;
      if (mod && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'b') { e.preventDefault(); document.execCommand('bold'); syncTb(); queueSave(task2); return; }
      if (mod && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'i') { e.preventDefault(); document.execCommand('italic'); syncTb(); queueSave(task2); return; }
      if (mod && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') { e.preventDefault(); var url = prompt('Link address (https://...)', 'https://'); if (url && /^https?:\/\//.test(url.trim())) { if (!String(window.getSelection() || '')) document.execCommand('insertText', false, url.trim()); document.execCommand('createLink', false, url.trim()); queueSave(task2); } return; }
      if (mod && e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); saveDesc(task2, edText(t)); t.blur(); return; }
      if (e.key === ' ') {
        /* "- " or "1. " at the start of a line becomes a list, like a notes app */
        var s2 = window.getSelection(); if (!s2 || !s2.rangeCount || !s2.isCollapsed) return;
        var r = s2.getRangeAt(0); var node = r.startContainer; if (node.nodeType !== 3) return;
        var before = node.nodeValue.slice(0, r.startOffset);
        if (node.parentNode && node.parentNode.closest && node.parentNode.closest('li')) return;
        var mUl = /^(?:-|\*)$/.test(before), mOl = /^1[.)]$/.test(before);
        if (mUl || mOl) { e.preventDefault(); node.nodeValue = node.nodeValue.slice(r.startOffset); var r2 = document.createRange(); r2.setStart(node, 0); r2.collapse(true); s2.removeAllRanges(); s2.addRange(r2); document.execCommand(mUl ? 'insertUnorderedList' : 'insertOrderedList'); queueSave(task2); }
      }
    }
  });
  function syncTb() { try { var tb = document.querySelector('#w116dr .w116ttb'); if (!tb) return; ['bold', 'italic'].forEach(function (c) { var b = tb.querySelector('[data-w116tf="' + c + '"]'); if (b) b.classList.toggle('on', document.queryCommandState(c)); }); var ul = tb.querySelector('[data-w116tf="ul"]'), ol = tb.querySelector('[data-w116tf="ol"]'); if (ul) ul.classList.toggle('on', document.queryCommandState('insertUnorderedList')); if (ol) ol.classList.toggle('on', document.queryCommandState('insertOrderedList')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function queueSave(task) { clearTimeout(K.saveT); var st = document.getElementById('w116tdst'); if (st) st.textContent = ''; K.saveT = setTimeout(function () { var ed = document.getElementById('w116ted'); if (ed && K.task === task.id) saveDesc(task, edText(ed)); }, 1200); }
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w116ted') { var task = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (task) queueSave(task); } });
  document.addEventListener('selectionchange', function () { if (document.activeElement && document.activeElement.id === 'w116ted') syncTb(); });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w116ted') { clearTimeout(K.saveT); var task = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (task) saveDesc(task, edText(t)); } });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-w116tfile')) { var task = taskById(t.getAttribute('data-w116tfile')); if (task) upload(task, t.files); t.value = ''; } });
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) { var z = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w116dr .drop'); if (z) { e.preventDefault(); z.classList.add('over'); } });
  document.addEventListener('dragleave', function (e) { var z = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w116dr .drop'); if (z) z.classList.remove('over'); });
  document.addEventListener('drop', function (e) { var z = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w116dr'); if (!z) return; e.preventDefault(); var d = z.querySelector('.drop'); if (d) d.classList.remove('over'); var task = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (!task || !e.dataTransfer) return; if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files.length) { upload(task, e.dataTransfer.files); return; } var dropped = ''; try { dropped = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/uri-list') || e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain') || ''; } catch (e2) { dropped = ''; } var dm = dropped.match(DRIVE_RE); if (dm) addLink(task, dm[0]); });
  document.addEventListener('paste', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w116ted')) return; var task = K.task ? taskById(K.task) : null; if (!task) return;
    var items = (e.clipboardData && e.clipboardData.items) || []; var files = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) { if (items[i].kind === 'file') { var f = items[i].getAsFile(); if (f) files.push(f); } }
    if (files.length) { e.preventDefault(); upload(task, files); return; }
    e.preventDefault();
    var html = e.clipboardData.getData('text/html'); var plain = e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain') || '';
    if (html) document.execCommand('insertHTML', false, cleanPaste(html));
    else document.execCommand('insertHTML', false, md(plain));
    queueSave(task);
  });
  window.w116tDebug = function () { return { task: K.task, files: K.files, tree: K.tree }; };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v116w Home: "Got it" on a mention works (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "In Home, when I look at mentions and click Got it, it doesn't
   go away." Two causes: wave65's handler looked for its own row class
   (.w65men) while wave74 renders the Home list as .w74men, so the row
   never changed on screen; and the mentions update policy only let a
   person mark their own mentions, so an admin reading everyone's saw
   the click do nothing (policy widened to admins in wa116w). This
   handler runs first, saves, and hides the mention whichever list it
   sits in; a failed save says so instead of pretending.
   ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  if (window.__wa116w) return; window.__wa116w = true;
  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-w65got]');
    if (!a) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    var id = Number(a.getAttribute('data-w65got'));
    var row = a.closest('.w74men') || a.closest('.w65men') || a.parentNode;
    a.textContent = 'Saving...';
    try {
      var r = await db.from('mentions').update({ seen: true }).eq('id', id).select('id');
      if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
      if (!r || !r.data || !r.data.length) throw new Error('not allowed');
      if (row) { row.classList.add('seen'); var nb = row.querySelector('.w74new, .w65new'); if (nb) nb.remove(); row.style.transition = 'opacity .3s, max-height .3s'; row.style.opacity = '.45'; }
      a.remove();
      try { toast('Marked as seen'); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
    } catch (e3) {
      a.textContent = 'Got it';
      try { toast('Could not mark it as seen: ' + (e3.message || e3)); } catch (e4) { /* fine */ }
    }
  }, true);
})();
