  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' ? isStaff() : true; } catch (e) { return true; } }
  function curEvent() { var ev = null; try { (WA_VOL_EVENTS || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ev = e; }); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } return ev; }
  function detail() { try { return (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ? WA_VOL_DETAIL : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function roleLabel(r) { try { return waVolRoleLabel(r); } catch (e) { return r; } }
  function daysTo(iso) { try { return waVolDaysTo(iso); } catch (e) { return Math.round((new Date(iso + 'T12:00:00').getTime() - Date.now()) / 86400000); } }
  function niceDate(iso, long) { try { return waVolDate(iso, long); } catch (e) { return iso; } }
  function statusClass(s) { s = lower(s); if (s.indexOf('confirm') === 0) return 's-confirmed'; if (s.indexOf('wait') === 0 || s === 'tbd') return 's-waiting'; if (s === 'unavailable') return 's-unavailable'; if (s === 'cancelled') return 's-cancelled'; return ''; }
  function tmplById(id) { try { return (WA_VOL_TMPL || []).filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0] || null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function phaseIndex(p) { try { return WA_VOL_PHASES.map(function (x) { return x[0]; }).indexOf(p || 'anytime'); } catch (e) { return 9; } }

  /* ---------- the event page ---------- */
  function ring(pct, label, ok) {
    var r = 30, c = 2 * Math.PI * r; var dash = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct)) / 100 * c;
    return '<div class="ring"><div class="wrap"><svg viewBox="0 0 74 74"><circle class="bg" cx="37" cy="37" r="' + r + '"></circle><circle class="fg' + (ok ? ' ok' : '') + '" cx="37" cy="37" r="' + r + '" stroke-dasharray="' + dash.toFixed(1) + ' ' + c.toFixed(1) + '"></circle></svg><span class="v">' + Math.round(pct) + '%</span></div>' + label + '</div>';
  }
  function mastHtml(ev, D) {
    var steps = D.steps || []; var done = steps.filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length; var pctS = steps.length ? done / steps.length * 100 : 0;
    var roster = D.roster || []; var conf = roster.filter(function (r) { return lower(r.status).indexOf('confirm') === 0; }).length;
    var target = 0; Object.keys(TARGET).forEach(function (k) { target += TARGET[k]; });
    var pctR = target ? Math.min(100, conf / target * 100) : 0;
    var d = daysTo(ev.event_date);
    var cd = ev.status === 'cancelled' ? 'Cancelled' : ev.status === 'completed' ? 'Done' : d === 0 ? 'Today' : d === 1 ? 'Tomorrow' : d > 1 ? '<b>' + d + '</b> days to go' : Math.abs(d) + ' days ago';
    var meals = ev.planned_meals ? '<span>' + E(ev.planned_meals) + ' meals planned</span>' : '';
    return '<div class="mast"><div><div class="k">' + E(ev.code || 'Serving event') + (ev.season ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(ev.season) : '') + '</div><h3>' + E(niceDate(ev.event_date, true)) + '</h3>' +
      '<div class="meta"><span class="st ' + E(ev.status || '') + '">' + E(ev.status || 'planned') + '</span>' + (ev.title ? '<span>' + E(ev.title) + '</span>' : '') + (ev.location ? '<span>' + E(ev.location) + '</span>' : '') + meals + '<span class="cd">' + cd + '</span></div></div>' +
      '<div class="rings">' + ring(pctS, 'Checklist', pctS >= 100) + ring(pctR, 'Roster', conf >= target) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="acts" id="w119acts"><span style="font-size:12.5px;color:rgba(255,250,242,.8)">' + done + ' of ' + steps.length + ' steps \u00b7 ' + conf + ' confirmed of ' + target + ' places' + (roster.length ? ' (' + roster.length + ' on the list)' : '') + '</span><span style="flex:1"></span></div></div>';
  }
  function nextHtml(D) {
    var rows = (D.steps || []).filter(function (s) { return !s.done; }).map(function (s) { var t = tmplById(s.template_id) || {}; return { s: s, t: t }; }).filter(function (r) { return r.t.id && r.t.active !== false; });
    rows.sort(function (a, b) { var ad = a.s.due_date || '9999', bd = b.s.due_date || '9999'; if (ad !== bd) return ad < bd ? -1 : 1; return phaseIndex(a.t.phase) - phaseIndex(b.t.phase) || (a.t.sort || 0) - (b.t.sort || 0); });
    var top = rows.slice(0, 3); if (!top.length) return '';
    var today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    return '<div class="next"><div class="lbl">What\u2019s next</div>' + top.map(function (r) {
      var over = r.s.due_date && r.s.due_date < today;
      return '<div class="n"><span class="tk" data-w119tick="' + r.s.id + '" title="Mark done"></span><div><b>' + E(r.t.name || 'Step') + '</b><span>' + E(r.s.owner || r.t.default_owner || 'no owner yet') + (r.s.due_date ? ' \u00b7 ' + (over ? '<i>due ' + E(r.s.due_date) + '</i>' : 'due ' + E(r.s.due_date)) : '') + (r.t.section ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(r.t.section) : '') + '</span></div></div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  /* wave200: the same counts as The team's line (wave191: its targets, partners counted, cancelled and declined left out) */
  function counts(D) {
    try { if (window.w191 && typeof window.w191.counts === 'function') return window.w191.counts(D); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    var by = {}, conf = 0; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { var s = lower(r.status); if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'unavailable') return; by[r.role || 'other'] = (by[r.role || 'other'] || 0) + 1; if (s.indexOf('confirm') === 0) conf++; });
    return { by: by, T: TARGET, conf: conf };
  }
  function isGone(r) { var s = lower(r.status); return s === 'cancelled' || s === 'unavailable' || r.confirm_status === 'declined'; }
  function allRoles(D) { var roles = ROLE_ORDER.slice(); ((D && D.roster) || []).forEach(function (r) { if (r.role && roles.indexOf(r.role) < 0) roles.push(r.role); }); return roles; }
  function one(role) { return roleLabel(role).replace(/s$/, ''); }
  function boardHtml(D) {
    var roster = D.roster || [], c = counts(D);
    return '<div class="board">' + allRoles(D).map(function (role) {
      var list = roster.filter(function (r) { return (r.role || 'other') === role; });
      list.sort(function (a, b) { return (isGone(a) ? 1 : 0) - (isGone(b) ? 1 : 0); });
      var on = c.by[role] || 0, target = (c.T || {})[role] || 0;
      var conf = list.filter(function (r) { return lower(r.status).indexOf('confirm') === 0 || r.confirm_status === 'confirmed'; }).length;
      var cls = target ? (on >= target ? 'full' : 'short') : '';
      return '<div class="rc" data-w119role="' + E(role) + '"><div class="h"><b>' + E(roleLabel(role)) + '</b><span class="n ' + cls + '" title="' + E(on + ' on the list' + (target ? ' of ' + target + ' needed' : '') + ', ' + conf + ' confirmed') + '">' + on + (target ? '/' + target : '') + '</span>' +
        (staff() ? '<button class="add" data-w119bench="' + E(role) + '" title="Add people as ' + E(one(role).toLowerCase()) + '">+ Add</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
        (list.length ? '<div class="sub">' + (conf ? conf + ' confirmed' : 'none confirmed yet') + (target && on < target ? ' · <em>' + (target - on) + ' more needed</em>' : '') + '</div>' + list.map(function (r) { return cardHtml(r, D); }).join('') : '') +
        (!list.length ? (staff() ? '<button class="empty" data-w119bench="' + E(role) + '">' + (target ? 'Need ' + target : 'Nobody yet') + '<span>+ Add people, or drag someone here</span></button>' : '<div class="empty">' + (target ? 'Need ' + target : 'Nobody yet') + '</div>') : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  /* the statuses keep their stored values; the menu shows them in plain words */
  var SHORT = { 'Confirmed': 'Confirmed', 'Waiting For Response': 'Waiting', 'TBD': 'To ask', 'Unavailable': 'Can’t make it', 'Cancelled': 'Cancelled' };
  function cardHtml(r, D) {
    var d = r.detail || {}; var phone = d.phone ? String(d.phone) : '';
    var opts = '<option value=""' + (r.status ? '' : ' selected') + '>No status</option>' + (WA_VOL_STATUSES || []).map(function (s) { return '<option value="' + E(s) + '"' + (r.status === s ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(SHORT[s] || s) + '</option>'; }).join('') + (r.status && (WA_VOL_STATUSES || []).indexOf(r.status) < 0 ? '<option selected>' + E(r.status) + '</option>' : '');
    var roles = allRoles(D || detail());
    var ropts = '<option value="" selected disabled>Move</option>' + roles.filter(function (rr) { return rr !== r.role; }).map(function (rr) { return '<option value="' + E(rr) + '">To ' + E(one(rr).toLowerCase()) + '</option>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="pc ' + statusClass(r.status) + (isGone(r) ? ' gone' : '') + '" draggable="' + (staff() ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w119card="' + r.id + '"><b>' + E(r.person_name || 'Unnamed') + '</b>' + (staff() ? '<button class="x" data-w119rm="' + r.id + '" title="Take ' + E(r.person_name || 'them') + ' off this serving" aria-label="Take ' + E(r.person_name || 'them') + ' off this serving">&#10005;</button>' : '') +
      '<div class="sel2"><select data-vlrstat="' + r.id + '"' + (staff() ? '' : ' disabled') + ' aria-label="Status of ' + E(r.person_name || 'this person') + '" title="Status">' + opts + '</select>' +
      (staff() ? '<select class="rolesel" data-w119mv="' + r.id + '" aria-label="Move ' + E(r.person_name || 'them') + ' to another role" title="Move to another role">' + ropts + '</select>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (phone || d.email ? '<span class="c" title="' + E([phone, d.email].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')) + '">' + (phone ? '<a href="tel:' + E(phone.replace(/[^\d+]/g, '')) + '">' + E(phone) + '</a>' : '') + (phone && d.email ? ' · ' : '') + (d.email ? E(d.email) : '') + '</span>' : '') + (r.notes ? '<span class="c" title="' + E(r.notes) + '">' + E(r.notes) + '</span>' : '') +
      (r.person_id ? '' : '<span class="flag" title="This name is not matched to anyone in Volunteers yet">No volunteer record</span>') + '</div>';
  }

  /* ---------- wave200: Add people ---------- */
  function members() { try { return (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' && DB_MEMBERS) || []; } catch (e) { return []; } }
  function teamLabel(d) { try { return (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.short && WA_TEAMS.short[d]) || (window.WA_TEAMS && WA_TEAMS.labels && WA_TEAMS.labels[d]) || d || ''; } catch (e) { return d || ''; } }
  /* every volunteer, then every team member who has no volunteer record yet (matched by email, else by name) */
  function pool() {
    var people = ((typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_PEOPLE : []) || []);
    var team = members().filter(function (m) { return m && m.name && m.active !== false; });
    var tByEmail = {}, tByName = {}; team.forEach(function (m) { if (m.email) tByEmail[lower(m.email)] = m; tByName[lower(m.name)] = m; });
    var seenEmail = {}, seenName = {};
    var out = people.map(function (p) {
      var ems = (p.emails || []).map(lower); ems.forEach(function (e) { seenEmail[e] = 1; }); seenName[lower(p.full_name)] = 1;
      var tm = null; ems.forEach(function (e) { if (!tm && tByEmail[e]) tm = tByEmail[e]; });
      if (!tm && tByName[lower(p.full_name)] && !ems.length) tm = tByName[lower(p.full_name)];
      return { key: String(p.id), p: p, name: p.full_name || '(no name)', roles: p.roles || [], phone: (p.phones || [])[0] || '', email: (p.emails || [])[0] || '', tags: p.tags || [], team: tm };
    });
    team.forEach(function (m) {
      var em = lower(m.email);
      if (em && seenEmail[em]) return;
      if (!em && seenName[lower(m.name)]) return;
      if (em && seenName[lower(m.name)] && people.some(function (p) { return lower(p.full_name) === lower(m.name) && !(p.emails || []).length; })) return;
      out.push({ key: 'm' + m.id, m: m, name: m.name, roles: [], phone: '', email: m.email || '', tags: [], team: m });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function onMap(D) {
    var byPid = {}, byName = {};
    ((D && D.roster) || []).forEach(function (r) { if (isGone(r)) return; if (r.person_id) byPid[String(r.person_id)] = r; byName[lower(r.person_name)] = r; });
    return { pid: byPid, name: byName };
  }
  function digits(s) { return String(s || '').replace(/\D/g, ''); }
  function matches(it, q) {
    if (!q) return true;
    var hay = [it.name, it.email, (it.tags || []).join(' '), (it.roles || []).map(roleLabel).join(' '), it.team ? 'team ' + (it.team.role || '') + ' ' + teamLabel(it.team.department) : ''].join(' ').toLowerCase();
    return q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).every(function (w) { var dw = digits(w); return hay.indexOf(w) >= 0 || (dw.length >= 3 && digits(it.phone).indexOf(dw) >= 0); });
  }
  function addList(role, D) {
    var q = lower(V.q), on = onMap(D), all = pool();
    var list = all.filter(function (it) {
      if (V.addTab === 'role' && (it.roles || []).indexOf(role) < 0) return false;
      if (V.addTab === 'team' && !it.team) return false;
      return matches(it, q);
    });
    list.forEach(function (it) { it.on = (it.p && on.pid[String(it.p.id)]) || (!it.p && on.name[lower(it.name)]) || null; it.fits = (it.roles || []).indexOf(role) >= 0; });
    list.sort(function (a, b) { return (a.on ? 1 : 0) - (b.on ? 1 : 0) || (b.fits ? 1 : 0) - (a.fits ? 1 : 0) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    return { list: list, all: all };
  }
  function initials(n) { var w = String(n || '?').replace(/[()]/g, '').trim().split(/\s+/); return ((w[0] || '?').charAt(0) + (w.length > 1 ? w[w.length - 1].charAt(0) : '')).toUpperCase(); }
  function addRowHtml(it, role) {
    var meta = [];
    if (it.team) meta.push('<span class="tm">Team' + (it.team.department ? ' · ' + E(teamLabel(it.team.department)) : '') + '</span>');
    var rl = (it.roles || []).map(function (r) { return '<span class="rl' + (r === role ? ' fit' : '') + '">' + E(one(r)) + '</span>'; }).join('');
    if (rl) meta.push(rl); else if (!it.team) meta.push('<span class="rl none">No role yet</span>');
    var contact = [it.phone, it.email].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    return '<div class="ar' + (it.on ? ' on' : '') + '" role="listitem"><span class="av">' + E(initials(it.name)) + '</span><div class="who"><b>' + E(it.name) + '</b><span class="meta">' + meta.join('') + '</span>' + (contact ? '<span class="ct">' + E(contact) + '</span>' : '') + (!it.p && it.m ? '<span class="ct">No volunteer record yet: adding makes one</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (it.on ? '<span class="onl">On this serving: ' + E(one(it.on.role)) + '</span>' : '<button class="go" data-w200add="' + E(it.key) + '" aria-label="Add ' + E(it.name) + ' as ' + E(one(role).toLowerCase()) + '">Add</button>') + '</div>';
  }
  function addHtml() {
    var role = V.benchRole, D = detail(); if (!role || !D) return '';
    var ev = curEvent(), c = counts(D), people = (typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_PEOPLE : []) || [];
    var res = addList(role, D), shown = res.list.slice(0, V.addShow), firstFree = res.list.filter(function (it) { return !it.on; })[0];
    var chips = allRoles(D).map(function (r) { var n = c.by[r] || 0, t = (c.T || {})[r] || 0; return '<button class="rchip' + (r === role ? ' on' : '') + (t && n < t ? ' short' : '') + '" data-w200role="' + E(r) + '" aria-pressed="' + (r === role) + '">' + E(roleLabel(r)) + ' <i>' + n + (t ? '/' + t : '') + '</i></button>'; }).join('');
    var tabs = [['all', 'Everyone'], ['role', roleLabel(role)], ['team', 'Team members']].map(function (x) { return '<button class="tab' + (V.addTab === x[0] ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w200tab="' + x[0] + '" aria-pressed="' + (V.addTab === x[0]) + '">' + E(x[1]) + '</button>'; }).join('');
    return '<div class="w200add" id="w200add" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="w200addh"><div class="pan">' +
      '<div class="ph"><div><h3 id="w200addh">Add people</h3><p>' + E(ev ? niceDate(ev.event_date, true) : '') + ' · ' + (c.on != null ? c.on + ' on the list' : '') + '</p></div><button class="xx" data-w119benchclose="1" aria-label="Close">&#10005;</button></div>' +
      '<div class="lb">Add as</div><div class="rchips">' + chips + '</div>' +
      '<div class="srch"><input type="search" id="w119bq" placeholder="Search a name, phone, email, tag or role" value="' + E(V.q) + '" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Search volunteers and team members"></div>' +
      '<div class="tabs">' + tabs + '<span class="cnt">' + (people.length ? res.list.length + ' ' + (res.list.length === 1 ? 'person' : 'people') : '') + '</span></div>' +
      (V.added.length ? '<div class="just"><b>Added:</b> ' + V.added.map(function (a) { return '<span>' + E(a.name) + ' <i>' + E(one(a.role).toLowerCase()) + '</i>' + (a.rid ? ' <button data-w200undo="' + E(a.rid) + '">Undo</button>' : '') + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      (V.addBusy ? '<div class="busy">' + E(V.addBusy) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="al" role="list">' + (!people.length ? '<div class="none">Loading the volunteers…</div>' : shown.length ? shown.map(function (it) { return addRowHtml(it, role); }).join('') : '<div class="none">Nobody matches ' + (V.q ? '“' + E(V.q) + '”' : 'this') + (V.addTab !== 'all' ? '. <button class="lnk" data-w200tab="all">Search everyone</button>' : '') + '</div>') +
        (res.list.length > shown.length ? '<button class="more" data-w200more="1">Show ' + Math.min(60, res.list.length - shown.length) + ' more of ' + (res.list.length - shown.length) + '</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="pf"><span>' + (firstFree && V.q ? 'Enter adds ' + E(firstFree.name) : 'Add keeps this open for the next person.') + '</span><button class="done" data-w119benchclose="1">Done</button></div>' +
      '</div></div>';
  }
  function paintAdd(keepFocus) {
    var root = document.getElementById('w119'); var cur = document.getElementById('w200add');
    if (!V.benchRole || !root) { if (cur) cur.remove(); document.documentElement.classList.remove('w200lock'); return; }
    var html = addHtml(); var q = document.getElementById('w119bq'); var hadFocus = q && document.activeElement === q; var pos = q ? q.selectionStart : null;
    var scroll = cur && cur.querySelector('.al') ? cur.querySelector('.al').scrollTop : 0;
    if (cur) cur.outerHTML = html; else root.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
    document.documentElement.classList.add('w200lock');
    var al = document.querySelector('#w200add .al'); if (al && keepFocus) al.scrollTop = scroll;
    var rc = document.querySelector('#w200add .rchips'), onc = rc && rc.querySelector('.rchip.on'); if (rc && onc && rc.scrollWidth > rc.clientWidth) rc.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, onc.offsetLeft - rc.offsetLeft - (rc.clientWidth - onc.offsetWidth) / 2);
    var q2 = document.getElementById('w119bq'); if (q2 && (hadFocus || !cur || keepFocus === 'q')) { q2.focus(); try { var n = pos == null ? q2.value.length : pos; q2.setSelectionRange(n, n); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  }
  function openAdd(role) { V.benchRole = role; V.q = ''; V.addTab = 'all'; V.addShow = 60; V.added = []; V.addBusy = ''; paintAdd(); if (typeof waVolLoadPeople === 'function') waVolLoadPeople().then(function () { paintAdd('q'); }); }
  function closeAdd() { V.benchRole = null; V.q = ''; V.added = []; paintAdd(); repaintBoard(); }
  function benchHtml(role) { return ''; }
  function layout() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
    if (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW === 'undefined' || WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'event') return;
    var card = sec.querySelector('.card'); if (!card || card.querySelector('#w119')) return;
    var ev = curEvent(); var D = detail(); if (!ev || !D) return;
    var back = card.querySelector('.vl-back'); if (!back) return;
    /* the pieces the older waves painted */
    var kids = Array.prototype.slice.call(card.children);
    var sub = card.querySelector('.vl-sub'); var start = kids.indexOf(sub) + 1;
    var tail = kids.slice(start);
    var hero = card.querySelector('.vl-hero'); var sum = card.querySelector('.w31sum');
    var h3s = Array.prototype.filter.call(card.querySelectorAll('h3.block'), function (h) { return true; });
    var rosterH = h3s.filter(function (h) { return /who is on/i.test(h.textContent); })[0];
    var extrasH = h3s.filter(function (h) { return /added for this event/i.test(h.textContent); })[0];
    var checkH = h3s.filter(function (h) { return /checklist/i.test(h.textContent); })[0];
    function between(a, b) { var out = []; var n = a ? a.nextElementSibling : null; while (n && n !== b) { out.push(n); n = n.nextElementSibling; } return out; }
    var rosterEls = rosterH ? [rosterH].concat(between(rosterH, extrasH || checkH)) : [];
    var extrasEls = extrasH ? [extrasH].concat(between(extrasH, checkH)) : [];
    var checkEls = checkH ? [checkH].concat(between(checkH, null)) : [];
    /* the top line: back link and the date control (wave28) stay above the masthead */
    var topEls = []; var n = back; while (n && n !== sum && n !== hero && !(n.tagName === 'H3')) { topEls.push(n); n = n.nextElementSibling; }
    var root = document.createElement('div'); root.id = 'w119';
    root.innerHTML = '<div class="top"></div>' + mastHtml(ev, D) + nextHtml(D) + '<div class="grid"><div class="col" id="w119left"><div class="cardy"><h4>Checklist</h4><div id="w119check"></div></div></div><div class="col" id="w119right"><div class="cardy"><h4>Roster <span style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:11.5px;font-weight:500;color:var(--wa-muted)">drag between roles \u00b7 + adds from the volunteer list</span></h4>' + boardHtml(D) + '</div><div id="w119rail"></div></div></div>';
    card.insertBefore(root, back);
    var top = root.querySelector('.top'); topEls.forEach(function (el) { top.appendChild(el); });
    var check = root.querySelector('#w119check'); checkEls.forEach(function (el) { if (el.tagName === 'H3') el.classList.add('w119hide'); check.appendChild(el); });
    var rail = root.querySelector('#w119rail');
    if (sum) { sum.classList.add('cardy'); rail.appendChild(sum); }
    if (extrasEls.length) { var ex = document.createElement('div'); ex.className = 'cardy'; ex.innerHTML = '<h4>From the meetings</h4>'; extrasEls.forEach(function (el) { if (el.tagName === 'H3') el.classList.add('w119hide'); ex.appendChild(el); }); rail.appendChild(ex); }
    rosterEls.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.add('w119hide'); rail.appendChild(el); });
    if (hero) { hero.classList.add('w119hide'); rail.appendChild(hero); }
    /* anything else that older waves appended after the checklist stays visible in the rail */
    Array.prototype.slice.call(card.children).forEach(function (el) { if (el !== root && el !== sub && !el.classList.contains('vl-sub') && el.tagName !== 'H2' && el.tagName !== 'P' && !el.classList.contains('vl-back')) { if (kids.indexOf(el) >= start) rail.appendChild(el); } });
    adopt();
    if (V.benchRole) paintAdd();
  }
  function repaintBoard() { var D = detail(); var b = document.querySelector('#w119 .board'); if (!b || !D) return; b.outerHTML = boardHtml(D); }
  function repaintMast() { var ev = curEvent(), D = detail(); var m = document.querySelector('#w119 .mast'); if (!m || !ev || !D) return; var acts = m.querySelector('#w119acts'); var keep = acts ? Array.prototype.slice.call(acts.querySelectorAll('button')) : []; m.outerHTML = mastHtml(ev, D); var acts2 = document.querySelector('#w119acts'); if (acts2) keep.forEach(function (b) { acts2.appendChild(b); }); var nx = document.querySelector('#w119 .next'); if (nx) nx.outerHTML = nextHtml(D); else { var m2 = document.querySelector('#w119 .mast'); if (m2) m2.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', nextHtml(D)); } }

  /* roster writes */
  async function moveCard(id, role) {
    var D = detail(); if (!D) return; var row = D.roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!row || row.role === role) return;
    var old = row.role; row.role = role; repaintBoard();
    var r = await db.from('event_roster').update({ role: role }).eq('id', Number(id));
    if (r && r.error) { row.role = old; repaintBoard(); toastS('Could not move: ' + r.error.message); return; }
    try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    toastS((row.person_name || 'Volunteer') + ' is now a ' + roleLabel(role).replace(/s$/, '').toLowerCase());
    try { logChange('event_roster', id, 'role', old, role); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    repaintMast();
  }
  async function addPerson(pid, role) {
    var D = detail(); var ev = curEvent(); if (!D || !ev) return;
    var key = String(pid), it = pool().filter(function (x) { return x.key === key; })[0];
    if (!it) { var p0 = ((typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_PEOPLE : []) || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === key; })[0]; if (p0) it = { key: key, p: p0, name: p0.full_name }; }
    if (!it) return;
    var p = it.p;
    if (!p && it.m) {
      /* a team member with no volunteer record: one is made, linked to their CRM contact by email (wave176's one record) */
      V.addBusy = 'Making ' + it.name + '’s volunteer record…'; paintAdd(true);
      var mk = await db.from('volunteer_people').insert({ full_name: it.m.name, emails: it.m.email ? [it.m.email] : [], phones: [] }).select('*').single();
      V.addBusy = '';
      if (mk && mk.error) { paintAdd(true); toastS('Could not make a volunteer record for ' + it.name + ': ' + mk.error.message); return; }
      p = mk.data; try { WA_VOL_PEOPLE.push(p); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
      try { logChange('volunteer_people', p.id, 'created', null, it.name + ' (team member, added to a serving)'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    }
    var row = { event_id: ev.id, person_id: p.id, person_name: p.full_name, role: role, status: 'Waiting For Response', notes: null, detail: { phone: (p.phones || [])[0] || null, email: (p.emails || [])[0] || null }, sort: 999 };
    var r = await db.from('event_roster').insert(row).select('*').single();
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not add: ' + r.error.message); return; }
    D.roster.push(r.data || row);
    try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { logChange('event_roster', (r.data || {}).id || 'new', 'added', null, p.full_name + ' as ' + role); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    toastS(p.full_name + ' added as ' + one(role).toLowerCase());
    if (V.benchRole) { V.added.push({ rid: r.data && r.data.id, name: p.full_name, role: role }); V.q = ''; paintAdd('q'); }
    repaintBoard(); repaintMast(); repaintTeam();
  }
  /* The team's header and line (wave191) count again */
  function repaintTeam() { try { if (window.w191 && typeof window.w191.refresh === 'function') window.w191.refresh(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  async function undoAdd(rid) {
    var D = detail(); if (!D) return;
    var r = await db.from('event_roster').delete().eq('id', Number(rid));
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not undo: ' + r.error.message); return; }
    var row = D.roster.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(rid); })[0];
    D.roster = D.roster.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(rid); });
    V.added = V.added.filter(function (a) { return String(a.rid) !== String(rid); });
    try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { logChange('event_roster', rid, 'removed', row ? row.person_name : null, null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    toastS((row ? row.person_name : 'They') + ' taken off again');
    paintAdd(true); repaintBoard(); repaintMast(); repaintTeam();
  }
  async function removeCard(id) {
    var D = detail(); if (!D) return; var row = D.roster.filter(function (r) { return String(r.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!row) return;
    if (!confirm('Take ' + (row.person_name || 'this person') + ' off this event?')) return;
    var r = await db.from('event_roster').delete().eq('id', Number(id));
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not remove: ' + r.error.message); return; }
    D.roster = D.roster.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(id); });
    try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    try { logChange('event_roster', id, 'removed', row.person_name, null); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    repaintBoard(); repaintMast();
  }

  /* ---------- calendar ---------- */
  function monthGrid() {
    var evs = (typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []; if (!evs.length) return '';
    var today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (!V.calMonth) { var nxt = evs.filter(function (e) { return e.event_date >= today; })[0]; V.calMonth = (nxt ? nxt.event_date : today).slice(0, 7); }
    var y = Number(V.calMonth.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(V.calMonth.slice(5, 7)) - 1;
    var first = new Date(V.calMonth + '-01T12:00:00'); var startDow = (first.getDay() + 6) % 7; var days = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
    var cells = ''; var dows = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'].map(function (d) { return '<div class="dow">' + d + '</div>'; }).join('');
    var prevDays = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 0)).getUTCDate();
    for (var i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
      var dn = i - startDow + 1; var out = dn < 1 || dn > days; var dateNum = dn < 1 ? prevDays + dn : dn > days ? dn - days : dn;
      var iso = out ? '' : y + '-' + String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(dn).padStart(2, '0');
      var mine = out ? [] : evs.filter(function (e) { return e.event_date === iso; });
      cells += '<div class="d' + (out ? ' out' : '') + (iso === today ? ' today' : '') + '">' + dateNum + mine.map(function (e) {
        var past = e.event_date < today; var stepsOk = e.steps_total && e.steps_done >= e.steps_total; var rosterOk = e.roster_count && e.roster_confirmed >= Math.min(e.roster_count, 8);
        return '<span class="ev ' + E(e.status || '') + (past ? ' past' : '') + '" data-vlev="' + e.id + '" title="' + E(e.code || '') + ' \u00b7 ' + (e.steps_done || 0) + '/' + (e.steps_total || 0) + ' steps \u00b7 ' + (e.roster_confirmed || 0) + '/' + (e.roster_count || 0) + ' confirmed">' + (past || e.status === 'cancelled' ? '' : '<i class="' + (stepsOk ? 'ok' : (e.steps_total && e.steps_done / e.steps_total < 0.3 && daysTo(e.event_date) < 7 ? 'bad' : '')) + '"></i><i class="' + (rosterOk ? 'ok' : (daysTo(e.event_date) < 7 && (e.roster_confirmed || 0) < 3 ? 'bad' : '')) + '"></i>') + E(e.title || e.code || 'Event') + '</span>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
      if (i >= 34 && (i - startDow + 2) > days) break;
    }
    var label = first.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
    return '<div id="w119cal"><div class="ch"><b>' + E(label) + '</b><button class="btn" data-w119cal="-1">&lsaquo;</button><button class="btn" data-w119cal="today">Today</button><button class="btn" data-w119cal="0">Next event</button><button class="btn" data-w119cal="1">&rsaquo;</button></div><div class="g">' + dows + cells + '</div></div>';
  }
  function calKey() { var evs = (typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []; return String(V.calMonth) + '|' + evs.map(function (e) { return e.id + ':' + e.event_date + ':' + (e.status || '') + ':' + (e.steps_done || 0) + '/' + (e.steps_total || 0) + ':' + (e.roster_confirmed || 0) + '/' + (e.roster_count || 0); }).join(','); }
  function decorateCalendar() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('volunteering'); if (!sec) return;
    if (typeof WA_VOL_VIEW === 'undefined' || WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'calendar') return;
    var card = sec.querySelector('.card'); if (!card) return;
    var old = card.querySelector('#w119cal');
    var html = monthGrid(); var key = calKey();
    if (old && old.getAttribute('data-key') === key) { /* nothing changed: leave it alone */ }
    else {
      if (old) old.remove();
      var hero = card.querySelector('.vl-hero'); var anchor = hero || card.querySelector('h3.block') || null;
      var box = document.createElement('div'); box.innerHTML = html;
      if (box.firstChild) { box.firstChild.setAttribute('data-key', key); if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', box.firstChild); else card.appendChild(box.firstChild); }
    }
    var today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    card.querySelectorAll('.vl-ev[data-vlev]').forEach(function (el) {
      if (el.querySelector('.w119gaps')) return;
      var e = ((typeof WA_VOL_EVENTS !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENTS : []) || []).filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === el.getAttribute('data-vlev'); })[0]; if (!e || e.event_date < today || e.status === 'cancelled') return;
      var gaps = [];
      var unconf = (e.roster_count || 0) - (e.roster_confirmed || 0);
      if (!e.roster_count) gaps.push('<span>no roster yet</span>'); else if (unconf > 0) gaps.push('<span>' + unconf + ' to confirm</span>'); else gaps.push('<span class="ok">roster confirmed</span>');
      var left = (e.steps_total || 0) - (e.steps_done || 0);
      if (e.steps_total && left === 0) gaps.push('<span class="ok">checklist done</span>'); else if (left) gaps.push('<span' + (daysTo(e.event_date) < 7 && left > 10 ? '' : ' class="ok"') + '>' + left + ' steps left</span>');
      el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<div class="w119gaps">' + gaps.join('') + '</div>');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- wiring ---------- */
  if (typeof waVolRenderEvent === 'function') {
    var __ev119 = waVolRenderEvent;
    waVolRenderEvent = function () { var out = __ev119.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(layout, 140); return out; };
  }
  if (typeof waVolRenderCalendar === 'function') {
    var __cal119 = waVolRenderCalendar;
    waVolRenderCalendar = function () { var out = __cal119.apply(this, arguments); setTimeout(decorateCalendar, 40); return out; };
  }
  /* pieces other waves add after the layout ran (wave31's summary is async,
     wave106's cancel button can be late): move them where they belong */
  function adopt() {
    var root = document.getElementById('w119'); if (!root) return;
    var rail = root.querySelector('#w119rail'); var acts = root.querySelector('#w119acts');
    var sum = root.querySelector('.w31sum'); if (sum && rail && !sum.classList.contains('cardy')) { sum.classList.add('cardy'); rail.insertBefore(sum, rail.firstChild); }
    var hero = root.querySelector('.vl-hero'); if (hero && acts) { Array.prototype.slice.call(hero.querySelectorAll('button')).forEach(function (b) { b.classList.add('btn'); if (b.id === 'w106can') b.classList.add('warn'); acts.appendChild(b); }); }
  }
  function own(m) {
    function is(n) { return n && n.nodeType === 1 && (n.id === 'w119' || n.id === 'w119cal' || (n.classList && n.classList.contains('w119gaps'))); }
    var i; for (i = 0; i < m.addedNodes.length; i++) if (is(m.addedNodes[i])) return true;
    for (i = 0; i < m.removedNodes.length; i++) if (is(m.removedNodes[i])) return true;
    return false;
  }
  try {
    new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      var outside = false;
      for (var i = 0; i < muts.length; i++) { var t = muts[i].target; if (t && t.closest && t.closest('#w119,#w119cal')) continue; if (own(muts[i])) continue; outside = true; break; }
      if (outside) V.outside = true;
      if (V.pend) return;
      V.pend = setTimeout(function () { V.pend = null; var o = V.outside; V.outside = false; try { adopt(); if (o) { layout(); decorateCalendar(); adopt(); } } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }, 160);
    }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
  } catch (e) { /* fine */ }

  document.addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var tk = t.closest('#w119 [data-w119tick]'); if (tk) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var sid = tk.getAttribute('data-w119tick'); var box = document.querySelector('#w119 input[data-vlchk="' + sid + '"]'); if (box) { box.click(); } else if (typeof waVolToggleStep === 'function') { await waVolToggleStep(sid, true, null); repaintMast(); } return; }
    var cal = t.closest('#w119cal [data-w119cal]'); if (cal) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var dv = cal.getAttribute('data-w119cal'); var d = Number(dv); if (dv === 'today') V.calMonth = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7); else if (d === 0) V.calMonth = null; else { var y = Number(V.calMonth.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(V.calMonth.slice(5, 7)) - 1 + d; if (m < 0) { m = 11; y--; } if (m > 11) { m = 0; y++; } V.calMonth = y + '-' + String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0'); } decorateCalendar(); return; }
    var bench = t.closest('#w119 [data-w119bench]'); if (bench) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); openAdd(bench.getAttribute('data-w119bench')); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w119 [data-w119benchclose]') || (t.id === 'w200add')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); closeAdd(); return; }
    var ap = t.closest('#w119 [data-w200add]'); if (ap) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (ap.disabled) return; ap.disabled = true; ap.textContent = 'Adding…'; await addPerson(ap.getAttribute('data-w200add'), V.benchRole); return; }
    var ar = t.closest('#w119 [data-w200role]'); if (ar) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); V.benchRole = ar.getAttribute('data-w200role'); V.addShow = 60; paintAdd(true); return; }
    var at = t.closest('#w119 [data-w200tab]'); if (at) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); V.addTab = at.getAttribute('data-w200tab'); V.addShow = 60; paintAdd(true); return; }
    if (t.closest('#w119 [data-w200more]')) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); V.addShow += 60; paintAdd(true); return; }
    var un = t.closest('#w119 [data-w200undo]'); if (un) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); un.disabled = true; await undoAdd(un.getAttribute('data-w200undo')); return; }
    var addp = t.closest('#w119 [data-w119addp]'); if (addp) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); await addPerson(addp.getAttribute('data-w119addp'), addp.getAttribute('data-role')); return; }
    var rm = t.closest('#w119 [data-w119rm]'); if (rm) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); await removeCard(rm.getAttribute('data-w119rm')); return; }
    var mv = t.closest('#w119 [data-w119mvbtn]'); if (mv) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var cur = (mv.closest('.rc') || {}).getAttribute ? mv.closest('.rc').getAttribute('data-w119role') : ''; var roles = ROLE_ORDER.slice(); document.querySelectorAll('#w119 .rc[data-w119role]').forEach(function (c) { var rr = c.getAttribute('data-w119role'); if (roles.indexOf(rr) < 0) roles.push(rr); }); mv.outerHTML = '<select class="mvsel" data-w119mv="' + mv.getAttribute('data-w119mvbtn') + '"><option value="">Move to\u2026</option>' + roles.filter(function (rr) { return rr !== cur; }).map(function (rr) { return '<option value="' + E(rr) + '">' + E(roleLabel(rr)) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'; var sel = document.querySelector('#w119 select[data-w119mv]'); if (sel) sel.focus(); return; }
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('input', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (t && t.id === 'w119bq') { V.q = t.value; V.addShow = 60; paintAdd('q'); } });
  /* wave200: Enter adds the first match; Escape closes Add people */
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (!V.benchRole || !document.getElementById('w200add')) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); closeAdd(); return; }
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && e.target && e.target.id === 'w119bq') {
      e.preventDefault(); var D = detail(); if (!D || !V.q.trim()) return;
      var first = addList(V.benchRole, D).list.filter(function (it) { return !it.on; })[0]; if (first) addPerson(first.key, V.benchRole);
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('change', function (e) { var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.getAttribute || !t.closest || !t.closest('#w119')) return; if (t.getAttribute('data-w119mv')) { if (t.value) moveCard(t.getAttribute('data-w119mv'), t.value); else repaintBoard(); return; } if (t.getAttribute('data-vlchk')) { setTimeout(repaintMast, 450); return; } if (t.getAttribute('data-vlrstat')) { setTimeout(function () { var card = t.closest('.pc'); if (card) { card.className = 'pc ' + statusClass(t.value); } repaintMast(); var b = document.querySelector('#w119 .board'); if (b) { var D = detail(); if (D) { /* header counts */ b.outerHTML = boardHtml(D); } } }, 250); } });
  /* drag and drop: roster cards between roles, bench people into a role */
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) { var c = e.target && e.target.closest && (e.target.closest('#w119 [data-w119card]') || e.target.closest('#w119 [data-w119pid]')); if (!c) return; V.drag = c.getAttribute('data-w119card') ? { kind: 'card', id: c.getAttribute('data-w119card') } : { kind: 'person', id: c.getAttribute('data-w119pid') }; c.classList.add('dragging'); try { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', 'w119'); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ } });
  document.addEventListener('dragend', function (e) { var c = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.dragging'); if (c) c.classList.remove('dragging'); document.querySelectorAll('#w119 .rc.over').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('over'); }); });
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) { if (!V.drag) return; var col = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w119 .rc'); if (col) { e.preventDefault(); document.querySelectorAll('#w119 .rc.over').forEach(function (x) { if (x !== col) x.classList.remove('over'); }); col.classList.add('over'); } });
  document.addEventListener('dragleave', function (e) { var col = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w119 .rc'); if (col && !col.contains(e.relatedTarget)) col.classList.remove('over'); });
  document.addEventListener('drop', async function (e) { if (!V.drag) return; var col = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#w119 .rc'); if (!col) return; e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); col.classList.remove('over'); var role = col.getAttribute('data-w119role'); var d = V.drag; V.drag = null; if (d.kind === 'card') await moveCard(d.id, role); else await addPerson(d.id, role); }, true);
  window.w119Debug = function () { return { view: typeof WA_VOL_VIEW !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_VIEW : null, event: typeof WA_VOL_EVENT !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_EVENT : null, laid: !!document.getElementById('w119'), bench: V.benchRole }; };
})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v119b MTL Volunteering, cut 2: the Volunteers page, planning the
   roster, the recap (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   - Volunteers: cards instead of the table. Initials, roles, tags,
     tap-to-call and text, email, the reliability line from the roster
     history (volunteer_history()), badges (reliable, new, quiet),
     sort by reliability, recency or name, the review flags, and
     duplicates (same phone, email or name) with a one-click merge
     (volunteer_merge(), moves the roster rows, unions the contact
     details, deletes the copy). Search and the role chips stay.
   - Event page: "Plan the roster" ranks who to ask for every role that
     is short (history score, then Jev's odds that each person confirms,
     through the router task decide) and adds the ticked ones as Waiting
     For Response. "Recap" drafts the event recap with the router (task
     draft) and posts it to a Slack channel through checkin-post v2
     (channels, say); #operations-mtl by default.
   ===================================================================== */

    '.w119brecap select{padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid rgba(16,63,57,.2);border-radius:10px;font-size:12.5px;font-family:' + BODY + ';}' +

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function roleLabel(r) { try { return waVolRoleLabel(r); } catch (e) { return r; } }
  function lower(s) { return String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function today() { return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function daysSince(iso) { if (!iso) return 9999; return Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').getTime()) / 86400000); }
  function nice(iso) { try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: daysSince(iso) > 300 ? 'numeric' : undefined }); } catch (e) { return iso; } }
  function initials(n) { var p = String(n || '?').trim().split(/\s+/); return ((p[0] || '')[0] || '') + ((p[1] || '')[0] || ''); }
  function hue(n) { var h = 0; for (var i = 0; i < n.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + n.charCodeAt(i)) % 360; return h; }
  function avatar(p) { var n = String(p.full_name || '?'); var h = hue(n); return '<span class="av" style="background:hsl(' + h + ',38%,38%)">' + E(initials(n).toUpperCase()) + '</span>'; }
  function digits(s) { return String(s || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10); }
  function normName(s) { return lower(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }
  function people() { return (typeof WA_VOL_PEOPLE !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_PEOPLE : []) || []; }

  /* ---------- history ---------- */
  async function loadHist(force) {
    if (!force && S.hist && Date.now() - S.histAt < 300000) return S.hist;
    try { var r = await db.rpc('volunteer_history'); var m = {}; ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (h) { m[h.person_id] = h; }); S.hist = m; S.histAt = Date.now(); }
    catch (e) { S.hist = S.hist || {}; }
    return S.hist;
  }
  function hist(p) { return (S.hist && S.hist[p.id]) || { events: 0, confirmed: 0, unavailable: 0, waiting: 0, last_event: null, last_confirmed: null, roles: [] }; }
  function rate(h) { return (h.confirmed + 1) / (h.events + 2); }
  function badges(p) {
    var h = hist(p); var out = [];
    if (!h.events) out.push('<span class="bd new">New</span>');
    else if (h.events >= 3 && h.confirmed / h.events >= 0.8) out.push('<span class="bd ok">Reliable</span>');
    if (h.events && daysSince(h.last_event) > 120) out.push('<span class="bd quiet">Quiet ' + Math.round(daysSince(h.last_event) / 30) + ' mo</span>');
    if (h.events >= 2 && h.unavailable / h.events >= 0.5) out.push('<span class="bd warn">Often unavailable</span>');
    if (p.needs_review) out.push('<span class="bd flag" title="' + E(p.review_note || 'needs review') + '">Review</span>');
    return out.join('');
  }
  function reliability(p) {
    var h = hist(p); if (!h.events) return '<div class="rl">No events yet' + (p.last_applied ? ' \u00b7 applied ' + E(nice(p.last_applied)) : '') + '</div>';
    var pct = Math.round(h.confirmed / h.events * 100); var cls = pct >= 75 ? '' : pct >= 40 ? 'mid' : 'low';
    return '<div class="rl">' + h.confirmed + ' of ' + h.events + ' events confirmed' + (h.last_confirmed ? ' \u00b7 last ' + E(nice(h.last_confirmed)) : h.last_event ? ' \u00b7 last asked ' + E(nice(h.last_event)) : '') + '<div class="b"><i class="' + cls + '" style="width:' + pct + '%"></i></div></div>';
  }
  /* ---------- duplicates ---------- */
  function dupGroups() {
    var ps = people(); var parent = {}; function find(x) { while (parent[x] !== x) { parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]; x = parent[x]; } return x; } function union(a, b) { a = find(a); b = find(b); if (a !== b) parent[b] = a; }
    ps.forEach(function (p) { parent[p.id] = p.id; });
    var byPhone = {}, byEmail = {}, byName = {};
    ps.forEach(function (p) {
      (p.phones || []).forEach(function (ph) { var d = digits(ph); if (d.length >= 10) { if (byPhone[d]) union(byPhone[d], p.id); else byPhone[d] = p.id; } });
      (p.emails || []).forEach(function (em) { var e = lower(em); if (e && e.indexOf('@') > 0) { if (byEmail[e]) union(byEmail[e], p.id); else byEmail[e] = p.id; } });
      var n = normName(p.full_name); if (n && n.indexOf(' ') > 0) { if (byName[n]) union(byName[n], p.id); else byName[n] = p.id; }
    });
    var groups = {}; ps.forEach(function (p) { var r = find(p.id); (groups[r] = groups[r] || []).push(p); });
    var out = {}; Object.keys(groups).forEach(function (k) { if (groups[k].length > 1) groups[k].forEach(function (p) { out[p.id] = groups[k].filter(function (x) { return x.id !== p.id; }); }); });
    return out;
  }
  function why(a, b) {
    var r = [];
    var pa = (a.phones || []).map(digits).filter(function (d) { return d.length >= 10; }), pb = (b.phones || []).map(digits);
    if (pa.some(function (d) { return pb.indexOf(d) >= 0; })) r.push('same phone');
    var ea = (a.emails || []).map(lower), eb = (b.emails || []).map(lower);
    if (ea.some(function (e) { return e && eb.indexOf(e) >= 0; })) r.push('same email');
    if (normName(a.full_name) && normName(a.full_name) === normName(b.full_name)) r.push('same name');
    return r.join(', ') || 'looks alike';
  }

  /* ---------- the Volunteers page ---------- */
  function searchText(p) { return lower([p.full_name, (p.phones || []).join(' '), (p.emails || []).join(' '), (p.roles || []).join(' '), (p.tags || []).join(' '), p.organization, p.notes].join(' ')); }
  function card(p, dups) {
    var h = hist(p); var roles = (p.roles || []); var tags = (p.tags || []).filter(function (t) { return roles.indexOf(t) < 0; });
    var ph = (p.phones || [])[0] || ''; var em = (p.emails || [])[0] || '';
    var d = dups[p.id] || [];
    return '<div class="pc' + (d.length ? ' dup' : '') + '" data-p26="' + p.id + '" data-search="' + E(searchText(p)) + '">' +
      '<div class="hd">' + avatar(p) + '<div class="nm"><b>' + E(p.full_name || 'Unnamed') + '</b><span>' + E([p.organization, p.car_type ? 'car: ' + p.car_type : '', h.roles && h.roles.length ? 'served as ' + h.roles.map(roleLabel).join(', ').toLowerCase() : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') || (roles.length ? roles.map(roleLabel).join(', ') : 'no role yet')) + '</span></div>' + (staff() ? '<a class="ed" data-w26e="' + p.id + '">edit</a>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div>' + badges(p) + '</div>' +
      ((roles.length || tags.length) ? '<div class="chips">' + roles.map(function (r) { return '<span>' + E(roleLabel(r)) + '</span>'; }).join('') + tags.slice(0, 4).map(function (t) { return '<span class="t">' + E(t) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="ct">' + (ph ? '<a href="tel:' + E(ph.replace(/[^\d+]/g, '')) + '" title="Call">' + E(ph) + '</a><a href="sms:' + E(ph.replace(/[^\d+]/g, '')) + '" title="Text">Text</a>' : '') + (em ? '<a href="mailto:' + E(em) + '" title="Email">' + E(em) + '</a>' : '') + (!ph && !em ? '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11.5px">no contact details</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      reliability(p) +
      (p.notes ? '<div class="note" title="' + E(p.notes) + '">' + E(p.notes) + '</div>' : '') +
      (d.length ? '<div class="dupbox">Looks like the same person as ' + d.map(function (x) { return '<b>' + E(x.full_name) + '</b> (' + E(why(p, x)) + ')'; }).join(', ') + (staff() ? '<br>' + d.map(function (x) { return '<button class="btn mini" data-w119bmerge="' + p.id + '" data-drop="' + x.id + '">Merge ' + E((x.full_name || '').split(' ')[0]) + ' into this</button>'; }).join('') : '') + '</div>' : '') +
      '</div>';
  }
  function render() {
    var ps = people(); var dups = dupGroups();
    var counts = {}; ps.forEach(function (p) { var set = {}; (p.roles || []).forEach(function (r) { set[r] = 1; }); (p.tags || []).forEach(function (r) { set[r] = 1; }); Object.keys(set).forEach(function (r) { counts[r] = (counts[r] || 0) + 1; }); });
    var filter = typeof WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER !== 'undefined' ? WA_VOL_ROLEFILTER : '';
    var chips = '<span class="fchip' + (filter ? '' : ' on') + '" data-vlrole="">Everyone (' + ps.length + ')</span>' + Object.keys(counts).sort().map(function (r) { return '<span class="fchip' + (filter === r ? ' on' : '') + '" data-vlrole="' + E(r) + '">' + E(roleLabel(r)) + ' (' + counts[r] + ')</span>'; }).join('');
    var list = ps.filter(function (p) { if (S.dupOnly && !dups[p.id]) return false; if (!filter) return true; return (p.roles || []).indexOf(filter) >= 0 || (p.tags || []).indexOf(filter) >= 0; });
    list.sort(function (a, b) {
      if (S.sort === 'name') return String(a.full_name || '').localeCompare(String(b.full_name || ''));
      var ha = hist(a), hb = hist(b);
      if (S.sort === 'recent') return String(hb.last_event || '').localeCompare(String(ha.last_event || '')) || String(a.full_name || '').localeCompare(String(b.full_name || ''));
      var sa = rate(ha) + Math.min(ha.events, 8) * 0.02, sb = rate(hb) + Math.min(hb.events, 8) * 0.02;
      return sb - sa || String(a.full_name || '').localeCompare(String(b.full_name || ''));
    });
    var active90 = ps.filter(function (p) { return daysSince(hist(p).last_event) <= 90; }).length;
    var withH = ps.filter(function (p) { return hist(p).events > 0; }); var ev = 0, cf = 0; withH.forEach(function (p) { ev += hist(p).events; cf += hist(p).confirmed; });
    var dupN = Object.keys(dups).length; var flagged = ps.filter(function (p) { return p.needs_review; }).length;
    var html = '<div id="w119b">' +
      '<div class="bar"><input type="search" id="w26q" placeholder="Search name, phone, email, role or tag" value="' + E(S.q) + '"><select data-w119bsort="1"><option value="reliable"' + (S.sort === 'reliable' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Most reliable first</option><option value="recent"' + (S.sort === 'recent' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Most recent first</option><option value="name"' + (S.sort === 'name' ? ' selected' : '') + '>By name</option></select>' +
      (dupN ? '<span class="fchip' + (S.dupOnly ? ' on' : '') + '" data-w119bdup="1">Possible duplicates (' + dupN + ')</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="filters" style="margin-bottom:12px">' + chips + '</div>' +
      '<div class="stats"><div><b>' + ps.length + '</b><span>volunteers</span></div><div><b>' + active90 + '</b><span>active, 90 days</span></div><div><b>' + (ev ? Math.round(cf / ev * 100) : 0) + '%</b><span>confirm rate</span></div><div><b>' + withH.length + '</b><span>have served</span></div>' + (flagged ? '<div><b style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + flagged + '</b><span>need a review</span></div>' : '') + '</div>' +
      (list.length ? '<div class="grid">' + list.map(function (p) { return card(p, dups); }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="empty">Nobody matches.</div>') + '</div>';
    try { waVolPaint(html); } catch (e) { return; }
    applySearch();
    var q = document.getElementById('w26q'); if (q) q.addEventListener('input', function () { S.q = this.value; try { WA26_Q = this.value; } catch (e) { /* fine */ } applySearch(); });
  }
  function applySearch() {
    var q = lower(S.q); var qd = q.replace(/\D/g, '');
    document.querySelectorAll('#w119b .pc[data-search]').forEach(function (c) { var hay = c.getAttribute('data-search') || ''; c.style.display = (!q || hay.indexOf(q) >= 0 || (qd.length >= 3 && hay.replace(/\D/g, '').indexOf(qd) >= 0)) ? '' : 'none'; });
  }
  var __people26 = typeof waVolRenderPeople === 'function' ? waVolRenderPeople : null;
  waVolRenderPeople = function () {
    try { S.q = typeof WA26_Q !== 'undefined' ? (WA26_Q || '') : S.q; } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    if (!S.hist) { render(); loadHist(false).then(render); }
    else render();
  };
  async function merge(keep, drop) {
    var ps = people(); var k = ps.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(keep); })[0], d = ps.filter(function (p) { return String(p.id) === String(drop); })[0];
    if (!k || !d) return;
    if (!confirm('Merge ' + (d.full_name || 'this record') + ' into ' + (k.full_name || 'this one') + '? Their event history and contact details move over and the copy is deleted.')) return;
    var r = await db.rpc('volunteer_merge', { p_keep: Number(keep), p_drop: Number(drop) });
    if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not merge: ' + r.error.message); return; }
    toastS('Merged ' + (d.full_name || '') + ' into ' + (k.full_name || '') + ((r.data && r.data.moved) ? ' (' + r.data.moved + ' roster rows moved)' : ''));
    try { WA_VOL_PEOPLE = []; await waVolLoadPeople(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    await loadHist(true); render();
  }

  /* ---------- plan the roster ---------- */
  function ev() { var e = null; try { (WA_VOL_EVENTS || []).forEach(function (x) { if (x.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) e = x; }); } catch (x) { /* fine */ } return e; }
  function det() { try { return (WA_VOL_DETAIL && WA_VOL_DETAIL.id === WA_VOL_EVENT) ? WA_VOL_DETAIL : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function score(p, role) {
    var h = hist(p); var r = rate(h);
    var rec = h.last_confirmed ? Math.max(0, 1 - daysSince(h.last_confirmed) / 365) : (h.last_event ? Math.max(0, 0.5 - daysSince(h.last_event) / 365) : 0);
    var fit = (p.roles || []).indexOf(role) >= 0 || (h.roles || []).indexOf(role) >= 0 ? 1 : (p.tags || []).indexOf(role) >= 0 ? 0.7 : 0.2;
    var s = 0.55 * r + 0.25 * rec + 0.2 * fit;
    if (h.events >= 2 && h.unavailable / h.events >= 0.5) s -= 0.15;
    var why = [];
    if (h.events) why.push(h.confirmed + ' of ' + h.events + ' confirmed'); else why.push('never asked');
    if (h.last_confirmed) why.push('last served ' + nice(h.last_confirmed));
    if (fit === 1) why.push(roleLabel(role).toLowerCase() + ' on file'); else if (fit === 0.7) why.push('tagged ' + role);
    if (p.needs_review) why.push('record needs a review');
    return { s: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, s)), why: why.join(' \u00b7 ') };
  }
  /* wave235 (Steel, 14/10/26): a Community lead comes from our pool of past community leads first, then from servers who
     have been to 2 or more servings (or people with the role on file and 2 or more servings); nobody else is suggested */
  function leadTier(p) {
    var h = hist(p), served = h.roles || [], onFile = p.roles || [];
    if (served.indexOf('community_debrief') >= 0 && h.confirmed >= 1) return 0;
    if (h.confirmed >= 2 && (served.indexOf('server') >= 0 || onFile.indexOf('server') >= 0 || onFile.indexOf('community_debrief') >= 0)) return 1;
    return -1;
  }
  var LEAD_NOTE = 'Past community leads first, then servers with 2 or more servings. They get the Community lead briefing on their page and in their text.';
  function planRows() {
    var D = det(); if (!D) return [];
    var on = {}; (D.roster || []).forEach(function (r) { if (r.person_id) on[r.person_id] = 1; });
    var out = []; var taken = {};
    ROLE_ORDER.forEach(function (role) {
      var have = (D.roster || []).filter(function (r) { return (r.role || '') === role && lower(r.status).indexOf('unavailable') < 0 && lower(r.status).indexOf('cancel') < 0; }).length;
      var TT = (typeof window.WA191TARGETS === 'function' && window.WA191TARGETS(D.id, TARGET)) || TARGET;   /* wave191o: a partner's people count toward the roles they cover */
      var need = (TT[role] || 0) - have; if (need <= 0) return;
      var cands = people().filter(function (p) { return !on[p.id]; }).map(function (p) { var sc = score(p, role); return { p: p, s: sc.s, why: sc.why, jev: null, pick: false }; });
      if (role === 'community_debrief') {
        cands = cands.map(function (c) { c.tier = leadTier(c.p); c.why = (c.tier === 0 ? 'led the community before' : c.tier === 1 ? hist(c.p).confirmed + ' servings' : '') + (c.why ? ' \u00b7 ' + c.why : ''); return c; }).filter(function (c) { return c.tier >= 0; });
        cands.sort(function (a, b) { return a.tier - b.tier || b.s - a.s; });
        out.push({ role: role, need: need, have: have, cands: cands.slice(0, 8), note: LEAD_NOTE });
        return;
      }
      cands.sort(function (a, b) { return b.s - a.s; });
      var fit = cands.filter(function (c) { return c.s >= 0.35; }).slice(0, 8);
      out.push({ role: role, need: need, have: have, cands: fit });
    });
    assignPicks(out);
    return out;
  }
  /* a person is pre-ticked for one role only: the first short role they top */
  function assignPicks(rows) {
    var taken = {};
    rows.forEach(function (r) { r.cands.forEach(function (c) { c.pick = false; }); });
    /* wave235: the Community lead is picked first, so a past lead is not used up as a server */
    rows.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return (b.role === 'community_debrief') - (a.role === 'community_debrief'); }).forEach(function (r) { var n = 0; r.cands.forEach(function (c) { if (n < r.need && !taken[c.p.id]) { c.pick = true; taken[c.p.id] = r.role; n++; } }); });
  }
  function planHtml() {
    var rows = S.plan || [];
    if (!rows.length) return '<p class="read">Every role has enough people (counting those still to confirm). Nothing to plan.</p>';
    return '<div class="w119bplan">' + rows.map(function (r) {
      return '<h4>' + E(roleLabel(r.role)) + ' <small>' + r.have + ' on the list, need ' + r.need + ' more</small></h4>' + (r.note ? '<p class="read" style="font-size:12.5px;margin:0 0 6px">' + E(r.note) + '</p>' : '') + (r.cands.length ? r.cands.map(function (c, i) {
        var final = c.jev != null ? 0.5 * c.s + 0.5 * c.jev : c.s;
        return '<label class="cand"><input type="checkbox" data-w119bpick="' + c.p.id + '" data-role="' + E(r.role) + '"' + (c.pick ? ' checked' : '') + '><div><b>' + E(c.p.full_name) + '</b><span>' + E(c.why) + '</span></div><div class="sc">' + Math.round(final * 100) + '<small>' + (c.jev != null ? '<span class="jev">Jev ' + Math.round(c.jev * 100) + '%</span>' : (S.jevBusy ? 'asking Jev\u2026' : 'history')) + '</small></div></label>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="read" style="font-size:12.5px">Nobody on file fits; add people on the Volunteers page or drop someone from another role.</p>');
    }).join('') + '</div>';
  }
  async function jevOdds(rows) {
    var e = ev(); if (!e) return;
    var jobs = []; rows.forEach(function (r) { r.cands.forEach(function (c) { jobs.push({ r: r, c: c }); }); });
    jobs = jobs.slice(0, 24); if (!jobs.length) return;
    S.jevBusy = true; repaintPlan();
    var i = 0;
    async function worker() {
      while (i < jobs.length) {
        var j = jobs[i++]; var h = hist(j.c.p);
        try {
          var res = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { headers: { 'x-wa-task': 'decide', 'x-wa-caller': 'volunteering' }, body: {
            state: { event: { date: e.event_date, code: e.code, venue: e.location, days_away: daysSince(e.event_date) * -1 }, role: roleLabel(j.r.role), volunteer: { name: j.c.p.full_name, events_asked: h.events, events_confirmed: h.confirmed, unavailable: h.unavailable, last_event: h.last_event, last_confirmed: h.last_confirmed, roles_on_file: (j.c.p.roles || []), roles_served: h.roles || [], notes: String(j.c.p.notes || '').slice(0, 200) }, guidance: 'A charity in Montreal asks volunteers to serve meals on a Sunday. People who confirmed most of the time and served recently tend to say yes again; people who were often unavailable or have gone quiet for months tend not to.' },
            questions: { confirm: { type: 'choice', instructions: 'If asked today for this role on this date, will this volunteer confirm and show up?', criteria: { yes: 'Confirms and shows up', no: 'Declines, goes quiet or drops out' } } } } });
          var a = res && res.data && res.data.answers && res.data.answers.confirm; var p = a && a.probabilities ? Number(a.probabilities.yes) : NaN;
          if (isFinite(p)) j.c.jev = p;
        } catch (e2) { /* history only */ }
      }
    }
    await Promise.all([worker(), worker(), worker(), worker()]);
    S.jevBusy = false;
    rows.forEach(function (r) { r.cands.sort(function (a, b) { if ((a.tier || 0) !== (b.tier || 0)) return (a.tier || 0) - (b.tier || 0); var fa = a.jev != null ? 0.5 * a.s + 0.5 * a.jev : a.s, fb = b.jev != null ? 0.5 * b.s + 0.5 * b.jev : b.s; return fb - fa; }); });
    if (!S.planTouched) assignPicks(rows);
    repaintPlan(!S.planTouched);
  }
  function repaintPlan(fresh) { var box = document.querySelector('#w119bplanbox'); if (!box) return; var picked = {}; if (!fresh) box.querySelectorAll('[data-w119bpick]').forEach(function (c) { picked[c.getAttribute('data-w119bpick') + '|' + c.getAttribute('data-role')] = c.checked; }); box.innerHTML = planHtml(); if (!fresh) box.querySelectorAll('[data-w119bpick]').forEach(function (c) { var k = c.getAttribute('data-w119bpick') + '|' + c.getAttribute('data-role'); if (picked.hasOwnProperty(k)) c.checked = picked[k]; }); }
  async function openPlan() {
    await loadHist(false);
    try { await waVolLoadPeople(); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
    S.plan = planRows(); S.planTouched = false;
    openModal('<h3>Plan the roster</h3><p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 6px">Who to ask for the roles that are short, ranked by how often they confirmed, how recently they served and whether the role is on file. Jev adds its odds that each person says yes. Tick who to add; they go on as Waiting For Response.</p><div id="w119bplanbox">' + planHtml() + '</div>' +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w119bplango">Add the ticked people</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w119bplanx">Close</button></div>');
    document.getElementById('w119bplanx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    document.getElementById('w119bplango').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var seen = {}; var picks = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#w119bplanbox [data-w119bpick]:checked')).map(function (c) { return { pid: c.getAttribute('data-w119bpick'), role: c.getAttribute('data-role') }; }).filter(function (x) { if (seen[x.pid]) return false; seen[x.pid] = 1; return true; });
      if (!picks.length) { toastS('Tick someone first'); return; }
      this.disabled = true; var e = ev(), D = det(); var n = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < picks.length; i++) {
        var p = people().filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === picks[i].pid; })[0]; if (!p || !e || !D) continue;
        var row = { event_id: e.id, person_id: p.id, person_name: p.full_name, role: picks[i].role, status: 'Waiting For Response', notes: null, detail: { phone: (p.phones || [])[0] || null, email: (p.emails || [])[0] || null }, sort: 999 };
        var r = await db.from('event_roster').insert(row).select('*').single();
        if (r && r.error) { toastS('Could not add ' + p.full_name + ': ' + r.error.message); continue; }
        D.roster.push(r.data || row); n++;
        try { logChange('event_roster', (r.data || {}).id || 'new', 'added', null, p.full_name + ' as ' + picks[i].role + ' (planned)'); } catch (e3) { /* fine */ }
      }
      try { WA_VOL_LOADED = false; } catch (e4) { /* fine */ }
      closeModal(); toastS('Added ' + n + ' ' + (n === 1 ? 'person' : 'people') + ' to ask');
      try { waVolRenderEvent(); } catch (e5) { /* fine */ }
    });
    jevOdds(S.plan);
  }

  /* ---------- the recap ---------- */
  function facts() {
    var e = ev(), D = det(); if (!e || !D) return null;
    var roster = D.roster || []; var byRole = {};
    roster.forEach(function (r) { var k = r.role || 'other'; byRole[k] = byRole[k] || { c: 0, n: 0, names: [] }; byRole[k].n++; if (lower(r.status).indexOf('confirm') === 0) { byRole[k].c++; byRole[k].names.push(r.person_name); } });
    var steps = D.steps || []; var done = steps.filter(function (s) { return s.done; }).length;
    var extras = (D.extras || []).map(function (x) { return (x.done ? '[done] ' : '[open] ') + x.title; });
    return { code: e.code, date: e.event_date, title: e.title, venue: e.location, season: e.season, status: e.status, meals: e.planned_meals, notes: e.notes, roster: Object.keys(byRole).map(function (k) { return roleLabel(k) + ': ' + byRole[k].c + ' confirmed of ' + byRole[k].n + (byRole[k].names.length ? ' (' + byRole[k].names.join(', ') + ')' : ''); }), steps: done + ' of ' + steps.length + ' checklist steps done', extras: extras };
  }
  async function draftRecap() {
    var f = facts(); if (!f) return '';
    var res = await db.functions.invoke('llm', { headers: { 'x-wa-task': 'draft', 'x-wa-caller': 'volunteering' }, body: {
      max_tokens: 700, temperature: 0.4,
      system: 'You write short Slack recaps for the Montreal serving events of Welfare Avenue, a charity. Plain, warm, specific, first names only, no em dashes, no hashtags, no exclamation marks. Slack formatting only: *bold* for the first line, then 4 to 7 lines starting with \u2022. Say the date and venue, meals, who served by role (names), what got done, what is still open, and one thank-you line. Never invent numbers or names beyond the facts.',
      messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'FACTS\n' + JSON.stringify(f, null, 1) }],
      tools: [{ name: 'recap', description: 'The recap text for Slack.', input_schema: { type: 'object', properties: { text: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['text'] } }], tool_choice: { type: 'tool', name: 'recap' } } });
    if (res && res.error) throw res.error;
    var tu = ((res && res.data && res.data.content) || []).filter(function (c) { return c.type === 'tool_use'; })[0];
    var t = tu && tu.input && tu.input.text ? String(tu.input.text) : '';
    if (!t) throw new Error('the model gave nothing back');
    return t.replace(/\u2014/g, ',');
  }
  function fallbackRecap() {
    var f = facts(); if (!f) return '';
    return '*' + (f.title || 'Serving event') + ' \u00b7 ' + f.date + (f.venue ? ' \u00b7 ' + f.venue : '') + '*\n' + (f.meals ? '\u2022 ' + f.meals + ' meals planned\n' : '') + f.roster.map(function (r) { return '\u2022 ' + r; }).join('\n') + '\n\u2022 ' + f.steps + (f.extras.length ? '\n\u2022 From the meetings: ' + f.extras.join('; ') : '') + '\n\u2022 Thank you to everyone who showed up.';
  }
  async function openRecap() {
    var e = ev(); if (!e) return;
    var chan = ''; try { chan = localStorage.getItem('wa119ch') || 'operations-mtl'; } catch (x) { chan = 'operations-mtl'; }
    openModal('<div class="w119brecap"><h3>Serving recap</h3><p class="read" style="margin:4px 0 8px">Drafted from the roster and the checklist. Edit it, then post it to Slack or copy it.</p><textarea id="w119brt" placeholder="Drafting\u2026"></textarea>' +
      '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:10px 0 0"><label style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted)">Channel</label><select id="w119bch"><option value="' + E(chan) + '">#' + E(chan) + '</option></select><button class="btn ghost mini" id="w119bredo">Draft again</button></div>' +
      '<div class="m-actions"><button class="btn" id="w119bpost" disabled>Post to Slack</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w119bcopy">Copy</button> <button class="btn ghost" id="w119bx">Close</button></div></div>');
    document.getElementById('w119bx').addEventListener('click', function () { closeModal(); });
    var ta = document.getElementById('w119brt');
    async function fill() { ta.value = ''; ta.placeholder = 'Drafting\u2026'; try { ta.value = await draftRecap(); } catch (err) { ta.value = fallbackRecap(); toastS('Used the plain version: ' + (err.message || err)); } var b = document.getElementById('w119bpost'); if (b) b.disabled = false; }
    fill();
    document.getElementById('w119bredo').addEventListener('click', fill);
    document.getElementById('w119bcopy').addEventListener('click', function () { try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(ta.value); toastS('Copied'); } catch (x) { ta.select(); toastS('Select and copy'); } });
    document.getElementById('w119bpost').addEventListener('click', async function () {
      var text = ta.value.trim(); if (!text) return; this.disabled = true;
      var ch = (document.getElementById('w119bch') || {}).value || chan;
      try { localStorage.setItem('wa119ch', ch); } catch (x) { /* fine */ }
      try { var r = await db.functions.invoke('checkin-post', { body: { action: 'say', channel: ch, text: text } }); if (r.error) throw r.error; if (r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error); toastS('Posted to #' + ch); closeModal(); }
      catch (err) { this.disabled = false; toastS('Could not post: ' + (err.message || err)); }
    });
    /* the channel list, lazily */
    (async function () { try { if (!S.channels) { var r = await db.functions.invoke('checkin-post', { body: { action: 'channels' } }); S.channels = (r.data && r.data.channels) || []; } var sel = document.getElementById('w119bch'); if (sel && S.channels.length) { sel.innerHTML = S.channels.map(function (c) { return '<option value="' + E(c.name) + '"' + (c.name === chan ? ' selected' : '') + '>#' + E(c.name) + (c.member ? '' : ' (bot not in it yet)') + '</option>'; }).join(''); } } catch (x) { /* keep the default */ } })();
  }
  /* buttons in the masthead of wave119 */
  function decorate() {
    if (!staff()) return;
    var acts = document.getElementById('w119acts'); if (!acts || acts.querySelector('[data-w119bplan]')) return;
    var e = ev(); if (!e) return;
    var b1 = document.createElement('button'); b1.className = 'btn'; b1.setAttribute('data-w119bplan', '1'); b1.textContent = 'Plan the roster';
    var b2 = document.createElement('button'); b2.className = 'btn'; b2.setAttribute('data-w119brecap', '1'); b2.textContent = 'Recap';
    var spacer = acts.querySelector('span[style*="flex:1"]');
    if (spacer && spacer.nextSibling) { acts.insertBefore(b2, spacer.nextSibling); acts.insertBefore(b1, spacer.nextSibling); } else { acts.appendChild(b1); acts.appendChild(b2); }
  }
  /* a throttle, not a debounce: the hub repaints small things constantly, so a reset timer would never fire */
