  function board() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function allowed() { return staff() || board(); }
  /* wave222: the menu entry shows only when the person's access list carries the page (wa40Enforce hides it otherwise; before, placeTab showed it again on every change of the menu and the two took turns) */
  function tabOk222() { try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf('socialstats') >= 0; } catch (e) { return true; } }
  function num(v) { v = Number(v); if (!isFinite(v)) return '\u2013'; var a = Math.abs(v); if (a >= 1e6) return (v / 1e6).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M'; if (a >= 1e4) return Math.round(v / 1e3) + 'k'; if (a >= 1e3) return (v / 1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k'; return String(Math.round(v)); }
  function delta(cur, prev, abs) {
    if (cur == null || prev == null) return '';
    cur = Number(cur); prev = Number(prev);
    if (abs) { var d = cur - prev; if (!d) return '<span class="d flat">no change</span>'; return '<span class="d' + (d < 0 ? ' down' : '') + '">' + (d > 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + num(Math.abs(d)) + '</span>'; }
    if (!prev) return cur ? '<span class="d">new</span>' : '';
    var pc = Math.round((cur - prev) / prev * 100);
    if (!pc) return '<span class="d flat">0%</span>';
    return '<span class="d' + (pc < 0 ? ' down' : '') + '">' + (pc > 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + Math.abs(pc) + '%</span>';
  }
  function ago(iso) { if (!iso) return 'never'; var m = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 60000); if (m < 2) return 'just now'; if (m < 60) return m + ' min ago'; var h = Math.round(m / 60); if (h < 36) return h + ' h ago'; return Math.round(h / 24) + ' days ago'; }
  function niceDay(iso) { try { return new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return iso; } }
  function mark(p, size) { var c = P[p] || { color: GREEN, mark: '?' }; return '<span class="mk" style="background:' + c.color + (size ? ';width:' + size + 'px;height:' + size + 'px' : '') + '">' + E(c.mark) + '</span>'; }
  function acctOf(p) { var d = S.data; return ((d && d.accounts) || []).filter(function (a) { return a.platform === p; })[0] || null; }
  function series(p, metric) { var d = S.data; var s = d && d.series && d.series[p] && d.series[p][metric]; return Array.isArray(s) ? s : []; }
  function bucketEnd(key) { var b = S.data && S.data.bucket; if (b !== 'week' && b !== 'month') return key; var dt = new Date(String(key).slice(0, 10) + 'T00:00:00Z'); return (b === 'week' ? new Date(dt.getTime() + 6 * 86400000) : new Date(Date.UTC(dt.getUTCFullYear(), dt.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0))).toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function windowed(pts) { var from = S.data && S.data.from; return from ? pts.filter(function (x) { return x[0] >= from || bucketEnd(x[0]) >= from; }) : pts; }
  /* wave149: every graph hands its points to the hover (window.__w149ch, read by wave149): hovering across shows the numbers */
  function hoverKey(k) { var b = S.data && S.data.bucket; var dt = new Date(String(k).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); try { if (b === 'month') return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }); if (b === 'week') return 'Week of ' + dt.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }); return dt.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return String(k).slice(0, 10); } }
  function full(v) { v = Number(v); return isFinite(v) ? Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-CA') : '\u2013'; }
  function spark(pts, color, key, label) {
    if (!pts || pts.length < 2) return '';
    var w = 200, h = 34, vals = pts.map(function (x) { return Number(x[1]) || 0; }); var mn = Math.min.apply(null, vals), mx = Math.max.apply(null, vals); var span = (mx - mn) || 1;
    var yOf = function (v) { return h - 3 - (v - mn) / span * (h - 8); };
    var d = vals.map(function (v, i) { return (i / (vals.length - 1) * w).toFixed(1) + ',' + yOf(v).toFixed(1); });
    if (key) { try { (window.__w149ch = window.__w149ch || {})[key] = { w: w, h: h, keys: pts.map(function (x, i) { return { k: String(x[0]).slice(0, 10), x: i / (pts.length - 1) * w, v: Number(x[1]) || 0 }; }), head: hoverKey, rows: function (k, kk) { return [{ label: label || '', value: full(kk.v), color: color, y: yOf(kk.v) }]; } }; } catch (e) { /* the hover is extra */ } }
    return '<svg class="sp" viewBox="0 0 ' + w + ' ' + h + '" preserveAspectRatio="none"' + (key ? ' data-w149h="' + E(key) + '" tabindex="0" role="img" aria-label="' + E((label || 'Trend') + ': use the arrow keys to read the numbers') + '"' : '') + '><polyline fill="none" stroke="' + color + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" points="' + d.join(' ') + '"></polyline><circle r="3" cx="' + w + '" cy="' + d[d.length - 1].split(',')[1] + '" fill="' + color + '"></circle></svg>';
  }
  function fn(body) {
    try { return db.functions.invoke('social-pull', { body: body }).then(function (r) { if (r.error) throw r.error; if (r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error); return r.data; }); }
    catch (e) { return Promise.reject(e); }
  }

  /* ---------- the panel ---------- */
  function panel() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('socialstats');
    if (!sec) { var main = document.querySelector('main'); if (!main) return null; sec = document.createElement('section'); sec.id = 'socialstats'; sec.className = 'panel'; main.appendChild(sec); }
    return sec;
  }
  async function load(force) {
    if (S.loading) return;
    var rd = rangeDates();
    if (!force && S.data && S.at && Date.now() - S.at < 60000 && S.data._key === S.range + '|' + rd.join('|')) { paint(); return; }
    S.loading = true; S.err = null;
    if (force || !S.data) paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.rpc('social_range', { p_from: rd[0], p_to: rd[1] });
      if (r.error) { var n = Math.min(90, Number(S.range) || 30); r = await db.rpc('social_overview', { p_days: n }); }
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      S.data = r.data || {}; S.data._key = S.range + '|' + rd.join('|'); S.at = Date.now();
      S.days = Number(S.data.days) || S.days;
    } catch (e) { S.err = e.message || String(e); }
    S.loading = false;
    paint();
    try { document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('w120:data')); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function header() {
    var d = S.data; var last = null;
    ((d && d.accounts) || []).forEach(function (a) { if (a.last_sync_at && (!last || a.last_sync_at > last)) last = a.last_sync_at; });
    return '<div class="w116crumb"><a data-w116go="#mypage">Home</a> &middot; Teams &middot; <a data-w116go="#team/marketing">Marketing, Branding &amp; Web</a> &middot; Analytics</div>' +
      '<div class="w116top"><div><h2><span class="dot" style="background:' + PURPLE + '"></span>Marketing analytics</h2><p class="sub">Followers, reach and what worked across Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, LinkedIn and the website. Numbers refresh every morning.</p></div>' +
      '<div class="w120ctl"><span class="w116seg w120rng">' + RANGES.map(function (r) { return '<a data-w120range="' + r[0] + '" class="' + (S.range === r[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + r[1] + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +
      (S.range === 'custom' ? '<span class="w120cust"><input type="date" id="w120cf" value="' + E(S.cfrom || '') + '"><span>to</span><input type="date" id="w120ct" value="' + E(S.cto || '') + '"><button class="btn mini" data-w120apply="1">Show</button></span>' : '') +
      (staff() ? '<button class="btn mini ghost" data-w120sync="all"' + (S.busy.sync ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy.sync ? 'Syncing\u2026' : 'Sync now') + '</button><button class="btn mini' + (S.setup ? '' : ' ghost') + '" data-w120setup="toggle">' + (S.setup ? 'Close set-up' : 'Set up') + '</button>' : '') +
      '<span class="meta">' + (last ? 'Last sync ' + E(ago(last)) : '') + '</span></div></div>';
  }
  function tile(p) {
    var c = P[p]; var a = acctOf(p); var d = S.data || {}; var tot = (d.totals || {})[p] || {}; var fol = (d.followers || {})[p];
    var st = !a ? '<span class="st off">Not connected</span>' : a.status === 'error' ? '<span class="st err" title="' + E(a.last_error || '') + '">Check</span>' : a.status === 'csv' ? '<span class="st csv">CSV</span>' : '<span class="st">Live</span>';
    var html = '<div class="tile"><div class="ph">' + mark(p) + '<b>' + E(c.label) + (a && a.handle ? ' <span style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:11.5px;font-weight:500;color:var(--wa-muted)">@' + E(a.handle) + '</span>' : '') + '</b>' + st + '</div>';
    if (!a) {
      html += '<div class="big" style="color:var(--wa-muted)">\u2013</div><div class="off">' + (staff() ? '<a data-w120setup="1">Connect ' + E(c.label) + '</a>' + (p === 'tiktok' || p === 'linkedin' ? ' (CSV export until the API is approved)' : '') : 'Not connected yet.') + '</div></div>';
      return html;
    }
    if (c.head === 'followers') {
      html += '<div class="big">' + (fol && fol.now != null ? num(fol.now) : '\u2013') + '<small>followers</small>' + (fol ? delta(fol.now, fol.then, true) : '') + '</div>';
    } else {
      var s = tot.sessions || {};
      html += '<div class="big">' + (s.cur != null ? num(s.cur) : '\u2013') + '<small>sessions</small>' + delta(s.cur, s.prev) + '</div>';
    }
    html += '<div class="row">' + c.stats.map(function (m) { var t = tot[m] || {}; return '<div><b>' + (t.cur != null ? num(t.cur) : '\u2013') + '</b>' + E(MLABEL[m] || m) + ' ' + delta(t.cur, t.prev) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    var spM = c.head === 'followers' ? 'followers' : 'sessions';
    var sp = series(p, spM);
    if (sp.length < 2) { spM = c.stats[0]; sp = series(p, spM); }
    html += spark(windowed(sp).length > 1 ? windowed(sp) : sp, c.color, 'tile:' + p, (MLABEL[spM] || spM) + (spM === 'followers' ? '' : ' (' + (S.data && S.data.bucket === 'month' ? 'month' : S.data && S.data.bucket === 'week' ? 'week' : 'day') + ')'));
    html += '<div class="off" style="font-size:11px">' + E(ago(a.last_sync_at)) + (a.status === 'error' && a.last_error ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(String(a.last_error).slice(0, 90)) : '') + '</div></div>';
    return html;
  }
  function chart() {
    var d = S.data || {}; var m = S.chart;
    var lines = []; var maxV = 0; var from = d.from || '', to = d.to || '';
    ORDER.forEach(function (p) { var pts = windowed(series(p, m)); if (pts.length > 1) { lines.push({ p: p, pts: pts }); pts.forEach(function (x) { maxV = Math.max(maxV, Number(x[1]) || 0); }); } });
    var seg = '<span class="w116seg">' + CHART_METRICS.map(function (k) { return '<a data-w120chart="' + k + '" class="' + (S.chart === k ? 'on' : '') + '">' + E(MLABEL[k] || k) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>';
    if (!lines.length) return '<div class="cardy chart"><h4>' + (d.bucket === 'month' ? 'By month' : d.bucket === 'week' ? 'By week' : 'By day') + seg + '</h4><div class="none">No ' + E((MLABEL[m] || m).toLowerCase()) + ' numbers in this window yet.</div></div>';
    var w = 800, h = 220, padL = 36, padB = 22, padT = 10;
    if (S.range === 'all') { var first = null; lines.forEach(function (l) { if (!first || l.pts[0][0] < first) first = l.pts[0][0]; }); if (first && first > from) from = first; }
    var t0 = new Date(from + 'T00:00:00Z').getTime(), t1 = new Date(to + 'T00:00:00Z').getTime(); var span = (t1 - t0) || 1;
    function X(day) { var k = String(day).slice(0, 10); if (k < from) k = from; return padL + (new Date(k + 'T00:00:00Z').getTime() - t0) / span * (w - padL - 8); }
    function Y(v) { return padT + (1 - (Number(v) || 0) / (maxV || 1)) * (h - padT - padB); }
    var grid = ''; for (var g = 0; g <= 4; g++) { var yv = maxV / 4 * g; grid += '<line x1="' + padL + '" x2="' + w + '" y1="' + Y(yv).toFixed(1) + '" y2="' + Y(yv).toFixed(1) + '" stroke="rgba(16,63,57,.08)"></line><text x="' + (padL - 6) + '" y="' + (Y(yv) + 4).toFixed(1) + '" font-size="10" text-anchor="end" fill="#797773">' + num(yv) + '</text>'; }
    var ticks = ''; var days = Math.round(span / 86400000) + 1; var target = (window.innerWidth || 1000) < 640 ? 4 : 8;
    var tick = function (iso, text) { var x = X(iso); if (x > w - 24 || x < padL - 2) return; ticks += '<text x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (h - 6) + '" font-size="10" text-anchor="middle" fill="#797773">' + E(text) + '</text>'; };
    if (days > 100) {
      var MSH = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
      var a0 = new Date(t0), a1 = new Date(t1); var months = (a1.getUTCFullYear() - a0.getUTCFullYear()) * 12 + a1.getUTCMonth() - a0.getUTCMonth() + 1;
      var k = months <= target ? 1 : months <= target * 2 ? 2 : months <= target * 3.5 ? 3 : months <= target * 6.5 ? 6 : 12 * Math.ceil(months / (target * 12));
      var yx = a0.getUTCFullYear() !== a1.getUTCFullYear();
      for (var mi = 0; mi <= months; mi++) { var md = new Date(Date.UTC(a0.getUTCFullYear(), a0.getUTCMonth() + mi, 1)); if (md.getTime() < t0 || md.getTime() > t1 || (md.getUTCFullYear() * 12 + md.getUTCMonth()) % k) continue; tick(md.toISOString().slice(0, 10), MSH[md.getUTCMonth()] + (yx ? ' ' + md.getUTCFullYear() : '')); }
    } else {
      var step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(days / target));
      for (var i = 0; i < days; i += step) { var dd = new Date(t0 + i * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10); tick(dd, niceDay(dd)); }
    }
    /* a week or month only partly inside the range (or the data) is drawn dashed, so a short last month does not read as a drop */
    var dashed = false, lastTo = d.to || to;
    var paths = lines.map(function (l) {
      var c = P[l.p].color, pts = l.pts, cov = (d.coverage || {})[l.p] || {};
      var line = function (a, dash) { return a.length > 1 ? '<polyline fill="none" stroke="' + c + '" stroke-width="2.2"' + (dash ? ' stroke-dasharray="5 4" opacity=".75"' : '') + ' stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" points="' + a.map(function (x) { return X(x[0]).toFixed(1) + ',' + Y(x[1]).toFixed(1); }).join(' ') + '"></polyline>' : ''; };
      if ((d.bucket !== 'week' && d.bucket !== 'month') || m === 'followers' || pts.length < 3) return line(pts);
      var dataFrom = cov.from && cov.from > (d.from || '') ? cov.from : d.from, dataTo = cov.to && cov.to < lastTo ? cov.to : lastTo;
      var headP = pts[0][0] < (dataFrom || ''), tailP = bucketEnd(pts[pts.length - 1][0]) > dataTo;
      if (headP || tailP) dashed = true;
      return (headP ? line(pts.slice(0, 2), true) : '') + line(pts.slice(headP ? 1 : 0, tailP ? pts.length - 1 : pts.length)) + (tailP ? line(pts.slice(-2), true) : '');
    }).join('');
    var lg = lines.map(function (l) { var tot = l.pts.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + (Number(x[1]) || 0); }, 0); return '<span><i style="background:' + P[l.p].color + '"></i>' + E(P[l.p].label) + ' · ' + num(m === 'followers' ? l.pts[l.pts.length - 1][1] : tot) + '</span>'; }).join('') + (dashed ? '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">Dashed: a ' + (d.bucket === 'week' ? 'week' : 'month') + ' only partly counted</span>' : '');
    try {
      var hk = {}; lines.forEach(function (l) { l.pts.forEach(function (x) { hk[String(x[0]).slice(0, 10)] = 1; }); });
      var hs = lines.map(function (l) { var by = {}; l.pts.forEach(function (x) { by[String(x[0]).slice(0, 10)] = Number(x[1]) || 0; }); return { label: P[l.p].label, color: P[l.p].color, by: by }; });
      (window.__w149ch = window.__w149ch || {}).main = { w: w, h: h, head: hoverKey,
        keys: Object.keys(hk).sort().map(function (k) { return { k: k, x: X(k) }; }),
        rows: function (k) {
          var out = hs.map(function (x) { return { label: x.label, value: x.by[k] != null ? full(x.by[k]) : '\u2013', color: x.color, y: x.by[k] != null ? Y(x.by[k]) : null }; });
          if (m !== 'followers' && hs.length > 1) { var tot = 0, any = false; hs.forEach(function (x) { if (x.by[k] != null) { tot += x.by[k]; any = true; } }); if (any) out.push({ label: 'All platforms', value: full(tot), color: null, y: null, strong: true }); }
          return out;
        } };
    } catch (e) { /* the hover is extra */ }
    return '<div class="cardy chart"><h4>' + (d.bucket === 'month' ? 'By month' : d.bucket === 'week' ? 'By week' : 'By day') + seg + '</h4><svg viewBox="0 0 ' + w + ' ' + h + '" preserveAspectRatio="none" data-w149h="main" tabindex="0" role="img" aria-label="' + E((MLABEL[m] || m) + ' by ' + (d.bucket || 'day') + ': hover or use the arrow keys to read the numbers') + '">' + grid + paths + ticks + '</svg><div class="lg">' + lg + '<span class="w149hint">Hover across the graph for the numbers</span></div></div>';
  }
  function noteCard() {

  }
  function postsCard() {

    var pages = (d.posts || []).filter(function (p) { return p.platform === 'ga4'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (Number(b.views) || 0) - (Number(a.views) || 0); }).slice(0, 6);
    var plats = []; (d.posts || []).forEach(function (p) { if (p.platform !== 'ga4' && plats.indexOf(p.platform) < 0) plats.push(p.platform); });

P[p].label : p) + '</span>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
    var html = '<div class="cardy"><h4>What worked <span style="font-family:' + BODY + ';font-size:12px;font-weight:500;color:var(--wa-muted)">posts from ' + E(rangeLabel()) + ', best first</span></h4>' + chips;

      if (p.saves != null) nums.push('<span><b>' + num(p.saves) + '</b>saves</span>');
      return '<a class="post" ' + (p.url ? 'href="' + E(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"' : '') + '><div class="th">' + (p.thumb_url ? P[p.platform].mark : '') + '</span>') + mark(p.platform) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="bd"><div class="cap">' + (p.caption ? E(p.caption) : '<span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">No caption</span>') + '</div><div class="when">' + E(dayR(p.published_at)) + (p.kind && p.kind !== 'post' ? ' \u00b7 ' + E(p.kind) : '') + '</div><div class="nums">' + nums.join('') + '</div></div></a>';
    }).join('') + '</div>';
    if (pages.length) html += '<h4 style="margin-top:16px;font-size:17px">Most read pages on the website</h4><div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.7">' + pages.map(function (p) { return '<div><b style="font-family:' + HEAD + ';font-size:15px;color:' + GREEN + ';margin-right:6px">' + num(p.views) + '</b>' + (p.url ? '<a href="' + E(p.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:' + GREEN + '">' + E(p.caption || p.external_id) + '</a>' : E(p.caption || p.external_id)) + ' <span style="color:var(--wa-muted);font-size:11.5px">' + E(p.external_id) + '</span></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function setupCard() {
    if (!S.setup || !staff()) return '';
    var st = S.status || {}; var accs = (S.data && S.data.accounts) || [];
    function accRows(p) { return accs.filter(function (a) { return a.platform === p; }).map(function (a) { return '<div class="acc"><div><b>' + E(a.name || a.handle || a.external_id) + '</b><span class="s">' + E(a.status) + ' \u00b7 ' + E(ago(a.last_sync_at)) + '</span></div><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w120sync="' + a.id + '">Sync</button><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w120rm="' + a.id + '" title="Remove this account and its numbers">Remove</button>' + (a.last_error ? '<span class="er" title="' + E(a.last_error) + '">' + E(String(a.last_error).slice(0, 140)) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join(''); }
    var pr = S.probe && S.probe.meta;
    var metaBody = !st.meta_token ? '<p>Add <code>META_SYSTEM_TOKEN</code> to the Supabase vault first (Business settings &gt; System users &gt; Generate token, permissions pages_show_list, pages_read_engagement, read_insights, instagram_basic, instagram_manage_insights).</p>' :
      '<p>Token found. Find the Pages the system user can see and connect them; the Instagram account linked to a Page comes with it.</p><button class="btn mini" data-w120probe="1"' + (S.busy.probe ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy.probe ? 'Looking\u2026' : 'Find my pages') + '</button>' +
      (pr ? (pr.ok ? (pr.pages.length ? pr.pages.map(function (pg) { return '<div class="pg">' + (pg.picture ? '<img src="' + E(pg.picture) + '" alt="">' : '') + '<span><b>' + E(pg.name) + '</b> \u00b7 ' + num(pg.followers) + ' followers' + (pg.instagram ? '<br><span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">Instagram @' + E(pg.instagram.username) + ' \u00b7 ' + num(pg.instagram.followers) + ' followers</span>' : '<br><span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">no Instagram account linked to this Page</span>') + '</span><button class="btn mini" style="margin-left:auto" data-w120connect="' + E(pg.id) + '"' + (S.busy.connect ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Connect</button></div>'; }).join('') : '<p style="color:' + FLAG + '">The token sees no Pages. In Business settings, give the system user the Page under Add assets.</p>') : '<p style="color:' + FLAG + '">' + E(pr.error) + '</p>') : '');
    var ga = '<p>In GA4: Admin &gt; Property access management &gt; Add users, paste <code>' + E(st.sa_email || 'the hub service account') + '</code> as Viewer. Then the property id (Admin &gt; Property details, the number top right).</p>' +
      '<input type="text" id="w120gaid" placeholder="Property id, e.g. 123456789" style="width:170px"><input type="text" id="w120gasite" placeholder="https://welfareavenue.com" style="width:200px"><button class="btn mini" data-w120ga="1"' + (S.busy.ga ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy.ga ? 'Checking\u2026' : 'Connect') + '</button>';
    function csvCard(p, hint) {
      var c = P[p]; var m = S.csv && S.csv.platform === p ? S.csv : null;
      return '<div class="sc"><h5>' + mark(p, 22) + E(c.label) + '</h5><p>' + hint + '</p>' +
        '<select data-w120csvkind="' + p + '"><option value="daily"' + (!m || m.kind === 'daily' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Daily numbers (overview export)</option><option value="posts"' + (m && m.kind === 'posts' ? ' selected' : '') + '>Posts (content export)</option></select>' +
        '<input type="file" accept=".csv,text/csv" data-w120csv="' + p + '" style="font-size:12px;margin:0 0 6px">' +
        (m ? mapper(m) : '') + accRows(p) + '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="setup"><h4>Set up the sources</h4><p class="sub">Meta and Google Analytics read themselves every morning. TikTok and LinkedIn need an app approval before their APIs open up; until then, export from their analytics pages and drop the CSV here (it maps the columns and keeps the days you already have).</p><div class="sgrid">' +
      '<div class="sc"><h5>' + mark('facebook', 22) + mark('instagram', 22) + 'Meta: Facebook Page and Instagram</h5>' + metaBody + accRows('facebook') + accRows('instagram') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sc"><h5>' + mark('ga4', 22) + 'Website: Google Analytics 4</h5>' + ga + accRows('ga4') + '</div>' +
      csvCard('tiktok', 'TikTok Studio &gt; Analytics &gt; Overview, pick the range, Download data (CSV). Columns like Date, Video views, Profile views, Likes, Comments, Shares are matched by name; Followers from the Followers tab export.') +
      csvCard('linkedin', 'Page admin &gt; Analytics &gt; Followers or Content &gt; Export. LinkedIn gives an .xls: open it in Sheets and download the sheet you want as CSV.') +
      '</div></div>';
  }
  function mapper(m) {
    var cols = m.cols; var guess = m.guess || {};
    function sel(name, key, opts) { return '<label>' + E(name) + '</label><select data-w120map="' + E(key) + '"><option value="">(skip)</option>' + cols.map(function (c, i) { return '<option value="' + i + '"' + (guess[key] === i ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(c) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>'; }
    var fields = m.kind === 'posts' ? [['Link or id', 'external_id'], ['Caption', 'caption'], ['Published', 'published_at'], ['Views', 'views'], ['Reach', 'reach'], ['Likes', 'likes'], ['Comments', 'comments'], ['Shares', 'shares'], ['Saves', 'saves']] : [['Date', 'day'], ['Followers (total)', 'followers'], ['New followers', 'new_followers'], ['Views', 'views'], ['Reach', 'reach'], ['Profile visits', 'profile_views'], ['Likes', 'likes'], ['Comments', 'comments'], ['Shares', 'shares'], ['Engagement', 'engagement']];
    return '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--wa-muted);margin:6px 0 0"><b>' + E(m.name) + '</b> \u00b7 ' + m.rows.length + ' rows. Match the columns:</div><div class="map">' + fields.map(function (f) { return sel(f[0], f[1]); }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><input type="text" id="w120handle" placeholder="@handle (optional)" style="width:150px" value="' + E(m.handle || '') + '"><button class="btn mini" data-w120import="1"' + (S.busy.import ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (S.busy.import ? 'Importing\u2026' : 'Import') + '</button><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w120csvx="1">Cancel</button></div>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var sec = panel(); if (!sec) return;
    var d = S.data; var body = '';
    if (S.err) body = '<div class="hero"><h3>Could not load the numbers</h3><p>' + E(S.err) + '</p></div>';
    else if (!d) body = '<div class="hero"><h3>Loading\u2026</h3></div>';
    else {
      var any = (d.accounts || []).length;
      body += setupCard();
      if (!any && !S.setup) body += '<div class="hero"><h3>Nothing connected yet</h3><p>Connect the Facebook Page and Instagram with the Meta token, add the hub\u2019s service account to Google Analytics, and drop TikTok and LinkedIn exports in. Five minutes for the first two.</p>' + (staff() ? '<button class="btn" data-w120setup="1">Set up the sources</button>' : '<span style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--wa-muted)">The marketing team is connecting the accounts.</span>') + '</div>';
      body += '<div class="w120tiles">' + ORDER.map(tile).join('') + '</div>';
      if (any) body += insightsCard() + chart() + postsCard() + noteCard();
    }
    sec.innerHTML = '<div class="w120wrap">' + header() + body + '</div>';
    if (d && !S.err) setTimeout(maybeAutoInsights, 400);
  }

  /* ---------- insights (wave120b): what the numbers say, what to do next ---------- */
  var TZ = 'America/Toronto';
  var WDS = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
  var WDL = { Mon: 'Monday', Tue: 'Tuesday', Wed: 'Wednesday', Thu: 'Thursday', Fri: 'Friday', Sat: 'Saturday', Sun: 'Sunday' };
  var TOD = [['Morning', 6, 11, 9], ['Midday', 11, 15, 12], ['Afternoon', 15, 18, 16], ['Evening', 18, 22, 19], ['Night', 22, 30, 22]];
  var CTA = /(volunteer|donat|sign ?up|register|link in (our )?bio|join us|help us|rsvp|b\u00e9n\u00e9vol|inscri|faites un don)/i;
  function whenOf(iso) {
    try {
      var parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: TZ, weekday: 'short', hour: 'numeric', hourCycle: 'h23' }).formatToParts(new Date(iso));
      var wd = '', h = 0; parts.forEach(function (p) { if (p.type === 'weekday') wd = String(p.value).slice(0, 3); if (p.type === 'hour') h = Number(p.value) % 24; });
      return { wd: wd, h: h };
    } catch (e) { var d = new Date(iso); return { wd: WDS[(d.getDay() + 6) % 7], h: d.getHours() }; }
  }
  function todOf(h) { for (var i = 0; i < TOD.length; i++) { var hh = h < 6 ? h + 24 : h; if (hh >= TOD[i][1] && hh < TOD[i][2]) return TOD[i][0]; } return 'Night'; }
  function engOf(p) { var e = Number(p.engagement); if (p.engagement != null && isFinite(e) && e > 0) return e; var s = 0, any = false; ['likes', 'comments', 'shares', 'saves'].forEach(function (k) { if (p[k] != null && isFinite(Number(p[k]))) { s += Number(p[k]); any = true; } }); return any ? s : null; }
  function valOf(p, m) { if (m === 'eng') return engOf(p); var v = p[m]; return v == null || !isFinite(Number(v)) ? null : Number(v); }
  function median(a) { if (!a.length) return 0; var b = a.slice().sort(function (x, y) { return x - y; }); var m = Math.floor(b.length / 2); return b.length % 2 ? b[m] : (b[m - 1] + b[m]) / 2; }
  function kindOf(p) { var k = String(p.kind || '').toLowerCase(); if (/reel|video|clip/.test(k)) return 'Reels and video'; if (/carousel|album/.test(k)) return 'Carousels'; return 'Photos and posts'; }
  function fx(x) { return (Math.round(x * 10) / 10).toFixed(1) + 'x'; }
  /* each post gets an index: its main number over the median of its own platform, so Instagram and Facebook compare fairly (1.0 = a typical post) */
  function indexed(posts) {
    var byP = {}; posts.forEach(function (p) { if (p.platform === 'ga4' || !p.published_at) return; (byP[p.platform] = byP[p.platform] || []).push(p); });
    var out = [];
    Object.keys(byP).forEach(function (pl) {
      var list = byP[pl], best = null, bestN = 0;
      ['reach', 'views', 'eng'].forEach(function (m) { var n = list.filter(function (p) { var v = valOf(p, m); return v != null && v > 0; }).length; if (n > bestN) { bestN = n; best = m; } });
      if (!best || bestN < 2) return;
      var vals = list.map(function (p) { return { p: p, v: valOf(p, best) }; }).filter(function (x) { return x.v != null && x.v > 0; });
      var med = median(vals.map(function (x) { return x.v; })); if (!med) return;
      vals.forEach(function (x) { var w = whenOf(x.p.published_at); var cap = String(x.p.caption || ''); out.push({ p: x.p, platform: pl, metric: best, v: x.v, idx: x.v / med, wd: w.wd, h: w.h, tod: todOf(w.h), len: cap.length, tags: (cap.match(/#[\w\u00c0-\u024f]+/g) || []).length, cta: CTA.test(cap), kind: kindOf(x.p) }); });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function compare(rows, keyFn, order) {
    var m = {}; rows.forEach(function (r) { var k = keyFn(r); if (k == null) return; (m[k] = m[k] || []).push(r.idx); });
    var base = median(rows.map(function (r) { return r.idx; })) || 1;
    return (order || Object.keys(m)).filter(function (k) { return m[k]; }).map(function (k) { var med = median(m[k]); return { label: k, n: m[k].length, raw: med, idx: med / base }; });
  }
  function conf(best, total, lift) { if (best.n >= 6 && total >= 24 && lift >= 1.3) return 'high'; if (best.n >= 3 && total >= 10) return 'medium'; return 'low'; }
  function barsOf(list, bestLabel) { var mx = Math.max.apply(null, list.map(function (b) { return b.idx; }).concat([0.0001])); return list.map(function (b) { return { label: b.label, n: b.n, idx: b.idx, w: Math.round(b.idx / mx * 100), best: b.label === bestLabel }; }); }
  /* the best group against everything else: "Evening posts do 1.9x the rest" */
  function bucketEv(key, rows, keyFn, order, nameFn, words) {
    var list = compare(rows, keyFn, order); var elig = list.filter(function (b) { return b.n >= 2; });
    if (rows.length < 6 || elig.length < 2) return null;
    var best = elig.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.idx - a.idx; })[0], worst = elig.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.idx - b.idx; })[0];
    var rest = rows.filter(function (r) { return keyFn(r) !== best.label; }); if (rest.length < 2) return null;
    var lift = best.raw / (median(rest.map(function (r) { return r.idx; })) || 0.0001);
    var clear = lift >= 1.25;
    return { key: key, best: clear ? best.label : null, lift: lift, title: clear ? words.best(nameFn(best.label)) : words.none, detail: nameFn(best.label) + ': ' + fx(lift) + ' the rest (' + best.n + ' post' + (best.n === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' against ' + rest.length + ')' + (worst.label !== best.label ? '; weakest: ' + nameFn(worst.label) + ' at ' + fx(worst.idx) + ' a typical post (' + worst.n + ').' : '.'), n: rows.length, confidence: clear ? conf(best, rows.length, lift) : 'low', bars: barsOf(list, clear ? best.label : null) };
  }
  function lastDays(posts, n) { var from = new Date(Date.now() - n * 86400000).toISOString(); return posts.filter(function (p) { return String(p.published_at || '') >= from; }); }
  function evidence(d) {
    d = d || S.data || {}; var posts = (d.posts_all || []).filter(function (p) { return p.platform !== 'ga4'; }); var ev = []; var rows = indexed(posts);
    var add = function (x) { if (x) ev.push(x); };
    add(bucketEv('weekday', rows, function (r) { return r.wd || null; }, WDS, function (l) { return WDL[l] || l; }, { best: function (l) { return 'Best day to post: ' + l; }, none: 'No clear best day yet' }));
    add(bucketEv('time', rows, function (r) { return r.tod; }, TOD.map(function (t) { return t[0]; }), function (l) { var t = TOD.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === l; })[0]; return l + (t ? ' (' + String(t[1]).padStart(2, '0') + ':00 to ' + String(t[2] % 24).padStart(2, '0') + ':00)' : ''); }, { best: function (l) { return 'Best time to post: ' + l.toLowerCase(); }, none: 'No clear best time yet' }));
    add(bucketEv('format', rows, function (r) { return r.kind; }, ['Reels and video', 'Carousels', 'Photos and posts'], function (l) { return l; }, { best: function (l) { return l + ' travel furthest'; }, none: 'Formats perform about the same' }));
    add(bucketEv('caption', rows, function (r) { return r.len < 80 ? 'Short' : r.len <= 250 ? 'Medium' : 'Long'; }, ['Short', 'Medium', 'Long'], function (l) { return l === 'Short' ? 'Short captions (under 80 characters)' : l === 'Medium' ? 'Medium captions (80 to 250)' : 'Long captions (over 250)'; }, { best: function (l) { return l + ' do best'; }, none: 'Caption length makes little difference' }));
    add(bucketEv('cta', rows, function (r) { return r.cta ? 'With an ask' : 'No ask'; }, ['With an ask', 'No ask'], function (l) { return l === 'With an ask' ? 'Posts with an ask (volunteer, donate, sign up)' : 'Posts without an ask'; }, { best: function (l) { return l + ' do better'; }, none: 'Asking (volunteer, donate) neither helps nor hurts reach' }));
    var igRows = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.platform === 'instagram'; });
    add(bucketEv('hashtags', igRows, function (r) { return r.tags <= 2 ? '0 to 2' : r.tags <= 8 ? '3 to 8' : '9 or more'; }, ['0 to 2', '3 to 8', '9 or more'], function (l) { return l + ' hashtags'; }, { best: function (l) { return 'On Instagram, ' + l + ' work best'; }, none: 'Hashtag count makes little difference on Instagram' }));
    /* cadence: weeks with more posts against weeks with fewer, from the daily reach */
    (function () {
      var daily = {}; ['instagram', 'facebook'].forEach(function (pl) { var s = d.series && d.series[pl] && d.series[pl].reach; (s || []).forEach(function (x) { daily[x[0]] = (daily[x[0]] || 0) + (Number(x[1]) || 0); }); });
      if (d.bucket === 'month') return; var wkB = d.bucket === 'week'; var days = Object.keys(daily); if (days.length < (wkB ? 8 : 28)) return;
      function wk(iso) { var dt = new Date(String(iso).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00'); var dow = (dt.getDay() + 6) % 7; return new Date(dt.getTime() - dow * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
      var W = {}; days.forEach(function (day) { var k = wk(day); W[k] = W[k] || { reach: 0, posts: 0, days: 0 }; W[k].reach += daily[day]; W[k].days += wkB ? 7 : 1; });
      if (wkB) { var wks = Object.keys(W).sort(); if (wks.length > 2) { delete W[wks[0]]; delete W[wks[wks.length - 1]]; } }
      posts.forEach(function (p) { if (p.platform !== 'instagram' && p.platform !== 'facebook') return; var k = wk(p.published_at); if (W[k]) W[k].posts++; });
      var full = Object.keys(W).filter(function (k) { return W[k].days >= 6; }).map(function (k) { return W[k]; });
      var busy = full.filter(function (w) { return w.posts >= 3; }), quiet = full.filter(function (w) { return w.posts <= 1; });
      if (busy.length < 2 || quiet.length < 2) return;
      var avg = function (a) { return a.reduce(function (s, w) { return s + w.reach; }, 0) / a.length; };
      var r = avg(busy) / (avg(quiet) || 1);
      ev.push({ key: 'cadence', title: r >= 1.2 ? 'Posting more often reaches more people' : r <= 0.85 ? 'More posts have not meant more reach' : 'Posting more often has not changed reach much', detail: 'Weeks with 3 or more posts reached ' + fx(r) + ' the people of weeks with 0 or 1 (' + busy.length + ' busy weeks, ' + quiet.length + ' quiet ones; ' + num(avg(busy)) + ' against ' + num(avg(quiet)) + ' daily reach summed).', n: busy.length + quiet.length, confidence: busy.length >= 4 && quiet.length >= 4 ? 'medium' : 'low', bars: barsOf([{ label: '3+ posts', n: busy.length, idx: avg(busy) }, { label: '0 or 1', n: quiet.length, idx: avg(quiet) }], r >= 1 ? '3+ posts' : '0 or 1') });
    })();
    /* growth: followers over the selected window */
    (function () {
      var g = []; Object.keys(d.followers || {}).forEach(function (pl) { var f = d.followers[pl]; if (f && f.now != null && f.then != null && Number(f.then) > 0) g.push({ pl: pl, now: Number(f.now), then: Number(f.then), pct: (Number(f.now) - Number(f.then)) / Number(f.then) * 100 }); });
      if (!g.length) return; g.sort(function (a, b) { return b.pct - a.pct; });
      var lead = g[0]; var name = function (pl) { return P[pl] ? P[pl].label : pl; };
      var sign = function (x) { return (x >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + Math.abs(x).toFixed(1) + '%'; };
      ev.push({ key: 'growth', title: lead.pct >= 1 ? name(lead.pl) + ' is growing fastest (' + sign(lead.pct) + ')' : 'Followers are flat', detail: g.map(function (x) { return name(x.pl) + ' ' + (x.now - x.then >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + num(Math.abs(x.now - x.then)) + ' (' + sign(x.pct) + ')'; }).join(', ') + ' ' + overLabel() + '.', n: g.length, confidence: 'high', bars: g.map(function (x) { var mx = Math.max.apply(null, g.map(function (y) { return Math.abs(y.pct); }).concat([0.1])); return { label: name(x.pl), n: 0, idx: x.pct, w: Math.round(Math.abs(x.pct) / mx * 100), best: x === lead && x.pct > 0, neg: x.pct < 0, text: sign(x.pct) }; }) });
    })();
    /* momentum: reach (or views) against the previous window */
    (function () {
      var m = []; Object.keys(d.totals || {}).forEach(function (pl) { if (pl === 'ga4') return; var t = d.totals[pl] || {}; var k = t.reach && t.reach.prev ? 'reach' : t.views && t.views.prev ? 'views' : null; if (!k) return; var cur = Number(t[k].cur) || 0, prev = Number(t[k].prev) || 0; if (!prev) return; m.push({ pl: pl, k: k, cur: cur, prev: prev, pct: (cur - prev) / prev * 100 }); });
      if (!m.length) return; m.sort(function (a, b) { return Math.abs(b.pct) - Math.abs(a.pct); });
      var top = m[0]; var name = function (pl) { return P[pl] ? P[pl].label : pl; };
      ev.push({ key: 'momentum', title: (top.k === 'reach' ? 'Reach' : 'Views') + ' ' + (top.pct >= 0 ? 'up ' : 'down ') + Math.abs(Math.round(top.pct)) + '% on ' + name(top.pl), detail: m.map(function (x) { return name(x.pl) + ' ' + x.k + ' ' + num(x.cur) + ' against ' + num(x.prev) + ' (' + (x.pct >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + Math.abs(Math.round(x.pct)) + '%)'; }).join('; ') + ', ' + vsLabel() + '.', n: m.length, confidence: 'high', bars: m.map(function (x) { var mx = Math.max.apply(null, m.map(function (y) { return Math.abs(y.pct); }).concat([1])); return { label: name(x.pl), n: 0, idx: x.pct, w: Math.round(Math.abs(x.pct) / mx * 100), best: x.pct > 0 && x === top, neg: x.pct < 0, text: (x.pct >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + Math.abs(Math.round(x.pct)) + '%' }; }) });
    })();
    /* website: what social brings */
    (function () {
      var t = (d.totals || {}).ga4; if (!t || !t.sessions || !t.sessions.cur) return;
      var ss = t.sessions_social || {}; var share = (Number(ss.cur) || 0) / Number(t.sessions.cur) * 100; var prevShare = t.sessions.prev ? (Number(ss.prev) || 0) / Number(t.sessions.prev) * 100 : null;
      var pages = (d.posts || []).filter(function (p) { return p.platform === 'ga4'; }).sort(function (a, b) { return (Number(b.views) || 0) - (Number(a.views) || 0); });
      ev.push({ key: 'website', title: 'Social brings ' + Math.round(share) + '% of website visits', detail: num(ss.cur || 0) + ' of ' + num(t.sessions.cur) + ' sessions came from social' + (prevShare != null ? ' (' + Math.round(prevShare) + '% the period before)' : '') + (pages[0] ? '; the most read page was ' + (pages[0].caption || pages[0].external_id) + ' (' + num(pages[0].views) + ' views).' : '.'), n: 0, confidence: 'medium' });
    })();
    /* the top post and the rhythm */
    (function () {
      if (!rows.length) return;
      var top = rows.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.idx - a.idx; })[0];
      var cap = String(top.p.caption || top.p.kind || 'a post').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); if (cap.length > 70) cap = cap.slice(0, 67) + '\u2026';
      ev.push({ key: 'top_post', title: 'Top post: \u201c' + cap + '\u201d', detail: top.kind + ' on ' + (P[top.platform] ? P[top.platform].label : top.platform) + ', ' + (WDL[top.wd] || top.wd) + ' ' + top.tod.toLowerCase() + ', ' + (top.len < 80 ? 'short' : top.len <= 250 ? 'medium' : 'long') + ' caption' + (top.tags ? ', ' + top.tags + ' hashtags' : '') + ': ' + fx(top.idx) + ' a typical post (' + num(top.v) + ' ' + (top.metric === 'eng' ? 'interactions' : top.metric) + ').', n: rows.length, confidence: rows.length >= 10 ? 'medium' : 'low' });
      var dates = posts.map(function (p) { return String(p.published_at).slice(0, 10); }).filter(Boolean).sort();
      if (dates.length >= 4) {
        var endDay = String(d.to || new Date().toISOString()).slice(0, 10); var startDay = d.from && S.range !== 'all' ? String(d.from).slice(0, 10) : dates[0];
        var span = Math.max(7, (new Date(endDay + 'T12:00:00').getTime() - new Date(startDay + 'T12:00:00').getTime()) / 86400000 + 1); var per = dates.length / (span / 7);
        var gap = 0, gFrom = null, gTo = null; var all = dates.concat([endDay]);
        for (var i = 1; i < all.length; i++) { var g = (new Date(all[i] + 'T12:00:00') - new Date(all[i - 1] + 'T12:00:00')) / 86400000; if (g > gap) { gap = g; gFrom = all[i - 1]; gTo = all[i]; } }
        var pls = []; posts.forEach(function (p) { var nm = P[p.platform] ? P[p.platform].label : p.platform; if (pls.indexOf(nm) < 0) pls.push(nm); });
        var plsTxt = pls.length > 1 ? pls.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + pls[pls.length - 1] : (pls[0] || 'the platforms');
        ev.push({ key: 'rhythm', title: (Math.round(per * 10) / 10) + ' posts a week' + (gap >= 10 ? ', with a ' + Math.round(gap) + '-day gap' : ''), detail: dates.length + ' posts across ' + plsTxt + ' ' + overLabel() + (gap >= 5 ? '; the longest quiet stretch was ' + Math.round(gap) + ' days (' + dayR(gFrom) + ' to ' + dayR(gTo) + ').' : '.'), n: dates.length, confidence: 'high' });
      }
    })();
    return ev;
  }
  function bestSlot(d) {
    var ev = evidence(d); var wd = ev.filter(function (e) { return e.key === 'weekday'; })[0], tm = ev.filter(function (e) { return e.key === 'time'; })[0];
    var day = wd && wd.best && wd.confidence !== 'low' ? wd.best : null, tod = tm && tm.best && tm.confidence !== 'low' ? tm.best : null;
    if (!day && !tod) return null;
    var t = TOD.filter(function (x) { return x[0] === tod; })[0]; var hour = t ? t[3] : 18;
    var hh = String(hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':00';
    return { wd: day, h: hour, tod: tod, label: day && tod ? WDL[day] + ' ' + tod.toLowerCase() + ', ' + hh : day ? WDL[day] + ', ' + hh : tod + 's, ' + hh, confidence: { day: wd ? wd.confidence : null, time: tm ? tm.confidence : null } };
  }

  function evCard(e) {

    return '<div class="ev" data-w120ev="' + E(e.key) + '"><b>' + E(e.title) + '</b><p>' + E(e.detail) + '</p>' + (bars ? '<div class="bars">' + bars + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="ft"><span class="c ' + E(e.confidence || '') + '">' + E(e.confidence || '') + '</span>' + (e.n ? ' \u00b7 ' + e.n + (e.key === 'growth' || e.key === 'momentum' ? ' platform' + (e.n === 1 ? '' : 's') : e.key === 'cadence' ? ' weeks' : ' posts') : '') + '</div></div>';
  }

  async function refreshInsights(auto) {
    var ev = evidence(S.data); if (ev.length < 3) return;
    S.busy.ins = true; if (!auto) paint();
    try { var r = await fn({ action: 'insights', days: 90, evidence: ev.map(function (e) { return { key: e.key, title: e.title, detail: e.detail, confidence: e.confidence, n: e.n }; }) }); if (!auto) toastS(r.insights ? 'Recommendations updated' : (r.skipped || 'Nothing new')); }
    catch (e) { if (!auto) toastS('Could not get recommendations: ' + (e.message || e)); }
    S.busy.ins = false; S.data = null; load(true);
  }
  function maybeAutoInsights() {
    if (S.insTried || !staff() || !S.data) return;
    var ev = evidence(S.data); if (ev.length < 3) return;
    var ins = S.data.insights; var age = ins ? (Date.now() - new Date(ins.created_at).getTime()) / 86400000 : 99;
    if (ins && age < 6) return;
    S.insTried = true; refreshInsights(true);
  }

  /* ---------- CSV ---------- */
  function parseCsv(text) {
    var rows = [], row = [], cur = '', q = false, i, ch;
    text = String(text || '').replace(/^\ufeff/, '');
    for (i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
      ch = text[i];
      if (q) { if (ch === '"') { if (text[i + 1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; } else q = false; } else cur += ch; }
      else if (ch === '"') q = true;
      else if (ch === ',' || ch === '\t' || ch === ';') { row.push(cur); cur = ''; }
      else if (ch === '\n' || ch === '\r') { if (ch === '\r' && text[i + 1] === '\n') i++; row.push(cur); cur = ''; if (row.some(function (c) { return c !== ''; })) rows.push(row); row = []; }
      else cur += ch;
    }
    row.push(cur); if (row.some(function (c) { return c !== ''; })) rows.push(row);
    return rows;
  }
  var GUESS = { day: /^(date|day|time|week|period)/i, followers: /^(followers?( total| count)?|total followers|fans)$/i, new_followers: /(new followers|net followers|follower growth|followers gained)/i, views: /^(video views|views|impressions|post views|total views)$/i, reach: /^(reach|unique|accounts reached)/i, profile_views: /profile (views|visits)/i, likes: /^(likes|reactions)$/i, comments: /^comments$/i, shares: /^(shares|reposts|shared)$/i, engagement: /^(engagements?|interactions|total engagement)/i, external_id: /(link|url|permalink|id)/i, caption: /(caption|title|description|text|post)/i, published_at: /(date|time|published|posted|created)/i, saves: /^(saves|saved|bookmarks?)$/i };
  function guessMap(cols, kind) { var g = {}; var keys = Object.keys(GUESS); cols.forEach(function (c, i) { keys.forEach(function (k) { if (g[k] == null && GUESS[k].test(String(c).trim())) g[k] = i; }); }); if (kind === 'daily') { delete g.external_id; delete g.caption; delete g.published_at; } else { delete g.day; delete g.followers; delete g.new_followers; } return g; }
  function toDay(v) { v = String(v || '').trim(); var m = /^(\d{4})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})/.exec(v); if (m) return m[1] + '-' + m[2].padStart(2, '0') + '-' + m[3].padStart(2, '0'); m = /^(\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{1,2})[-\/.](\d{4})/.exec(v); if (m) return m[3] + '-' + m[1].padStart(2, '0') + '-' + m[2].padStart(2, '0'); var d = new Date(v); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d.toISOString().slice(0, 10); }
  function toNum(v) { v = String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[,\s%]/g, '').replace(/^\$/, ''); if (!v) return null; var k = /^([\d.]+)([kKmM])$/.exec(v); var n = k ? Number(k[1]) * (k[2].toLowerCase() === 'k' ? 1e3 : 1e6) : Number(v); return isFinite(n) ? n : null; }
  function buildImport(m) {
    var map = {}; document.querySelectorAll('#socialstats [data-w120map]').forEach(function (s) { if (s.value !== '') map[s.getAttribute('data-w120map')] = Number(s.value); });
    var handle = (document.getElementById('w120handle') || {}).value || '';
    var body = { action: 'import_csv', platform: m.platform, handle: handle.replace(/^@/, '') || null, rows: [], posts: [] };
    if (m.kind === 'daily') {
      if (map.day == null) throw new Error('Pick the date column');
      m.rows.forEach(function (r) { var day = toDay(r[map.day]); if (!day) return; Object.keys(map).forEach(function (k) { if (k === 'day') return; var v = toNum(r[map[k]]); if (v != null) body.rows.push({ day: day, metric: k, value: v }); }); });
      if (!body.rows.length) throw new Error('No dated numbers found in those columns');
    } else {
      if (map.external_id == null) throw new Error('Pick the link or id column');
      m.rows.forEach(function (r) { var id = String(r[map.external_id] || '').trim(); if (!id) return; var p = { external_id: id, url: /^https?:/.test(id) ? id : null, caption: map.caption != null ? String(r[map.caption] || '').slice(0, 600) : null, published_at: map.published_at != null ? (toDay(r[map.published_at]) ? toDay(r[map.published_at]) + 'T12:00:00Z' : null) : null }; ['views', 'reach', 'likes', 'comments', 'shares', 'saves'].forEach(function (k) { if (map[k] != null) { var v = toNum(r[map[k]]); if (v != null) p[k] = v; } }); body.posts.push(p); });
      if (!body.posts.length) throw new Error('No posts found in that column');
    }
    return body;
  }

/* =====================================================================
   WA v121 Marketing: the content calendar (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "The marketing department should have a content calendar module
   that is capable of posting content to Instagram and Facebook using the
   Meta Business Suite APIs. Only if we are given enough functionality to
   upload posts; if not, keep it to a highly functional content calendar
   with confirmations of posts going up on social media based on the
   connections we are getting."
   - Month, week and list views; drag a post to another day (the time
     stays), drag an undated idea onto a day, drag back to the tray to
     unschedule; Undo on every move. Filters by platform and state,
     search, the week at a glance, what needs attention.
   - The composer (a side panel): Instagram and Facebook, photos (turned
     into JPEG, checked against Instagram's 4:5 to 1.91:1, one-click crop),
     a video for a reel, caption counts (2,200 characters, 30 hashtags),
     AI help (write, shorten, hashtags; English, French or both), link,
     date and time with the best slot from the insights, owner, campaign,
     notes, live Instagram and Facebook previews.
   - Publishing follows the connection (social-publish capabilities):
     when the Meta token carries pages_manage_posts and
     instagram_content_publish, Publish now and Schedule to publish send
     the post (a cron every 5 minutes); when it does not, the calendar
     plans, reminds when a post is due, and confirms it anyway: every
     morning's analytics pull matches what went up (by id, link or
     caption) and the post shows Live with its reach and likes.
   - Optional Slack confirmations to a channel (Settings on the page).
   Data: content_posts (wa121), social-publish v2, social-pull v1.2.
   ===================================================================== */

  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function E(s) { return (typeof esc === 'function') ? esc(s) : String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]; }); }
  function toastS(t) { try { toast(t); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }
  function staff() { try { return typeof isStaff === 'function' && isStaff(); } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function board() { try { return typeof MODE !== 'undefined' && MODE === 'board'; } catch (e) { return false; } }
  function allowed() { return staff() || board(); }
  /* wave222: the menu entry shows only when the person's access list carries the page (wa40Enforce hides it otherwise; before, placeTab showed it again on every change of the menu and the two took turns) */
  function tabOk222() { try { return typeof wa40Allowed !== 'function' || (wa40Allowed() || []).indexOf('contentcal') >= 0; } catch (e) { return true; } }
  function me() { try { return (typeof USER !== 'undefined' && USER && USER.name) || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function today() { return ymd(new Date()); }
  function addDays(ds, n) { var d = new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00'); return ymd(new Date(d.getTime() + n * 86400000)); }
  function dowOf(ds) { return (new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00').getDay() + 6) % 7; }
  function mondayOf(ds) { return addDays(ds, -dowOf(ds)); }
  function addMonths(ds, n) { var y = Number(ds.slice(0, 4)), m = Number(ds.slice(5, 7)) - 1 + n; y += Math.floor(m / 12); m = ((m % 12) + 12) % 12; return y + '-' + String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-01'; }
  function dayOf(iso) { return iso ? ymd(new Date(iso)) : null; }
  function hm(iso) { if (!iso) return ''; var d = new Date(iso); return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'); }
  function niceDate(ds, opts) { try { return new Date(ds + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', opts || { weekday: 'short', month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); } catch (e) { return ds; } }
  function niceWhen(iso) { if (!iso) return 'No date'; return niceDate(dayOf(iso)) + ', ' + hm(iso); }
  function isoOf(ds, t) { return new Date(ds + 'T' + (t || '18:00') + ':00').toISOString(); }
  function num(v) { v = Number(v); if (!isFinite(v)) return '\u2013'; var a = Math.abs(v); if (a >= 1e6) return (v / 1e6).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'M'; if (a >= 1e4) return Math.round(v / 1e3) + 'k'; if (a >= 1e3) return (v / 1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') + 'k'; return String(Math.round(v)); }
  function tz() { try { return Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || ''; } catch (e) { return ''; } }
  function marks(pls, liveMap) { return '<span class="pm">' + (pls || []).map(function (p) { var c = PL[p]; if (!c) return ''; return '<i class="' + (liveMap && liveMap[p] ? 'ok' : '') + '" style="background:' + c.color + '" title="' + E(c.label) + (liveMap && liveMap[p] ? ': live' : '') + '">' + E(c.short) + '</i>'; }).join('') + '</span>'; }
  function thumbOf(p) { var m = (p.media || []).filter(function (x) { return x && x.url && String(x.kind || x.mime || '').indexOf('video') < 0; })[0]; return m ? m.url : null; }
  function hasVideo(p) { return (p.media || []).some(function (x) { return x && String(x.kind || x.mime || '').indexOf('video') >= 0; }); }
  function titleOf(p) { var t = String(p.title || '').trim(); if (t) return t; var c = String(p.caption || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); return c ? (c.length > 60 ? c.slice(0, 57) + '\u2026' : c) : 'Untitled post'; }
  function fn(name, body) { return db.functions.invoke(name, { body: body }).then(function (r) { if (r.error) throw r.error; if (r.data && r.data.error) throw new Error(r.data.error); return r.data; }); }
  /* wave225b: a post can be scheduled to go out when Instagram and Facebook can publish (the Meta token); every other platform
     goes out directly when connected, or by the reminder flow (social-publish tells the owner at the post's time) */
  /* wave241: the partner organizations (wa_partners), loaded once */
  var PARTNERS = null;
  function loadPartners() { if (PARTNERS) return Promise.resolve(PARTNERS); return db.from('wa_partners').select('id,name,status').order('name').then(function (r) { PARTNERS = ((r && r.data) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.status !== 'declined'; }); return PARTNERS; }).catch(function () { PARTNERS = []; return PARTNERS; }); }
  function partnerOpts(sel) { return (PARTNERS || []).map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + x.id + '"' + (String(sel || '') === String(x.id) ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(x.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + (sel && !(PARTNERS || []).some(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(sel); }) ? '<option value="' + E(String(sel)) + '" selected>Partner ' + E(String(sel)) + '</option>' : ''); }
  function canPublish(pls) { var c = C.cap; if (!c) return false; return (pls || []).length > 0 && (pls || []).every(function (p) { return (p === 'instagram' || p === 'facebook') ? !!(c.token && c.can && c.can[p] === true) : true; }); }
  function modeOf(p) { var m = C.cap && C.cap.mode && C.cap.mode[p]; if (m) return m; if (p === 'instagram' || p === 'facebook') return C.cap && C.cap.can && C.cap.can[p] ? 'direct' : 'reminder'; return 'reminder'; }
  function isPub() { return C.pub === true; }
  function hashCap() { var s = C.cap && C.cap.studio; var n = s && Number(s.hashtag_cap); return n > 0 ? n : 5; }
  function vOf(e, pl) { e.variants = e.variants || {}; return e.variants[pl] = e.variants[pl] || {}; }
  function capOf(e, pl) { var v = (e.variants || {})[pl] || {}; return (v.caption != null && String(v.caption).trim() !== '') ? String(v.caption) : (e.caption || ''); }
  function kindOf(e, pl) { var v = (e.variants || {})[pl] || {}; if (v.kind) return v.kind; if (pl === 'instagram' && vids(e).length) return 'reel'; return 'feed'; }
  function tagColor(n) { var t = (C.tags || []).filter(function (x) { return x.name === n; })[0]; return t ? t.color : '#6F64AB'; }
  function anyPublish() { var c = C.cap; return !!(c && c.token && c.can && (c.can.instagram || c.can.facebook)); }

  /* the state a post is really in, including what the analytics confirmed */
  function stateOf(p) {
    var now = Date.now(); var due = p.scheduled_at ? new Date(p.scheduled_at).getTime() : null;
    var liveMap = {}; Object.keys(p.live || {}).forEach(function (k) { if (p.live[k] && (p.live[k].external_id || p.live[k].url)) liveMap[k] = p.live[k]; });
    var results = p.results || {};
    var up = Object.keys(results).filter(function (k) { return results[k] && (results[k].id || results[k].url); });
    var s = p.status || 'draft'; var cls = (ST[s] || ST.draft).cls; var label = (ST[s] || ST.draft).label; var attn = false; var note = '';
    /* wave225b: approvals come first while a post has not gone out */
    var ap = p.approval_status || 'none', notOut = ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(s) >= 0;
    if (notOut && ap === 'pending') return { cls: 'appr', label: 'Waiting for approval', attn: false, note: 'Sent for approval' + (p.submitted_by ? ' by ' + String(p.submitted_by).split('@')[0] : '') + '; a marketing lead or an admin approves it', live: liveMap, up: up };
    if (notOut && ap === 'changes') return { cls: 'bad', label: 'Changes asked', attn: true, note: p.approval_note || 'Changes asked before it can go out', live: liveMap, up: up };
    if (s === 'scheduled' && due && due < now - 15 * 60000) { cls = 'due'; label = 'Due'; attn = true; note = canPublish(p.platforms) ? 'Was due at ' + hm(p.scheduled_at) + '; publishing did not run' : 'Due: post it in Meta Business Suite; the hub confirms it when it shows up'; }
    else if (s === 'scheduled') note = canPublish(p.platforms) ? 'Publishes automatically at ' + hm(p.scheduled_at) : 'Planned for ' + niceWhen(p.scheduled_at) + '; post it by hand (publishing is off)';
    else if (s === 'failed') { attn = true; if (up.length) { label = 'Partly published'; } note = p.error || 'Publishing failed'; }
    else if (s === 'publishing') { var pend = Object.keys(results).some(function (k) { return results[k] && results[k].pending; }); note = pend ? 'Instagram is processing the video; it goes up within a few minutes' : 'Publishing now'; if (now - new Date(p.updated_at || p.created_at || now).getTime() > 15 * 60000) attn = true; }
    else if (s === 'published' || s === 'posted') {
      var missing = (p.platforms || []).filter(function (k) { return !liveMap[k]; });
      var ago = p.published_at ? (now - new Date(p.published_at).getTime()) / 3600000 : 0;
      var connected = function (k) { return ((C.cap && C.cap.accounts) || []).some(function (a) { return a.platform === k; }); };
      var byHand = (p.platforms || []).filter(function (k) { var r = (p.results || {})[k]; return r && r.manual && !r.manual_done && !liveMap[k]; });
      if (byHand.length) { note = 'Waiting to be posted by hand on ' + byHand.map(function (k) { return PL[k] ? PL[k].label : k; }).join(' and '); attn = (now - new Date(p.scheduled_at || p.published_at || now).getTime()) > 6 * 3600000; }
      else if (!missing.length) note = 'Live on ' + (p.platforms || []).map(function (k) { return PL[k] ? PL[k].label : k; }).join(' and ');
      else if (ago > 36 && missing.some(connected)) { note = 'Not seen in the analytics yet on ' + missing.filter(connected).map(function (k) { return PL[k] ? PL[k].label : k; }).join(' and '); attn = true; }
      else note = s === 'posted' ? 'Marked posted; the numbers arrive with the next morning pull' : 'Published; the numbers arrive with the next morning pull';
    }
    return { cls: cls, label: label, attn: attn, note: note, live: liveMap, up: up };
  }
  function draggable(p) { return staff() && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'failed'].indexOf(p.status || 'draft') >= 0 && !(p.status === 'failed' && stateOf(p).up.length); }
  function matches(p) {
    var f = C.filter;
    if (f.pl && (p.platforms || []).indexOf(f.pl) < 0) return false;
    if (f.st) {
      var s = stateOf(p);
      if (f.st === 'planned' && ['draft', 'scheduled'].indexOf(p.status) < 0) return false;
      if (f.st === 'live' && ['published', 'posted'].indexOf(p.status) < 0) return false;
      if (f.st === 'attn' && !s.attn) return false;
      if (f.st === 'drafts' && p.status !== 'draft') return false;
      if (f.st === 'appr' && ['pending', 'changes'].indexOf(p.approval_status || 'none') < 0) return false;
    }
    if (f.tag && (p.tags || []).indexOf(f.tag) < 0) return false;
    if (f.q) { var q = f.q.toLowerCase(); if ([p.title, p.caption, p.campaign, p.owner, p.notes, (p.tags || []).join(' ')].join(' ').toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false; }
    return true;
  }
  function dated() { return C.posts.filter(function (p) { return p.scheduled_at && p.status !== 'cancelled' && matches(p); }); }
  function undated() { return C.posts.filter(function (p) { return !p.scheduled_at && p.status !== 'cancelled' && matches(p); }); }
  function byDay() { var m = {}; dated().forEach(function (p) { var d = dayOf(p.scheduled_at); (m[d] = m[d] || []).push(p); }); Object.keys(m).forEach(function (k) { m[k].sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.scheduled_at).localeCompare(String(b.scheduled_at)); }); }); return m; }

  /* ---------- data ---------- */
  async function load(force) {
    if (C.loading) return;
    if (!force && C.loaded && Date.now() - C.at < 30000) { paint(); return; }
    C.loading = true; C.err = null; if (!C.loaded) paint();
    try {
      var r = await db.from('content_posts').select('*').order('scheduled_at', { ascending: true }).limit(1500);
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var all121 = (r.data || []).map(function (p) { p.platforms = p.platforms || []; p.media = p.media || []; p.results = p.results || {}; p.live = p.live || {}; p.variants = p.variants || {}; p.tags = p.tags || []; p.manual = p.manual || {}; return p; });
      loadStudio();
      /* wave136: an idea in the bank is out of the calendar until its date (the bank page lists it) */
      C.banked = all121.filter(function (p) { return !!p.banked_at; });
      C.posts = all121.filter(function (p) { return !p.banked_at; });
      loadSources();
      C.loaded = true; C.at = Date.now();
    } catch (e) { C.err = e.message || String(e); }
    C.loading = false; paint();
    if (!C.cap && staff() && !C.capBusy) loadCap();
    if (!C.perf && !C.perfBusy) loadPerf();
  }
  /* wave225b: the Content Studio's own data (who publishes, tags, the library, the posting slots) */
  async function loadStudio() {
    try {
      var rs = await Promise.all([
        C.pub === null ? db.rpc('wa225_publisher') : Promise.resolve({ data: C.pub }),
        db.from('content_tags').select('*').order('name'),
        db.from('content_snippets').select('*').order('name'),
        db.from('content_slots').select('*').order('weekday').order('at')
      ]);
      C.pub = !!(rs[0] && rs[0].data === true);
      C.tags = (rs[1] && rs[1].data) || []; C.lib = (rs[2] && rs[2].data) || []; C.slots = (rs[3] && rs[3].data) || [];
      if (C.edit) paintDrawer(); else paint();
    } catch (e) { /* the calendar works without them */ }
  }
  /* the next free posting slot for these platforms (content_slots), after now and not already taken by a post */
  function nextQueueSlot(pls) {
    var slots = (C.slots || []).filter(function (s) { return !pls || !pls.length || pls.indexOf(s.platform) >= 0; });
    if (!slots.length) return null;
    var taken = {}; C.posts.forEach(function (p) { if (p.scheduled_at && p.status !== 'cancelled') taken[dayOf(p.scheduled_at) + ' ' + hm(p.scheduled_at)] = 1; });
    var t = today(), nowHm = hm(new Date().toISOString());
    for (var i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
      var d = addDays(t, i), wd = dowOf(d);
      var opts = slots.filter(function (s) { return Number(s.weekday) === wd; }).map(function (s) { return String(s.at).slice(0, 5); }).sort();
      for (var j = 0; j < opts.length; j++) { if (i === 0 && opts[j] <= nowHm) continue; if (!taken[d + ' ' + opts[j]]) return { date: d, time: opts[j] }; }
    }
    return null;
  }
  /* wave136: the meeting each idea came from */
  async function loadSources() {
    try {
      C.src = C.src || {}; var ids = [];
      C.posts.concat(C.banked || []).forEach(function (p) { var s = p.source_transcript_id; if (s && !C.src[s] && ids.indexOf(s) < 0) ids.push(s); });
      if (!ids.length) return;
      var r = await db.from('transcripts').select('id, title, meeting_date').in('id', ids.slice(0, 200));
      ((r && r.data) || []).forEach(function (x) { C.src[x.id] = { title: x.title || null, date: x.meeting_date || null }; });
      ids.forEach(function (id) { if (!C.src[id]) C.src[id] = { title: null, date: null }; });
      paint(); if (C.edit) paintDrawer();
    } catch (e) { /* the ideas still show, without the meeting's name */ }
  }
  function srcOf(p) { if (!p || !p.source_transcript_id) return null; return (C.src && C.src[p.source_transcript_id]) || { title: null, date: null }; }
  function shortDay(ds) { return niceDate(ds, String(ds).slice(0, 4) === today().slice(0, 4) ? { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' } : { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); }
  function ideaLine(p) {
    var bits = [], s = srcOf(p);
    if (p.resurfaced_at && (Date.now() - new Date(p.resurfaced_at).getTime()) < 14 * 86400000) bits.push('<i>Back from the bank</i>');
    if (p.idea_for) bits.push('For ' + E(shortDay(p.idea_for)));
    if (s) bits.push('From ' + (s.title ? E(s.title) : 'a meeting'));
    else if (p.suggested_by) bits.push('Idea from ' + E(p.suggested_by));
    return bits.join(' \u00b7 ');
  }
  window.w121Stale = function () { C.at = 0; };
  async function loadCap() {
    C.capBusy = true;
    try { C.cap = await fn('social-publish', { action: 'capabilities' }); applyRegistry(); } catch (e) { C.cap = { token: false, error: e.message || String(e), can: {}, accounts: [] }; }
    C.capBusy = false; paint(); if (C.edit) paintDrawer();
  }
  /* the analytics (90 days) for the best slot and the handle */
  async function loadPerf() {
    C.perfBusy = true;
    try {
      var d = window.w120Data ? window.w120Data() : null;
      if (!d || !d.posts_all) { var r = await db.rpc('social_overview', { p_days: 90 }); if (r && !r.error) d = r.data; }
      C.perf = d || {};
    } catch (e) { C.perf = {}; }
    C.perfBusy = false; paint();
  }
  function bestSlot() { try { return C.perf && window.w120BestSlot ? window.w120BestSlot(C.perf) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
  function igHandle() { var a = ((C.perf && C.perf.accounts) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.platform === 'instagram'; })[0] || ((C.cap && C.cap.accounts) || []).filter(function (x) { return x.platform === 'instagram'; })[0]; return (a && a.handle) || 'welfareavenue'; }
  function nextSlotDate(slot) {
    var t = today(); var h = slot && slot.h != null ? String(slot.h).padStart(2, '0') + ':00' : '18:00';
    if (!slot || !slot.wd) { var d0 = addDays(t, 1); return { date: d0, time: h }; }
    var want = WD.indexOf(slot.wd); for (var i = 1; i <= 7; i++) { var d = addDays(t, i); if (dowOf(d) === want) return { date: d, time: h }; }
    return { date: addDays(t, 1), time: h };
  }
  async function saveRow(id, patch) {
    patch.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); patch.updated_by = me() || 'staff';
    var r = await db.from('content_posts').update(patch).eq('id', id).select('*');
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    var row = (r.data && r.data[0]) || null;
    C.posts = C.posts.map(function (p) { if (String(p.id) !== String(id)) return p; var n = Object.assign({}, p, patch, row || {}); n.platforms = n.platforms || []; n.media = n.media || []; n.results = n.results || {}; n.live = n.live || {}; return n; });
    return row;
  }
  async function insertRow(row) {
    row.created_by = me() || 'staff'; row.updated_by = row.created_by;
    var r = await db.from('content_posts').insert(row).select('*').single();
    if (r.error) throw r.error;
    var n = Object.assign({}, row, r.data || {}); if (n.id == null) n.id = 'tmp' + Date.now();
    n.platforms = n.platforms || []; n.media = n.media || []; n.results = n.results || {}; n.live = n.live || {};
    C.posts.push(n); return n;
  }
  function logIt(id, field, oldV, newV) { try { logChange('content_post', id, field, oldV, newV); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } }

  /* ---------- the page ---------- */
  function panel() {
    var sec = document.getElementById('contentcal');
    if (!sec) { var main = document.querySelector('main'); if (!main) return null; sec = document.createElement('section'); sec.id = 'contentcal'; sec.className = 'panel'; main.appendChild(sec); }
    return sec;
  }
  function capBanner() {
    /* wave225c: one line per way of posting, from social-publish capabilities */
    if (!staff()) return '';
    var c = C.cap;
    if (!c) return '<div class="cap"><div><b>Checking the connections\u2026</b></div></div>';
    var by = {}; PORDER.forEach(function (k) { var m = modeOf(k); (by[m] = by[m] || []).push(PL[k].label); });
    var bits = []; ['direct', 'make', 'drafts', 'reminder'].forEach(function (m) { if (by[m]) bits.push('<span style="display:block">' + E(MODE_L[m]) + ': <b style="display:inline">' + E(by[m].join(', ')) + '</b></span>'); });
    var allOn = !by.reminder;
    return '<div class="cap' + (allOn || (c.can && c.can.instagram && c.can.facebook) ? ' on' : '') + '"><div><b>' + (c.can && c.can.instagram && c.can.facebook ? 'Instagram and Facebook publish from here.' : 'Plan and confirm mode for Meta') + '</b>' + bits.join('') + '<span style="display:block;margin-top:4px">Reminders reach the post\u2019s owner at its time (a Hub notification and a Slack DM) with the caption and media to post by hand. <a data-w121go="#studio/connections">Connections</a></span></div></div>';
  }
  function glance() {
    var now = Date.now(); var t = today(); var mon = mondayOf(t), sun = addDays(mon, 6);
    var wk = C.posts.filter(function (p) { var d = dayOf(p.scheduled_at || p.published_at); return d && d >= mon && d <= sun && p.status !== 'cancelled'; });
    var planned = wk.filter(function (p) { return ['draft', 'scheduled', 'publishing'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; }).length;
    var live = wk.filter(function (p) { return ['published', 'posted'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; }).length;
    var next = C.posts.filter(function (p) { return p.scheduled_at && new Date(p.scheduled_at).getTime() > now && ['draft', 'scheduled'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.scheduled_at).localeCompare(String(b.scheduled_at)); })[0];
    var attn = C.posts.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'cancelled' && stateOf(p).attn; }).length;
    var ideas = C.posts.filter(function (p) { return !p.scheduled_at && p.status === 'draft'; }).length;
    var reach = 0; C.posts.forEach(function (p) { var d = dayOf(p.published_at); if (!d || (now - new Date(p.published_at).getTime()) > 30 * 86400000) return; Object.keys(p.live || {}).forEach(function (k) { reach += Number((p.live[k] || {}).reach || 0); }); });
    var slot = bestSlot();
    return '<div class="glance">' +
      '<div><b>' + planned + '</b><span>planned this week</span></div>' +
      '<div><b>' + live + '</b><span>live this week</span></div>' +
      '<div' + (next ? ' data-w121open="' + next.id + '"' + ' data-w121jump="1"' : '') + '><b>' + (next ? E(niceDate(dayOf(next.scheduled_at), { weekday: 'short' }) + ' ' + hm(next.scheduled_at)) : '\u2013') + '</b><span>next post</span>' + (next ? '<em>' + E(titleOf(next)) + '</em>' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div data-w121jump="attn"><b class="' + (attn ? 'warn' : '') + '">' + attn + '</b><span>need attention</span></div>' +
      '<div><b>' + ideas + '</b><span>ideas without a date' + (C.banked && C.banked.length ? ', ' + C.banked.length + ' more in the bank' : '') + '</span></div>' +
      '<div><b>' + (reach ? num(reach) : '\u2013') + '</b><span>reach from calendar posts, 30 days</span></div>' +
      (slot ? '<div title="From the analytics insights"><b style="font-size:20px">' + E(slot.label) + '</b><span>best slot to post</span></div>' : '') +
      '</div>';
  }

    var a = C.anchor, v = C.view; var label = v === 'month' ? niceDate(a, { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }) : v === 'week' ? (function () { var m = mondayOf(a); return niceDate(m, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }) + ' to ' + niceDate(addDays(m, 6), { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' }); })() : 'All posts';
    return '<div class="w116crumb"><a data-w116go="#mypage">Home</a> &middot; Teams &middot; <a data-w116go="#team/marketing">Marketing, Branding &amp; Web</a> &middot; Content calendar</div>' +

Drag a post to move it; drop an idea on a day to plan it.</p></div>' +
      '<div class="w120ctl" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">' + (staff() ? '<button class="btn" data-w121new="1">New post</button><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w121set="1" title="Slack confirmations">Settings</button>' : '') + '<a class="btn mini ghost" data-w121go="#socialstats">Analytics</a></div></div>' +
      '<div class="ctl"><span class="w116seg">' + [['month', 'Month'], ['week', 'Week'], ['list', 'List']].map(function (x) { return '<a data-w121view="' + x[0] + '" class="' + (v === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '">' + x[1] + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</span>' +

      ((C.tags || []).length ? '<i></i><select data-w225ftag aria-label="Tag"><option value="">Every tag</option>' + C.tags.map(function (t) { return '<option' + (C.filter.tag === t.name ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(t.name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function settingsPanel() {
    if (!C.settingsOpen || !staff()) return '';
    var s = (C.cap && C.cap.settings) || {}; var chans = C.channels || [];
    var cur = s.slack_channel || 'social-media';
