' checked' : '') + '> Confirm in Slack when a post goes live</label><select id="w121chan">' + opts + '</select><button class="btn mini" data-w121saveset="1">Save</button><span style="color:var(--wa-muted)">One message per post and platform: published from here, or seen in the analytics.</span></div>';
  }
  function chip(p) {
    var s = stateOf(p); var th = thumbOf(p);
    return '<div class="ch st-' + s.cls + '" data-w121open="' + p.id + '" draggable="' + (draggable(p) ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w121card="' + p.id + '" title="' + E(titleOf(p) + ' \u00b7 ' + s.label + (s.note ? ' \u00b7 ' + s.note : '')) + '">' + (th ? '<img src="' + E(th) + '" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '') + '<span class="tm">' + hm(p.scheduled_at) + '</span>' + marks(p.platforms, s.live) + '<span class="tt">' + E(titleOf(p)) + '</span></div>';
  }
  function monthView() {
    var a = C.anchor; var first = a.slice(0, 8) + '01'; var start = mondayOf(first); var m = byDay(); var t = today(); var slot = bestSlot();
    var html = '<div class="mg">' + WD.map(function (d) { return '<div class="dow">' + d + '</div>'; }).join('');
    var monthNo = first.slice(5, 7);
    for (var i = 0; i < 42; i++) {
      var d = addDays(start, i); if (i >= 35 && d.slice(5, 7) !== monthNo) break;
      var list = m[d] || []; var shown = list.slice(0, 4);
      var cls = 'd' + (d.slice(5, 7) !== monthNo ? ' out' : '') + (d === t ? ' today' : '') + (d < t ? ' past' : '') + (slot && slot.wd && WD[dowOf(d)] === slot.wd && d >= t ? ' best' : '');
      html += '<div class="' + cls + '" data-w121day="' + d + '"' + (slot && slot.wd && WD[dowOf(d)] === slot.wd ? ' title="Best day to post, from the insights"' : '') + '><div class="dh"><span class="dn">' + Number(d.slice(8, 10)) + '</span>' + (staff() && d >= t ? '<button class="add" data-w121new="' + d + '" title="New post on this day">+</button>' : '') + '</div>' + shown.map(chip).join('') + (list.length > shown.length ? '<div class="more" data-w121more="' + d + '">+' + (list.length - shown.length) + ' more</div>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function weekCard(p) {
    var s = stateOf(p); var th = thumbOf(p);
    return '<div class="wk st-' + s.cls + '" data-w121open="' + p.id + '" draggable="' + (draggable(p) ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w121card="' + p.id + '">' + (th ? '<img src="' + E(th) + '" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '') + '<div class="row"><span class="tm">' + hm(p.scheduled_at) + '</span>' + marks(p.platforms, s.live) + '<span style="flex:1"></span><span class="pill ' + s.cls + '">' + E(s.label) + '</span></div><b>' + E(titleOf(p)) + '</b>' + (p.caption ? '<div class="cap2">' + E(p.caption) + '</div>' : '') + confLine(p, s) + '</div>';
  }
  function confLine(p, s) {
    s = s || stateOf(p);
    var bits = Object.keys(s.live).map(function (k) { var l = s.live[k]; var n = []; if (l.reach != null) n.push(num(l.reach) + ' reach'); else if (l.views != null) n.push(num(l.views) + ' views'); if (l.likes != null) n.push(num(l.likes) + ' likes'); return (PL[k] ? PL[k].label : k) + (n.length ? ' ' + n.join(', ') : ' live'); });
    if (bits.length) return '<div class="cf ok">\u2713 ' + E(bits.join(' \u00b7 ')) + '</div>';
    if (s.note) return '<div class="cf ' + (s.attn ? 'bad' : s.cls === 'sched' ? 'warn' : 'muted') + '">' + E(s.note) + '</div>';
    return '';
  }
  function weekView() {
    var mon = mondayOf(C.anchor); var m = byDay(); var t = today();
    var html = '<div class="wg">';
    for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      var d = addDays(mon, i); var list = m[d] || [];
      html += '<div class="wc' + (d === t ? ' today' : '') + '" data-w121day="' + d + '"><div class="wh"><b>' + WD[i] + ' ' + Number(d.slice(8, 10)) + '</b><span>' + list.length + ' post' + (list.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>' + (staff() && d >= t ? '<button class="add" data-w121new="' + d + '" title="New post on this day">+</button>' : '') + '</div>' + list.map(weekCard).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    return html + '</div>';
  }
  function listRow(p) {
    var s = stateOf(p); var th = thumbOf(p);
    return '<div class="lr" data-w121open="' + p.id + '"><div class="when">' + (p.scheduled_at ? E(niceDate(dayOf(p.scheduled_at))) + '<span>' + hm(p.scheduled_at) + '</span>' : 'No date<span>' + (p.idea_for ? 'for ' + E(shortDay(p.idea_for)) : 'idea') + '</span>') + '</div>' + (th ? '<img src="' + E(th) + '" alt="" loading="lazy">' : '<div class="ph">' + (hasVideo(p) ? '\u25b6' : '\u270e') + '</div>') + '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + E(titleOf(p)) + '</b><div class="cap2">' + E(p.caption || p.notes || '') + '</div>' + (function () { var il = p.scheduled_at ? '' : ideaLine(p); return il ? '<div class="src">' + il + '</div>' : ''; })() + confLine(p, s) + '</div><div class="rt"><span class="pill ' + s.cls + '">' + E(s.label) + '</span>' + marks(p.platforms, s.live) + (p.owner ? '<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--wa-muted)">' + E(p.owner) + '</span>' : '') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function listView() {
    var all = C.posts.filter(function (p) { return p.status !== 'cancelled' && matches(p); }); var now = Date.now();
    var attn = all.filter(function (p) { return stateOf(p).attn; });
    var up = all.filter(function (p) { return p.scheduled_at && new Date(p.scheduled_at).getTime() >= now - 15 * 60000 && !stateOf(p).attn && ['draft', 'scheduled', 'publishing'].indexOf(p.status) >= 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(a.scheduled_at).localeCompare(String(b.scheduled_at)); });
    var ideas = all.filter(function (p) { return !p.scheduled_at && !stateOf(p).attn; });
    var past = all.filter(function (p) { return attn.indexOf(p) < 0 && up.indexOf(p) < 0 && ideas.indexOf(p) < 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return String(b.published_at || b.scheduled_at || '').localeCompare(String(a.published_at || a.scheduled_at || '')); });
    function grp(title, sub, rows) { if (!rows.length) return ''; return '<h4>' + E(title) + ' <small>' + E(sub) + '</small></h4>' + rows.map(listRow).join(''); }
    var html = '<div class="lg">' + grp('Needs attention', attn.length + ' post' + (attn.length === 1 ? '' : 's'), attn) + grp('Coming up', up.length + ' planned', up) + grp('Ideas and drafts without a date', ideas.length + '', ideas) + grp('Posted', past.length + ' in the calendar', past.slice(0, 80)) + '</div>';
    if (!all.length) html = '<div class="loading">' + (C.filter.q || C.filter.pl || C.filter.st ? 'Nothing matches the filters.' : 'Nothing planned yet. ' + (staff() ? 'Start with New post, or plan one from the insights on the Analytics page.' : '')) + '</div>';
    return html;
  }
  function tray() {
    var list = undated();
    return '<div class="tray" data-w121tray="1"><h4>Ideas</h4><p>Posts without a date. Drag one onto a day to plan it; drag a planned post here to take it off the calendar. Ideas from marketing meetings land here too.</p>' + (list.length ? list.map(function (p) { var th = thumbOf(p); return '<div class="tr" draggable="' + (draggable(p) ? 'true' : 'false') + '" data-w121card="' + p.id + '" data-w121open="' + p.id + '">' + (th ? '<img src="' + E(th) + '" alt="">' : '') + '<div style="min-width:0"><b>' + E(titleOf(p)) + '</b><span>' + (p.campaign ? E(p.campaign) + ' \u00b7 ' : '') + (p.platforms || []).map(function (k) { return PL[k] ? PL[k].label : k; }).join(', ') + '</span>' + (function () { var il = ideaLine(p); return il ? '<em>' + il + '</em>' : ''; })() + '</div></div>'; }).join('') : '<div class="empty">No ideas waiting.</div>') + (staff() ? '<button class="btn mini ghost" style="width:100%;margin-top:6px" data-w121new="idea">New idea</button>' : '') + (C.banked && C.banked.length ? '<a class="bnk" href="#bank/marketing">' + C.banked.length + (C.banked.length === 1 ? ' idea waits' : ' ideas wait') + ' in the bank</a>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function paint() {
    var sec = panel(); if (!sec) return;
    if (!C.view) C.view = (window.innerWidth || 1200) < 700 ? 'list' : 'month';
    if (!C.anchor) C.anchor = today();
    var body;
    if (C.err) body = '<div class="loading">Could not load the calendar: ' + E(C.err) + '</div>';
    else if (!C.loaded) body = '<div class="loading">Loading the calendar\u2026</div>';
    else body = capBanner() + settingsPanel() + glance() + '<div class="lay' + (C.view === 'list' ? ' list' : '') + '"><div>' + (C.view === 'month' ? monthView() : C.view === 'week' ? weekView() : listView()) + '</div>' + (C.view !== 'list' ? tray() : '') + '</div>';
    var q = document.getElementById('w121q'); var hadFocus = q && document.activeElement === q; var pos = hadFocus ? q.selectionStart : 0;
    sec.innerHTML = '<div class="w121wrap">' + header() + body + '</div>' + (C.undo ? '<div class="undo"><span>' + E(C.undo.text) + '</span><a data-w121undo="1">Undo</a></div>' : '');
    if (hadFocus) { var q2 = document.getElementById('w121q'); if (q2) { q2.focus(); try { q2.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (e) { /* fine */ } } }
  }

  /* ---------- moves (drag and drop), with Undo ---------- */
  async function move(id, day) {
    var p = C.posts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0]; if (!p || !draggable(p)) return;
    var before = { scheduled_at: p.scheduled_at, status: p.status };
    var patch;
    if (!day) patch = { scheduled_at: null, status: 'draft' };
    else {
      var t = p.scheduled_at ? hm(p.scheduled_at) : (function () { var s = bestSlot(); return s && s.h != null ? String(s.h).padStart(2, '0') + ':00' : '18:00'; })();
      patch = { scheduled_at: isoOf(day, t) };
      if (p.status === 'failed') patch.status = 'scheduled';
      if (dayOf(p.scheduled_at) === day) return;
    }
    try {
      await saveRow(p.id, patch);
      logIt(p.id, 'scheduled_at', before.scheduled_at, patch.scheduled_at);
      C.undo = { id: p.id, patch: before, text: day ? 'Moved to ' + niceDate(day) + ', ' + hm(patch.scheduled_at) : 'Taken off the calendar' };
      clearTimeout(C.undoT); C.undoT = setTimeout(function () { C.undo = null; paint(); }, 7000);
      paint();
    } catch (e) { toastS('Could not move it: ' + (e.message || e)); }
  }
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function (e) {
    var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var c = t.closest('#contentcal [data-w121card]'); if (!c || c.getAttribute('draggable') !== 'true') return;
    C.drag = c.getAttribute('data-w121card'); c.classList.add('dragging');
    try { e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move'; e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', 'w121:' + C.drag); } catch (e2) { /* fine */ }
  });
  document.addEventListener('dragend', function () { C.drag = null; document.querySelectorAll('#contentcal .dragging').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('dragging'); }); document.querySelectorAll('#contentcal .over').forEach(function (x) { x.classList.remove('over'); }); });
  document.addEventListener('dragover', function (e) {
    if (!C.drag) return; var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var z = t.closest('#contentcal [data-w121day]') || t.closest('#contentcal [data-w121tray]'); if (!z) return;
    e.preventDefault(); document.querySelectorAll('#contentcal .over').forEach(function (x) { if (x !== z) x.classList.remove('over'); }); z.classList.add('over');
  });
  document.addEventListener('drop', async function (e) {
    if (!C.drag) return; var t = e.target; if (!t || !t.closest) return;
    var z = t.closest('#contentcal [data-w121day]') || t.closest('#contentcal [data-w121tray]'); if (!z) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var id = C.drag; C.drag = null; z.classList.remove('over');
    var day = z.getAttribute('data-w121day'); if (day && day < today()) { toastS('That day has passed; pick today or later'); return; }
    await move(id, day || null);
  }, true);

  /* ---------- the composer ---------- */
  function blank(day) {
    var slot = bestSlot(); var ns = day && day !== '1' && day !== 'idea' ? { date: day, time: slot && slot.h != null ? String(slot.h).padStart(2, '0') + ':00' : '18:00' } : (day === 'idea' ? { date: '', time: '' } : nextSlotDate(slot));
    return { id: null, title: '', platforms: ['instagram', 'facebook'], caption: '', link_url: '', media: [], date: ns.date, time: ns.time, owner: me(), campaign: '', notes: '', status: 'draft', results: {}, live: {}, error: null, dirty: false, tab: 'instagram', ai: null, aiBusy: false, lang: 'en', manual: false,
      /* wave225d: the Content Studio */
      variants: {}, first_comment: '', tags: [], repeat: '', repeat_times: 4, track_links: true, utm_campaign: '', approval_status: 'none', approval_note: '', submitted_by: null, manualMap: {}, vtab: 'instagram', altIdx: null, apNote: '',
      /* wave241 */ canva_url: '', partner_id: null, article: false };
  }
  function fromPost(p) {
    var rr = p.repeat_rule || {};
    return { id: p.id, title: p.title || '', platforms: (p.platforms || []).slice(), caption: p.caption || '', link_url: p.link_url || '', media: (p.media || []).map(function (m) { var c = Object.assign({}, m); if (m.tags) c.tags = m.tags.map(function (t) { return Object.assign({}, t); }); return c; }), date: p.scheduled_at ? dayOf(p.scheduled_at) : '', time: p.scheduled_at ? hm(p.scheduled_at) : '', owner: p.owner || '', campaign: p.campaign || '', notes: p.notes || '', status: p.status || 'draft', results: p.results || {}, live: p.live || {}, error: p.error || null, published_at: p.published_at || null, dirty: false, tab: (p.platforms || [])[0] || 'instagram', ai: null, aiBusy: false, lang: 'en', manual: false,
      variants: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.variants || {})), first_comment: p.first_comment || '', tags: (p.tags || []).slice(), repeat: rr.every || '', repeat_times: rr.times || 4, track_links: p.track_links !== false, utm_campaign: (p.utm && p.utm.campaign) || '', approval_status: p.approval_status || 'none', approval_note: p.approval_note || '', submitted_by: p.submitted_by || null, manualMap: p.manual || {}, vtab: (p.platforms || [])[0] || 'instagram', altIdx: null, apNote: '',
      /* wave241 */ canva_url: p.canva_url || '', partner_id: p.partner_id || null, article: p.format === 'article' };
  }
  function openComposer(pOrDay, prefill) {
    if (!allowed()) return;
    var p = pOrDay && typeof pOrDay === 'object' ? pOrDay : null;
    C.edit = p ? fromPost(p) : blank(pOrDay);
    if (prefill) { if (prefill.title) C.edit.title = prefill.title; if (prefill.notes) C.edit.notes = prefill.notes; if (prefill.campaign) C.edit.campaign = prefill.campaign; if (prefill.slot) { var ns = nextSlotDate(prefill.slot); C.edit.date = ns.date; C.edit.time = ns.time; }
      /* wave225d: the Content Studio's templates, queue and approvals open the composer with more */
      if (prefill.platforms) C.edit.platforms = prefill.platforms.slice(); if (prefill.caption != null) C.edit.caption = prefill.caption; if (prefill.first_comment != null) C.edit.first_comment = prefill.first_comment; if (prefill.tags) C.edit.tags = prefill.tags.slice(); if (prefill.variants) C.edit.variants = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prefill.variants)); if (prefill.date) { C.edit.date = prefill.date; C.edit.time = prefill.time || C.edit.time || '18:00'; }
      if (prefill.media) C.edit.media = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prefill.media)); /* wave277 */
      C.edit.dirty = true; }
    paintDrawer();
    if (!C.cap && staff() && !C.capBusy) loadCap();
    if (!C.perf && !C.perfBusy) loadPerf();
    setTimeout(function () { var t = document.getElementById(C.edit && C.edit.id ? 'w121cap' : 'w121title'); if (t && staff() && !readOnly()) t.focus(); }, 60);
  }
  function readOnly() { var e = C.edit; if (!e) return true; if (!staff()) return true; return ['publishing', 'published', 'posted'].indexOf(e.status) >= 0; }
  function closeComposer(force) {
    if (!C.edit) return;
    if (!force && C.edit.dirty && !readOnly() && !confirm('Close without saving the changes?')) return;
    C.edit = null; var d = document.getElementById('w121dr'); if (d) d.remove();
    try { if (/^#contentcal\/./.test(location.hash)) history.replaceState(null, '', '#contentcal'); } catch (e) { /* fine */ }
  }
  function imgs(e) { return (e.media || []).filter(function (m) { return String(m.kind || m.mime || '').indexOf('video') < 0; }); }
  function vids(e) { return (e.media || []).filter(function (m) { return String(m.kind || m.mime || '').indexOf('video') >= 0; }); }
  function ratioOf(m) { return m && m.w && m.h ? m.w / m.h : null; }
  function ratioLabel(r) { if (!r) return ''; var known = [[1, '1:1'], [0.8, '4:5'], [1.91, '1.91:1'], [0.5625, '9:16'], [1.7778, '16:9'], [0.6667, '2:3'], [1.5, '3:2'], [0.75, '3:4'], [1.3333, '4:3']]; for (var i = 0; i < known.length; i++) if (Math.abs(known[i][0] - r) < 0.02) return known[i][1]; return r.toFixed(2) + ':1'; }
  function igRatioOk(r) { return r != null && r >= 0.795 && r <= 1.915; }
  function tagsOf(s) { return (String(s || '').match(/#[\w\u00c0-\u024f]+/g) || []); }
  function validate(e) {
    /* wave225d: one list per platform, plus the shared one */
    var out = { all: [], p: {}, errors: 0 };
    function L(k) { return out.p[k] = out.p[k] || []; }
    function err(list, t) { list.push({ t: t, k: 'e' }); out.errors++; } function warn(list, t) { list.push({ t: t, k: 'w' }); } function ok(list, t) { list.push({ t: t, k: 'k' }); }
    var im = imgs(e), vi = vids(e), cap = hashCap();
    if (!e.platforms.length) err(out.all, 'Pick at least one platform.');
    if ((e.media || []).some(function (m) { return m.uploading; })) err(out.all, 'Wait for the uploads to finish.');
    e.platforms.forEach(function (k) {
      var l = L(k), c = capOf(e, k), kind = kindOf(e, k), md = modeOf(k), nm = PL[k] ? PL[k].label : k, dur = vi[0] && vi[0].dur;
      if (k === 'instagram') {
        if (kind === 'story') { if (!im.length && !vi.length) err(l, 'An Instagram story needs a photo or a video.'); else ok(l, 'Instagram: a story (' + (vi.length ? 'the video' : 'the first photo') + ').'); if (vi.length && dur > 60) warn(l, 'Stories longer than 60 seconds are cut.'); }
        else if (kind === 'reel' || (vi.length && !im.length)) { if (!vi.length) err(l, 'An Instagram reel needs a video.'); else if (dur != null && (dur < 3 || dur > 900)) err(l, 'Reels run from 3 seconds to 15 minutes; this video is ' + Math.round(dur) + ' seconds.'); else ok(l, 'Instagram: a reel' + (dur ? ' (' + Math.round(dur) + ' s)' : '') + '.'); if (im.length) warn(l, 'A reel uses the video only; the photos stay off Instagram.'); }
        else if (!im.length && !vi.length) err(l, 'Instagram needs a photo or a video.');
        else {
          var igMax = capsOf(k).max_images || 10; if (im.length > igMax) err(l, 'Instagram takes up to ' + igMax + ' photos in a carousel.');
          var r0 = ratioOf(im[0]);
          if (r0 != null && !igRatioOk(r0)) err(l, 'The first photo is ' + ratioLabel(r0) + '; Instagram takes 4:5 to 1.91:1. Crop it below.');
          else if (im.length > 1 && im.some(function (m) { var r = ratioOf(m); return r != null && Math.abs(r - r0) > 0.03; })) warn(l, 'Instagram crops every photo in a carousel to the first one\u2019s shape (' + ratioLabel(r0) + ').');
          if (!l.filter(function (x) { return x.k === 'e'; }).length) ok(l, 'Instagram: ' + (im.length > 1 ? 'a carousel of ' + im.length : 'one photo') + (r0 ? ', ' + ratioLabel(r0) : '') + '.');
          if (im.some(function (m) { return !m.alt; })) warn(l, 'Add alt text to each photo (Alt on the photo) so people using screen readers know what it shows.');
        }
        if (kind !== 'story') {
          if (c.length > 2200) err(l, 'Instagram captions stop at 2,200 characters (' + c.length + ' now).');
          if (tagsOf(c).length > cap) err(l, 'Instagram takes ' + cap + ' hashtags a post since December 2025 (' + tagsOf(c).length + ' now).');
          if ((c.match(/(^|\s)@[\w.]+/g) || []).length > 20) err(l, 'Instagram allows 20 @mentions.');
          if (e.link_url && c.indexOf('bio') < 0) warn(l, 'Links in Instagram captions are not clickable; say \u201clink in bio\u201d.');
        }
        var col = ((e.variants.instagram || {}).collaborators || []); if (col.length > 3) err(l, 'Instagram takes up to 3 collaborators.');
      } else if (k === 'facebook') {
        if (kind === 'reel') { if (!vi.length) err(l, 'A Facebook Reel needs a video.'); else if (dur != null && (dur < 3 || dur > 90)) err(l, 'Facebook Reels run from 3 to 90 seconds; this video is ' + Math.round(dur) + '.'); else ok(l, 'Facebook: a Reel.'); }
        else if (kind === 'story') { if (!im.length && !vi.length) err(l, 'A Facebook story needs a photo or a video.'); else ok(l, 'Facebook: a story.'); }
        else if (!c.trim() && !e.link_url && !im.length && !vi.length) err(l, 'Facebook needs words, a link or media.');
        else ok(l, 'Facebook: ' + (vi.length ? 'a video' : im.length > 1 ? im.length + ' photos' : im.length ? 'a photo' : e.link_url ? 'a link post' : 'a text post') + (e.link_url && (im.length || vi.length) ? ' with the link in the text' : '') + '.');
        if (kind === 'feed' && im.length > 10) warn(l, 'Facebook gets the first 10 photos.');
      } else if (k === 'threads') {
        if (c.length > 500) err(l, 'Threads posts stop at 500 characters (' + c.length + ' now). Write a shorter one under Customize.');
        if (!c.trim() && !im.length && !vi.length) err(l, 'Threads needs words or media.');
        var thMax = capsOf(k).max_images || 20; if (im.length > thMax) err(l, 'Threads takes up to ' + thMax + ' photos.');
        if (!out.p[k].length) ok(l, 'Threads: ' + (vi.length ? 'a video' : im.length > 1 ? 'a carousel of ' + im.length : im.length ? 'a photo' : 'a text post') + '.');
      } else if (k === 'linkedin') {
        if (c.length > 3000) err(l, 'LinkedIn posts stop at 3,000 characters.');
        if (!c.trim() && !im.length && !vi.length) err(l, 'LinkedIn needs words or media.');
        if (im.length > (capsOf(k).max_images || 20)) err(l, 'LinkedIn takes up to ' + (capsOf(k).max_images || 20) + ' photos.');
        if (vi.length > 1 || (vi.length && im.length)) err(l, 'LinkedIn takes one video, or photos, not both.');
        if (!out.p[k].some(function (x) { return x.k !== 'k'; })) ok(l, 'LinkedIn: ' + (vi.length ? 'a video' : im.length ? 'a photo' : 'a text post') + '.');
      } else if (k === 'tiktok') {
        if (!vi.length) warn(l, im.length ? 'TikTok photo posts come to you as a reminder to post in the app.' : 'TikTok needs a video.');
        var ttMax = capsOf(k).max_video_s || 600; if (vi.length && dur != null && dur > ttMax) err(l, 'TikTok videos run up to ' + Math.round(ttMax / 60) + ' minutes.');
        if (vi.length) ok(l, md === 'drafts' ? 'TikTok: the video lands in WA\u2019s TikTok drafts; add sound and post it in the app.' : 'TikTok: a video.');
        if (tagsOf(c).length > 5) warn(l, 'TikTok works best with 3 to 5 hashtags.');
      } else if (k === 'youtube') {
        if (!vi.length) err(l, 'A YouTube Short needs a video.');
        else if (dur != null && dur > (capsOf(k).max_video_s || 180)) err(l, 'Shorts run up to ' + Math.round((capsOf(k).max_video_s || 180) / 60) + ' minutes; this video is ' + Math.round(dur) + ' seconds.');
        else { var r = ratioOf(vi[0]); if (r != null && r > 1.01) warn(l, 'Shorts are vertical or square; this video is wide.'); ok(l, 'YouTube: a Short.'); }
        var yt = (e.variants.youtube || {}).title || e.title; if (!String(yt || '').trim()) warn(l, 'Give the Short a title (Customize, YouTube), or the post\u2019s name is used.');
      }
      /* wave277: the registry's caps, only for the keys a platform's caps carry */
      var cp = capsOf(k), said = function (re) { return l.some(function (x) { return re.test(x.t); }); };
      if (vi.length && cp.video === false) err(l, nm + ' does not take video.');
      else if (vi.length && cp.max_video_s && !(k === 'facebook' && kind !== 'reel') && dur != null && dur > cp.max_video_s && !said(/run up to|run from/)) err(l, nm + ' videos run up to ' + (cp.max_video_s % 60 === 0 ? Math.round(cp.max_video_s / 60) + ' minutes' : cp.max_video_s + ' seconds') + '; this video is ' + Math.round(dur) + ' seconds.');
      if (im.length && cp.images === false && !said(/reminder to post in the app/)) warn(l, nm + ' does not take photos; the photos are left out.');
      if (md === 'reminder') warn(l, nm + ' is not connected: at the post\u2019s time the owner gets a reminder to post it by hand.');
    });
    /* the old names some waves read */
    out.ig = out.p.instagram || []; out.fb = out.p.facebook || [];
    return out;
  }
  function valHtml(v) {
    var rows = v.all.slice(); PORDER.forEach(function (k) { (v.p[k] || []).forEach(function (x) { rows.push(x); }); });
    return rows.length ? '<div class="val">' + rows.map(function (x) { return '<div class="' + x.k + '">' + (x.k === 'e' ? '\u2715 ' : x.k === 'w' ? '! ' : '\u2713 ') + E(x.t) + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
  }
  function thumbHtml(m, i) {
    var r = ratioOf(m); var isV = String(m.kind || m.mime || '').indexOf('video') >= 0; var bad = !isV && r != null && !igRatioOk(r) && C.edit && C.edit.platforms.indexOf('instagram') >= 0;
    return '<div class="th" draggable="' + (readOnly() ? 'false' : 'true') + '" data-w121mi="' + i + '">' + (isV ? '<video src="' + E(m.local || m.url || '') + '" muted preload="metadata"></video>' : '<img src="' + E(m.local || m.url || '') + '" alt="' + E(m.alt || '') + '">') +
      (r ? '<span class="rt' + (bad ? ' bad' : '') + '">' + E(isV ? (m.dur ? Math.round(m.dur) + ' s' : 'video') : ratioLabel(r)) + '</span>' : '') + (i === 0 && C.edit && C.edit.media.length > 1 ? '<span class="n1">cover</span>' : '') +
      (readOnly() ? '' : '<button class="rm" data-w121mrm="' + i + '" title="Remove">\u2715</button>') + (m.uploading ? '<div class="up">Uploading\u2026</div>' : '') +
      (!isV && !m.uploading ? '<button class="altb' + (m.alt ? ' ok' : '') + '" data-w225alt="' + i + '" title="Alt text and people tags">' + (m.alt ? 'ALT \u2713' : 'ALT') + ((m.tags || []).length ? ' \u00b7 ' + m.tags.length + '@' : '') + '</button>' : '') +
      (!isV && !readOnly() && !m.uploading ? '<div class="crop">' + [['0.8', '4:5'], ['1', '1:1'], ['1.91', '1.91'], ['0.5625', '9:16']].map(function (c) { return '<button data-w121crop="' + i + '" data-r="' + c[0] + '" title="Crop to ' + c[1] + '">' + c[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '<div class="meta">' + (m.size ? num(m.size / 1024) + ' KB' : '') + '</div>') + '</div>';
  }
  /* wave225d: alt text and people tags for one photo */
  function altHtml(e) {

    return '<div class="w225alt"><div class="pic" data-w225pic="' + i + '"><img src="' + E(m.local || m.url || '') + '" alt="">' + (m.tags || []).map(function (t) { return '<b style="left:' + (t.x * 100).toFixed(1) + '%;top:' + (t.y * 100).toFixed(1) + '%">@' + E(t.username) + '</b>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div><span class="lbl">Alt text (photo ' + (i + 1) + ')</span><textarea id="w225alttext" maxlength="1000" placeholder="What the photo shows, for people using screen readers: who, what, where."' + (ro ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + E(m.alt || '') + '</textarea>' +
      (ro ? '' : '<div class="w225lib"><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w225altai="' + i + '"' + (e.altBusy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (e.altBusy ? 'Writing\u2026' : 'Write it with the Hub') + '</button></div>') +

  function linkify(s) { return E(s).replace(/(#[\w\u00c0-\u024f]+)/g, '<span class="ht">$1</span>'); }
  function previewHtml(e) {

    var html = '';

      else {
        var r0 = vi.length ? 0.5625 : (ratioOf(im[0]) || 1); r0 = Math.max(0.8, Math.min(1.91, r0)); if (vi.length) r0 = 0.5625;
        var short = cap.length > 125 ? cap.slice(0, 125) : cap;

      }

  /* wave225d: the approval, at the top of the composer */
  function apHtml(e) {
    var ap = e.approval_status || 'none', out = ['published', 'posted', 'publishing'].indexOf(e.status) >= 0;
    if (out || !staff()) return '';
    if (ap === 'pending') {
      var who0 = e.submitted_by ? String(e.submitted_by).split('@')[0] : 'Someone';
      if (isPub()) return '<div class="w225ap pending"><b>' + E(who0) + ' sent this for approval.</b>' + (e.approval_note ? E(e.approval_note) : 'Check the caption, the media and the date, then approve it or send it back with a note.') + '<textarea id="w225apnote" placeholder="A note for ' + E(who0) + ' (needed to send it back)">' + E(e.apNote || '') + '</textarea><div class="ac"><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w225decide="changes">Send it back</button><button class="btn mini go" data-w225decide="approve">Approve' + (e.date ? ' and schedule' : '') + '</button></div></div>';
      return '<div class="w225ap pending"><b>Waiting for approval.</b>A marketing lead or an admin approves it; you hear back in the Hub and on Slack. You can still edit it.</div>';
    }
    if (ap === 'changes') return '<div class="w225ap changes"><b>Changes asked.</b>' + E(e.approval_note || 'See the note from the approver.') + ' Make the changes, then send it for approval again.</div>';
    if (ap === 'approved' && !isPub()) return '<div class="w225ap approved"><b>Approved.</b>You can schedule it. Changing the caption, the media or the platforms sends it back for approval.</div>';
    if (!isPub()) return '<div class="w225ap pending" style="background:rgba(16,63,57,.05);color:var(--wa-text)"><b>Send it for approval when it is ready.</b>Marketing leads and admins approve posts before they go out.</div>';
    return '';
  }

  function ownerOpts(cur) {
    var ms = (typeof DB_MEMBERS !== 'undefined' ? DB_MEMBERS : []).filter(function (m) { return m && m.active !== false && m.name && !/\btest\b/i.test(m.name); });
    ms.sort(function (a, b) { var am = a.department === 'marketing' ? 0 : 1, bm = b.department === 'marketing' ? 0 : 1; return am - bm || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); });
    var html = '<option value="">No owner</option>'; var seen = false;
    ms.forEach(function (m) { if (m.name === cur) seen = true; html += '<option' + (m.name === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + E(m.name) + '</option>'; });
    if (cur && !seen) html += '<option selected>' + E(cur) + '</option>';
    return html;
  }
  function campaigns() { var s = {}; C.posts.forEach(function (p) { if (p.campaign) s[p.campaign] = 1; }); return Object.keys(s).sort(); }
  /* wave136: where an idea came from, and putting it in the bank */
  function ideaBox(e) {
    var p = e && e.id ? C.posts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(e.id); })[0] : null, h = '';
    if (p && (p.source_transcript_id || p.idea_for || p.suggested_by)) {
      var s = srcOf(p), bits = [];
      if (p.source_transcript_id) bits.push('From the meeting <b>' + E((s && s.title) || 'a meeting') + '</b>' + (s && s.date ? ' (' + E(shortDay(s.date)) + ')' : ''));
      if (p.suggested_by) bits.push((bits.length ? 'raised by ' : 'Raised by ') + E(p.suggested_by));
      if (p.idea_for) bits.push((bits.length ? 'for ' : 'For ') + E(shortDay(p.idea_for)));
      h += '<div class="w136src">' + bits.join(', ') + '.' + (p.source_transcript_id ? ' <a href="#tx/' + E(p.source_transcript_id) + '" data-w136tx="1">Open the meeting</a>' : '') + '</div>';
    }
    if (e && e.bank) {
      h += '<div class="w136bank"><label>Put this idea in the bank</label><p>It leaves the calendar and comes back to the Ideas on the day you pick. The bank page lists it with the rest of the work that can wait.</p>'
        + '<div class="r2"><input type="date" id="w136until" value="' + E(e.bank.until) + '" min="' + E(addDays(today(), 1)) + '" aria-label="Comes back on"><input type="text" id="w136note" maxlength="300" placeholder="Why it waits (optional), such as For Giving Tuesday" value="' + E(e.bank.note || '') + '" aria-label="Why it waits"></div>'
        + '<div class="ac"><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w136bankx="1">Cancel</button><button class="btn mini go" data-w136bankgo="1"' + (e.bank.busy ? ' disabled' : '') + '>Put it in the bank</button></div></div>';
    }
    return h;
  }
  function bankErr(x) {
    var m = String((x && (x.message || x.details)) || x || '');
    if (/staff only/i.test(m)) return 'Your access can plan posts but not move ideas to the bank.';
    if (/after today/i.test(m)) return 'Pick a day after today.';
    if (/three years/i.test(m)) return 'Pick a day in the next three years.';
    if (/without a date/i.test(m)) return 'Only an idea without a date can go in the bank.';
    return 'Could not put it in the bank: ' + m;
  }
  async function bankIdea() {
    var ed = C.edit; if (!ed || !ed.bank || !ed.id) return;
    var u = document.getElementById('w136until'), n = document.getElementById('w136note');
    if (u) ed.bank.until = u.value; if (n) ed.bank.note = n.value;
    if (!ed.bank.until || ed.bank.until <= today()) { toastS('Pick a day after today'); return; }
    if (ed.dirty) { try { readForm(); await saveRow(ed.id, rowFromEdit(ed)); ed.dirty = false; } catch (x) { toastS('Could not save the idea first: ' + (x.message || x)); return; } }
    ed.bank.busy = true; paintDrawer();
    try {
      var id = ed.id, until = ed.bank.until;
      var r = await db.rpc('bank_idea', { p_id: Number(id), p_until: until, p_note: String(ed.bank.note || '').trim() || null });
      if (r.error) throw r.error;
      var p = C.posts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(id); })[0];
      C.posts = C.posts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) !== String(id); });
      if (p) { p.banked_at = new Date().toISOString(); p.bank_until = until; (C.banked = C.banked || []).push(p); }
      closeComposer(true);
      C.undo = { id: id, text: 'In the bank until ' + shortDay(until), fn: async function () { var r2 = await db.rpc('bank_undo', { p_kind: 'idea', p_id: Number(id) }); if (r2.error) throw r2.error; await load(true); } };
      clearTimeout(C.undoT); C.undoT = setTimeout(function () { C.undo = null; paint(); }, 7000);
      paint();
    } catch (x) { if (C.edit && C.edit.bank) { C.edit.bank.busy = false; paintDrawer(); } toastS(bankErr(x)); }
  }

  function footerHtml(e, v, pubOk, ro) {
    if (!staff()) return '<footer><span class="ro">Read only.</span><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn mini ghost" data-w121close="1">Close</button></footer>';
    if (ro) return '<footer>' + (e.id ? '<button class="lnk" data-w121dup="1">Duplicate</button>' : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' + (e.status === 'posted' || e.status === 'published' ? '<button class="lnk" data-w121reopen="1" title="Back to a draft (does not remove the post from the platforms)">Back to draft</button>' : '') + '<button class="btn mini ghost" data-w121close="1">Close</button></footer>';
    var bad = v.errors > 0; var hasDate = !!(e.date && e.time);
    var parts = '<footer>' + (e.id ? '<button class="lnk del" data-w121del="1">Delete</button><button class="lnk" data-w121dup="1">Duplicate</button>' + (!e.date && e.status === 'draft' && !e.bank ? '<button class="lnk" data-w136bank="1" title="Out of the calendar until a day you pick">Put in the bank</button>' : '') : '') + '<span class="sp"></span>' +
      '<button class="btn mini ghost" data-w121save="draft">Save draft</button>';
    /* wave225d: who publishes (marketing leads and admins) schedules and publishes; everyone else sends it for approval first */
    var approved = e.approval_status === 'approved';
    if (!isPub() && !approved) {
      if (e.approval_status !== 'pending') parts += '<button class="btn mini go" data-w121save="submit"' + (bad ? ' disabled title="Fix the items marked \u2715 first"' : '') + (hasDate ? ' title="Keeps the date: once approved it is scheduled"' : '') + '>' + (hasDate ? 'Schedule for approval' : 'Send for approval') + '</button>';
      return parts + '<button class="lnk" data-w121man="1">Mark as posted</button></footer>';
    }
    if (hasDate) parts += '<button class="btn mini go" data-w121save="schedule"' + (bad ? ' disabled title="Fix the items marked \u2715 first"' : '') + '>' + (pubOk ? 'Schedule' : 'Plan it') + '</button>';
    if (pubOk) parts += '<button class="btn mini pub" data-w121save="publish"' + (bad ? ' disabled title="Fix the items marked \u2715 first"' : '') + '>' + (e.status === 'failed' ? 'Try again now' : 'Publish now') + '</button>';
    if (e.status !== 'draft' || hasDate || !pubOk) parts += '<button class="lnk" data-w121man="1">Mark as posted</button>';
    return parts + '</footer>';
  }
  function readForm() {
    var e = C.edit; if (!e) return;
    var g = function (id) { var x = document.getElementById(id); return x ? x.value : null; };
    var c = function (id) { var x = document.getElementById(id); return x ? !!x.checked : null; };
    var v;
    if ((v = g('w121title')) != null) e.title = v;
    if ((v = g('w121cap')) != null) e.caption = v;
    if ((v = g('w121link')) != null) e.link_url = v.trim();
    if ((v = g('w121date')) != null) e.date = v;
    if ((v = g('w121time')) != null) e.time = v;
    if ((v = g('w121owner')) != null) e.owner = v;
    if ((v = g('w121camp')) != null) e.campaign = v.trim();
    if ((v = g('w121notes')) != null) e.notes = v;
    /* wave225d */
    if ((v = g('w225fc')) != null) e.first_comment = v;
    if ((v = c('w225track')) != null) e.track_links = v;
    if ((v = g('w225utm')) != null) e.utm_campaign = v.trim();
    /* wave241 */
    if ((v = g('w241canva')) != null) e.canva_url = v.trim();
    if ((v = g('w241partner')) != null) e.partner_id = v ? Number(v) : null;
    if ((v = c('w241art')) != null) e.article = v;
    if ((v = g('w225rep')) != null) e.repeat = v;
    if ((v = g('w225rept')) != null) e.repeat_times = Math.max(2, Math.min(52, Number(v) || 4));
    if ((v = g('w225apnote')) != null) e.apNote = v;
    if (e.vtab && e.platforms.indexOf(e.vtab) >= 0) {
      var vv = vOf(e, e.vtab);
      if ((v = g('w225vcap')) != null) { if (String(v).trim()) vv.caption = v; else delete vv.caption; }
      if ((v = g('w225vkind')) != null) vv.kind = v;
      if ((v = g('w225vfc')) != null) { if (String(v).trim()) vv.first_comment = v; else delete vv.first_comment; }
      if ((v = g('w225vcol')) != null) vv.collaborators = String(v).split(/[\s,]+/).map(function (x) { return x.replace(/^@/, '').trim(); }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 5);
      if ((v = g('w225vcov')) != null) { if (String(v).trim() === '') delete vv.cover_ms; else vv.cover_ms = Math.round(Number(v) * 1000) || 0; }
      if ((v = c('w225vstf')) != null) vv.share_to_feed = v;
      if ((v = g('w225vyt')) != null) vv.title = v;
      if ((v = g('w225vprv')) != null) vv.privacy = v;
      if ((v = c('w225vmfk')) != null) vv.made_for_kids = v;
    }
    if (e.altIdx != null && e.media[e.altIdx] && (v = g('w225alttext')) != null) e.media[e.altIdx].alt = v;
  }
  function rowFromEdit(e) {
    var variants = {}; Object.keys(e.variants || {}).forEach(function (k) { if (e.platforms.indexOf(k) >= 0) { var vv = Object.assign({}, e.variants[k]); Object.keys(vv).forEach(function (x) { if (vv[x] === '' || vv[x] == null) delete vv[x]; }); if (Object.keys(vv).length) variants[k] = vv; } });
    return { title: e.title.trim() || null, platforms: e.platforms.slice(), format: e.article && e.platforms.indexOf('linkedin') >= 0 ? 'article' : vids(e).length ? 'reel' : imgs(e).length > 1 ? 'carousel' : imgs(e).length ? 'post' : e.link_url ? 'link' : 'post', canva_url: String(e.canva_url || '').trim() || null, partner_id: e.partner_id || null, caption: e.caption || null, link_url: e.link_url || null,
      media: e.media.filter(function (m) { return m.url && !m.uploading; }).map(function (m) { var o = { path: m.path || null, url: m.url, mime: m.mime || null, kind: m.kind || (String(m.mime || '').indexOf('video') >= 0 ? 'video' : 'image'), w: m.w || null, h: m.h || null, size: m.size || null, dur: m.dur || null }; if (m.alt) o.alt = String(m.alt).slice(0, 1000); if (m.tags && m.tags.length) o.tags = m.tags; if (m.photo_id) o.photo_id = m.photo_id; return o; }),
      scheduled_at: e.date && e.time ? isoOf(e.date, e.time) : (e.date ? isoOf(e.date, '18:00') : null), owner: e.owner || null, campaign: e.campaign || null, notes: e.notes || null,
      variants: variants, first_comment: String(e.first_comment || '').trim() || null, tags: (e.tags || []).slice(), repeat_rule: e.repeat ? { every: e.repeat, times: e.repeat_times || 4 } : null, track_links: e.track_links !== false, utm: e.utm_campaign ? { campaign: e.utm_campaign } : null };
  }
  function saveErr(x) { var m = String((x && (x.message || x.details)) || x || ''); return /needs approval/i.test(m) ? 'A marketing lead or an admin approves this post before it is scheduled. Send it for approval.' : 'Could not save: ' + m; }
  async function saveEdit(mode) {
    readForm(); var e = C.edit; if (!e) return;
    var v = validate(e);
    if (mode !== 'draft' && v.errors) { toastS('Fix the items marked \u2715 first'); paintDrawer(); return; }
    if (mode === 'schedule' && !(e.date && e.time)) { toastS('Pick a date and a time'); return; }
    if (mode === 'schedule' && isoOf(e.date, e.time) < new Date().toISOString()) { if (!confirm('That time has passed. Plan it anyway?')) return; }
    if (mode === 'publish' && !confirm('Publish to ' + e.platforms.map(function (k) { return PL[k].label; }).join(', ') + ' now?')) return;
    var row = rowFromEdit(e);
    if (mode === 'draft' || mode === 'submit') row.status = e.status === 'scheduled' && mode === 'draft' ? 'scheduled' : (e.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'draft');
    if (mode === 'schedule') row.status = 'scheduled';
    if (mode === 'publish') row.status = e.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'draft';
    if (mode === 'schedule' || row.status !== 'failed') row.error = null;
    C.busy = true;
    try {
      var saved;
      if (e.id) { saved = await saveRow(e.id, row); logIt(e.id, 'status', e.status, row.status); }
      else { saved = await insertRow(row); e.id = saved.id; logIt(saved.id, 'created', null, (row.title || row.caption || '').slice(0, 80)); }
      e.status = row.status; e.dirty = false;
      if (mode === 'submit') {
        var sr = await db.rpc('studio_submit', { p_id: Number(e.id), p_note: String(e.apNote || '').trim() || null });
        if (sr.error) throw sr.error;
        toastS(e.date && e.time ? 'Sent for approval: the approvers are told; once approved it is scheduled for ' + niceDate(e.date) + ' at ' + e.time : 'Sent for approval: the approvers are told');
        await load(true); closeComposer(true); paint(); C.busy = false; return;
      }
      if (mode === 'publish') {
        toastS('Publishing\u2026'); paintDrawer();
        var r = await fn('social-publish', { action: 'publish', post_id: e.id });
        var errs = (r.errors || []).length;
        toastS(r.status === 'published' ? ((r.reminded || []).length ? 'Published; a reminder went out for ' + r.reminded.join(', ') : 'Published') : r.status === 'publishing' ? 'The video is processing; it goes up in a few minutes' : r.status === 'needs approval' ? 'This post needs an approval first' : 'Publishing failed' + (errs ? ': ' + r.errors[0] : ''));
        await load(true);
        var p2 = C.posts.filter(function (x) { return String(x.id) === String(e.id); })[0]; if (p2) { C.edit = fromPost(p2); paintDrawer(); } else closeComposer(true);
        C.busy = false; return;
      }
      toastS(mode === 'schedule' ? (canPublish(e.platforms) ? 'Scheduled: it goes out at ' + e.time : 'Planned for ' + niceDate(e.date) + ' at ' + e.time) : 'Saved');
      closeComposer(true); paint();
    } catch (x) { toastS(saveErr(x)); }
    C.busy = false;
  }

  /* media: upload as it arrives (photos become JPEGs), crop, reorder */
  function loadImg(src, cors) { return new Promise(function (res, rej) { var i = new Image(); if (cors) i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; i.onload = function () { res(i); }; i.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('Could not read that image')); }; i.src = src; }); }
  async function toJpeg(src, ratio, cors) {
    var img = await loadImg(src, cors); var w = img.naturalWidth, h = img.naturalHeight; var sx = 0, sy = 0, sw = w, sh = h;
    if (ratio) { if (w / h > ratio) { sw = Math.round(h * ratio); sx = Math.round((w - sw) / 2); } else { sh = Math.round(w / ratio); sy = Math.round((h - sh) / 2); } }
    var scale = Math.min(1, 1440 / sw); var cw = Math.max(1, Math.round(sw * scale)), ch = Math.max(1, Math.round(sh * scale));
    var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = cw; c.height = ch; var ctx = c.getContext('2d'); ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFAF2'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, cw, ch); ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh, 0, 0, cw, ch);
    var blob = await new Promise(function (res) { c.toBlob(function (b) { res(b); }, 'image/jpeg', 0.9); });
    if (!blob) throw new Error('Could not convert the image');
    return { blob: blob, w: cw, h: ch };
  }
  function videoMeta(url) { return new Promise(function (res) { var v = document.createElement('video'); v.preload = 'metadata'; v.muted = true; var done = false; var fin = function (x) { if (!done) { done = true; res(x); } }; v.onloadedmetadata = function () { fin({ w: v.videoWidth, h: v.videoHeight, dur: v.duration }); }; v.onerror = function () { fin({}); }; setTimeout(function () { fin({}); }, 6000); v.src = url; }); }
  function rid() { return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); }
  async function upload(blob, ext, mime) {
    var path = 'calendar/' + today().slice(0, 7) + '/' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' + rid() + '.' + ext;
    var r = await db.storage.from('content-images').upload(path, blob, { contentType: mime, upsert: false });
    if (r && r.error) throw r.error;
    var pub = db.storage.from('content-images').getPublicUrl(path);
    return { path: path, url: pub && pub.data ? pub.data.publicUrl : null };
  }
  async function addFiles(files) {
    var e = C.edit; if (!e || readOnly()) return;
    var list = Array.prototype.slice.call(files || []).filter(function (f) { return /^image\//.test(f.type) || /^video\//.test(f.type); });
    if (!list.length) { toastS('Photos (JPEG, PNG, HEIC as JPEG) or an MP4 video'); return; }
    for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      var f = list[i]; var isV = /^video\//.test(f.type); var local = URL.createObjectURL(f);
      if (isV && f.size > 50 * 1024 * 1024) { toastS(f.name + ' is over 50 MB; trim or compress it first'); continue; }
      var m = { local: local, uploading: true, kind: isV ? 'video' : 'image', mime: isV ? f.type : 'image/jpeg', size: f.size, name: f.name };
      e.media.push(m); e.dirty = true; paintDrawer();
      try {
        if (isV) { var meta = await videoMeta(local); m.w = meta.w; m.h = meta.h; m.dur = meta.dur; var up = await upload(f, (f.name.split('.').pop() || 'mp4').toLowerCase(), f.type || 'video/mp4'); m.path = up.path; m.url = up.url; }
        else { var j = await toJpeg(local, null, false); m.w = j.w; m.h = j.h; m.size = j.blob.size; var up2 = await upload(j.blob, 'jpg', 'image/jpeg'); m.path = up2.path; m.url = up2.url; m.local = URL.createObjectURL(j.blob); m.file = j.blob; }
        m.uploading = false;
      } catch (x) { e.media = e.media.filter(function (y) { return y !== m; }); toastS('Could not add ' + f.name + ': ' + (x.message || x)); }
      if (C.edit === e) paintDrawer();
    }
  }
  async function cropAt(i, ratio) {
    var e = C.edit; if (!e) return; var m = e.media[i]; if (!m) return;
    m.uploading = true; paintDrawer();
    try {
      var src = m.file ? URL.createObjectURL(m.file) : (m.local || m.url);
      var j = await toJpeg(src, ratio, !m.file && !m.local);
      var up = await upload(j.blob, 'jpg', 'image/jpeg');
      Object.assign(m, { path: up.path, url: up.url, w: j.w, h: j.h, size: j.blob.size, local: URL.createObjectURL(j.blob), file: j.blob, uploading: false }); e.dirty = true;
    } catch (x) { m.uploading = false; toastS('Could not crop it: ' + (x.message || x)); }
    paintDrawer();
  }

})();
/* =====================================================================
   WA v122 the Training academy (25/09/26)
   =====================================================================
   Steel: "revamp the trainings and allow for loom videos to be embedded
   in to trainings members open. For non video trainings, build out some
   well designed html trainings like our 8 charitable activities. A
   training on all our partners and how we collaborate with them. Apple
   feel to UI and UX with a robust academy feel to the progress they
   make. The accomplishments members make are super important. Store the
   onboarding in that page. Each training should open up wide enough to
   complete it." Then: "there should be a training for each page of the
   hub that walks them through each section of the page", and "pull
   Onboarding into its own section at the top".

   The Training page (#training) becomes the academy:
   - Onboarding first, in its own section: the person's onboarding plan
     (onboardings / onboarding_steps, the wave37 checklist and its tick
     handler), the Hub first steps (wave112 wa_onboarding via
     window.w112Api) and the policies to sign (wa_policies,
     wa_policy_signoffs), with "updated since you finished" acks.
   - A hero with three rings (onboarding, required learning, level), XP,
     level, streak, certificates and badges; Up next; learning paths
     (academy_paths, path_group) with a certificate at the end; a page
     tour for every page; the library; the trophy case; and what people
     earned recently across Welfare Avenue.
   - Every training opens in a wide sheet: Loom (and YouTube, Vimeo,
     Drive) embedded, HTML lessons from trainings.body with chapters and
     a knowledge check, readable articles for text trainings, documents
     with an inline preview, and tour intros.
   - Page tours: a spotlight coach mark engine walks each page section
     by section (the steps live in TOURS below, next to the DOM they
     point at; the catalogue rows are trainings with kind 'tour').
   - Accomplishments: XP and levels computed from progress, badges and
     certificates persisted in academy_awards (celebrated once, printable
     certificates, optional shout-out to the team), and an Academy card
     on every person page.
   - Leaders: assign any training to people, add or edit a training, and
     the HR training board is one click away. The classic page stays
     reachable ("Classic view").
   Data: wa122_academy migration (trainings kind/key/body/minutes/xp,
   training_progress started_at/pct/last_step/score/seconds/data/email,
   academy_paths, academy_awards). Everything older keeps working
   underneath, hidden while the academy is on.
   ===================================================================== */
